```css
/* ==========================================================================
   OWL — global styles: desk, themes, phone chrome, OS primitives
   ========================================================================== */

* { box-sizing: border-box; }
html, body, #root { height: 100%; }
html { -webkit-text-size-adjust: 100%; }

body {
  margin: 0;
  font-family: 'Inter', system-ui, -apple-system, 'Segoe UI', sans-serif;
  background: #05070d;
  color: #e8eefc;
  overflow: hidden;
  -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
}

button { font-family: inherit; color: inherit; }
input, textarea, select { font-family: inherit; }

/* ---------- Theme tokens ---------- */
:root {
  --desk-1: #101a30;
  --desk-2: #03060c;
  --body-1: #23262e;
  --body-2: #0a0b10;
  --chrome-1: #f2f5fa;
  --chrome-2: #8e97a8;
  --chrome-3: #3b414c;

  --screen-bg: #07101f;
  --panel: #0b1729;
  --panel-2: #12253f;
  --panel-3: #17304f;
  --text: #e9effb;
  --muted: #8ba1c4;
  --line: rgba(140, 175, 230, 0.18);
  --hl: #f6b93f;
  --hl-2: #ffd27a;
  --hl-text: #1d1405;
  --blue: #4a8cff;
  --green: #35d39a;
  --red: #f2585b;
  --screen-shadow: inset 0 0 42px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.65);
  --font-ui: 'Inter', system-ui, sans-serif;
  --font-display: 'Rajdhani', 'Inter', sans-serif;
  --radius: 10px;
  --glow: 0 0 12px rgba(246, 185, 63, 0.35);
}

[data-theme='owl-night'] { /* default above */ }

[data-theme='classic-silver'] {
  --desk-1: #2b313c;
  --desk-2: #0d1015;
  --body-1: #4b525e;
  --body-2: #15181e;
  --screen-bg: #dfe4ec;
  --panel: #eef1f6;
  --panel-2: #cfd7e2;
  --panel-3: #b6c2d3;
  --text: #17202e;
  --muted: #5c6b80;
  --line: rgba(20, 35, 60, 0.18);
  --hl: #2a6fdb;
  --hl-2: #6fa4f7;
  --hl-text: #ffffff;
  --blue: #2a6fdb;
  --screen-shadow: inset 0 0 24px rgba(30, 40, 60, 0.25);
  --glow: 0 0 10px rgba(42, 111, 219, 0.4);
}

[data-theme='forest'] {
  --desk-1: #10241a;
  --desk-2: #030806;
  --body-1: #232d27;
  --body-2: #070c09;
  --screen-bg: #071a12;
  --panel: #0b2a1d;
  --panel-2: #10412c;
  --panel-3: #14543a;
  --text: #e6f6ec;
  --muted: #8bbda4;
  --line: rgba(130, 210, 165, 0.18);
  --hl: #63d29b;
  --hl-2: #a7f2c9;
  --hl-text: #052216;
  --blue: #3fc9a0;
  --screen-shadow: inset 0 0 42px rgba(0, 20, 10, 0.6);
  --glow: 0 0 12px rgba(99, 210, 155, 0.4);
}

[data-theme='ruby'] {
  --desk-1: #2b1119;
  --desk-2: #0a0305;
  --body-1: #2c2126;
  --body-2: #0c0709;
  --screen-bg: #1a0710;
  --panel: #2a0b17;
  --panel-2: #421124;
  --panel-3: #571630;
  --text: #ffe9ee;
  --muted: #cf93a4;
  --line: rgba(255, 140, 170, 0.18);
  --hl: #ff5e7d;
  --hl-2: #ff9cb1;
  --hl-text: #2a0410;
  --blue: #ff6b8a;
  --chrome-1: #ffe8ee;
  --chrome-2: #c48e9b;
  --screen-shadow: inset 0 0 42px rgba(30, 0, 10, 0.6);
  --glow: 0 0 12px rgba(255, 94, 125, 0.45);
}

[data-theme='ocean'] {
  --desk-1: #0a1f33;
  --desk-2: #02070d;
  --body-1: #1d2a34;
  --body-2: #060b10;
  --screen-bg: #041426;
  --panel: #072033;
  --panel-2: #0b3350;
  --panel-3: #0f456b;
  --text: #e6f6ff;
  --muted: #8ab6d1;
  --line: rgba(120, 200, 255, 0.18);
  --hl: #45c8ff;
  --hl-2: #9fe6ff;
  --hl-text: #01202f;
  --blue: #37b6f0;
  --screen-shadow: inset 0 0 42px rgba(0, 10, 20, 0.6);
  --glow: 0 0 12px rgba(69, 200, 255, 0.4);
}

[data-theme='lcd'] {
  --desk-1: #1b2118;
  --desk-2: #060805;
  --body-1: #2a2f26;
  --body-2: #0a0c09;
  --screen-bg: #b9c9a4;
  --panel: #c3d1af;
  --panel-2: #aab896;
  --panel-3: #93a17f;
  --text: #1b2415;
  --muted: #4a5739;
  --line: rgba(30, 45, 20, 0.25);
  --hl: #33471f;
  --hl-2: #4b632f;
  --hl-text: #dfe8cf;
  --blue: #3c5c2c;
  --screen-shadow: inset 0 0 30px rgba(40, 60, 25, 0.35);
  --font-ui: 'Press Start 2P', monospace;
  --font-display: 'Press Start 2P', monospace;
  --glow: 0 0 8px rgba(120, 160, 90, 0.5);
}

[data-contrast='high'] {
  --line: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.5);
  --muted: #d7e2f5;
  --panel: #000;
  --panel-2: #0b0b0b;
  --panel-3: #161616;
  --text: #fff;
  --hl: #ffd400;
  --hl-text: #000;
}

/* ---------- Desk ---------- */
.desk {
  position: fixed;
  inset: 0;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 40px;
  padding: 18px;
  background:
    radial-gradient(1100px 700px at 50% 8%, rgba(246, 185, 63, 0.07), transparent 62%),
    radial-gradient(900px 700px at 78% 90%, rgba(74, 140, 255, 0.09), transparent 64%),
    linear-gradient(180deg, var(--desk-1), var(--desk-2) 76%);
  overflow: hidden;
}
.desk::after {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  background: radial-gradient(circle at 50% 45%, transparent 42%, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55) 100%);
}
.deskGlow {
  position: absolute;
  width: 70vmin;
  height: 70vmin;
  border-radius: 50%;
  filter: blur(70px);
  opacity: 0.35;
  background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(246, 185, 63, 0.25), transparent 65%);
  pointer-events: none;
}

/* ---------- Phone shell ---------- */
.phoneStage {
  position: relative;
  perspective: 1600px;
  perspective-origin: 50% 30%;
  z-index: 2;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  flex: none;
}
.phoneTilt {
  transform-style: preserve-3d;
  will-change: transform;
  transition: transform 260ms cubic-bezier(0.22, 0.61, 0.36, 1);
}
.phone {
  --pw: 424px;
  width: var(--pw);
  max-height: calc(100dvh - 34px);
  position: relative;
  padding: calc(var(--pw) * 0.028) calc(var(--pw) * 0.032) calc(var(--pw) * 0.034);
  border-radius: calc(var(--pw) * 0.085);
  background:
    linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.16), transparent 9%),
    linear-gradient(168deg, var(--body-1), var(--body-2) 46%, #05060a 100%);
  box-shadow:
    0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.22) inset,
    0 -2px 6px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.8) inset,
    0 38px 70px -18px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9),
    0 8px 22px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6),
    0 0 0 1px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.09);
  container-type: inline-size;
  user-select: none;
}
.phone::before {
  /* chrome edge highlight */
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  border-radius: inherit;
  padding: 1.5px;
  background: linear-gradient(150deg, var(--chrome-1), transparent 22%, transparent 78%, var(--chrome-2));
  -webkit-mask: linear-gradient(#000 0 0) content-box, linear-gradient(#000 0 0);
  -webkit-mask-composite: xor;
  mask-composite: exclude;
  opacity: 0.55;
  pointer-events: none;
}
.phone::after {
  /* glossy sheen */
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  border-radius: inherit;
  background: linear-gradient(118deg, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12) 0%, transparent 26%, transparent 74%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05) 100%);
  pointer-events: none;
}

.topBezel {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  padding: 0 2% 2.2cqw;
  height: 6.4cqw;
}
.earpiece {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 2px;
  padding: 2px 8px;
  border-radius: 99px;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #0b0e14, #191d25);
  box-shadow: 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.14), 0 2px 3px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.8) inset;
}
.earpiece i {
  display: block;
  width: 2px;
  height: 5px;
  border-radius: 1px;
  background: #2b323d;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 0.5px #05070a;
}
.owlPlate {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 1.6cqw;
  color: #cfd7e6;
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: 0.34em;
  font-size: 2.6cqw;
  text-shadow: 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2), 0 -1px 0 #000;
  opacity: 0.9;
}
.sensorDot {
  width: 1.6cqw;
  height: 1.6cqw;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: radial-gradient(circle at 35% 30%, #3d4a5e, #070a0f 70%);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 0.5cqw rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4);
}

/* ---------- Screen ---------- */
.screenBezel {
  position: relative;
  border-radius: calc(var(--pw) * 0.038);
  padding: calc(var(--pw) * 0.016);
  background: linear-gradient(170deg, #05070b, #12161d 55%, #04050a);
  box-shadow:
    0 2px 4px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1) inset,
    0 -3px 8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9) inset,
    0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.16);
}
.screenClip {
  position: relative;
  aspect-ratio: 4 / 3;
  width: 100%;
  border-radius: calc(var(--pw) * 0.024);
  overflow: hidden;
  background: #000;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px #000, 0 0 20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9) inset;
}
.screenScale {
  width: 480px;
  height: 360px;
  transform-origin: top left;
}
.screenRoot {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  width: 480px;
  height: 360px;
  background: var(--screen-bg);
  color: var(--text);
  font-family: var(--font-ui);
  font-size: 13px;
  overflow: hidden;
  box-shadow: var(--screen-shadow);
  direction: ltr;
}
.screenRoot[dir='rtl'] { direction: rtl; }
.screenRoot[data-lcd='true'] { font-size: 11px; }
.screenGlass {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  z-index: 60;
  background:
    linear-gradient(122deg, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.11) 0%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03) 18%, transparent 32%),
    radial-gradient(140% 90% at 50% -20%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08), transparent 60%);
  mix-blend-mode: screen;
}
.screenRoot[style*='--dim'] { }

/* Screen inner scrollbars */
.screenRoot ::-webkit-scrollbar { width: 4px; height: 4px; }
.screenRoot ::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb { background: rgba(150, 180, 230, 0.35); border-radius: 4px; }
.screenRoot ::-webkit-scrollbar-track { background: transparent; }

/* ---------- Nav row ---------- */
.navRow {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 2.2cqw;
  padding: 2.4cqw 1.6cqw 2.2cqw;
}
.navKey {
  position: relative;
  flex: 1 1 auto;
  height: 7.4cqw;
  max-width: 13cqw;
  border-radius: 2cqw;
  border: none;
  cursor: pointer;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #31363f, #14171d 60%, #0a0c10);
  box-shadow:
    0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.22) inset,
    0 -2px 3px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.8) inset,
    0 2px 0 #05070a,
    0 3px 5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6);
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  transition: transform 70ms ease, box-shadow 70ms ease, filter 140ms ease;
  padding: 0;
}
.navKey:hover { filter: brightness(1.18); }
.navKey:active, .navKey.isDown {
  transform: translateY(1.4px) scale(0.985);
  box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9) inset, 0 0 10px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
}
.navKey.call { color: #5fe3a1; }
.navKey.end { color: #ff6b6e; }
.navKey.menu { color: var(--hl-2); }
.navKey.back { color: #d7e3f5; }
.navKey svg { width: 4.4cqw; height: 4.4cqw; filter: drop-shadow(0 1px 1px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9)); }
.navKey .navBadge {
  position: absolute;
  bottom: 0.5cqw;
  font-size: 1.9cqw;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
  color: rgba(220, 232, 248, 0.42);
  font-weight: 600;
}

/* Trackpad */
.trackpadWrap {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  width: 16.5cqw;
  height: 16.5cqw;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  position: relative;
  margin-top: -0.6cqw;
}
.trackpadRing {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  border-radius: 2.4cqw;
  background: conic-gradient(from 210deg, #f7fafc, #7d8798 18%, #fdfefe 34%, #616b7c 52%, #e8eef6 68%, #5b6577 84%, #f7fafc);
  box-shadow: 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.5), 0 3px 7px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.75);
  padding: 0.55cqw;
}
.trackpadRing::after {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0.55cqw;
  border-radius: 2cqw;
  background: radial-gradient(120% 100% at 50% 0%, #2c333e, #0b0e13 70%);
  box-shadow: 0 3px 8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9) inset;
}
.trackpad {
  position: relative;
  width: 12.6cqw;
  height: 12.6cqw;
  border-radius: 1.8cqw;
  border: none;
  cursor: pointer;
  background:
    radial-gradient(80% 70% at 40% 25%, rgba(140, 175, 235, 0.22), transparent 70%),
    linear-gradient(160deg, #232a35, #0a0d12 70%);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06) inset, 0 2px 6px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.8) inset;
  z-index: 1;
  touch-action: none;
  transition: box-shadow 160ms ease;
}
.trackpad::after {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  border-radius: inherit;
  background: repeating-linear-gradient(45deg, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03) 0 1px, transparent 1px 3px);
}
.trackpad.glow {
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px rgba(246, 185, 63, 0.45) inset, 0 0 14px rgba(246, 185, 63, 0.35), 0 2px 6px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.8) inset;
}
.trackpad.pressed { transform: scale(0.97); }

/* Side buttons */
.sideBtn {
  position: absolute;
  width: 0.9cqw;
  border-radius: 2px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #3a404b, #0b0d11);
  cursor: pointer;
  z-index: 0;
  border: none;
  padding: 0;
  box-shadow: 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12) inset;
}
.sideBtn:active { filter: brightness(1.6); }
.sideBtn.right { right: -0.55cqw; }
.sideBtn.left { left: -0.55cqw; }

/* ---------- Keyboard ---------- */
.kb {
  margin-top: 0.6cqw;
  padding: 1.5cqw 0 0.4cqw;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.9cqw;
  border-top: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
  box-shadow: 0 -1px 0 rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.7);
}
.kbRow {
  display: flex;
  gap: 0.7cqw;
  width: 100%;
}
.kbRow .key:last-child { }
.key {
  position: relative;
  flex: 1 1 0;
  min-width: 0;
  height: 4.45cqw;
  padding: 0;
  border: none;
  border-radius: 0.9cqw;
  cursor: pointer;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #2b303a 0%, #1a1e25 48%, #10131a 100%);
  color: #e3eaf6;
  box-shadow:
    0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.28) inset,
    0 -1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6) inset,
    0 1.6px 0 #05070a,
    0 2.4px 4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  transition: transform 60ms ease, box-shadow 60ms ease, background 120ms ease;
  touch-action: none;
  overflow: hidden;
  font-family: var(--font-ui);
}
.key .legend {
  display: flex;
  align-items: baseline;
  gap: 0.35cqw;
  line-height: 1;
  transform: translateY(0.05cqw);
}
.key .legend b {
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 1.95cqw;
  letter-spacing: 0.01em;
  text-shadow: 0 1px 0 rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9), 0 -0.5px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.16);
}
.key .legend s {
  text-decoration: none;
  font-size: 1.15cqw;
  color: #b9c6da;
  opacity: 0.75;
  text-shadow: 0 1px 0 #000;
}
.key .arr { font-size: 1.9cqw; color: #d5e0f0; }
.key.small b, .key.small .arr { font-size: 1.35cqw; }
.key.space .legend b { font-size: 1.1cqw; letter-spacing: 0.24em; opacity: 0.6; }
.key.mod { background: linear-gradient(180deg, #343a45, #1d222a 55%, #12151b); }
.key.lit {
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #6b5a2c, #4c3d18 55%, #33290f);
  color: #ffe6a8;
  box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(246, 185, 63, 0.45), 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 240, 200, 0.5) inset, 0 1px 0 #000;
}
.key.down {
  transform: translateY(1.4px);
  box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.8) inset, 0 0 8px rgba(246, 185, 63, 0.15);
}
.key.on { color: var(--hl-2); }
.leds {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 1.6cqw;
  justify-content: flex-end;
  padding: 0.2cqw 0.4cqw 0;
  font-size: 1.4cqw;
  color: #8694a9;
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
}
.led {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.5cqw;
}
.led i {
  width: 1.1cqw;
  height: 1.1cqw;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: #1b2028;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 0.4cqw rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5) inset;
}
.led.on i { background: #7dfab2; box-shadow: 0 0 6px #38e58c; }
.led.on { color: #b9f8d4; }

/* key preview bubble for touch */
.keyPreview {
  position: fixed;
  transform: translate(-50%, -140%);
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #fdfefe, #cfd8e6);
  color: #0a0e15;
  font-weight: 700;
  font-size: 16px;
  padding: 5px 11px;
  border-radius: 7px;
  box-shadow: 0 8px 18px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55);
  pointer-events: none;
  z-index: 9999;
  font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif;
}

/* Desktop helper panel */
.helper {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 2;
  width: min(330px, 30vw);
  color: #cfe0f7;
}
.helper h1 {
  font-family: 'Rajdhani', 'Inter', sans-serif;
  font-size: 42px;
  letter-spacing: 0.16em;
  margin: 0 0 2px;
  line-height: 1;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #fff, #f6b93f 70%, #b57c14);
  -webkit-background-clip: text;
  background-clip: text;
  color: transparent;
  text-shadow: 0 2px 24px rgba(246, 185, 63, 0.25);
}
.helper .tag {
  font-size: 12px;
  color: #8fa7c8;
  line-height: 1.6;
  margin: 8px 0 18px;
  max-width: 30ch;
}
.helper details {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04);
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.09);
  border-radius: 12px;
  padding: 12px 14px;
  backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
}
.helper summary {
  cursor: pointer;
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.08em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: #dbe8fa;
}
.helper table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-top: 10px; font-size: 11px; }
.helper td { padding: 3px 0; color: #9db4d3; }
.helper td:first-child { color: #f1f6ff; font-weight: 600; white-space: nowrap; padding-right: 10px; }
.helper kbd {
  background: #1b2433;
  border: 1px solid #334155;
  border-bottom-width: 2px;
  border-radius: 4px;
  padding: 1px 5px;
  font-family: inherit;
  font-size: 10px;
}
.helper a {
  color: var(--hl);
  font-size: 12px;
  text-decoration: none;
  display: inline-flex;
  gap: 6px;
  align-items: center;
  margin-top: 14px;
  border-bottom: 1px dotted rgba(246, 185, 63, 0.4);
}
.helper .legal { font-size: 10px; color: #63748c; margin-top: 14px; line-height: 1.5; }

@media (max-width: 1100px) {
  .helper { display: none; }
}
@media (pointer: coarse), (max-width: 620px) {
  .desk { padding: 0; gap: 0; }
  .phoneStage { width: 100%; height: 100%; }
  .phone {
    --pw: 100vw;
    width: 100vw;
    height: 100dvh;
    max-height: none;
    border-radius: 0;
    border: none;
    padding: max(2px, env(safe-area-inset-top)) 2px max(2px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom));
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 1.4cqw;
  }
  .phone::before, .phone::after { display: none; }
  .topBezel { height: 4.2cqw; padding-bottom: 1cqw; }
  .key { height: 4.1cqw; }
  .navKey { height: 6.4cqw; }
  .helper { display: none; }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  * { animation-duration: 0.001ms !important; transition-duration: 0.001ms !important; }
  .phoneTilt { transition: none; }
}

/* ==========================================================================
   OS UI primitives (inside 480x360 screen)
   ========================================================================== */
.owlScreenShell {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  background: var(--screen-bg);
}
[data-nav] { outline: none; }
[data-nav]:focus { outline: none; }
[data-nav]:focus-visible { outline: none; }
.navFocus {
  position: relative;
}
.navFocus::after {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  inset: -1px;
  border-radius: 5px;
  border: 1.5px solid var(--hl);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5), 0 0 9px var(--hl);
  pointer-events: none;
  z-index: 5;
}
.screenRoot.navFocusVisible [data-nav]:focus::after {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  inset: -1px;
  border-radius: 5px;
  border: 1.5px solid var(--hl);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5), 0 0 9px var(--hl);
  pointer-events: none;
  z-index: 5;
}

/* status bar */
.statusbar {
  flex: none;
  height: 21px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 7px;
  padding: 0 7px;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35), rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08));
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
  font-size: 9.5px;
  color: var(--text);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
  z-index: 20;
}
.statusbar .grow { flex: 1; }
.statusbar .sbItem { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 3px; opacity: 0.92; }
.statusbar .sbBtn { background: none; border: 0; padding: 0 1px; color: inherit; cursor: pointer; display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 3px; font-size: 9.5px; }
.statusbar .carrier { font-family: var(--font-display); letter-spacing: 0.16em; font-size: 9px; opacity: 0.8; }
.batt {
  width: 15px; height: 8px; border: 1.2px solid currentColor; border-radius: 2px; position: relative; opacity: 0.9;
}
.batt::after { content: ''; position: absolute; right: -3px; top: 2px; width: 1.6px; height: 3.4px; background: currentColor; border-radius: 0 1px 1px 0; }
.batt i { position: absolute; inset: 1px; background: var(--green); border-radius: 1px; }
.batt.low i { background: var(--red); }

/* app frame */
.appFrame {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 21px 0 0;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  overflow: hidden;
  animation: appIn 170ms ease-out;
}
@keyframes appIn { from { opacity: 0; transform: scale(0.985) translateY(4px); } to { opacity: 1; transform: none; } }
.appHeader {
  flex: none;
  height: 24px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  padding: 0 7px;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--panel-2), var(--panel));
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: 0.05em;
  font-size: 12.5px;
  color: var(--text);
}
.appHeader .title { flex: 1; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
.appHeader .hbtn {
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08), transparent);
  color: var(--text);
  font-size: 9px;
  border-radius: 4px;
  padding: 2px 5px;
  cursor: pointer;
  font-weight: 600;
}
.appBody { flex: 1; min-height: 0; overflow: auto; position: relative; }
.appBody.pad { padding: 7px; }
.owlTabBar {
  flex: none;
  display: flex;
  gap: 2px;
  padding: 3px 4px;
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
  overflow-x: auto;
}
.owlTab {
  flex: 1 0 auto;
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  background: none;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 9.5px;
  font-weight: 600;
  padding: 2px 6px;
  border-radius: 4px;
  cursor: pointer;
  white-space: nowrap;
}
.owlTab.active { background: var(--hl); color: var(--hl-text); border-color: var(--hl-2); }
.owlSection { font-size: 9px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.1em; color: var(--muted); padding: 5px 6px 3px; }
.card {
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--panel), rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15));
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 6px;
  padding: 7px;
}
.rowBtn {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  width: 100%;
  text-align: left;
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
  background: none;
  color: var(--text);
  padding: 6px 7px;
  font-size: 11.5px;
  cursor: pointer;
  min-height: 26px;
}
.rowBtn:hover { background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04); }
.rowBtn:focus { background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--hl-2), var(--hl)); color: var(--hl-text); }
.rowBtn .meta { margin-left: auto; font-size: 9.5px; opacity: 0.7; white-space: nowrap; }
.rowBtn:focus .meta { opacity: 0.9; }
.pill {
  display: inline-block;
  font-size: 8.5px;
  font-weight: 700;
  padding: 1px 5px;
  border-radius: 99px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  color: var(--text);
  letter-spacing: 0.04em;
}
.pill.amber { background: var(--hl); color: var(--hl-text); }
.pill.red { background: var(--red); color: #fff; }
.pill.green { background: var(--green); color: #06231a; }

.field {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 4px;
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.28);
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 5px;
  padding: 3px 6px;
  font-size: 11.5px;
  color: var(--text);
  min-height: 24px;
  width: 100%;
  cursor: text;
}
[data-theme='classic-silver'] .field, [data-theme='lcd'] .field { background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.55); }
.field.placeholder { color: var(--muted); }
.field .caret {
  display: inline-block;
  width: 1.4px;
  height: 12px;
  background: var(--hl);
  animation: blink 1s steps(1) infinite;
  vertical-align: middle;
  margin-left: 0.5px;
}
@keyframes blink { 50% { opacity: 0; } }
.field:focus { border-color: var(--hl); box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px var(--hl) inset; }
.fieldLabel { font-size: 9px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.08em; color: var(--muted); margin: 5px 0 2px; }
textarea.field { resize: none; min-height: 48px; align-items: flex-start; }

.btn {
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--panel-3), var(--panel));
  color: var(--text);
  border-radius: 5px;
  padding: 3px 9px;
  font-size: 10.5px;
  font-weight: 600;
  cursor: pointer;
  min-height: 22px;
}
.btn:hover { filter: brightness(1.15); }
.btn:focus { background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--hl-2), var(--hl)); color: var(--hl-text); border-color: var(--hl-2); }
.btn.primary { background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--hl-2), var(--hl)); color: var(--hl-text); border-color: var(--hl-2); }
.btn.primary:focus { outline: 1.5px solid var(--text); }
.btn.danger { background: linear-gradient(180deg, #ff7b7e, var(--red)); color: #fff; border-color: #ff9b9d; }
.btn:disabled { opacity: 0.45; cursor: default; }
.btnRow { display: flex; gap: 5px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.iconBtn {
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  color: var(--text);
  border-radius: 4px;
  width: 21px; height: 21px;
  display: grid; place-items: center;
  cursor: pointer;
  font-size: 11px;
  padding: 0;
  flex: none;
}
.iconBtn:focus { background: var(--hl); color: var(--hl-text); }

.empty {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  text-align: center;
  gap: 6px;
  padding: 24px 16px;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 11px;
  line-height: 1.5;
}
.empty .big { font-size: 22px; opacity: 0.8; }

.spinner {
  width: 18px; height: 18px; border-radius: 50%;
  border: 2px solid var(--line);
  border-top-color: var(--hl);
  animation: spin 700ms linear infinite;
}
@keyframes spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg); } }

/* dialog + toast */
.dialogLayer {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  z-index: 90;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55);
  backdrop-filter: blur(1.5px);
  animation: fadeIn 120ms ease;
  padding: 14px;
}
@keyframes fadeIn { from { opacity: 0; } }
.dialog {
  width: 100%;
  max-width: 290px;
  border-radius: 7px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--panel-2), var(--panel));
  box-shadow: 0 14px 34px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.7), 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15) inset;
  overflow: hidden;
}
.dialog h3 { margin: 0; padding: 8px 10px 6px; font-family: var(--font-display); font-size: 13px; letter-spacing: 0.04em; }
.dialog .dBody { padding: 0 10px 10px; font-size: 11px; color: var(--muted); line-height: 1.5; }
.dialog .dBody strong { color: var(--text); }
.dialog .dActions { display: flex; gap: 5px; justify-content: flex-end; padding: 7px 8px; background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2); border-top: 1px solid var(--line); }
.toastStack {
  position: absolute;
  left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 10px;
  z-index: 95;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 5px;
  pointer-events: none;
}
.toast {
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(30, 44, 68, 0.97), rgba(10, 18, 32, 0.97));
  border: 1px solid var(--hl);
  color: var(--text);
  border-radius: 99px;
  padding: 5px 12px;
  font-size: 10.5px;
  box-shadow: 0 6px 18px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6);
  max-width: 86%;
  text-align: center;
  animation: toastIn 180ms ease;
}
@keyframes toastIn { from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(8px); } }

/* context / soft menu */
.softMenuLayer {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  z-index: 85;
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35);
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-end;
  justify-content: center;
  animation: fadeIn 100ms ease;
}
.softMenu {
  width: 78%;
  margin-bottom: 12px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  overflow: hidden;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--panel-2), var(--panel));
  box-shadow: 0 18px 40px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.75);
  animation: menuUp 140ms ease-out;
}
@keyframes menuUp { from { transform: translateY(14px); opacity: 0; } }
.softMenu .smTitle { font-size: 9px; color: var(--muted); padding: 5px 9px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.12em; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); }
.softMenu button {
  display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 7px;
  width: 100%; border: 0; background: none; color: var(--text);
  padding: 6px 9px; font-size: 11.5px; text-align: left; cursor: pointer;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
}
.softMenu button:last-child { border-bottom: 0; }
.softMenu button:focus { background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--hl-2), var(--hl)); color: var(--hl-text); }
.softMenu button.danger { color: var(--red); }
.softMenu button.danger:focus { color: #fff; background: var(--red); }

/* home screen */
.home { position: absolute; inset: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; }
.homeTop {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 3;
  padding: 5px 9px 4px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  gap: 8px;
  text-shadow: 0 1px 3px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.8);
}
.homeClock { flex: 1; }
.homeClock .time {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-size: 29px;
  font-weight: 700;
  line-height: 0.95;
  letter-spacing: 0.01em;
  color: #fff;
}
.homeClock .date { font-size: 10px; opacity: 0.88; margin-top: 1px; color: #fff; }
.homeCounters { display: flex; gap: 4px; margin-top: 4px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.counter {
  display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 3px;
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.28);
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4);
  color: #fff;
  border-radius: 99px;
  padding: 1px 6px 1px 4px;
  font-size: 9.5px;
  font-weight: 700;
  cursor: pointer;
  backdrop-filter: blur(3px);
}
.counter.active { border-color: var(--hl); background: var(--hl); color: var(--hl-text); }
.counter .cicon { font-size: 10px; }
.homeRight { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: flex-end; gap: 3px; }
.profileChip {
  font-size: 8px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.1em;
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4); border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.22);
  border-radius: 99px; padding: 1px 5px; color: #fff;
}
.homeMid { flex: 1; position: relative; }
.homeCaption {
  position: absolute; left: 9px; right: 9px; bottom: 4px;
  font-size: 10px; color: #fff; text-shadow: 0 1px 4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);
  font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.02em;
}
.homeCaption b { color: var(--hl-2); }
.homeSearchWrap { padding: 0 9px 5px; position: relative; z-index: 4; }
.homeSearch {
  display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 5px;
  background: rgba(6, 12, 24, 0.78);
  border: 1px solid var(--hl);
  border-radius: 99px;
  padding: 2px 8px;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 16px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55);
}
.homeSearch .field { border: 0; background: none; min-height: 20px; padding: 0; }
.searchResults {
  position: absolute; left: 9px; right: 9px; bottom: 32px;
  top: auto; max-height: 150px; overflow: auto;
  background: rgba(8, 15, 28, 0.97);
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 7px;
  box-shadow: 0 12px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.7);
  z-index: 10;
}
.dock {
  flex: none;
  position: relative;
  z-index: 4;
  padding: 4px 6px 5px;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.12), rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5));
  border-top: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12);
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(6, 1fr);
  gap: 4px;
  backdrop-filter: blur(3px);
}
.dock.twoRows { grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr); }
.dock.hidden { display: none; }
.dockItem {
  display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; gap: 2px;
  background: none; border: 1px solid transparent; border-radius: 5px;
  padding: 2px 0 1px; cursor: pointer; color: #fff;
}
.dockItem .di { width: 24px; height: 24px; display: grid; place-items: center; border-radius: 6px; }
.dockItem .dl { font-size: 8px; letter-spacing: 0.02em; opacity: 0.92; text-shadow: 0 1px 3px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9); white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; max-width: 100%; }
.dockItem:focus { border-color: var(--hl); background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35); box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px var(--hl), 0 0 12px rgba(246, 185, 63, 0.35); }
.dockItem:focus .di { transform: translateY(-1px) scale(1.06); }
.dockGrab { position: absolute; left: 50%; top: 1px; width: 28px; height: 2px; margin-left: -14px; border-radius: 2px; background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.35); }

.appIcon {
  width: 24px; height: 24px; border-radius: 6px;
  display: grid; place-items: center;
  font-size: 12px; font-weight: 700;
  box-shadow: 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.28) inset, 0 2px 5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45);
  color: #fff;
  flex: none;
  position: relative;
  overflow: hidden;
}
.appIcon::after {
  content: ''; position: absolute; inset: 0;
  background: linear-gradient(160deg, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.35), transparent 48%);
  pointer-events: none;
}
.appIcon.lg { width: 34px; height: 34px; border-radius: 9px; font-size: 17px; }

/* drawer */
.drawerLayer {
  position: absolute; inset: 0; z-index: 70;
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45);
  display: flex; flex-direction: column; justify-content: flex-end;
  animation: fadeIn 100ms ease;
}
.drawer {
  height: 86%;
  display: flex; flex-direction: column;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--panel-2), var(--panel) 40%, var(--screen-bg));
  border-top: 1px solid var(--hl);
  border-radius: 10px 10px 0 0;
  box-shadow: 0 -14px 34px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.65);
  animation: drawerUp 180ms cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.7, 0.3, 1);
  overflow: hidden;
}
@keyframes drawerUp { from { transform: translateY(100%); } }
.drawerHead { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 5px; padding: 5px 7px 3px; }
.drawerHead .grab { width: 30px; height: 3px; border-radius: 2px; background: var(--muted); opacity: 0.6; margin: 0 auto; }
.drawerTabs { display: flex; gap: 2px; padding: 0 5px 4px; overflow-x: auto; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); }
.drawerGrid {
  flex: 1; overflow: auto; padding: 6px;
  display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr); gap: 4px 2px;
}
.drawerCell {
  display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; gap: 2px;
  border: 1px solid transparent; border-radius: 6px; background: none;
  padding: 4px 1px; cursor: pointer; color: var(--text);
}
.drawerCell span { font-size: 8.5px; text-align: center; line-height: 1.15; max-width: 100%; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; }
.drawerCell:focus { background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08); border-color: var(--hl); box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(246, 185, 63, 0.25); }
.drawerCell:focus .appIcon { transform: scale(1.06); }
.folderGrid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr); gap: 6px; padding: 8px; }

/* boot / lock */
.bootScreen {
  position: absolute; inset: 0; z-index: 80;
  display: grid; place-items: center;
  background: #000; color: #cfd8e8;
}
.bootInner { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; gap: 10px; }
.bootLogo { filter: drop-shadow(0 0 18px rgba(246, 185, 63, 0.55)); animation: bootPulse 2.4s ease-in-out infinite; }
@keyframes bootPulse { 0%, 100% { filter: drop-shadow(0 0 10px rgba(246, 185, 63, 0.3)); transform: scale(0.98); } 50% { filter: drop-shadow(0 0 26px rgba(246, 185, 63, 0.75)); transform: scale(1.02); } }
.bootBar { width: 150px; height: 4px; border-radius: 3px; background: #1a2333; overflow: hidden; }
.bootBar i { display: block; height: 100%; background: linear-gradient(90deg, #b57c14, var(--hl-2)); transition: width 200ms ease; box-shadow: 0 0 8px var(--hl); }
.bootText { font-size: 9px; letter-spacing: 0.3em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #8a9bb5; }
.bootBrand { font-family: var(--font-display); letter-spacing: 0.5em; font-size: 22px; color: #e9eef8; margin-top: 2px; }
.lockScreen {
  position: absolute; inset: 0; z-index: 75;
  display: flex; flex-direction: column; justify-content: space-between;
  padding: 12px; color: #fff; overflow: hidden;
}
.lockScreen .bg { position: absolute; inset: 0; background-size: cover; background-position: center; }
.lockScreen .bg::after { content: ''; position: absolute; inset: 0; background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45), rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15) 40%, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.65)); }
.lockContent { position: relative; z-index: 2; text-align: center; padding-top: 14px; }
.lockContent .lt { font-family: var(--font-display); font-size: 46px; font-weight: 700; line-height: 0.9; text-shadow: 0 3px 12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.7); }
.lockContent .ld { font-size: 11px; opacity: 0.9; margin-top: 2px; }
.lockContent .lbadge { font-family: var(--font-display); letter-spacing: 0.35em; font-size: 10px; opacity: 0.75; margin-top: 6px; }
.lockHint { position: relative; z-index: 2; text-align: center; font-size: 10px; color: #e6eefb; text-shadow: 0 1px 4px #000; }
.lockHint kbd { background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.16); border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.3); padding: 1px 4px; border-radius: 3px; font-size: 9px; }
.lockPassWrap { display: grid; place-items: center; gap: 7px; }
.lockPassWrap .field { max-width: 150px; text-align: center; letter-spacing: 0.3em; }
.powerMenu { position: absolute; inset: 0; z-index: 88; background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.65); display: grid; place-items: center; }
.powerMenu .pmCard { width: 190px; border-radius: 8px; border: 1px solid var(--line); background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--panel-2), var(--panel)); overflow: hidden; box-shadow: 0 20px 50px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.75); }
.powerMenu h4 { margin: 0; padding: 7px 10px; font-size: 10px; letter-spacing: 0.14em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--muted); border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); }
.powerMenu button { width: 100%; border: 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); background: none; color: var(--text); padding: 7px 10px; font-size: 11.5px; text-align: left; cursor: pointer; display: flex; gap: 7px; align-items: center; }
.powerMenu button:last-child { border: 0; }
.powerMenu button:focus { background: var(--hl); color: var(--hl-text); }
.offScreen { position: absolute; inset: 0; background: #000; display: grid; place-items: center; z-index: 100; }
.offScreen .pwHint { text-align: center; color: #6e7f98; font-size: 9px; letter-spacing: 0.24em; text-transform: uppercase; }
.offScreen .pwBtn {
  margin-top: 10px; width: 34px; height: 34px; border-radius: 50%;
  border: 1px solid #3a465c; background: radial-gradient(circle at 40% 35%, #2a3444, #080c13);
  color: var(--red); display: grid; place-items: center; cursor: pointer;
  box-shadow: 0 0 18px rgba(242, 88, 91, 0.35);
  animation: powerGlow 2.6s ease-in-out infinite;
}
@keyframes powerGlow { 50% { box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(242, 88, 91, 0.65); } }
.shutdownAnim { position: absolute; inset: 0; background: #000; z-index: 99; display: grid; place-items: center; animation: fadeIn 300ms ease; }

/* volume HUD */
.volHud {
  position: absolute; top: 26px; right: 7px; z-index: 92;
  display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 5px;
  background: rgba(6, 12, 24, 0.9);
  border: 1px solid var(--hl);
  color: #fff; border-radius: 99px; padding: 3px 8px;
  font-size: 9.5px; font-weight: 600;
  box-shadow: 0 6px 18px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6);
  animation: fadeIn 120ms ease;
}
.volHud .bar { width: 44px; height: 4px; border-radius: 3px; background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.18); overflow: hidden; }
.volHud .bar i { display: block; height: 100%; background: var(--hl); }

/* apps: misc shared */
.kv { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; gap: 8px; font-size: 10.5px; padding: 4px 0; border-bottom: 1px dashed var(--line); }
.kv b { font-weight: 600; }
.listScroll { position: relative; }
.countPill { font-size: 8px; background: var(--hl); color: var(--hl-text); border-radius: 99px; padding: 0 4px; font-weight: 700; min-width: 12px; display: inline-block; text-align: center; }
.chipRow { display: flex; gap: 4px; flex-wrap: wrap; padding: 5px; }
.chip {
  font-size: 9px; padding: 2px 7px; border-radius: 99px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line); background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  color: var(--text); cursor: pointer;
}
.chip.on { background: var(--hl); color: var(--hl-text); border-color: var(--hl); }
.progress { height: 4px; background: rgba(255,255,255,0.12); border-radius: 3px; overflow: hidden; }
.progress i { display: block; height: 100%; background: var(--hl); }
.tiny { font-size: 9px; color: var(--muted); }
.mono { font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
hr.sep { border: 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--line); margin: 6px 0; }

/* recents */
.recentLayer { position: absolute; inset: 0; z-index: 84; background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6); display: flex; flex-direction: column; justify-content: center; gap: 7px; padding: 12px; }
.recentRow { display: flex; gap: 6px; overflow-x: auto; padding-bottom: 4px; }
.recentCard {
  flex: 0 0 96px; height: 110px; border-radius: 8px; overflow: hidden;
  border: 1px solid var(--line); background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--panel-2), var(--panel));
  display: flex; flex-direction: column; cursor: pointer;
}
.recentCard .rcHead { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 4px; padding: 4px; font-size: 9px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); }
.recentCard .rcBody { flex: 1; display: grid; place-items: center; font-size: 26px; opacity: 0.75; }
.recentCard:focus { border-color: var(--hl); box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(246, 185, 63, 0.4); }

/* games shared */
.gameWrap { position: absolute; inset: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; background: #0b1210; }
.gameCanvasWrap { flex: 1; display: grid; place-items: center; background: #0a0f0d; position: relative; overflow: hidden; }
.gameCanvas { image-rendering: pixelated; box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px #0f1a14, 0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6); max-width: 100%; max-height: 100%; }
.dpad {
  position: absolute; right: 6px; bottom: 6px; z-index: 6;
  display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 22px); grid-template-rows: repeat(3, 22px);
  gap: 2px; opacity: 0.85;
}
.dpad button {
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.25); background: rgba(10, 18, 26, 0.8); color: #fff;
  border-radius: 4px; font-size: 11px; cursor: pointer; padding: 0;
}
.dpad button:active { background: var(--hl); color: #000; }

/* ================================ GAMES ================================= */
.gameWrap .gameCanvasWrap { flex-direction: column; gap: 0; }
.gameCanvasWrap > canvas { display: block; }

/* grid of game cards */
.gameGrid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(196px, 1fr));
  gap: 5px;
  overflow: auto;
  padding-bottom: 4px;
}
.gameTile {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 34px 1fr;
  gap: 6px;
  align-items: start;
  text-align: left;
  padding: 6px;
  border-radius: 7px;
  cursor: pointer;
  color: var(--text);
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  background: linear-gradient(170deg, var(--panel-2), var(--panel));
  transition: border-color 140ms ease, transform 140ms ease, box-shadow 140ms ease;
}
.gameTile:hover,
.gameTile:focus-visible,
.gameTile.focus {
  border-color: var(--hl);
  transform: translateY(-1px);
  box-shadow: 0 6px 16px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45), 0 0 0 1px rgba(246, 185, 63, 0.35) inset;
  outline: none;
}
.gameTileIcon {
  width: 34px; height: 34px; border-radius: 8px;
  display: grid; place-items: center; color: #fff;
  box-shadow: inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.35), 0 2px 5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4);
}
.gameTileBody { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 1px; min-width: 0; }
.gameTileBody strong { font-family: var(--font-display); font-size: 11px; letter-spacing: 0.03em; }
.gameTileMeta { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; gap: 6px; margin-top: 2px; opacity: 0.9; }

/* solitaire */
.solitaire {
  position: absolute; inset: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 4px;
  padding: 5px; overflow: auto; border-radius: 3px;
}
.solTop { display: flex; gap: 4px; align-items: flex-start; flex: none; }
.solStock,
.solWaste,
.solFoundation {
  width: 34px; height: 46px; border-radius: 4px; position: relative;
  border: 1px dashed rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.28);
}
.solStock {
  display: grid; place-items: center; font-size: 18px; cursor: pointer;
  color: #cfe8d8; background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
}
.solStock.empty { opacity: 0.6; }
.solWaste { width: 46px; }
.solEmpty { position: absolute; inset: 0; display: grid; place-items: center; font-size: 18px; color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.3); }
.solCard {
  position: absolute; left: 0; width: 34px; height: 46px; border-radius: 4px;
  background: linear-gradient(160deg, #fdfdf8, #e6e2d4);
  border: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35);
  box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45);
  display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: flex-start; padding: 1px 2px;
  font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 700; line-height: 1;
  user-select: none;
}
.solCard.red { color: #c02c3c; }
.solCard.black { color: #1a2230; }
.solCard .solRank { font-size: 9px; }
.solCard .solSuit { font-size: 14px; align-self: center; margin-top: 2px; }
.solCard.back {
  background:
    repeating-linear-gradient(45deg, #1d4d6b 0 4px, #123a52 4px 8px);
  border: 1px solid rgba(200, 230, 255, 0.35);
}
.solCard.sel { outline: 2px solid var(--hl); outline-offset: -1px; box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(246, 185, 63, 0.6); }
.solCard.cursor { outline: 2px solid #4fc3ff; outline-offset: -1px; }
.solColumn { position: relative; flex: 1; min-width: 34px; height: 100%; min-height: 190px; }

/* game shell footer rows keep the phone's tiny type scale legible */
.gameWrap .chip { font-size: 8px; padding: 1px 5px; }
.gameWrap .btn { font-size: 9px; }
.gameWrap .tiny { font-size: 8px; }

@media (max-height: 460px) {
  .solCard, .solStock, .solWaste, .solFoundation { height: 42px; }
  .solColumn { min-height: 168px; }
}
```

### `src/os/api.ts`

```ts
/**
 * Browser-facing network helpers for Owl Web.
 * All requests are plain fetch() calls to free, CORS-enabled public APIs —
 * no keys, no tracking. The only project-owned endpoint is /api/reader, the
 * serverless reader-mode proxy.
 */

export interface SearchResult {
  title: string;
  snippet: string;
  url: string;
  domain: string;
  image?: string;
  source: 'wikipedia' | 'ddg' | 'web';
}

export interface ReaderResult {
  title: string;
  byline: string;
  content: string;
  siteName: string;
  url: string;
  excerpt: string;
}

const WIKI_REST = 'https://en.wikipedia.org/w/api.php';
const DDG = 'https://api.duckduckgo.com/';

async function fetchJson<T>(url: string, timeoutMs = 7000): Promise<T | null> {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = window.setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, { signal: controller.signal, headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } });
    if (!res.ok) return null;
    return (await res.json()) as T;
  } catch {
    return null;
  } finally {
    window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

export function domainOf(url: string): string {
  try {
    return new URL(url).hostname.replace(/^www\./, '');
  } catch {
    return url;
  }
}

export function normalizeUrl(input: string): string | null {
  const raw = input.trim();
  if (!raw) return null;
  const looksLikeUrl = /^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:\/\//i.test(raw) || /^[\w-]+(\.[\w-]+)+(\/|$)/.test(raw);
  if (!looksLikeUrl) return null;
  try {
    const withProto = /^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:\/\//i.test(raw) ? raw : `https://${raw}`;
    const url = new URL(withProto);
    if (!['http:', 'https:'].includes(url.protocol)) return null;
    return url.toString();
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/** Combined Owl Search: Wikipedia search + summaries + DuckDuckGo instant answers. */
export async function owlSearch(query: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<SearchResult[]> {
  const q = query.trim();
  if (!q) return [];
  const wikiUrl = `${WIKI_REST}?action=query&format=json&origin=*&generator=search&gsrsearch=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&gsrlimit=6&prop=extracts|pageimages&exintro=1&explaintext=1&exsentences=2&piprop=thumbnail&pithumbsize=160`;
  const ddgUrl = `${DDG}?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&format=json&no_html=1&skip_disambig=1&t=owlphone`;

  const [wiki, ddg] = await Promise.all([
    fetchJson<any>(wikiUrl),
    fetchJson<any>(ddgUrl),
  ]);

  if (signal?.aborted) return [];
  const results: SearchResult[] = [];

  if (ddg && (ddg.AbstractText || (ddg.RelatedTopics && ddg.RelatedTopics.length))) {
    if (ddg.AbstractText) {
      results.push({
        title: ddg.Heading || q,
        snippet: ddg.AbstractText,
        url: ddg.AbstractURL || `https://duckduckgo.com/?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`,
        domain: ddg.AbstractSource ? `duckduckgo.com · ${ddg.AbstractSource}` : 'duckduckgo.com',
        image: ddg.Image ? `https://duckduckgo.com${ddg.Image}` : undefined,
        source: 'ddg',
      });
    }
    (ddg.RelatedTopics || []).slice(0, 4).forEach((topic: any) => {
      if (!topic.FirstURL || !topic.Text) return;
      results.push({
        title: topic.Text.split(' - ')[0].slice(0, 90),
        snippet: topic.Text,
        url: topic.FirstURL,
        domain: domainOf(topic.FirstURL),
        source: 'ddg',
      });
    });
  }

  const pages = wiki?.query?.pages ? Object.values(wiki.query.pages as Record<string, any>) : [];
  pages.forEach((page: any) => {
    if (!page.title) return;
    const url = `https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/${encodeURIComponent(String(page.title).replace(/ /g, '_'))}`;
    results.push({
      title: page.title,
      snippet: (page.extract || '').split('\n')[0].slice(0, 240),
      url,
      domain: 'en.wikipedia.org',
      image: page.thumbnail?.source,
      source: 'wikipedia',
    });
  });

  // de-duplicate by url
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  return results.filter((r) => {
    if (seen.has(r.url)) return false;
    seen.add(r.url);
    return true;
  });
}

/** Wikimedia Commons image results for the search page. */
export async function commonsImages(query: string): Promise<{ title: string; url: string; page: string }[]> {
  const url = `https://commons.wikimedia.org/w/api.php?action=query&format=json&origin=*&generator=search&gsrsearch=${encodeURIComponent(query)}&gsrnamespace=6&gsrlimit=6&prop=imageinfo&iiprop=url&iiurlwidth=320`;
  const data = await fetchJson<any>(url);
  const pages = data?.query?.pages ? Object.values(data.query.pages as Record<string, any>) : [];
  return pages.map((p: any) => ({
    title: p.title?.replace(/^File:/, '') || 'Image',
    url: p.imageinfo?.[0]?.thumburl || p.imageinfo?.[0]?.url || '',
    page: p.imageinfo?.[0]?.descriptionurl || '',
  })).filter((i) => i.url);
}

/** Reader-mode fetch through the project's serverless function. */
export async function fetchReader(url: string): Promise<ReaderResult | null> {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`/api/reader?url=${encodeURIComponent(url)}`, { headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } });
    if (!res.ok) return null;
    const data = await res.json();
    if (!data || !data.content) return null;
    return data as ReaderResult;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/** Wikipedia page rendered directly in reader mode (always CORS-safe). */
export async function wikipediaReader(title: string): Promise<ReaderResult | null> {
  const url = `https://en.wikipedia.org/api/rest_v1/page/html/${encodeURIComponent(title.replace(/ /g, '_'))}`;
  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, { headers: { Accept: 'text/html' } });
    if (!res.ok) return null;
    const html = await res.text();
    const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
    doc.querySelectorAll('script, style, .mw-editsection, sup.reference, table.navbox, .navbox, .metadata, .hatnote').forEach((el) => el.remove());
    doc.querySelectorAll('a[href]').forEach((a) => {
      const href = a.getAttribute('href') || '';
      if (href.startsWith('./')) a.setAttribute('href', `https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/${href.slice(2)}`);
      else if (href.startsWith('/wiki/')) a.setAttribute('href', `https://en.wikipedia.org${href}`);
      a.setAttribute('data-external', '1');
    });
    doc.querySelectorAll('img[src]').forEach((img) => {
      const src = img.getAttribute('src') || '';
      if (src.startsWith('//')) img.setAttribute('src', `https:${src}`);
      else if (src.startsWith('/')) img.setAttribute('src', `https://en.wikipedia.org${src}`);
    });
    return {
      title: title.replace(/_/g, ' '),
      byline: 'Wikipedia',
      siteName: 'Wikipedia',
      url: `https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/${encodeURIComponent(title.replace(/ /g, '_'))}`,
      excerpt: '',
      content: doc.body.innerHTML,
    };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export const SEARCH_ENGINES = [
  { id: 'google', name: 'Google', url: (q: string) => `https://www.google.com/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}` },
  { id: 'bing', name: 'Bing', url: (q: string) => `https://www.bing.com/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}` },
  { id: 'ddg', name: 'DuckDuckGo', url: (q: string) => `https://duckduckgo.com/?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}` },
  { id: 'wikipedia', name: 'Wikipedia', url: (q: string) => `https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?search=${encodeURIComponent(q)}` },
];
```

```ts
export const APP_MAP: Record<string, AppMeta> = Object.fromEntries(APPS.map((a) => [a.id, a]));

export const GAME_IDS = APPS.filter((a) => a.category === 'games' && a.dock === false && a.id !== 'games').map((a) => a.id);

export const DOCK_IDS = ['messages', 'messenger', 'contacts', 'web', 'music', 'calendar'];

export const MEDIA_IDS = ['music', 'videos', 'pictures', 'voice', 'ringtones', 'radio', 'camera'];

export function appName(id: string, lang: Lang): string {
  const app = APP_MAP[id];
  if (!app) return id;
  return app.name[lang] || app.name.en;
}

export function appIcon(id: string): string {
  return APP_MAP[id]?.icon || 'info';
}

export function appColor(id: string): string {
  return APP_MAP[id]?.color || '#4a5568';
}

export function searchApps(query: string, lang: Lang): AppMeta[] {
  const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!q) return APPS;
  return APPS.filter(
    (a) => a.name.en.toLowerCase().includes(q) || a.name.ar.includes(query) || a.id.includes(q)
  );
}

export default APPS;
```

```tsx
import React, { Suspense, Component, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { useStore } from './store';
import { APP_MAP } from './appRegistry';
import { OwlLogo } from '../components/OwlLogo';
import { Icon } from '../components/Icon';

/* Error boundary: a broken app must never blank the phone. */
class AppErrorBoundary extends Component<{ children: ReactNode; appId: string }, { error: Error | null }> {
  constructor(props: { children: ReactNode; appId: string }) {
    super(props);
    this.state = { error: null };
  }
  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error) {
    return { error };
  }
  componentDidCatch(error: Error) {
    console.warn('[OWL] app crashed', this.props.appId, error);
  }
  render() {
    if (this.state.error) {
      const back = () => useStore.getState().closeApp();
      return (
        <div className="empty" style={{ paddingTop: 60 }}>
          <div className="big">🦉</div>
          <div><strong>This app hit a snag.</strong></div>
          <div className="tiny" style={{ maxWidth: 260 }}>{String(this.state.error.message || this.state.error)}</div>
          <button className="btn" onClick={() => this.setState({ error: null })}>Try again</button>
          <button className="btn primary" onClick={back}>Home screen</button>
        </div>
      );
    }
    return this.props.children;
  }
}

function Loading() {
  return (
    <div className="appFrame">
      <div className="empty" style={{ paddingTop: 80 }}>
        <OwlLogo size={34} glow />
        <div className="spinner" style={{ marginTop: 4 }} />
        <div className="tiny">Loading…</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function AppMissing({ id }: { id: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="empty">
      <div className="big">🦉</div>
      <div>Unknown application “{id}”.</div>
      <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => useStore.getState().closeApp()}>Home</button>
    </div>
  );
}

export function AppWindow() {
  const appId = useStore((s) => s.appId);
  const appParams = useStore((s) => s.appParams);
  if (!appId) return null;
  const meta = APP_MAP[appId];
  if (!meta) return <AppMissing id={appId} />;
  const Lazy = React.lazy(meta.load);
  return (
    <AppErrorBoundary appId={appId}>
      <Suspense fallback={<Loading />}>
        <Lazy {...appParams} />
      </Suspense>
    </AppErrorBoundary>
  );
}

export default AppWindow;
```

```ts
/**
 * OWL audio engine.
 * Every sound in the phone is synthesised at runtime with the Web Audio API.
 * No samples, no copyrighted music — only oscillators and noise.
 */

let ctx: AudioContext | null = null;
let master: GainNode | null = null;

export function audioContext(): AudioContext | null {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
  try {
    if (!ctx) {
      const AC = window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext;
      if (!AC) return null;
      ctx = new AC();
      master = ctx.createGain();
      master.gain.value = 0.9;
      master.connect(ctx.destination);
    }
    if (ctx.state === 'suspended') void ctx.resume();
    return ctx;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function unlockAudio() {
  audioContext();
}

function tone(
  freq: number,
  start: number,
  duration: number,
  type: OscillatorType = 'sine',
  gain = 0.18,
  slideTo?: number
) {
  const ac = audioContext();
  if (!ac || !master) return;
  const osc = ac.createOscillator();
  const g = ac.createGain();
  osc.type = type;
  osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, start);
  if (slideTo) osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(20, slideTo), start + duration);
  g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, start);
  g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(0.0002, gain), start + Math.min(0.012, duration * 0.3));
  g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, start + duration);
  osc.connect(g);
  g.connect(master);
  osc.start(start);
  osc.stop(start + duration + 0.02);
}

function noise(start: number, duration: number, gain = 0.14, hp = 1200) {
  const ac = audioContext();
  if (!ac || !master) return;
  const len = Math.max(1, Math.floor(ac.sampleRate * duration));
  const buf = ac.createBuffer(1, len, ac.sampleRate);
  const data = buf.getChannelData(0);
  for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) data[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * (1 - i / len) ** 2;
  const src = ac.createBufferSource();
  src.buffer = buf;
  const filter = ac.createBiquadFilter();
  filter.type = 'highpass';
  filter.frequency.value = hp;
  const g = ac.createGain();
  g.gain.value = gain;
  src.connect(filter);
  filter.connect(g);
  g.connect(master);
  src.start(start);
}

/* ----------------------------- UI sounds ----------------------------- */

export function keyClick(volume = 0.5) {
  const ac = audioContext();
  if (!ac || volume <= 0) return;
  const t = ac.currentTime;
  tone(2100 + Math.random() * 380, t, 0.018, 'triangle', 0.05 * volume);
  noise(t, 0.02, 0.05 * volume, 2600);
}

export function navBlip(volume = 0.5) {
  const ac = audioContext();
  if (!ac) return;
  tone(880, ac.currentTime, 0.045, 'square', 0.035 * volume);
}

export function successChime(volume = 0.6) {
  const ac = audioContext();
  if (!ac) return;
  const t = ac.currentTime;
  [660, 880, 1320].forEach((f, i) => tone(f, t + i * 0.075, 0.16, 'triangle', 0.07 * volume));
}

export function errorBuzz(volume = 0.6) {
  const ac = audioContext();
  if (!ac) return;
  const t = ac.currentTime;
  tone(200, t, 0.12, 'sawtooth', 0.06 * volume);
  tone(150, t + 0.13, 0.16, 'sawtooth', 0.06 * volume);
}

export function shutterSound(volume = 0.7) {
  const ac = audioContext();
  if (!ac) return;
  const t = ac.currentTime;
  noise(t, 0.035, 0.16 * volume, 900);
  noise(t + 0.06, 0.05, 0.11 * volume, 1600);
}

export function tickSound(volume = 0.4) {
  const ac = audioContext();
  if (!ac) return;
  tone(1500, ac.currentTime, 0.012, 'square', 0.03 * volume);
}

/* ----------------------------- Ringtones ----------------------------- */

export interface Ringtone {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  /** [frequency, beatOffset, durationInBeats, wave] */
  notes: [number, number, number, OscillatorType?][];
  tempo: number; // BPM
  loopBeats: number;
}

const R = (id: string, name: string, tempo: number, loopBeats: number, notes: Ringtone['notes']): Ringtone => ({
  id, name, tempo, loopBeats, notes,
});

export const RINGTONES: Ringtone[] = [
  R('owl-call', 'Owl Call', 132, 8, [
    [523, 0, 0.5], [659, 0.5, 0.5], [784, 1, 0.8], [659, 2, 0.4], [523, 2.5, 1.2],
    [440, 4, 0.5], [523, 4.5, 0.5], [659, 5, 1.4], [0, 6.6, 0.6],
  ]),
  R('midnight-tuft', 'Midnight Tuft', 108, 8, [
    [330, 0, 1, 'triangle'], [392, 1, 1, 'triangle'], [494, 2, 2, 'triangle'],
    [392, 4, 1, 'triangle'], [330, 5, 2.4, 'triangle'],
  ]),
  R('amber-eye', 'Amber Eye', 148, 8, [
    [880, 0, 0.25, 'square'], [1174, 0.25, 0.25, 'square'], [880, 0.5, 0.25, 'square'],
    [1318, 0.75, 0.5, 'square'], [0, 1.4, 0.6], [988, 2, 0.25, 'square'], [784, 2.25, 0.25, 'square'],
    [988, 2.5, 0.8, 'square'], [0, 3.5, 1],
  ]),
  R('barn-owl', 'Barn Owl', 120, 8, [
    [440, 0, 0.3], [554, 0.35, 0.3], [659, 0.7, 0.3], [880, 1.05, 0.6],
    [659, 2, 0.3], [554, 2.35, 0.3], [440, 2.7, 0.9], [330, 4, 1.6],
  ]),
  R('glide', 'Silent Glide', 96, 8, [
    [196, 0, 1.5, 'sine'], [294, 1.5, 1.5, 'sine'], [247, 3, 1.5, 'sine'], [330, 4.5, 2.4, 'sine'],
  ]),
  R('chrome-bell', 'Chrome Bell', 132, 8, [
    [1046, 0, 0.5, 'sine'], [1318, 0.2, 0.6, 'sine'], [1568, 0.4, 1.2, 'sine'],
    [1046, 2.5, 0.4, 'sine'], [1568, 2.7, 1.4, 'sine'],
  ]),
  R('pixel-owl', 'Pixel Owl', 160, 8, [
    [523, 0, 0.15, 'square'], [523, 0.2, 0.15, 'square'], [784, 0.4, 0.3, 'square'],
    [659, 0.8, 0.15, 'square'], [784, 1, 0.5, 'square'], [0, 2, 0.4],
    [880, 2.4, 0.15, 'square'], [784, 2.6, 0.15, 'square'], [659, 2.8, 0.6, 'square'],
  ]),
  R('deep-woods', 'Deep Woods', 84, 8, [
    [147, 0, 2, 'triangle'], [220, 2, 2, 'triangle'], [185, 4, 2, 'triangle'], [147, 6, 2, 'triangle'],
  ]),
  R('night-hunt', 'Night Hunt', 144, 8, [
    [392, 0, 0.2, 'sawtooth'], [587, 0.2, 0.2, 'sawtooth'], [784, 0.4, 0.4, 'sawtooth'],
    [0, 1, 0.5], [659, 1.5, 0.2, 'sawtooth'], [523, 1.7, 0.2, 'sawtooth'], [392, 1.9, 0.7, 'sawtooth'],
  ]),
  R('solar-wind', 'Solar Wind', 112, 8, [
    [262, 0, 1, 'triangle'], [330, 1, 1, 'triangle'], [392, 2, 1, 'triangle'], [523, 3, 2, 'triangle'],
    [392, 5, 1, 'triangle'], [330, 6, 2, 'triangle'],
  ]),
  R('alert-seven', 'Alert Seven', 176, 8, [
    [1200, 0, 0.1, 'square'], [0, 0.16, 0.1], [1200, 0.32, 0.1, 'square'], [0, 0.48, 0.1],
    [1200, 0.64, 0.1, 'square'], [900, 0.8, 0.3, 'square'],
  ]),
  R('soft-chime', 'Soft Chime', 100, 8, [
    [698, 0, 1.2, 'sine'], [880, 1.2, 1.2, 'sine'], [1046, 2.4, 2.2, 'sine'], [880, 5, 2.4, 'sine'],
  ]),
];

const toneById = Object.fromEntries(RINGTONES.map((r) => [r.id, r]));

let ringStop: (() => void) | null = null;

/** Play a ringtone (looping until stop() is called). Returns a stop function. */
export function playRingtone(id: string, volume = 0.7, loops = Infinity): () => void {
  const ac = audioContext();
  if (!ac) return () => {};
  stopRingtone();
  const rt = toneById[id] || RINGTONES[0];
  const beat = 60 / rt.tempo;
  const loopLen = rt.loopBeats * beat;
  let stopped = false;
  let timer: number | undefined;

  const schedule = (at: number) => {
    if (stopped) return;
    rt.notes.forEach(([f, off, dur, wave]) => {
      if (f <= 0) return;
      tone(f, at + off * beat, dur * beat, wave || 'sine', 0.13 * volume);
      tone(f * 2, at + off * beat, dur * beat * 0.5, 'sine', 0.045 * volume);
    });
  };

  let count = 0;
  const run = () => {
    if (stopped) return;
    schedule(ac.currentTime + 0.05);
    count += 1;
    if (count >= loops) return;
    timer = window.setTimeout(run, loopLen * 1000);
  };
  run();

  ringStop = () => {
    stopped = true;
    if (timer) window.clearTimeout(timer);
    if (ringStop === stopFn) ringStop = null;
  };
  const stopFn = ringStop;
  return stopFn;
}

export function stopRingtone() {
  if (ringStop) {
    ringStop();
    ringStop = null;
  }
}

export function playToneOnce(id: string, volume = 0.7) {
  playRingtone(id, volume, 1);
}

export function messageTone(id: string, volume = 0.7) {
  const ac = audioContext();
  if (!ac) return;
  const rt = toneById[id] || RINGTONES[0];
  const beat = 60 / rt.tempo;
  rt.notes.slice(0, 6).forEach(([f, off, dur, wave]) => {
    if (f > 0) tone(f, ac.currentTime + 0.03 + off * beat, Math.min(dur * beat, 0.4), wave || 'sine', 0.1 * volume);
  });
}

/* ----------------------------- Game SFX ----------------------------- */

export type GameSfx = 'eat' | 'bonus' | 'die' | 'hit' | 'brick' | 'shoot' | 'flip' | 'win' | 'click' | 'jump' | 'drop' | 'tick' | 'error';

const SFX: Record<GameSfx, (t: number, v: number) => void> = {
  eat: (t, v) => { tone(880, t, 0.07, 'square', 0.09 * v); tone(1320, t + 0.06, 0.09, 'square', 0.07 * v); },
  bonus: (t, v) => { [880, 1108, 1318, 1760].forEach((f, i) => tone(f, t + i * 0.055, 0.1, 'triangle', 0.09 * v)); },
  die: (t, v) => { tone(440, t, 0.5, 'sawtooth', 0.1 * v, 80); noise(t, 0.3, 0.08 * v, 200); },
  error: (t, v) => { tone(180, t, 0.12, 'square', 0.07 * v); tone(140, t + 0.1, 0.16, 'square', 0.06 * v); },
  hit: (t, v) => { tone(240, t, 0.09, 'square', 0.09 * v, 120); },
  brick: (t, v) => { tone(660, t, 0.05, 'square', 0.08 * v); tone(990, t + 0.02, 0.05, 'square', 0.05 * v); },
  shoot: (t, v) => { tone(1400, t, 0.07, 'sawtooth', 0.06 * v, 500); },
  flip: (t, v) => { tone(720, t, 0.05, 'sine', 0.07 * v); },
  win: (t, v) => { [523, 659, 784, 1046, 1318].forEach((f, i) => tone(f, t + i * 0.09, 0.2, 'triangle', 0.09 * v)); },
  click: (t, v) => { tone(1100, t, 0.03, 'square', 0.05 * v); },
  jump: (t, v) => { tone(400, t, 0.12, 'sine', 0.07 * v, 900); },
  drop: (t, v) => { tone(300, t, 0.1, 'triangle', 0.07 * v, 120); },
  tick: (t, v) => { tone(1800, t, 0.02, 'square', 0.03 * v); },
};

export function gameSfx(name: GameSfx, volume = 0.6) {
  const ac = audioContext();
  if (!ac) return;
  SFX[name]?.(ac.currentTime, volume);
}

/* ----------------------------- Ring/vibrate helpers ----------------------------- */

export function vibrate(pattern: number | number[]) {
  try {
    if ('vibrate' in navigator) navigator.vibrate(pattern as any);
  } catch { /* ignore */ }
}
```

### `src/os/db.ts`

_3547 bytes · 129 lines_

```ts
import { createStore, get, set, del, keys, clear } from 'idb-keyval';

/**
 * OWL local storage.
 * Everything the user creates (photos, notes, voice notes, wallpapers, high
 * scores, ...) lives here, in the browser's IndexedDB. Nothing is uploaded.
 */
const store = createStore('owl-db', 'kv');

export async function dbGet<T>(key: string, fallback: T): Promise<T> {
  try {
    const value = await get<T>(key, store);
    return value === undefined ? fallback : value;
  } catch {
    return fallback;
  }
}

export async function dbSet<T>(key: string, value: T): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await set(key, value, store);
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[OWL] could not persist', key, err);
  }
}

export async function dbDel(key: string): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await del(key, store);
  } catch { /* ignore */ }
}

export async function dbKeys(prefix?: string): Promise<string[]> {
  try {
    const all = (await keys(store)) as string[];
    return prefix ? all.filter((k) => typeof k === 'string' && k.startsWith(prefix)) : all;
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export async function dbClear(): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await clear(store);
  } catch { /* ignore */ }
}

export async function dbEstimate(): Promise<{ usage: number; quota: number }> {
  try {
    if (navigator.storage && navigator.storage.estimate) {
      const e = await navigator.storage.estimate();
      return { usage: e.usage || 0, quota: e.quota || 0 };
    }
  } catch { /* ignore */ }
  return { usage: 0, quota: 0 };
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* Tiny JSON-backed collections with an in-memory cache + subscribers  */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

type Listener = () => void;
const cache = new Map<string, unknown>();
const listeners = new Map<string, Set<Listener>>();

export function subscribeKey(key: string, fn: Listener): () => void {
  let set_ = listeners.get(key);
  if (!set_) { set_ = new Set(); listeners.set(key, set_); }
  set_.add(fn);
  return () => set_!.delete(fn);
}

function emit(key: string) {
  listeners.get(key)?.forEach((fn) => fn());
}

export async function loadKey<T>(key: string, fallback: T): Promise<T> {
  if (cache.has(key)) return cache.get(key) as T;
  const value = await dbGet<T>(key, fallback);
  cache.set(key, value);
  return value;
}

export function peekKey<T>(key: string): T | undefined {
  return cache.get(key) as T | undefined;
}

export async function saveKey<T>(key: string, value: T): Promise<void> {
  cache.set(key, value);
  emit(key);
  await dbSet(key, value);
}

export function invalidateKey(key: string) {
  cache.delete(key);
  emit(key);
}

/* React hook friendly collection helpers */
export async function collectionGet<T>(name: string): Promise<T[]> {
  return loadKey<T[]>(`col:${name}`, []);
}
export async function collectionSet<T>(name: string, items: T[]): Promise<void> {
  return saveKey<T[]>(`col:${name}`, items);
}

export const DB_KEYS = {
  settings: 'owl:settings',
  contacts: 'col:contacts',
  smsThreads: 'col:smsThreads',
  smsMessages: 'col:smsMessages',
  notes: 'col:notes',
  tasks: 'col:tasks',
  events: 'col:events',
  vault: 'col:vault',
  alarms: 'col:alarms',
  chats: 'col:chats',
  callLog: 'col:callLog',
  voiceNotes: 'col:voiceNotes',
  photos: 'col:photos',
  bookmarks: 'col:bookmarks',
  history: 'col:history',
  scores: 'col:scores',
  wallpapers: 'col:wallpapers',
  frequency: 'owl:frequency',
  bootCount: 'owl:boots',
  vaultMeta: 'owl:vaultMeta',
};
```

### `src/os/effects.ts`

```ts
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useStore } from './store';
import { loadKey } from './db';
import { DB_KEYS } from './db';
import type { Alarm, CalEvent } from './types';
import { playRingtone, stopRingtone, messageTone, vibrate } from './audio';

/**
 * Background OS services: clock tick, alarm checks, calendar reminders,
 * simulated incoming SMS. These run while the phone is on, across apps —
 * like a real handset.
 */
export function useEffects() {
  useEffect(() => {
    const clock = window.setInterval(() => useStore.getState().tickClock(), 1000);

    /* ---------------- alarms ---------------- */
    let lastAlarmMinute = '';
    const alarmTimer = window.setInterval(async () => {
      const s = useStore.getState();
      if (!s.powered || s.booting || s.alarmRinging) return;
      const alarms = await loadKey<Alarm[]>(DB_KEYS.alarms, []);
      const now = new Date();
      const key = `${now.getDate()}-${now.getHours()}-${now.getMinutes()}`;
      if (lastAlarmMinute === key) return;
      const due = alarms.find(
        (a) => a.enabled && a.hour === now.getHours() && a.minute === now.getMinutes()
        && (a.days.length === 0 || a.days.includes(now.getDay()))
      );
      if (due) {
        lastAlarmMinute = key;
        s.setAlarmRinging(due.label || 'Alarm');
        if (s.settings.profile === 'silent') {
          s.toast('Alarm (silent profile)');
        }
      }
    }, 15000);

    /* ---------------- calendar reminders ---------------- */
    const notified = new Set<string>();
    const reminderTimer = window.setInterval(async () => {
      const s = useStore.getState();
      if (!s.powered || s.booting) return;
      const events = await loadKey<CalEvent[]>(DB_KEYS.events, []);
      const now = Date.now();
      for (const e of events) {
        if (e.reminderMinutes === null || e.reminderMinutes === undefined) continue;
        const trigger = e.start - e.reminderMinutes * 60000;
        if (now >= trigger && now - trigger < 90000 && !notified.has(`${e.id}:${e.start}`)) {
          notified.add(`${e.id}:${e.start}`);
          s.notify(`Reminder: ${e.title}`, 'calendar');
          break;
        }
      }
      void s.refreshCounters();
    }, 30000);

    /* ---------------- simulated incoming SMS ---------------- */
    const smsTimer = window.setTimeout(async () => {
      const s = useStore.getState();
      if (!s.settings.onboarded || !s.powered) return;
      if (localStorage.getItem('owl:nudge-sent')) return;
      localStorage.setItem('owl:nudge-sent', '1');
      const messages = await loadKey<any[]>(DB_KEYS.smsMessages, []);
      const threads = await loadKey<any[]>(DB_KEYS.smsThreads, []);
      const welcome = threads.find((t) => t.address === 'OWL');
      if (!welcome || !messages.length) return;
      s.notify('New message from Owl Team');
    }, 45000);

    /* ---------------- low battery ---------------- */
    let warned = false;
    const unsub = useStore.subscribe((state) => {
      if (!warned && state.battery.level < 0.12 && !state.battery.charging) {
        warned = true;
        state.toast('Battery low — power saver recommended');
      }
      if (state.battery.level > 0.3) warned = false;
    });

    return () => {
      window.clearInterval(clock);
      window.clearInterval(alarmTimer);
      window.clearInterval(reminderTimer);
      window.clearTimeout(smsTimer);
      unsub();
      stopRingtone();
    };
  }, []);

  /* preview the chosen ringtone when the user changes it in settings */
  useEffect(() => {
    let stop: (() => void) | null = null;
    const unsub = useStore.subscribe((state, prev) => {
      if (state.settings.ringtone !== prev.settings.ringtone) {
        stop?.();
        stop = playRingtone(state.settings.ringtone, state.settings.ringVolume, 1);
      }
      if (state.settings.messageTone !== prev.settings.messageTone) {
        messageTone(state.settings.messageTone, state.settings.volume);
      }
      if (state.settings.profile !== prev.settings.profile) {
        if (state.settings.profile === 'silent') {
          stopRingtone();
          state.toast('Silent profile');
        } else if (state.settings.profile === 'vibrate') {
          state.toast('Vibrate profile');
          if (state.settings.vibrate) vibrate([200, 100, 200]);
        } else {
          state.toast(`${state.settings.profile} profile`);
        }
      }
    });
    return () => {
      unsub();
      stop?.();
    };
  }, []);
}
```

### `src/os/expr.ts`

_5314 bytes · 150 lines_

```ts
/**
 * Safe arithmetic expression parser (shunting-yard → RPN). NEVER uses eval.
 * Supports + - * / % ^ parentheses, unary minus, and functions
 * sqrt, sin, cos, tan, log, ln, abs, and constants pi/e.
 */
type Token = { type: 'num'; value: number } | { type: 'op'; value: string } | { type: 'paren'; value: '(' | ')' };

const FUNCS: Record<string, (n: number) => number> = {
  sqrt: Math.sqrt,
  sin: (x) => Math.sin((x * Math.PI) / 180),
  cos: (x) => Math.cos((x * Math.PI) / 180),
  tan: (x) => Math.tan((x * Math.PI) / 180),
  log: Math.log10,
  ln: Math.log,
  abs: Math.abs,
};

const PRECEDENCE: Record<string, number> = {
  '+': 1, '-': 1, '*': 2, '/': 2, '%': 2, '^': 3, 'u-': 4,
};

const RIGHT_ASSOC = new Set(['^', 'u-']);

export function evaluate(input: string): number {
  const tokens = tokenize(input);
  const rpn = toRPN(tokens);
  return evalRPN(rpn);
}

function tokenize(input: string): Token[] {
  const tokens: Token[] = [];
  let i = 0;
  const s = input.replace(/\s+/g, '').replace(/×/g, '*').replace(/÷/g, '/').replace(/−/g, '-');
  let expectUnary = true;
  while (i < s.length) {
    const c = s[i];
    if (/[0-9.]/.test(c)) {
      let num = '';
      while (i < s.length && /[0-9.]/.test(s[i])) num += s[i++];
      if ((num.match(/\./g) || []).length > 1) throw new Error('Bad number');
      tokens.push({ type: 'num', value: parseFloat(num) });
      expectUnary = false;
      continue;
    }
    if (/[a-z]/i.test(c)) {
      let name = '';
      while (i < s.length && /[a-z]/i.test(s[i])) name += s[i++];
      const lower = name.toLowerCase();
      if (lower === 'pi') { tokens.push({ type: 'num', value: Math.PI }); expectUnary = false; continue; }
      if (lower === 'e') { tokens.push({ type: 'num', value: Math.E }); expectUnary = false; continue; }
      if (!FUNCS[lower]) throw new Error(`Unknown function ${name}`);
      if (s[i] !== '(') throw new Error(`${name} needs parentheses`);
      tokens.push({ type: 'op', value: lower });
      expectUnary = false;
      continue;
    }
    if (c === '(') { tokens.push({ type: 'paren', value: '(' }); i++; expectUnary = true; continue; }
    if (c === ')') { tokens.push({ type: 'paren', value: ')' }); i++; expectUnary = false; continue; }
    if ('+-*/%^'.includes(c)) {
      if (expectUnary && (c === '-' || c === '+')) {
        tokens.push({ type: 'op', value: c === '-' ? 'u-' : 'u+' });
        i++;
        continue;
      }
      tokens.push({ type: 'op', value: c });
      i++;
      expectUnary = true;
      continue;
    }
    throw new Error(`Unexpected "${c}"`);
  }
  return tokens;
}

function toRPN(tokens: Token[]): Token[] {
  const out: Token[] = [];
  const stack: Token[] = [];
  for (const t of tokens) {
    if (t.type === 'num') { out.push(t); continue; }
    if (t.type === 'op') {
      if (FUNCS[t.value]) { stack.push(t); continue; }
      if (t.value === 'u+') continue;
      while (stack.length) {
        const top = stack[stack.length - 1];
        if (top.type !== 'op') break;
        if (FUNCS[top.value]) { out.push(stack.pop()!); continue; }
        const p1 = PRECEDENCE[t.value] ?? 0;
        const p2 = PRECEDENCE[top.value] ?? 0;
        if (p2 > p1 || (p2 === p1 && !RIGHT_ASSOC.has(t.value))) out.push(stack.pop()!);
        else break;
      }
      stack.push(t);
      continue;
    }
    if (t.value === '(') { stack.push(t); continue; }
    if (t.value === ')') {
      while (stack.length && stack[stack.length - 1].type !== 'paren') out.push(stack.pop()!);
      stack.pop();
      if (stack.length && stack[stack.length - 1].type === 'op' && FUNCS[(stack[stack.length - 1] as any).value]) {
        out.push(stack.pop()!);
      }
      continue;
    }
  }
  while (stack.length) {
    const t = stack.pop()!;
    if (t.type === 'paren') throw new Error('Mismatched parentheses');
    out.push(t);
  }
  return out;
}

function evalRPN(rpn: Token[]): number {
  const stack: number[] = [];
  for (const t of rpn) {
    if (t.type === 'num') { stack.push(t.value); continue; }
    const op = t.value;
    if (FUNCS[op]) {
      const a = stack.pop();
      if (a === undefined) throw new Error('Syntax error');
      stack.push(FUNCS[op](a));
      continue;
    }
    if (op === 'u-') { const a = stack.pop(); if (a === undefined) throw new Error('Syntax error'); stack.push(-a); continue; }
    const b = stack.pop();
    const a = stack.pop();
    if (a === undefined || b === undefined) throw new Error('Syntax error');
    switch (op) {
      case '+': stack.push(a + b); break;
      case '-': stack.push(a - b); break;
      case '*': stack.push(a * b); break;
      case '/': if (b === 0) throw new Error('Divide by zero'); stack.push(a / b); break;
      case '%': stack.push(a % b); break;
      case '^': stack.push(Math.pow(a, b)); break;
      default: throw new Error(`Bad operator ${op}`);
    }
  }
  const result = stack.pop();
  if (result === undefined || !Number.isFinite(result)) throw new Error('Invalid result');
  return result;
}

export function formatResult(n: number): string {
  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 'Error';
  const abs = Math.abs(n);
  if (abs !== 0 && (abs >= 1e12 || abs < 1e-7)) return n.toExponential(6).replace(/e([+-])/, 'e$1');
  const rounded = Math.round(n * 1e10) / 1e10;
  return String(rounded);
}
```
