### `src/os/focus.ts`

_3209 bytes · 102 lines_

```ts
/**
 * Virtual focus system.
 * The OS never relies on a real <input>: every on-screen text field registers
 * here, and characters are routed to the active one. This keeps the mobile OS
 * keyboard from ever appearing and makes the physical keyboard the source of
 * truth.
 */

export type FieldKind = 'text' | 'password' | 'number' | 'decimal' | 'phone' | 'multiline';

export interface FieldApi {
  id: string;
  kind: FieldKind;
  getValue: () => string;
  setValue: (value: string) => void;
  setCaret?: (caret: number) => void;
  onSubmit?: () => void;
  onMove?: (dir: 'up' | 'down' | 'left' | 'right') => boolean;
  onEscape?: () => void;
  maxLength?: number;
  nextFieldId?: string;
}

const registry = new Map<string, FieldApi>();

export function registerField(api: FieldApi): () => void {
  registry.set(api.id, api);
  return () => {
    if (registry.get(api.id) === api) registry.delete(api.id);
  };
}

export function getField(id: string | null): FieldApi | undefined {
  return id ? registry.get(id) : undefined;
}

export function hasField(id: string | null): boolean {
  return !!(id && registry.has(id));
}

export const CARET = '\u0001';

/** Insert a character at the caret marker inside a raw stored value. */
export function insertAtCaret(raw: string, caret: number, text: string, maxLength?: number): { value: string; caret: number } {
  const clean = raw.replace(CARET, '');
  if (maxLength && clean.length >= maxLength) return { value: raw, caret };
  const pos = clampCaret(raw, caret);
  return { value: clean.slice(0, pos) + text + clean.slice(pos), caret: pos + text.length };
}

export function clampCaret(raw: string, caret: number): number {
  const len = raw.replace(CARET, '').length;
  return Math.max(0, Math.min(caret, len));
}

export function setCaretInRaw(raw: string, caret: number): string {
  const clean = raw.replace(CARET, '');
  const pos = clampCaret(clean, caret);
  return clean.slice(0, pos) + CARET + clean.slice(pos);
}

export function caretOf(raw: string): number {
  const idx = raw.indexOf(CARET);
  return idx < 0 ? raw.replace(CARET, '').length : idx;
}

export function fieldValue(raw: string): string {
  return raw.replace(CARET, '');
}

export function typeIntoField(field: FieldApi, text: string): void {
  const raw = field.getValue();
  const caret = caretOf(raw);
  const { value, caret: next } = insertAtCaret(raw, caret, text, field.maxLength);
  field.setValue(value);
  field.setCaret?.(next);
}

export function backspaceField(field: FieldApi): void {
  const raw = field.getValue();
  const caret = caretOf(raw);
  if (caret <= 0) return;
  const clean = raw.replace(CARET, '');
  field.setValue(clean.slice(0, caret - 1) + clean.slice(caret));
  field.setCaret?.(caret - 1);
}

export function deleteField(field: FieldApi): void {
  const raw = field.getValue();
  const caret = caretOf(raw);
  const clean = raw.replace(CARET, '');
  if (caret >= clean.length) return;
  field.setValue(clean.slice(0, caret) + clean.slice(caret + 1));
  field.setCaret?.(caret);
}

export function moveCaret(field: FieldApi, delta: number): void {
  const raw = field.getValue();
  const caret = caretOf(raw);
  field.setCaret?.(caret + delta);
}
```

```ts
  'battery.charging': { en: 'Charging', ar: 'جارٍ الشحن' },

};

export function translate(lang: Lang, key: string): string {
  const entry = dict[key];
  if (!entry) return key;
  return entry[lang] || entry.en;
}

export function allKeys(): string[] {
  return Object.keys(dict);
}

export function isRTL(lang: Lang) {
  return lang === 'ar';
}

export function formatNumber(lang: Lang, value: number) {
  try {
    return new Intl.NumberFormat(lang === 'ar' ? 'ar-EG' : 'en-US').format(value);
  } catch {
    return String(value);
  }
}

export function formatDate(
  lang: Lang,
  date: Date | number,
  opts: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions = { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }
) {
  try {
    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(lang === 'ar' ? 'ar-EG' : 'en-US', opts).format(date);
  } catch {
    return String(date);
  }
}

export function formatTime(lang: Lang, date: Date | number, clock24: boolean) {
  try {
    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(lang === 'ar' ? 'ar-EG' : 'en-US', {
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit',
      hour12: !clock24,
    }).format(date);
  } catch {
    const d = new Date(date);
    return `${d.getHours()}:${String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  }
}
```

### `src/os/kernel.ts`

```ts
import { useStore } from './store';
import { APP_MAP, appName } from './appRegistry';
import { backspaceField, deleteField, getField, moveCaret, typeIntoField } from './focus';
import type { KeyEventLike, NavDir } from './kernelTypes';
import { keyClick, vibrate } from './audio';

/**
 * OWL OS kernel: single entry point for every key event (real keyboard, touch
 * keyboard or virtual). Turns hardware keys into OS actions and routes text to
 * the active virtual field.
 */

type Handler = (e: KeyEventLike) => boolean;
const appHandlers = new Set<Handler>();

/** Apps register a handler to consume keys they care about (arrow navigation, etc.). */
export function registerAppKeys(handler: Handler): () => void {
  appHandlers.add(handler);
  return () => appHandlers.delete(handler);
}

function runAppHandlers(e: KeyEventLike): boolean {
  for (const h of Array.from(appHandlers)) {
    try {
      if (h(e)) return true;
    } catch { /* keep other handlers alive */ }
  }
  return false;
}

export function typeText(text: string): boolean {
  const state = useStore.getState();
  const field = getField(state.activeFieldId);
  if (!field) return false;
  if (state.settings.keyClick) keyClick(state.settings.keyClickVolume);
  typeIntoField(field, text);
  if (state.settings.vibrateKeys) vibrate(6);
  if (field.id === 'home-search' || field.id === 'lock-pass' || field.id === 'dialog-input') {
    // keep the virtual focus on modal fields even if a re-render drops it
    state.focusField(field.id);
  }
  return true;
}

export function handleBackspace(): boolean {
  const state = useStore.getState();
  const field = getField(state.activeFieldId);
  if (field) {
    backspaceField(field);
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

export function handleDelete(): boolean {
  const field = getField(useStore.getState().activeFieldId);
  if (field) {
    deleteField(field);
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

function isRTL(): boolean {
  return !!document.querySelector('.screenRoot[dir="rtl"]');
}

function moveFocusInDOM(dir: NavDir): boolean {
  const root = document.querySelector('.screenRoot');
  if (!root) return false;
  const current = document.activeElement && root.contains(document.activeElement)
    ? (document.activeElement as HTMLElement)
    : null;
  const selector = '[data-nav], button, [role="option"], [role="menuitem"], .rowBtn, .dockItem, .drawerCell, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])';
  const nodes = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(selector)).filter((el) => {
    if (el.hasAttribute('disabled') || el.getAttribute('aria-hidden') === 'true') return false;
    if (!(el as any).dataset || el.closest('[aria-hidden="true"]')) return false;
    const style = window.getComputedStyle(el);
    if (style.visibility === 'hidden' || style.display === 'none' || style.opacity === '0') return false;
    if (el.offsetParent === null && el.tagName !== 'BODY' && style.position !== 'fixed') return false;
    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    return r.width > 0 && r.height > 0;
  });

  if (!nodes.length) return false;

  if (!current) {
    // focus the first sensible element
    focusEl(nodes[0]);
    return true;
  }

  const currentRect = current.getBoundingClientRect();
  const cx = currentRect.left + currentRect.width / 2;
  const cy = currentRect.top + currentRect.height / 2;
  const rtl = isRTL();
  const dirX = rtl && (dir === 'left' || dir === 'right') ? (dir === 'left' ? 'right' : 'left') : dir;

  const scored = nodes
    .filter((el) => el !== current)
    .map((el) => {
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      const ex = r.left + r.width / 2;
      const ey = r.top + r.height / 2;
      const dx = ex - cx;
      const dy = ey - cy;
      return { el, dx, dy, r };
    });

  let best: { el: HTMLElement; score: number } | null = null;
  for (const { el, dx, dy } of scored) {
    let primary = 0;
    let secondary = 0;
    if (dirX === 'up' && dy < -2) { primary = -dy; secondary = Math.abs(dx); }
    else if (dirX === 'down' && dy > 2) { primary = dy; secondary = Math.abs(dx); }
    else if (dirX === 'left' && dx < -2) { primary = -dx; secondary = Math.abs(dy); }
    else if (dirX === 'right' && dx > 2) { primary = dx; secondary = Math.abs(dy); }
    else continue;
    // favor alignment: perpendicular offset costs a lot
    const score = primary + secondary * 1.8;
    if (!best || score < best.score) best = { el, score };
  }

  if (best) {
    focusEl(best.el);
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

function focusEl(el: HTMLElement) {
  try {
    el.focus({ preventScroll: false });
  } catch {
    el.focus();
  }
  el.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest', inline: 'nearest' });
}

export function moveFocus(dir: NavDir): boolean {
  const state = useStore.getState();
  const activeField = getField(state.activeFieldId);
  if (activeField?.onMove) {
    if (activeField.onMove(dir)) return true;
  }
  if (isTextFieldNav(dir)) {
    if (activeField) moveCaret(activeField, dir === 'left' ? -1 : 1);
    return true;
  }
  return moveFocusInDOM(dir);
}

function isTextFieldNav(dir: NavDir): boolean {
  const field = getField(useStore.getState().activeFieldId);
  return !!field && (dir === 'left' || dir === 'right');
}

/* --------------------------------- Actions -------------------------------- */

function homeAction() {
  const s = useStore.getState();
  s.closeApp();
}

function menuAction() {
  const s = useStore.getState();
  if (s.screen === 'lock') {
    s.openSearch();
    return;
  }
  if (s.appId) {
    if (s.appMenu) { s.setSoftMenu(s.appMenu); }
    else s.openDrawer();
    return;
  }
  if (s.drawerOpen) { s.closeDrawer(); return; }
  if (s.searchOpen) { s.closeSearch(); return; }
  s.openDrawer();
}

function callAction() {
  const s = useStore.getState();
  if (s.screen === 'lock') {
    void s.unlock();
    return;
  }
  s.openApp('phone');
}

function endAction() {
  const s = useStore.getState();
  if (s.screen === 'off') { s.powerOn(); return; }
  if (s.incomingCall) { s.setIncomingCall(null); return; }
  if (s.alarmRinging) { s.setAlarmRinging(null); return; }
  s.lock();
}

function escapeAction() {
  const s = useStore.getState();
  if (s.softMenu) { s.setSoftMenu(null); return; }
  if (s.powerMenuOpen) { s.setPowerMenuOpen(false); return; }
  if (s.recentOpen) { s.setRecentOpen(false); return; }
  if (s.dialog) { s.closeDialog(); return; }
  const field = getField(s.activeFieldId);
  field?.onEscape?.();
  if (s.screen === 'lock') return;
  s.back();
}

function enterAction() {
  const s = useStore.getState();
  if (s.screen === 'lock') {
    if (s.settings.passwordHash) {
      const field = getField('lock-pass');
      field?.onSubmit?.();
    } else {
      void s.unlock();
    }
    return;
  }
  const field = getField(s.activeFieldId);
  if (field) {
    field.onSubmit?.();
    return;
  }
  const active = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
  if (active && active !== document.body && document.querySelector('.screenRoot')?.contains(active)) {
    active.click();
    return;
  }
  moveFocusInDOM('down');
}

/* --------------------------------- Router -------------------------------- */

const HOTKEY_HOLD_MS = 380;
let holdTimer: number | undefined;
let holdLetter: string | null = null;

export function onKeyUp(e: { key: string; code: string }) {
  if (holdTimer) window.clearTimeout(holdTimer);
  const letter = holdLetter;
  holdLetter = null;
  if (!letter) return;
  const s = useStore.getState();
  // A quick tap already opened the search and put the letter in it; only a
  // hold that has not fired yet should launch the hotkey here.
  if (s.searchOpen || s.appId) return;
  const target = s.settings.hotkeys[letter];
  if (target && APP_MAP[target] && s.screen === 'home' && s.settings.onboarded) {
    s.toast(`Hotkey ${letter.toUpperCase()} → ${appName(target, s.settings.language)}`);
    s.openApp(target);
  }
}

/** Main key router. Returns true when the event was consumed. */
export function dispatchKey(e: KeyEventLike): boolean {
  const s = useStore.getState();
  if (!s.powered) {
    if (e.key === 'F3' || e.code === 'Power') { s.powerOn(); return true; }
    return false;
  }

  // real hardware shortcuts that must always pass through untouched
  if (e.ctrl && e.dispatch === 'physical') {
    const k = e.key.toLowerCase();
    if (['r', 'c', 'v', 'x', 'a', 'z', 't', 'w', 'n', 'f', 'p', 's', 'd', 'l'].includes(k)) {
      // only browser-level combos that the OS does not use
      if (!['c', 'v', 'x', 'a', 'z'].includes(k)) return false;
      if (!useStore.getState().activeFieldId) return false;
    }
  }

  if (runAppHandlers(e)) return true;

  if (e.code === 'F1') { menuAction(); return true; }
  if (e.code === 'F2') { callAction(); return true; }
  if (e.code === 'F3') {
    if (s.screen === 'off') s.powerOn();
    else endAction();
    return true;
  }
  if (e.code === 'PageUp') { s.bumpVolume(0.05, 'media'); return true; }
  if (e.code === 'PageDown') { s.bumpVolume(-0.05, 'media'); return true; }
  if (e.key === 'Alt' || e.code === 'AltLeft' || e.code === 'AltRight') {
    // Alt alone acts as Menu on release; handled in App via keyup, so just mark
    return true;
  }

  if (e.key === 'Escape') { escapeAction(); return true; }

  const field = getField(s.activeFieldId);

  if (e.key === 'Backspace') {
    if (!handleBackspace()) {
      if (s.drawerFolder) { s.setDrawerFolder(null); return true; }
      if (s.searchOpen) { s.closeSearch(); return true; }
    }
    return true;
  }
  if (e.key === 'Delete') {
    if (!handleDelete()) return true;
    return true;
  }

  if (e.key === 'Enter') {
    if (field) { field.onSubmit?.(); return true; }
    enterAction();
    return true;
  }
  if (e.key === 'Tab') {
    moveFocus(e.shift ? 'up' : 'down');
    return true;
  }

  if (e.key === 'ArrowUp' || e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
    const dir = e.key.replace('Arrow', '').toLowerCase() as NavDir;
    if (field) {
      const moved = field.onMove?.(dir);
      if (moved) return true;
      if (dir === 'left' || dir === 'right') { moveCaret(field, dir === 'left' ? -1 : 1); return true; }
      if (dir === 'up' || dir === 'down') {
        moveFocusInDOM(dir);
        return true;
      }
    }
    const moved = moveFocusInDOM(dir);
    if (!moved && dir === 'down') moveFocusInDOM('up');
    return true;
  }

  if (e.key === ' ') {
    if (field) { typeText(' '); return true; }
    // Space activates the focused control
    const active = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
    if (active && active !== document.body) { active.click(); return true; }
    return true;
  }

  // printable character
  if (e.key.length === 1) {
    // Typing another character means the user is searching, not holding a
    // letter to launch its hotkey. Key repeats of the held letter keep it alive.
    if (holdLetter !== null && !e.repeat) {
      window.clearTimeout(holdTimer);
      holdLetter = null;
    }
    // On the lock screen the only sink is the password field; never fall
    // through to search or app shortcuts.
    if (s.screen === 'lock' && !field) return true;
    if (field) {
      if (e.key >= '0' && e.key <= '9' && field.kind === 'number') { typeText(e.key); return true; }
      if (field.kind === 'number' && !/[0-9]/.test(e.key)) return true;
      if (field.kind === 'decimal' && !/[0-9.]/.test(e.key)) return true;
      if (field.kind === 'phone' && !/[0-9+\-*#() ]/.test(e.key)) return true;
      if (field.maxLength && field.getValue().replace('\u0001', '').length >= field.maxLength) return true;
      typeText(e.key);
      return true;
    }
    if (s.appId || s.screen === 'lock') {
      // app-level unhandled char: apps with their own keyboard handling get it
      return false;
    }
    if (s.drawerOpen) {
      s.closeDrawer();
      s.openSearch(e.key);
      window.setTimeout(() => s.focusField('home-search'), 30);
      return true;
    }
    if (s.searchOpen) {
      s.setSearchQuery(s.searchQuery + e.key);
      window.setTimeout(() => s.focusField('home-search'), 10);
      return true;
    }
    // Hold-to-launch hotkeys on the home screen. A quick tap opens the
    // universal search with the letter already typed; holding the letter for
    // HOTKEY_HOLD_MS launches that app instead.
    if (!s.searchOpen) {
      holdLetter = e.key.toLowerCase();
      if (!e.repeat) {
        window.clearTimeout(holdTimer);
        holdTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
          const st = useStore.getState();
          const letter = holdLetter;
          if (!letter || !st.searchOpen) return;
          const target = st.settings.hotkeys[letter];
          if (target && APP_MAP[target]) {
            st.closeSearch();
            st.toast(`Hotkey ${letter.toUpperCase()} → ${appName(target, st.settings.language)}`);
            st.openApp(target);
          }
          holdLetter = null;
        }, HOTKEY_HOLD_MS);
      }
    }
    s.openSearch(e.key);
    window.setTimeout(() => s.focusField('home-search'), 30);
    return true;
  }

  return false;
}

export function registerPowerLongPress() {
  /* handled inside PhoneShell */
}

export { homeAction, menuAction, callAction, endAction, escapeAction, enterAction };
```

```ts
/**
 * Physical + on-screen keyboard model.
 * The device has a full laptop-style QWERTY layout; event.code is the source
 * of truth so layout/IME doesn't break the mapping.
 */

export type KeyKind = 'char' | 'action' | 'modifier' | 'nav';

export interface KeyDef {
  code: string;
  label?: string;
  legend?: string; // secondary symbol
  width: number;
  kind: KeyKind;
  aria: string;
  icon?: string;
}

export interface KeyDescriptor {
  code: string;
  key: string;          // logical key value: 'a', 'A', 'Enter', 'Backspace', 'ArrowLeft', ...
  kind: KeyKind;
  shift: boolean;
  caps: boolean;
  ctrl: boolean;
  alt: boolean;
  highlightCode?: string;
  repeat?: boolean;
}

const K = (code: string, label: string, width: number, kind: KeyKind, legend?: string, aria?: string, icon?: string): KeyDef => ({
  code, label, width, kind, legend, aria: aria || label, icon,
});

export const KEY_ROWS: KeyDef[][] = [
  [
    K('Escape', 'Esc', 1.25, 'action', undefined, 'Escape / Back'),
    K('Digit1', '1', 1, 'char', '!'),
    K('Digit2', '2', 1, 'char', '@'),
    K('Digit3', '3', 1, 'char', '#'),
    K('Digit4', '4', 1, 'char', '$'),
    K('Digit5', '5', 1, 'char', '%'),
    K('Digit6', '6', 1, 'char', '^'),
    K('Digit7', '7', 1, 'char', '&'),
    K('Digit8', '8', 1, 'char', '*'),
    K('Digit9', '9', 1, 'char', '('),
    K('Digit0', '0', 1, 'char', ')'),
    K('Minus', '-', 1, 'char', '_'),
    K('Equal', '=', 1, 'char', '+'),
    K('Backspace', '⌫', 2, 'action', undefined, 'Backspace'),
  ],
  [
    K('Tab', 'Tab', 1.6, 'action'),
    K('KeyQ', 'Q', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyW', 'W', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyE', 'E', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyR', 'R', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyT', 'T', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyY', 'Y', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyU', 'U', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyI', 'I', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyO', 'O', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyP', 'P', 1, 'char'),
    K('BracketLeft', '[', 1, 'char', '{'),
    K('BracketRight', ']', 1, 'char', '}'),
    K('Backslash', '\\', 1.4, 'char', '|'),
  ],
  [
    K('CapsLock', 'Caps', 1.9, 'modifier', undefined, 'Caps Lock'),
    K('KeyA', 'A', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyS', 'S', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyD', 'D', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyF', 'F', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyG', 'G', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyH', 'H', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyJ', 'J', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyK', 'K', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyL', 'L', 1, 'char'),
    K('Semicolon', ';', 1, 'char', ':'),
    K('Quote', "'", 1, 'char', '"'),
    K('Enter', 'Enter', 2.2, 'action', undefined, 'Enter / OK'),
  ],
  [
    K('ShiftLeft', '⇧ Shift', 2.4, 'modifier', undefined, 'Shift'),
    K('KeyZ', 'Z', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyX', 'X', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyC', 'C', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyV', 'V', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyB', 'B', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyN', 'N', 1, 'char'),
    K('KeyM', 'M', 1, 'char'),
    K('Comma', ',', 1, 'char', '<'),
    K('Period', '.', 1, 'char', '>'),
    K('Slash', '/', 1, 'char', '?'),
    K('ShiftRight', '⇧ Shift', 2.4, 'modifier', undefined, 'Shift'),
  ],
  [
    K('ControlLeft', 'Ctrl', 1.5, 'modifier'),
    K('AltLeft', 'Alt', 1.4, 'modifier'),
    K('Space', 'space', 6, 'char', undefined, 'Space'),
    K('AltRight', 'Alt', 1.4, 'modifier'),
    K('ControlRight', 'Ctrl', 1.5, 'modifier'),
    K('ArrowLeft', '←', 1, 'nav', undefined, 'Left arrow'),
    K('ArrowUp', '↑', 1, 'nav', undefined, 'Up arrow'),
    K('ArrowDown', '↓', 1, 'nav', undefined, 'Down arrow'),
    K('ArrowRight', '→', 1, 'nav', undefined, 'Right arrow'),
  ],
];

const SHIFT_DIGITS: Record<string, string> = {
  Digit1: '!', Digit2: '@', Digit3: '#', Digit4: '$', Digit5: '%',
  Digit6: '^', Digit7: '&', Digit8: '*', Digit9: '(', Digit0: ')',
  Minus: '_', Equal: '+', BracketLeft: '{', BracketRight: '}', Backslash: '|',
  Semicolon: ':', Quote: '"', Comma: '<', Period: '>', Slash: '?', Backquote: '~',
};

const PLAIN: Record<string, string> = {
  Minus: '-', Equal: '=', BracketLeft: '[', BracketRight: ']', Backslash: '\\',
  Semicolon: ';', Quote: "'", Comma: ',', Period: '.', Slash: '/', Backquote: '`',
};

const NUMPAD_CHARS: Record<string, string> = {
  Numpad0: '0', Numpad1: '1', Numpad2: '2', Numpad3: '3', Numpad4: '4',
  Numpad5: '5', Numpad6: '6', Numpad7: '7', Numpad8: '8', Numpad9: '9',
  NumpadMultiply: '*', NumpadAdd: '+', NumpadSubtract: '-', NumpadDecimal: '.', NumpadDivide: '/',
};

/** Map a DOM KeyboardEvent to an OWL key descriptor, or null when not consumed. */
export function describeKey(e: KeyboardEvent, caps: boolean): KeyDescriptor | null {
  const code = e.code;
  const shift = e.shiftKey;
  const ctrl = e.ctrlKey;
  const alt = e.altKey;

  const base = { code, shift, caps, ctrl, alt, repeat: e.repeat } as KeyDescriptor;

  if (/^Key[A-Z]$/.test(code)) {
    const letter = code.slice(3);
    const upper = shift !== caps; // XOR
    return { ...base, key: upper ? letter : letter.toLowerCase(), kind: 'char' };
  }
  if (/^Digit[0-9]$/.test(code)) {
    const digit = code.slice(5);
    return { ...base, key: shift ? SHIFT_DIGITS[code] || digit : digit, kind: 'char', highlightCode: code };
  }
  if (NUMPAD_CHARS[code]) {
    return { ...base, key: NUMPAD_CHARS[code], kind: 'char', highlightCode: null as any };
  }
  if (SHIFT_DIGITS[code]) {
    return { ...base, key: shift ? SHIFT_DIGITS[code] : PLAIN[code], kind: 'char' };
  }
  if (PLAIN[code]) {
    return { ...base, key: shift ? SHIFT_DIGITS[code] : PLAIN[code], kind: 'char' };
  }
  if (code === 'Space') return { ...base, key: ' ', kind: 'char' };
  if (code === 'NumpadSpace') return { ...base, key: ' ', kind: 'char' };
  if (code === 'Enter' || code === 'NumpadEnter') return { ...base, key: 'Enter', kind: 'action' };
  if (code === 'Backspace') return { ...base, key: 'Backspace', kind: 'action' };
  if (code === 'Delete') return { ...base, key: 'Delete', kind: 'action' };
  if (code === 'Tab') return { ...base, key: 'Tab', kind: 'action' };
  if (code === 'Escape') return { ...base, key: 'Escape', kind: 'action' };
  if (code === 'ShiftLeft' || code === 'ShiftRight') return { ...base, key: 'Shift', kind: 'modifier' };
  if (code === 'CapsLock') return { ...base, key: 'CapsLock', kind: 'modifier' };
  if (code === 'ControlLeft' || code === 'ControlRight') return { ...base, key: 'Control', kind: 'modifier' };
  if (code === 'AltLeft' || code === 'AltRight') return { ...base, key: 'Alt', kind: 'modifier' };
  if (code === 'MetaLeft' || code === 'MetaRight') return { ...base, key: 'Meta', kind: 'modifier' };
  if (code === 'ArrowUp' || code === 'ArrowDown' || code === 'ArrowLeft' || code === 'ArrowRight') {
    return { ...base, key: code, kind: 'nav' };
  }
  if (code === 'F1') return { ...base, key: 'F1', kind: 'action' };
  if (code === 'F2') return { ...base, key: 'F2', kind: 'action' };
  if (code === 'F3') return { ...base, key: 'F3', kind: 'action' };
  if (code === 'PageUp') return { ...base, key: 'PageUp', kind: 'action' };
  if (code === 'PageDown') return { ...base, key: 'PageDown', kind: 'action' };
  if (code === 'Home') return { ...base, key: 'Home', kind: 'action' };
  if (code === 'End') return { ...base, key: 'End', kind: 'action' };
  return null;
}

export function keyLabel(key: string): string {
  switch (key) {
    case 'Backspace': return '⌫';
    case 'Enter': return 'OK';
    case 'Escape': return 'Esc';
    case 'ArrowUp': return '↑';
    case 'ArrowDown': return '↓';
    case 'ArrowLeft': return '←';
    case 'ArrowRight': return '→';
    case 'Tab': return '⇥';
    case ' ': return '␣';
    default: return key;
  }
}

export const SHORTCUT_LEGEND: [string, string][] = [
  ['Esc', 'Back key'],
  ['Enter', 'Trackpad click / OK'],
  ['Arrows', 'Trackpad movement'],
  ['F1 / Alt', 'Menu key'],
  ['F2', 'Call / Answer'],
  ['F3', 'End / Power'],
  ['PgUp / PgDn', 'Volume up / down'],
  ['Letter (home)', 'Universal search'],
  ['Hold letter', 'Launch hotkey app'],
  ['Hold Menu', 'Switch application'],
];
```

### `src/os/media.ts`

_5213 bytes · 133 lines_

```ts
/* Bundled copy of the jsmediatags browser build (no ESM entry upstream), so
   ID3 reading works offline. Nothing leaves the device. */
import tagReaderUrl from 'jsmediatags/dist/jsmediatags.min.js?url';

/** Shared media helpers: file picking, ID3 tags, object URLs. Nothing is uploaded. */

export interface PickedFile {
  name: string;
  size: number;
  type: string;
  file: File;
  path?: string;
}

export function pickFiles(accept: string, multiple = true, directory = false): Promise<PickedFile[]> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file';
    input.accept = accept;
    input.multiple = multiple;
    if (directory) {
      input.setAttribute('webkitdirectory', '');
      input.setAttribute('directory', '');
    }
    input.style.display = 'none';
    document.body.appendChild(input);
    let settled = false;
    const finish = (files: PickedFile[]) => {
      if (settled) return;
      settled = true;
      input.remove();
      resolve(files);
    };
    input.onchange = () => {
      const list = Array.from(input.files || []);
      finish(list.map((file) => ({
        name: file.name,
        size: file.size,
        type: file.type || guessType(file.name),
        file,
        path: (file as any).webkitRelativePath || undefined,
      })));
    };
    // Some browsers never fire change when cancelled; clean up on focus return.
    window.addEventListener('focus', () => window.setTimeout(() => finish([]), 600), { once: true });
    input.click();
  });
}

function guessType(name: string): string {
  const ext = name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || '';
  const map: Record<string, string> = {
    mp3: 'audio/mpeg', m4a: 'audio/mp4', wav: 'audio/wav', ogg: 'audio/ogg', flac: 'audio/flac', aac: 'audio/aac', opus: 'audio/opus',
    mp4: 'video/mp4', webm: 'video/webm', mov: 'video/quicktime', mkv: 'video/x-matroska',
    jpg: 'image/jpeg', jpeg: 'image/jpeg', png: 'image/png', gif: 'image/gif', webp: 'image/webp', svg: 'image/svg+xml', bmp: 'image/bmp',
    pdf: 'application/pdf', txt: 'text/plain', md: 'text/markdown', json: 'application/json', vtt: 'text/vtt', srt: 'application/x-subrip',
  };
  return map[ext] || 'application/octet-stream';
}

export interface TrackTags {
  title?: string;
  artist?: string;
  album?: string;
  picture?: string;
}

let tagReaderPromise: Promise<any> | null = null;

function loadTagReader(): Promise<any> {
  if (tagReaderPromise) return tagReaderPromise;
  tagReaderPromise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const w = window as any;
    if (w.jsmediatags?.read) { resolve(w.jsmediatags); return; }
    const script = document.createElement('script');
    script.src = tagReaderUrl;
    script.async = true;
    script.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
    script.onload = () => (w.jsmediatags?.read ? resolve(w.jsmediatags) : reject(new Error('tag reader unavailable')));
    script.onerror = () => reject(new Error('tag reader blocked'));
    document.head.appendChild(script);
    setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('tag reader timeout')), 6000);
  }).catch(() => null);
  return tagReaderPromise;
}

/** Read ID3 tags locally with jsmediatags. Never sends the file anywhere. */
export async function readTags(file: File): Promise<TrackTags> {
  try {
    // jsmediatags ships only a browser IIFE bundle (no ESM entry), so it is
    // loaded from a CDN at runtime. If it cannot load, tag reading silently
    // falls back to the file name — no metadata is ever uploaded anywhere.
    const jsmediatags = await loadTagReader();
    return await new Promise<TrackTags>((resolve) => {
      jsmediatags.read(file, {
        onSuccess: (tag: any) => {
          const t = tag?.tags || {};
          let picture: string | undefined;
          if (t.picture) {
            const { data, format } = t.picture;
            try {
              let binary = '';
              const bytes = new Uint8Array(data as number[]);
              const step = 4096;
              for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += step) {
                binary += String.fromCharCode.apply(null, Array.from(bytes.subarray(i, i + step)) as any);
              }
              picture = `data:${format};base64,${btoa(binary)}`;
            } catch { /* picture optional */ }
          }
          resolve({ title: t.title, artist: t.artist, album: t.album, picture });
        },
        onError: () => resolve({}),
      });
    });
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

export function isAudio(file: { type: string; name: string }): boolean {
  return file.type.startsWith('audio/') || /\.(mp3|m4a|wav|ogg|flac|aac|opus|weba)$/i.test(file.name);
}
export function isVideo(file: { type: string; name: string }): boolean {
  return file.type.startsWith('video/') || /\.(mp4|webm|mov|mkv|ogv|m4v)$/i.test(file.name);
}
export function isImage(file: { type: string; name: string }): boolean {
  return file.type.startsWith('image/') || /\.(jpg|jpeg|png|gif|webp|svg|bmp|avif)$/i.test(file.name);
}
export function isText(file: { type: string; name: string }): boolean {
  return file.type.startsWith('text/') || /\.(txt|md|json|csv|log|ini|yml|yaml|html|css|js|ts|xml)$/i.test(file.name);
}
```

```ts
import { useStore } from './store';

/** Opens the setup wizard once for brand-new installs (after the boot screen). */
export function openSetupIfNeeded() {
  const state = useStore.getState();
  if (state.settings.onboarded) return;
  if (state.screen !== 'home') return;
  state.openApp('setup');
}
```

```tsx
import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../store';
import { OwlLogo } from '../../components/OwlLogo';

export function BootScreen() {
  const progress = useStore((s) => s.bootProgress);
  return (
    <div className="bootScreen" role="status" aria-label="Booting OWL OS">
      <div className="bootInner">
        <div className="bootLogo">
          <OwlLogo size={72} glow />
        </div>
        <div className="bootBrand">OWL</div>
        <div className="bootText">Loading</div>
        <div className="bootBar"><i style={{ width: `${progress}%` }} /></div>
        <div className="bootText" style={{ fontSize: 7, letterSpacing: '0.2em', opacity: 0.5 }}>OS 1.0 · RETRO EDITION</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/** Drives the ~2.5s boot animation. */
export function useBootSequence() {
  const booting = useStore((s) => s.booting);
  const finishBoot = useStore((s) => s.finishBoot);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!booting) return;
    let value = 0;
    useStore.setState({ bootProgress: 0 });
    const started = Date.now();
    const id = window.setInterval(() => {
      const t = (Date.now() - started) / 2200;
      value = Math.min(100, Math.round(t * 100));
      const eased = Math.round(Math.min(100, value * (1 + Math.max(0, 0.08 - t * 0.1))));
      useStore.setState({ bootProgress: eased });
      if (t >= 1) {
        window.clearInterval(id);
        finishBoot();
      }
    }, 60);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, [booting, finishBoot]);
}

export function ShutdownScreen() {
  return (
    <div className="shutdownAnim">
      <div className="bootInner">
        <OwlLogo size={46} glow />
        <div className="bootText">Shutting down</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function PoweredOff() {
  const powerOn = useStore((s) => s.powerOn);
  return (
    <div className="offScreen">
      <div className="pwHint">
        <div style={{ color: '#4b5c74', letterSpacing: '0.3em', fontSize: 8 }}>OWL</div>
        <div style={{ marginTop: 6 }}>Powered off — press Power to start</div>
        <button
          className="pwBtn"
          style={{ marginLeft: 'auto', marginRight: 'auto' }}
          onClick={powerOn}
          aria-label="Power on"
        >
          <span style={{ fontSize: 15 }}>⏻</span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default BootScreen;
```

```tsx
import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../store';
import { APPS, APP_MAP, GAME_IDS, MEDIA_IDS, appName } from '../appRegistry';
import { AppIcon } from '../../components/AppIcon';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';

const TABS = [
  { id: 'all', en: 'All', ar: 'الكل' },
  { id: 'favorites', en: 'Favorites', ar: 'المفضلة' },
  { id: 'media', en: 'Media', ar: 'الوسائط' },
  { id: 'games', en: 'Games', ar: 'الألعاب' },
  { id: 'downloads', en: 'Downloads', ar: 'التنزيلات' },
  { id: 'frequent', en: 'Frequent', ar: 'الأكثر استخداماً' },
];

export function Drawer() {
  const open = useStore((s) => s.drawerOpen);
  const tab = useStore((s) => s.drawerTab);
  const setTab = useStore((s) => s.setDrawerTab);
  const closeDrawer = useStore((s) => s.closeDrawer);
  const openApp = useStore((s) => s.openApp);
  const settings = useStore((s) => s.settings);
  const updateSettings = useStore((s) => s.updateSettings);
  const frequency = useStore((s) => s.frequency);
  const folder = useStore((s) => s.drawerFolder);
  const setFolder = useStore((s) => s.setDrawerFolder);
  const setSoftMenu = useStore((s) => s.setSoftMenu);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const showDialog = useStore((s) => s.showDialog);
  const lang = settings.language;

  const list = useMemo(() => {
    const base = APPS.filter((a) => !settings.hiddenApps.includes(a.id) && a.id !== 'snake' && a.id !== 'breaker'
      && a.id !== 'skyowl' && a.id !== 'pairs' && a.id !== 'mancala' && a.id !== 'sudoku'
      && a.id !== 'blocks' && a.id !== 'wordowl' && a.id !== 'solitaire');
    const ordered = settings.appOrder.length
      ? [...base].sort((a, b) => {
        const ia = settings.appOrder.indexOf(a.id);
        const ib = settings.appOrder.indexOf(b.id);
        return (ia < 0 ? 999 : ia) - (ib < 0 ? 999 : ib);
      })
      : base;
    switch (tab) {
      case 'favorites':
        return ordered.filter((a) => settings.favorites.includes(a.id));
      case 'media':
        return ordered.filter((a) => a.category === 'media' || a.id === 'camera');
      case 'games':
        return APPS.filter((a) => GAME_IDS.includes(a.id) || a.id === 'games');
      case 'downloads':
        return [];
      case 'frequent':
        return [...ordered].sort((a, b) => (frequency[b.id] || 0) - (frequency[a.id] || 0)).slice(0, 12);
      default:
        return ordered;
    }
  }, [tab, settings.appOrder, settings.hiddenApps, settings.favorites, frequency]);

  if (!open) return null;

  const openMenuFor = (id: string) => {
    const fav = settings.favorites.includes(id);
    const isGame = GAME_IDS.includes(id);
    setSoftMenu({
      title: appName(id, lang),
      actions: [
        { id: 'open', label: 'Open', icon: 'play', run: () => openApp(id) },
        {
          id: 'fav', label: fav ? 'Remove from favorites' : 'Add to favorites', icon: 'star',
          run: () => {
            updateSettings({ favorites: fav ? settings.favorites.filter((f) => f !== id) : [...settings.favorites, id] });
            toast(fav ? 'Removed from favorites' : 'Added to favorites');
          },
        },
        {
          id: 'folder', label: 'Add to folder…', icon: 'folder',
          run: () => showDialog({
            title: 'New folder',
            body: `Create a folder for <strong>${appName(id, lang)}</strong> and other apps.`,
            input: { label: 'Folder name', value: '', placeholder: 'e.g. Daily' },
            confirmLabel: 'Create',
            onConfirm: (name) => {
              if (!name.trim()) return;
              updateSettings({ folders: [...settings.folders, { id: `f${Date.now()}`, name: name.trim(), apps: [id] }] });
              toast('Folder created');
            },
          }),
        },
        { id: 'hide', label: 'Hide application', icon: 'eyeOff', run: () => { updateSettings({ hiddenApps: [...settings.hiddenApps, id] }); toast('Hidden — unhide in Settings › Home screen'); } },
        isGame ? { id: 'score', label: 'High scores', icon: 'star', run: () => openApp('games', { view: 'scores', game: id }) } : null,
      ].filter(Boolean) as any,
    });
  };

  const moveApp = (id: string, dir: -1 | 1) => {
    const order = list.map((a) => a.id).filter((x) => x !== id);
    const currentIdx = list.findIndex((a) => a.id === id);
    const next = [...order];
    next.splice(Math.max(0, currentIdx + dir), 0, id);
    updateSettings({ appOrder: next });
    toast('Reordered');
  };

  return (
    <div className="drawerLayer" onClick={closeDrawer}>
      <div className="drawer" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()} role="dialog" aria-label="Applications">
        <div className="drawerHead">
          <span className="grab" />
        </div>
        <div className="drawerTabs">
          {TABS.map((t) => (
            <button
              key={t.id}
              className={`owlTab ${tab === t.id ? 'active' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => setTab(t.id)}
              style={{ fontSize: 9 }}
            >
              {lang === 'ar' ? t.ar : t.en}
            </button>
          ))}
          <button className="iconBtn" style={{ marginLeft: 'auto' }} onClick={closeDrawer} aria-label="Close drawer">✕</button>
        </div>

        {folder ? (
          <>
            <div style={{ padding: '4px 8px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
              <button className="iconBtn" onClick={() => setFolder(null)} aria-label="Back">‹</button>
              <strong style={{ fontSize: 11 }}>{settings.folders.find((f) => f.id === folder)?.name}</strong>
            </div>
            <div className="folderGrid">
              {(settings.folders.find((f) => f.id === folder)?.apps || []).map((id) => (
                <button key={id} className="drawerCell" onClick={() => openApp(id)}>
                  <AppIcon id={id} size={30} />
                  <span>{appName(id, lang)}</span>
                </button>
              ))}
              <button
                className="drawerCell"
                onClick={() => {
                  showDialog({
                    title: 'Remove folder',
                    body: 'Apps inside will stay installed, just ungrouped.',
                    confirmLabel: 'Remove',
                    danger: true,
                    onConfirm: () => { updateSettings({ folders: settings.folders.filter((f) => f.id !== folder) }); setFolder(null); },
                  });
                }}
                aria-label="Remove folder"
              >
                <span className="di" style={{ width: 30, height: 30, display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center' }}><Icon name="trash" size={18} /></span>
                <span>Delete</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          <div className="drawerGrid">
            {settings.folders.map((f) => (
              <button key={f.id} className="drawerCell" onClick={() => setFolder(f.id)}>
                <span
                  className="appIcon"
                  style={{ width: 30, height: 30, background: 'linear-gradient(160deg,#556a86,#26303f)', borderRadius: 8 }}
                >
                  <Icon name="folder" size={17} />
                </span>
                <span>{f.name}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
            {list.map((a) => (
              <button
                key={a.id}
                className="drawerCell"
                onClick={() => openApp(a.id)}
                onContextMenu={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); openMenuFor(a.id); }}
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (!e.shiftKey) return;
                  if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'ArrowUp') { e.stopPropagation(); moveApp(a.id, -1); }
                  if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'ArrowDown') { e.stopPropagation(); moveApp(a.id, 1); }
                }}
                title={`${a.name[lang] || a.name.en} · Menu key for options`}
                aria-label={`${a.name[lang] || a.name.en}. Press Menu for options.`}
              >
                <AppIcon id={a.id} size={30} />
                <span>{a.name[lang] || a.name.en}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
            {tab === 'downloads' && (
              <div className="empty" style={{ gridColumn: '1 / -1' }}>
                <div className="big">⬇</div>
                <div>Downloads are disabled in this simulator.</div>
                <div className="tiny">Everything else lives on your device in IndexedDB.</div>
              </div>
            )}
            {tab === 'frequent' && list.length === 0 && (
              <div className="empty" style={{ gridColumn: '1 / -1' }}><div className="big">🦉</div><div>Open a few apps and they'll show up here.</div></div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
        <div style={{ padding: '3px 7px 5px', borderTop: '1px solid var(--line)', display: 'flex', gap: 5, alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <span className="tiny" style={{ flex: 1 }}>Menu on an icon → move · folder · hide · favorites</span>
          <button className="btn" style={{ fontSize: 9 }} onClick={() => { closeDrawer(); openApp('settings', { section: 'home' }); }}>
            Settings
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default Drawer;
```

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore, dockIds, profileLabel } from '../store';
import { APP_MAP, appName, searchApps } from '../appRegistry';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';
import { formatDate, formatTime } from '../i18n';
import { useWallpaperUrl } from '../../hooks/useWallpaper';
import { AppIcon } from '../../components/AppIcon';

export function HomeScreen() {
  const settings = useStore((s) => s.settings);
  const counters = useStore((s) => s.counters);
  const openApp = useStore((s) => s.openApp);
  const searchOpen = useStore((s) => s.searchOpen);
  const searchQuery = useStore((s) => s.searchQuery);
  const openSearch = useStore((s) => s.openSearch);
  const closeSearch = useStore((s) => s.closeSearch);
  const activeFieldId = useStore((s) => s.activeFieldId);
  const openDrawer = useStore((s) => s.openDrawer);
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => new Date());
  const [activeCounter, setActiveCounter] = useState<'messages' | 'calls' | 'calendar' | null>(null);
  const wp = useWallpaperUrl(settings.wallpaperId);
  const lang = settings.language;
  const dock = useMemo(() => dockIds(settings), [settings.appOrder]);
  const isRTL = lang === 'ar';

  useEffect(() => {
    const t = window.setInterval(() => setNow(new Date()), 1000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(t);
  }, []);

  const caption = activeCounter === 'messages'
    ? `(${counters.messages}) Messages`
    : activeCounter === 'calls'
      ? `(${counters.missedCalls}) Missed Calls`
      : activeCounter === 'calendar'
        ? `(${counters.reminders}) Calendar`
        : '';

  return (
    <div className="home" dir={isRTL ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}>
      <div
        className="homeBg"
        style={{
          position: 'absolute', inset: 0,
          backgroundImage: wp || undefined,
          backgroundSize: 'cover',
          backgroundPosition: 'center',
        }}
        aria-hidden="true"
      />
      <div
        style={{
          position: 'absolute', inset: 0,
          background: 'linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(0,0,0,.4), rgba(0,0,0,.02) 34%, rgba(0,0,0,.55))',
          pointerEvents: 'none',
        }}
        aria-hidden="true"
      />

      <div className="homeTop">
        <div className="homeClock">
          <div className="time" aria-label="Current time">
            {formatTime(lang, now, settings.clock24)}
          </div>
          <div className="date">{formatDate(lang, now, { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })}</div>
          <div className="homeCounters">
            <button
              className={`counter ${activeCounter === 'messages' ? 'active' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => { setActiveCounter('messages'); openApp('messages'); }}
              aria-label={`${counters.messages} unread messages`}
            >
              <span className="cicon">✉</span>{counters.messages}
            </button>
            <button
              className={`counter ${activeCounter === 'calls' ? 'active' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => { setActiveCounter('calls'); openApp('phone', { tab: 'log' }); }}
              aria-label={`${counters.missedCalls} missed calls`}
            >
              <span className="cicon">☎</span>{counters.missedCalls}
            </button>
            <button
              className={`counter ${activeCounter === 'calendar' ? 'active' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => { setActiveCounter('calendar'); openApp('calendar'); }}
              aria-label={`${counters.reminders} calendar reminders`}
            >
              <span className="cicon">▦</span>{counters.reminders}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="homeRight">
          <div className="profileChip">{profileLabel(settings.profile, lang)}</div>
          {settings.userName && (
            <div style={{ fontSize: 9, color: '#fff', opacity: 0.85, textShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,.9)' }}>
              {settings.userName}
            </div>
          )}
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4, opacity: 0.8 }}>
            <Icon name={settings.bluetooth ? 'bluetooth' : 'wifi'} size={10} />
            <Icon name="battery" size={10} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="homeMid">
        {caption && <div className="homeCaption">{(caption)}</div>}
        {!caption && (
          <div className="homeCaption" style={{ opacity: 0.62, fontWeight: 500 }}>
            {isRTL ? 'اضغط القائمة لفتح التطبيقات' : 'Press MENU for all applications'}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {searchOpen && (
        <div className="homeSearchWrap">
          <div className="homeSearch">
            <Icon name="search" size={11} />
            <SearchField />
            <button
              className="iconBtn"
              aria-label="Close search"
              onClick={closeSearch}
              style={{ width: 16, height: 16, fontSize: 9 }}
            >
              ✕
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {searchOpen && (
        <SearchResults
          query={searchQuery}
          onPick={(id) => { closeSearch(); openApp(id); }}
          onWeb={(q) => { closeSearch(); openApp('web', { query: q }); }}
          onCounter={setActiveCounter}
        />
      )}

      <div
        className={`dock ${settings.dockRows === 'two' ? 'twoRows' : ''} ${settings.dockRows === 'hidden' ? 'hidden' : ''}`}
        role="toolbar"
        aria-label="Shortcuts"
      >
        <span className="dockGrab" aria-hidden="true" />
        {dock.map((id) => {
          const meta = APP_MAP[id];
          if (!meta) return null;
          return (
            <button
              key={id}
              className="dockItem"
              onClick={() => openApp(id)}
              onContextMenu={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                openDrawer();
              }}
              title={appName(id, lang)}
              aria-label={appName(id, lang)}
            >
              <span className="di"><AppIcon id={id} size={26} /></span>
              <span className="dl">{appName(id, lang)}</span>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function SearchField() {
  const query = useStore((s) => s.searchQuery);
  const setQuery = useStore((s) => s.setSearchQuery);
  const active = useStore((s) => s.activeFieldId);
  const focusField = useStore((s) => s.focusField);
  // Local mirror so a keystroke is visible to the kernel immediately; the
  // store remains the source of truth for everything else.
  const valueRef = useRef(query);
  valueRef.current = query;

  useEffect(() => {
    let off: (() => void) | undefined;
    void import('../focus').then(({ registerField }) => {
      off = registerField({
        id: 'home-search',
        kind: 'text',
        getValue: () => {
          const value = valueRef.current;
          return useStore.getState().activeFieldId === 'home-search' ? value + '\u0001' : value;
        },
        setValue: (raw) => {
          const clean = raw.replace('\u0001', '');
          valueRef.current = clean;
          setQuery(clean);
        },
        onSubmit: () => {
          const st = useStore.getState();
          const first = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-search-result]');
          if (first) first.click();
          else if (st.searchQuery.trim()) {
            st.openApp('web', { query: st.searchQuery.trim() });
            st.closeSearch();
          }
        },
      });
    });
    return () => off?.();
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  return (
    <div
      className="field"
      role="searchbox"
      aria-label="Search"
      onClick={() => focusField('home-search')}
      style={{ fontSize: 11 }}
    >
      {active === 'home-search' && <span className="caret" />}
      {query || <span style={{ opacity: 0.55 }}>Search apps, contacts, notes…</span>}
    </div>
  );
}

function SearchResults({
  query, onPick, onWeb, onCounter,
}: {
  query: string;
  onPick: (id: string) => void;
  onWeb: (q: string) => void;
  onCounter: (c: 'messages' | 'calls' | 'calendar' | null) => void;
}) {
  const settings = useStore((s) => s.settings);
  const openApp = useStore((s) => s.openApp);
  const [extras, setExtras] = useState<{ id: string; label: string; sub: string; app: string }[]>([]);
  const apps = useMemo(() => searchApps(query, settings.language).slice(0, 5), [query, settings.language]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!query.trim()) { setExtras([]); return; }
    let alive = true;
    void (async () => {
      const { collectionGet, DB_KEYS } = await import('../db');
      const [contacts, notes, messages] = await Promise.all([
        collectionGet<any>(DB_KEYS.contacts),
        collectionGet<any>(DB_KEYS.notes),
        collectionGet<any>(DB_KEYS.smsMessages),
      ]);
      if (!alive) return;
      const q = query.toLowerCase();
      const found: { id: string; label: string; sub: string; app: string }[] = [];
      contacts.filter((c) => `${c.firstName} ${c.lastName}`.toLowerCase().includes(q))
        .slice(0, 3)
        .forEach((c) => found.push({ id: `c-${c.id}`, label: `${c.firstName} ${c.lastName}`, sub: c.phone || 'Contact', app: 'contacts' }));
      notes.filter((n) => `${n.title} ${n.body}`.toLowerCase().includes(q))
        .slice(0, 3)
        .forEach((n) => found.push({ id: `n-${n.id}`, label: n.title || n.body.slice(0, 28), sub: 'Memo', app: 'memo' }));
      messages.filter((m) => String(m.body).toLowerCase().includes(q))
        .slice(0, 3)
        .forEach((m) => found.push({ id: `m-${m.id}`, label: m.address, sub: 'Message', app: 'messages' }));
      setExtras(found);
    })();
    return () => { alive = false; };
  }, [query]);

  const hasQuery = query.trim().length > 0;

  return (
    <div className="searchResults" role="listbox" aria-label="Search results">
      {!hasQuery && (
        <div className="owlSection" style={{ padding: '6px 8px' }}>Type to search · try “notes”, “snake”, “cafe”</div>
      )}
      {apps.map((a) => (
        <button key={a.id} data-search-result className="rowBtn" onClick={() => onPick(a.id)} role="option">
          <AppIcon id={a.id} size={18} />
          <span>{a.name[settings.language] || a.name.en}</span>
          <span className="meta">App</span>
        </button>
      ))}
      {extras.map((e) => (
        <button key={e.id} data-search-result className="rowBtn" onClick={() => { onCounter(null); openApp(e.app); }} role="option">
          <Icon name={APP_MAP[e.app]?.icon || 'info'} size={13} />
          <span style={{ overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap', maxWidth: 210 }}>{e.label}</span>
          <span className="meta">{e.sub}</span>
        </button>
      ))}
      {hasQuery && (
        <button data-search-result className="rowBtn" onClick={() => onWeb(query)} role="option" style={{ borderBottom: 0 }}>
          <Icon name="globe" size={13} />
          <span>Search “{query}” with Owl Web</span>
        </button>
      )}
      {hasQuery && apps.length === 0 && extras.length === 0 && (
        <div className="tiny" style={{ padding: '2px 8px 6px' }}>No local matches — web search is ready above.</div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default HomeScreen;
```

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../store';
import { wallpaperName } from '../../assets/wallpapers';
import { useWallpaperUrl } from '../../hooks/useWallpaper';
import { OwlLogo } from '../../components/OwlLogo';
import { formatDate, formatTime } from '../i18n';
import { errorBuzz, successChime } from '../audio';

export function LockScreen() {
  const settings = useStore((s) => s.settings);
  const unlock = useStore((s) => s.unlock);
  const failedAttempts = useStore((s) => s.failedAttempts);
  const cooldownUntil = useStore((s) => s.cooldownUntil);
  // The password lives in ONE place: the virtual field registered by
  // `useLockField` (mounted from App). The lock screen only displays it.
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => new Date());
  const [cooldown, setCooldown] = useState(0);
  const pass = useLockFieldValue();
  const hasPassword = !!settings.passwordHash;
  const wpId = settings.useSeparateLockWallpaper ? settings.lockWallpaperId : settings.wallpaperId;
  const wp = useWallpaperUrl(wpId);
  const lang = settings.language;

  useEffect(() => {
    const t = window.setInterval(() => setNow(new Date()), 1000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(t);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!cooldownUntil) return;
    const t = window.setInterval(() => {
      setCooldown(Math.max(0, Math.ceil((cooldownUntil - Date.now()) / 1000)));
    }, 250);
    return () => window.clearInterval(t);
  }, [cooldownUntil]);

  // Menu opens the password prompt; as soon as the field is visible we type in it.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!hasPassword) return;
    useStore.getState().focusField('lock-pass');
  }, [hasPassword]);

  const tryUnlock = async () => {
    if (Date.now() < cooldownUntil) return;
    await unlock(pass);
  };

  return (
    <div className="lockScreen" aria-label="Locked">
      <div className="bg" style={{ backgroundImage: wp || undefined, backgroundColor: wp ? undefined : '#08111f' }} />
      <div className="lockContent">
        <div className="lt">{formatTime(lang, now, settings.clock24)}</div>
        <div className="ld">{formatDate(lang, now, { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' })}</div>
        <div className="lbadge"><OwlLogo size={12} /> OWL</div>
      </div>

      <div className="lockHint">
        {hasPassword ? (
          <div className="lockPassWrap">
            <div style={{ fontSize: 9, letterSpacing: '0.1em', opacity: 0.9 }}>
              {cooldown > 0
                ? `Too many attempts. Wait ${cooldown}s.`
                : 'Enter password'}
            </div>
            <div
              className="field"
              style={{ borderColor: failedAttempts ? 'var(--red)' : undefined }}
              role="textbox"
              aria-label="Password"
              onClick={() => useStore.getState().focusField('lock-pass')}
            >
              {pass ? '•'.repeat(pass.length) : <span style={{ opacity: 0.5 }}>{settings.passwordIsPin ? 'PIN' : 'password'}</span>}
              <span className="caret" />
            </div>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 5 }}>
              <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => void tryUnlock()} style={{ fontSize: 10 }}>
                Unlock
              </button>
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 8, opacity: 0.65, maxWidth: 190 }}>
              Forgot it? Settings → Reset OS wipes local data.
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            Press <kbd>Menu</kbd> then <kbd>Enter</kbd> to unlock
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default LockScreen;

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- lock field
 * The password field is registered once (from App) and its value is published
 * through a tiny external store, so the lock screen renders exactly what the
 * keyboard has typed without keeping a second copy of the password.
 */
let lockValue = '';
const lockListeners = new Set<(value: string) => void>();
function publishLockValue(next: string) {
  lockValue = next;
  lockListeners.forEach((l) => l(next));
}

export function useLockFieldValue(): string {
  const [value, setValue] = useState(lockValue);
  useEffect(() => {
    lockListeners.add(setValue);
    setValue(lockValue);
    return () => { lockListeners.delete(setValue); };
  }, []);
  return value;
}

/* Register the lock password as a virtual field so the physical keyboard types into it. */
export function useLockField() {
  const hasPassword = useStore((s) => !!s.settings.passwordHash);
  const passwordIsPin = useStore((s) => s.settings.passwordIsPin);
  // Refs, not state: the field is read synchronously by the kernel on every
  // keystroke, and a stale closure would drop the first characters typed.
  const valueRef = useRef('');
  const caretRef = useRef(0);

  useEffect(() => { publishLockValue(''); }, [hasPassword]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!hasPassword) return;
    let off: (() => void) | undefined;
    void import('../focus').then(({ registerField }) => {
      off = registerField({
        id: 'lock-pass',
        kind: passwordIsPin ? 'number' : 'password',
        getValue: () => {
          const value = valueRef.current;
          const caret = caretRef.current;
          const active = useStore.getState().activeFieldId === 'lock-pass';
          return active ? value.slice(0, caret) + '\u0001' + value.slice(caret) : value;
        },
        setValue: (raw) => {
          const i = raw.indexOf('\u0001');
          const clean = raw.replace('\u0001', '');
          valueRef.current = clean;
          caretRef.current = i >= 0 ? i : clean.length;
          publishLockValue(clean);
        },
        setCaret: (c) => {
          caretRef.current = Math.max(0, Math.min(c, valueRef.current.length));
        },
        maxLength: 12,
        onSubmit: () => {
          const attempt = valueRef.current;
          valueRef.current = '';
          caretRef.current = 0;
          publishLockValue('');
          void useStore.getState().unlock(attempt).then((ok) => {
            if (ok) successChime(0.6);
            else errorBuzz(0.6);
          });
        },
      });
    });
    return () => off?.();
  }, [hasPassword, passwordIsPin]);

  return { getValue: () => valueRef.current };
}
```

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../store';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';

export function PowerMenu() {
  const open = useStore((s) => s.powerMenuOpen);
  const setOpen = useStore((s) => s.setPowerMenuOpen);
  const lock = useStore((s) => s.lock);
  const powerOff = useStore((s) => s.powerOff);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  if (!open) return null;
  return (
    <div className="powerMenu" role="dialog" aria-label="Device power menu" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>
      <div className="pmCard" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <h4>Device</h4>
        <button onClick={() => { setOpen(false); lock(); }} autoFocus>
          <Icon name="lock" size={12} /> Lock keyboard
        </button>
        <button onClick={() => { setOpen(false); toast('Restarting…'); window.setTimeout(() => useStore.getState().powerOn(), 260); }}>
          <Icon name="repeat" size={12} /> Restart
        </button>
        <button onClick={() => { setOpen(false); powerOff(); }}>
          <Icon name="power" size={12} /> Shutdown
        </button>
        <button onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>
          <Icon name="close" size={12} /> Cancel
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default PowerMenu;
```

### `src/os/security.ts`

```ts
/**
 * Web Crypto helpers: PBKDF2 password hashing (lock screen) and
 * AES-GCM encryption (Password Keeper vault).
 */

const enc = new TextEncoder();
const dec = new TextDecoder();

function toHex(buf: ArrayBuffer): string {
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(buf))
    .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
    .join('');
}

function fromHex(hex: string): Uint8Array {
  const out = new Uint8Array(hex.length / 2);
  for (let i = 0; i < out.length; i++) out[i] = parseInt(hex.slice(i * 2, i * 2 + 2), 16);
  return out;
}

export function randomSalt(bytes = 16): string {
  const arr = new Uint8Array(bytes);
  crypto.getRandomValues(arr);
  return toHex(arr.buffer as ArrayBuffer);
}

const PBKDF2_ITERATIONS = 150000;

export async function hashPassword(password: string, saltHex: string): Promise<string> {
  const keyMaterial = await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw', enc.encode(password), 'PBKDF2', false, ['deriveBits']);
  const bits = await crypto.subtle.deriveBits(
    { name: 'PBKDF2', salt: fromHex(saltHex) as unknown as BufferSource, iterations: PBKDF2_ITERATIONS, hash: 'SHA-256' },
    keyMaterial,
    256
  );
  return toHex(bits);
}

export async function verifyPassword(password: string, saltHex: string, expectedHex: string): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!expectedHex) return false;
  const hash = await hashPassword(password, saltHex);
  // constant-time-ish compare
  if (hash.length !== expectedHex.length) return false;
  let diff = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < hash.length; i++) diff |= hash.charCodeAt(i) ^ expectedHex.charCodeAt(i);
  return diff === 0;
}

/* ----------------------------- Vault crypto ----------------------------- */

async function deriveAesKey(master: string, saltHex: string, iterations = 120000): Promise<CryptoKey> {
  const keyMaterial = await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw', enc.encode(master), 'PBKDF2', false, ['deriveKey']);
  return crypto.subtle.deriveKey(
    { name: 'PBKDF2', salt: fromHex(saltHex) as unknown as BufferSource, iterations, hash: 'SHA-256' },
    keyMaterial,
    { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 },
    false,
    ['encrypt', 'decrypt']
  );
}

export interface EncryptedBlob {
  iv: string;
  data: string;
  salt: string;
  verifier: string;
}

export async function encryptVault(master: string, plaintext: string, saltHex?: string): Promise<EncryptedBlob> {
  const salt = saltHex || randomSalt();
  const key = await deriveAesKey(master, salt);
  const iv = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12));
  const cipher = await crypto.subtle.encrypt({ name: 'AES-GCM', iv: iv as unknown as BufferSource }, key, enc.encode(plaintext));
  const verifier = await hashPassword(master, salt);
  return { iv: toHex(iv.buffer as ArrayBuffer), data: toHex(cipher), salt, verifier };
}

export async function decryptVault(master: string, blob: EncryptedBlob): Promise<string | null> {
  try {
    const key = await deriveAesKey(master, blob.salt);
    const plain = await crypto.subtle.decrypt(
      { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: fromHex(blob.iv) as any },
      key,
      fromHex(blob.data) as any
    );
    return dec.decode(plain);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function generatePassword(length = 16, opts = { upper: true, digits: true, symbols: true }): string {
  const lower = 'abcdefghijkmnopqrstuvwxyz';
  const upper = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ';
  const digits = '23456789';
  const symbols = '!@#$%^&*()-_=+[]{}?';
  let pool = lower;
  if (opts.upper) pool += upper;
  if (opts.digits) pool += digits;
  if (opts.symbols) pool += symbols;
  const arr = new Uint32Array(length);
  crypto.getRandomValues(arr);
  let out = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) out += pool[arr[i] % pool.length];
  return out;
}

export function passwordStrength(pw: string): { score: number; label: string } {
  let score = 0;
  if (pw.length >= 8) score++;
  if (pw.length >= 12) score++;
  if (/[A-Z]/.test(pw) && /[a-z]/.test(pw)) score++;
  if (/\d/.test(pw)) score++;
  if (/[^A-Za-z0-9]/.test(pw)) score++;
  const labels = ['Very weak', 'Weak', 'Fair', 'Good', 'Strong', 'Excellent'];
  return { score, label: labels[Math.min(score, 5)] };
}
```
