```ts
import { create } from 'zustand';
import type {
  DialogSpec, Lang, ProfileId, Screen, Settings, SoftMenuSpec, ToastSpec, ThemeId, DockRows,
} from './types';
import { APP_MAP, DOCK_IDS } from './appRegistry';
import { verifyPassword } from './security';
import { DB_KEYS, collectionGet, loadKey, saveKey, subscribeKey } from './db';
import { audioContext, messageTone, playRingtone, stopRingtone, vibrate } from './audio';

const SETTINGS_KEY = 'owl:settings:v1';

export function defaultSettings(now = Date.now()): Settings {
  return {
    language: 'en',
    userName: '',
    theme: 'owl-night',
    highContrast: false,
    largeText: false,
    reduceMotion: false,
    uiScale: 1,
    fontScale: 1,
    brightness: 1,
    backlight: 30,
    volume: 0.7,
    ringVolume: 0.75,
    alarmVolume: 0.85,
    keyClick: true,
    keyClickVolume: 0.5,
    vibrateKeys: false,
    vibrate: true,
    profile: 'normal',
    ringtone: 'owl-call',
    messageTone: 'soft-chime',
    alarmTone: 'alert-seven',
    clock24: false,
    timezone: 'local',
    showSeconds: false,
    wallpaperId: 'owl-night-glow',
    lockWallpaperId: 'owl-night-glow',
    useSeparateLockWallpaper: false,
    dockRows: 'one',
    hotkeys: {
      m: 'messages', o: 'messenger', a: 'contacts', b: 'web', c: 'calendar', p: 'phone',
      k: 'calculator', n: 'memo', t: 'flashlight', g: 'tasks', s: 'settings', f: 'files',
      x: 'games', w: 'music', i: 'pictures', h: 'camera', d: 'clock', l: 'passwords',
      e: 'email', v: 'videos', r: 'voice', u: 'themes', z: 'compass',
    },
    favorites: ['messages', 'web', 'music', 'snake', 'camera', 'memo'],
    hiddenApps: [],
    appOrder: [],
    folders: [],
    passwordHash: '',
    passwordSalt: '',
    passwordIsPin: false,
    autoLock: 60,
    powerSaver: false,
    bluetooth: true,
    wifiOnly: false,
    onboarded: false,
    homePage: 'owl://home',
    installedAt: now,
  };
}

function deepMergeSettings(base: Settings, patch: Partial<Settings>): Settings {
  return { ...base, ...patch, hotkeys: { ...base.hotkeys, ...(patch.hotkeys || {}) } };
}

export function loadSettingsSync(): Settings {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(SETTINGS_KEY);
    if (!raw) return defaultSettings();
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<Settings>;
    return deepMergeSettings(defaultSettings(), parsed);
  } catch {
    return defaultSettings();
  }
}

let saveTimer: number | undefined;
function persistSettings(settings: Settings) {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  if (saveTimer) window.clearTimeout(saveTimer);
  saveTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(SETTINGS_KEY, JSON.stringify(settings));
    } catch { /* quota */ }
  }, 180);
}

export interface AppStackEntry { appId: string; params?: Record<string, unknown> }

export interface BatteryState { level: number; charging: boolean }

interface OwlStore {
  /* boot / power */
  booting: boolean;
  bootProgress: number;
  powered: boolean;
  screen: Screen;
  locked: boolean;
  bootStart: () => void;
  finishBoot: () => void;
  powerOff: () => void;
  powerOn: () => void;
  lock: () => void;
  unlock: (password?: string) => Promise<boolean>;
  failedAttempts: number;
  cooldownUntil: number;

  /* settings */
  settings: Settings;
  updateSettings: (patch: Partial<Settings>) => void;
  setSetting: <K extends keyof Settings>(key: K, value: Settings[K]) => void;
  toggleThemePreview: (t: ThemeId | null) => void;
  themePreview: ThemeId | null;

  /* navigation */
  appId: string | null;
  appParams: Record<string, unknown>;
  appStack: AppStackEntry[];
  openApp: (id: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>) => void;
  closeApp: () => void;
  back: () => void;
  recentApps: string[];
  clearRecent: () => void;

  /* ui layer */
  drawerOpen: boolean;
  drawerTab: string;
  drawerFolder: string | null;
  openDrawer: (tab?: string) => void;
  closeDrawer: () => void;
  setDrawerTab: (tab: string) => void;
  setDrawerFolder: (id: string | null) => void;

  powerMenuOpen: boolean;
  setPowerMenuOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  recentOpen: boolean;
  setRecentOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  searchOpen: boolean;
  searchQuery: string;
  openSearch: (initial?: string) => void;
  closeSearch: () => void;
  setSearchQuery: (q: string) => void;

  /* focus / input */
  activeFieldId: string | null;
  focusField: (id: string | null) => void;

  /* modifiers */
  shift: boolean;
  caps: boolean;
  ctrl: boolean;
  alt: boolean;
  setShift: (v: boolean) => void;
  toggleShift: () => void;
  toggleCaps: () => void;
  setCtrl: (v: boolean) => void;
  setAlt: (v: boolean) => void;
  shiftOnce: boolean;
  consumeShiftOnce: () => void;

  pressedCodes: string[];
  setKeyPressed: (code: string, down: boolean) => void;
  clearPressed: () => void;

  /* dialogs / toasts / menus */
  dialog: DialogSpec | null;
  showDialog: (d: Omit<DialogSpec, 'id'>) => void;
  closeDialog: () => void;
  toasts: ToastSpec[];
  toast: (text: string) => void;
  dismissToast: (id: number) => void;
  softMenu: SoftMenuSpec | null;
  setSoftMenu: (s: SoftMenuSpec | null) => void;
  appMenu: SoftMenuSpec | null;
  setAppMenu: (s: SoftMenuSpec | null) => void;

  /* runtime info */
  battery: BatteryState;
  setBattery: (b: BatteryState) => void;
  online: boolean;
  setOnline: (v: boolean) => void;
  counters: { messages: number; missedCalls: number; reminders: number };
  setCounters: (c: Partial<{ messages: number; missedCalls: number; reminders: number }>) => void;
  refreshCounters: () => Promise<void>;
  volumeHud: { value: number; label: string } | null;
  bumpVolume: (delta: number, which?: 'media' | 'ring' | 'alarm') => void;
  showVolume: (label: string) => void;
  frequency: Record<string, number>;
  recordAppOpen: (id: string) => void;
  nowPlaying: { title: string; playing: boolean } | null;
  setNowPlaying: (v: { title: string; playing: boolean } | null) => void;
  wallpaperVersion: number;
  bumpWallpaper: () => void;
  clockTick: number;
  tickClock: () => void;
  alarmRinging: string | null;
  setAlarmRinging: (v: string | null) => void;
  incomingCall: { number: string; name?: string } | null;
  setIncomingCall: (v: { number: string; name?: string } | null) => void;
  notify: (title: string, kind?: 'message' | 'call' | 'calendar') => void;
}

const initialSettings = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? loadSettingsSync() : defaultSettings();

export const useStore = create<OwlStore>((set, get) => ({
  booting: true,
  bootProgress: 0,
  powered: true,
  screen: 'boot',
  locked: false,
  failedAttempts: 0,
  cooldownUntil: 0,
  bootStart: () => set({ booting: true, bootProgress: 0, screen: 'boot', powered: true }),
  finishBoot: () =>
    set((s) => ({
      booting: false,
      bootProgress: 100,
      // Never clobber an app that was opened while the logo was still animating
      // (the first-run wizard can do exactly that).
      screen: s.appId ? 'app' : s.locked ? 'lock' : 'home',
    })),
  powerOff: () => {
    stopRingtone();
    set({ powered: false, screen: 'off', drawerOpen: false, appId: null, appStack: [], softMenu: null, dialog: null });
  },
  powerOn: () => {
    set({ powered: true, booting: true, bootProgress: 0, screen: 'boot', locked: true, appId: null, appStack: [] });
  },
  lock: () => {
    set({
      locked: true,
      screen: 'lock',
      drawerOpen: false,
      softMenu: null,
      dialog: null,
      recentOpen: false,
      activeFieldId: null,
    });
  },
  unlock: async (password) => {
    const { settings, failedAttempts, cooldownUntil } = get();
    if (Date.now() < cooldownUntil) return false;
    if (!settings.passwordHash) {
      set({ locked: false, screen: get().appId ? 'app' : 'home', failedAttempts: 0 });
      return true;
    }
    if (!password) return false;
    const ok = await verifyPassword(password, settings.passwordSalt, settings.passwordHash);
    if (ok) {
      set({ locked: false, screen: 'home', failedAttempts: 0 });
      return true;
    }
    const attempts = failedAttempts + 1;
    if (attempts >= 5) {
      set({ failedAttempts: 0, cooldownUntil: Date.now() + 10000 });
    } else {
      set({ failedAttempts: attempts });
    }
    return false;
  },

  settings: initialSettings,
  updateSettings: (patch) =>
    set((s) => {
      const settings = deepMergeSettings(s.settings, patch);
      persistSettings(settings);
      return { settings };
    }),
  setSetting: (key, value) =>
    set((s) => {
      const settings = { ...s.settings, [key]: value };
      persistSettings(settings);
      return { settings };
    }),
  themePreview: null,
  toggleThemePreview: (t) => set({ themePreview: t }),

  appId: null,
  appParams: {},
  appStack: [],
  openApp: (id, params) => {
    const state = get();
    if (!APP_MAP[id]) return;
    if (state.locked) return;
    const normalizedId = id;
    const stack = state.appId ? [...state.appStack, { appId: state.appId, params: state.appParams }] : state.appStack;
    const recent = [normalizedId, ...state.recentApps.filter((r) => r !== normalizedId)].slice(0, 8);
    set({
      appId: normalizedId,
      appParams: params || {},
      appStack: stack,
      screen: 'app',
      drawerOpen: false,
      drawerFolder: null,
      searchOpen: false,
      searchQuery: '',
      softMenu: null,
      appMenu: null,
      activeFieldId: null,
      recentApps: recent,
    });
    get().recordAppOpen(normalizedId);
  },
  closeApp: () =>
    set({ appId: null, appParams: {}, appStack: [], screen: 'home', softMenu: null, appMenu: null, activeFieldId: null }),
  back: () => {
    const s = get();
    if (s.softMenu) { set({ softMenu: null }); return; }
    if (s.dialog) { get().closeDialog(); return; }
    if (s.recentOpen) { set({ recentOpen: false }); return; }
    if (s.drawerFolder) { set({ drawerFolder: null }); return; }
    if (s.drawerOpen) { set({ drawerOpen: false }); return; }
    if (s.searchOpen) { set({ searchOpen: false, searchQuery: '' }); return; }
    if (s.powerMenuOpen) { set({ powerMenuOpen: false }); return; }
    if (s.appId) {
      if (s.appStack.length > 0) {
        const prev = s.appStack[s.appStack.length - 1];
        set({
          appId: prev.appId,
          appParams: prev.params || {},
          appStack: s.appStack.slice(0, -1),
          screen: 'app',
          softMenu: null,
          appMenu: null,
          activeFieldId: null,
        });
      } else {
        get().closeApp();
      }
      return;
    }
  },
  recentApps: [],
  clearRecent: () => set({ recentApps: [] }),

  drawerOpen: false,
  drawerTab: 'all',
  drawerFolder: null,
  openDrawer: (tab) => set({ drawerOpen: true, drawerTab: tab || 'all', activeFieldId: null, searchOpen: false }),
  closeDrawer: () => set({ drawerOpen: false, drawerFolder: null }),
  setDrawerTab: (tab) => set({ drawerTab: tab, drawerFolder: null }),
  setDrawerFolder: (id) => set({ drawerFolder: id }),

  powerMenuOpen: false,
  setPowerMenuOpen: (v) => set({ powerMenuOpen: v, activeFieldId: null }),
  recentOpen: false,
  setRecentOpen: (v) => set({ recentOpen: v }),
  searchOpen: false,
  searchQuery: '',
  openSearch: (initial) => set({ searchOpen: true, searchQuery: initial || '', activeFieldId: null }),
  closeSearch: () => set({ searchOpen: false, searchQuery: '' }),
  setSearchQuery: (q) => set({ searchQuery: q }),

  activeFieldId: null,
  focusField: (id) => set({ activeFieldId: id }),

  shift: false,
  caps: false,
  ctrl: false,
  alt: false,
  shiftOnce: false,
  setShift: (v) => set({ shift: v }),
  toggleShift: () => set((s) => ({ shift: !s.shift || s.shiftOnce ? !s.shift : !s.shift, shiftOnce: false })),
  toggleCaps: () => set((s) => ({ caps: !s.caps })),
  setCtrl: (v) => set({ ctrl: v }),
  setAlt: (v) => set({ alt: v }),
  consumeShiftOnce: () => set({ shiftOnce: false, shift: false }),

  pressedCodes: [],
  setKeyPressed: (code, down) =>
    set((s) => {
      if (down) return s.pressedCodes.includes(code) ? s : { pressedCodes: [...s.pressedCodes, code] };
      return { pressedCodes: s.pressedCodes.filter((c) => c !== code) };
    }),
  clearPressed: () => set({ pressedCodes: [] }),

  dialog: null,
  showDialog: (d) =>
    set((s) => ({
      dialog: { ...d, id: Date.now() + Math.random(), showCancel: d.showCancel ?? true } as DialogSpec,
      activeFieldId: null,
    })),
  closeDialog: () => set({ dialog: null }),
  toasts: [],
  toast: (text) => {
    const id = Date.now() + Math.random();
    set((s) => ({ toasts: [...s.toasts.slice(-2), { id, text }] }));
    window.setTimeout(() => get().dismissToast(id), 2400);
  },
  dismissToast: (id) => set((s) => ({ toasts: s.toasts.filter((t) => t.id !== id) })),
  softMenu: null,
  setSoftMenu: (s) => set({ softMenu: s, activeFieldId: null }),
  appMenu: null,
  setAppMenu: (s) => set({ appMenu: s }),

  battery: { level: 0.82, charging: false },
  setBattery: (b) => set({ battery: b }),
  online: typeof navigator !== 'undefined' ? navigator.onLine : true,
  setOnline: (v) => set({ online: v }),
  counters: { messages: 0, missedCalls: 0, reminders: 0 },
  setCounters: (c) => set((s) => ({ counters: { ...s.counters, ...c } })),
  refreshCounters: async () => {
    const [threads, messages, calls, events] = await Promise.all([
      loadKey<{ id: string; address: string; name?: string; updatedAt: number }[]>(DB_KEYS.smsThreads, []),
      loadKey<{ threadId: string; read: boolean; direction: 'in' | 'out' }[]>(DB_KEYS.smsMessages, []),
      loadKey<{ type: 'in' | 'out' | 'missed'; at: number }[]>(DB_KEYS.callLog, []),
      loadKey<{ start: number }[]>(DB_KEYS.events, []),
    ]);
    void threads;
    const unread = messages.filter((m) => m.direction === 'in' && !m.read).length;
    const missed = calls.filter((c) => c.type === 'missed' && Date.now() - c.at < 86400000).length;
    const today = new Date();
    const reminders = events.filter((e) => {
      const d = new Date(e.start);
      return d.toDateString() === today.toDateString();
    }).length;
    set({ counters: { messages: unread, missedCalls: missed, reminders } });
  },
  volumeHud: null,
  bumpVolume: (delta, which = 'media') => {
    const s = get();
    if (s.settings.profile === 'silent' && which === 'media') {
      s.showVolume('Silent profile');
      return;
    }
    const key = which === 'ring' ? 'ringVolume' : which === 'alarm' ? 'alarmVolume' : 'volume';
    const current = s.settings[key] as number;
    const next = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, Math.round((current + delta) * 20) / 20));
    if (next === current) {
      set({ volumeHud: { value: next, label: which } });
    } else {
      get().setSetting(key as any, next as any);
      set({ volumeHud: { value: next, label: which } });
      if (which === 'media' && next > 0) {
        audioContext();
        vibrate(6);
      }
    }
    window.setTimeout(() => set({ volumeHud: null }), 1400);
  },
  showVolume: (label) => {
    set({ volumeHud: { value: get().settings.volume, label } });
    window.setTimeout(() => set({ volumeHud: null }), 1200);
  },
  frequency: {},
  recordAppOpen: (id) => {
    const freq = { ...get().frequency, [id]: (get().frequency[id] || 0) + 1 };
    set({ frequency: freq });
    void saveKey(DB_KEYS.frequency, freq);
  },
  nowPlaying: null,
  setNowPlaying: (v) => set({ nowPlaying: v }),
  wallpaperVersion: 0,
  bumpWallpaper: () => set((s) => ({ wallpaperVersion: s.wallpaperVersion + 1 })),
  clockTick: 0,
  tickClock: () => set((s) => ({ clockTick: s.clockTick + 1 })),
  alarmRinging: null,
  setAlarmRinging: (v) => {
    if (v) {
      const s = get();
      if (s.settings.profile !== 'silent') playRingtone(s.settings.alarmTone, s.settings.alarmVolume);
      if (s.settings.vibrate) vibrate([400, 200, 400, 200, 400]);
      set({ alarmRinging: v });
    } else {
      stopRingtone();
      set({ alarmRinging: null });
    }
  },
  incomingCall: null,
  setIncomingCall: (v) => {
    const s = get();
    if (v) {
      if (s.settings.profile !== 'silent') playRingtone(s.settings.ringtone, s.settings.ringVolume);
      if (s.settings.vibrate) vibrate([500, 300, 500, 300, 500]);
      set({ incomingCall: v });
    } else {
      stopRingtone();
      set({ incomingCall: null });
    }
  },
  notify: (title, kind = 'message') => {
    const s = get();
    if (s.settings.profile !== 'silent') messageTone(s.settings.messageTone, s.settings.volume);
    if (s.settings.vibrate) vibrate([120, 60, 120]);
    void s.refreshCounters();
    if (kind === 'message') {
      const c = get().counters;
      set({ counters: { ...c, messages: c.messages + 1 } });
    }
    if (typeof Notification !== 'undefined' && Notification.permission === 'granted' && document.hidden) {
      try { new Notification('OWL', { body: title, icon: '/pwa-192.png' }); } catch { /* ignore */ }
    }
    if (!s.appId) s.toast(title);
  },
}));

/* subscribe helpers exported for data hooks */
export { subscribeKey };

/* Restore persisted frequency + battery/wifi listeners once, on first import. */
if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
  void loadKey<Record<string, number>>(DB_KEYS.frequency, {}).then((freq) => {
    if (freq && Object.keys(freq).length) useStore.setState({ frequency: freq });
  });
  void useStore.getState().refreshCounters();

  window.addEventListener('online', () => useStore.getState().setOnline(true));
  window.addEventListener('offline', () => useStore.getState().setOnline(false));

  const nav = navigator as any;
  if (nav.getBattery) {
    nav.getBattery().then((b: any) => {
      const update = () => useStore.getState().setBattery({ level: b.level, charging: b.charging });
      update();
      b.addEventListener('levelchange', update);
      b.addEventListener('chargingchange', update);
    }).catch(() => {});
  } else {
    // simulated drain: 1% every 90s
    let level = 0.82;
    window.setInterval(() => {
      level = Math.max(0.03, level - 0.01 / 9);
      useStore.getState().setBattery({ level, charging: level > 0.95 });
    }, 10000);
  }
}

export const useSetting = <K extends keyof Settings>(key: K): Settings[K] =>
  useStore((s) => s.settings[key]);

export const useT = () => {
  const lang = useStore((s) => s.settings.language);
  return (en: string, ar: string) => (lang === 'ar' ? ar : en);
};

export const dockIds = (s: Settings): string[] => {
  const ids = s.appOrder.length ? s.appOrder.filter((id) => DOCK_IDS.includes(id)).slice(0, 6) : [];
  return ids.length ? ids : DOCK_IDS;
};

export function profileVolume(s: Settings): number {
  switch (s.profile) {
    case 'loud': return 1;
    case 'vibrate': return 0;
    case 'silent': return 0;
    case 'meeting': return 0.25;
    default: return s.volume;
  }
}

export function profileLabel(p: ProfileId, lang: Lang): string {
  const map: Record<ProfileId, [string, string]> = {
    normal: ['Normal', 'عادي'],
    loud: ['Loud', 'عالي'],
    vibrate: ['Vibrate', 'اهتزاز'],
    silent: ['Silent', 'صامت'],
    meeting: ['Meeting', 'اجتماع'],
    custom: ['Custom', 'مخصص'],
  };
  return map[p][lang === 'ar' ? 1 : 0];
}

export type { OwlStore, DockRows };
```

```ts
/* Shared OS types for OWL OS */

export type ThemeId = 'owl-night' | 'classic-silver' | 'forest' | 'ruby' | 'ocean' | 'lcd';
export type Lang = 'en' | 'ar';
export type ProfileId = 'normal' | 'loud' | 'vibrate' | 'silent' | 'meeting' | 'custom';
export type Screen = 'boot' | 'home' | 'lock' | 'off' | 'app';
export type DockRows = 'one' | 'two' | 'hidden';

export interface Settings {
  language: Lang;
  userName: string;
  theme: ThemeId;
  highContrast: boolean;
  largeText: boolean;
  reduceMotion: boolean;
  uiScale: number;
  fontScale: number;
  brightness: number;
  backlight: number; // seconds, 0 = off
  volume: number;
  ringVolume: number;
  alarmVolume: number;
  keyClick: boolean;
  keyClickVolume: number;
  vibrateKeys: boolean;
  vibrate: boolean;
  profile: ProfileId;
  ringtone: string;
  messageTone: string;
  alarmTone: string;
  clock24: boolean;
  timezone: string;
  showSeconds: boolean;
  wallpaperId: string;
  lockWallpaperId: string;
  useSeparateLockWallpaper: boolean;
  dockRows: DockRows;
  hotkeys: Record<string, string>;
  favorites: string[];
  hiddenApps: string[];
  appOrder: string[];
  folders: { id: string; name: string; apps: string[] }[];
  passwordHash: string;
  passwordSalt: string;
  passwordIsPin: boolean;
  autoLock: number; // seconds, 0 = never
  powerSaver: boolean;
  bluetooth: boolean;
  wifiOnly: boolean;
  onboarded: boolean;
  homePage: string;
  installedAt: number;
}

export interface AppMeta {
  id: string;
  name: Record<Lang, string>;
  icon: string;
  color: string;
  category: 'communication' | 'internet' | 'media' | 'camera' | 'utilities' | 'games' | 'system';
  hotkey?: string;
  dock?: boolean;
  load: () => Promise<{ default: React.ComponentType<any> }>;
}

export interface NavSnapshot {
  items: string[];
  index: number;
}

export interface DialogSpec {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  body: string;
  confirmLabel?: string;
  cancelLabel?: string;
  danger?: boolean;
  input?: { label: string; value: string; placeholder?: string; password?: boolean; multiline?: boolean };
  onConfirm: (value: string) => void;
  onCancel?: () => void;
  showCancel?: boolean;
}

export interface SoftMenuAction {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  icon?: string;
  danger?: boolean;
  run: () => void;
}

export interface SoftMenuSpec {
  title: string;
  actions: SoftMenuAction[];
}

export interface ToastSpec { id: number; text: string }

export interface Contact {
  id: string;
  firstName: string;
  lastName: string;
  phone: string;
  email: string;
  notes: string;
  group: string;
  favorite: boolean;
  speedDial: number | null;
  photo?: string; // data URL
  createdAt: number;
}

export interface SmsMessage {
  id: string;
  threadId: string;
  address: string;
  body: string;
  at: number;
  direction: 'in' | 'out';
  status: 'sent' | 'delivered' | 'draft';
  read: boolean;
}

export interface SmsThread {
  id: string;
  address: string;
  name?: string;
  updatedAt: number;
  archived?: boolean;
}

export interface Note {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  body: string;
  category: string;
  at: number;
  updatedAt: number;
}

export interface TaskItem {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  notes: string;
  due?: string;
  priority: 'low' | 'normal' | 'high';
  status: 'not-started' | 'in-progress' | 'completed';
  category: string;
  createdAt: number;
}

export interface CalEvent {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  location: string;
  notes: string;
  start: number; // epoch ms
  end: number;
  allDay: boolean;
  reminderMinutes: number | null;
  repeat: 'none' | 'daily' | 'weekly' | 'monthly' | 'yearly';
  color: string;
  taskId?: string;
}

export interface VaultEntry {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  username: string;
  password: string;
  website: string;
  notes: string;
  updatedAt: number;
}

export interface Alarm {
  id: string;
  hour: number;
  minute: number;
  label: string;
  days: number[]; // 0=Sun
  enabled: boolean;
  sound: string;
  snoozeUntil?: number;
}

export interface ChatMessage {
  id: string;
  from: 'me' | 'them';
  body: string;
  at: number;
  read: boolean;
}

export interface ChatThread {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  avatar: string;
  color: string;
  messages: ChatMessage[];
  typing?: boolean;
}

export interface CallLogEntry {
  id: string;
  number: string;
  name?: string;
  type: 'in' | 'out' | 'missed';
  at: number;
  duration: number;
}

export interface VoiceNote {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  blob: Blob;
  at: number;
  duration: number;
}

export interface PhotoItem {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  blob: Blob;
  at: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

export interface BrowserBookmark { id: string; title: string; url: string; at: number }
export interface BrowserHistory { id: string; title: string; url: string; at: number }
export interface BrowserTab { id: string; title: string; url: string; historyIndex: number; history: string[] }

export interface GameScore { game: string; score: number; extra?: string; at: number }
```

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import { APP_MAP } from '../os/appRegistry';
import { Icon } from './Icon';

const THEME_ICON_STYLES: Record<string, { radius: string; gradient?: string; filter?: string; flat?: boolean }> = {
  'owl-night': { radius: '7px', gradient: 'linear-gradient(155deg, rgba(255,255,255,.38), rgba(255,255,255,.03) 52%, rgba(0,0,0,.35))' },
  'classic-silver': { radius: '5px', gradient: 'linear-gradient(160deg, rgba(255,255,255,.85), rgba(255,255,255,.2) 50%, rgba(0,0,0,.25))' },
  'forest': { radius: '7px', gradient: 'linear-gradient(155deg, rgba(255,255,255,.3), rgba(0,0,0,.3))' },
  'ruby': { radius: '50%', gradient: 'linear-gradient(155deg, rgba(255,255,255,.35), rgba(0,0,0,.35))' },
  'ocean': { radius: '10px', gradient: 'linear-gradient(155deg, rgba(255,255,255,.32), rgba(0,0,0,.3))' },
  'lcd': { radius: '0px', gradient: 'none', filter: 'grayscale(.85) contrast(1.15)' },
};

export function AppIcon({ id, size = 24 }: { id: string; size?: number }) {
  const meta = APP_MAP[id];
  const theme = typeof document !== 'undefined' ? document.documentElement.dataset.theme || 'owl-night' : 'owl-night';
  const style = THEME_ICON_STYLES[theme] || THEME_ICON_STYLES['owl-night'];
  const color = meta?.color || '#4a5568';
  const iconSize = Math.round(size * 0.56);
  return (
    <span
      className="appIcon"
      style={{
        width: size,
        height: size,
        borderRadius: style.radius,
        background: style.flat ? 'transparent' : `linear-gradient(160deg, ${color}, ${shade(color, -38)})`,
        filter: style.filter,
        boxShadow: theme === 'lcd' ? '0 0 0 1.5px #33471f' : undefined,
      }}
      aria-hidden="true"
    >
      <span style={{ position: 'relative', zIndex: 1, display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center' }}>
        <Icon name={meta?.icon || 'info'} size={iconSize} strokeWidth={1.9} />
      </span>
      {!style.flat && (
        <span
          style={{
            position: 'absolute', inset: 0, borderRadius: 'inherit', background: style.gradient, pointerEvents: 'none',
          }}
        />
      )}
    </span>
  );
}

function shade(hex: string, amount: number): string {
  const h = hex.replace('#', '');
  const num = parseInt(h.length === 3 ? h.split('').map((c) => c + c).join('') : h, 16);
  let r = (num >> 16) + amount;
  let g = ((num >> 8) & 0xff) + amount;
  let b = (num & 0xff) + amount;
  r = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, r));
  g = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, g));
  b = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, b));
  return `#${((r << 16) | (g << 8) | b).toString(16).padStart(6, '0')}`;
}

export default AppIcon;
```

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useId, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../os/store';
import { registerField, caretOf, fieldValue, type FieldKind } from '../os/focus';

interface FieldProps {
  value: string;
  onChange: (v: string) => void;
  kind?: FieldKind;
  placeholder?: string;
  multiline?: boolean;
  maxLength?: number;
  onSubmit?: () => void;
  onMove?: (dir: 'up' | 'down' | 'left' | 'right') => boolean;
  className?: string;
  ariaLabel?: string;
  autoFocus?: boolean;
  style?: React.CSSProperties;
}

/**
 * A controlled text field that never takes real DOM focus. Characters are
 * routed from the OS keyboard (real or on-screen) using the virtual focus
 * registry, so no mobile keyboard ever pops up.
 */
export function Field({
  value, onChange, kind = 'text', placeholder, multiline, maxLength, onSubmit, onMove,
  className = '', ariaLabel, autoFocus, style,
}: FieldProps) {
  const id = useId();
  const activeFieldId = useStore((s) => s.activeFieldId);
  const focusField = useStore((s) => s.focusField);
  const setShift = useStore((s) => s.setShift);
  const shift = useStore((s) => s.shift);
  const caps = useStore((s) => s.caps);
  const [caret, setCaret] = useState(0);
  const active = activeFieldId === id;
  const caretRef = useRef(caret);
  caretRef.current = caret;

  const kindToInput: Record<FieldKind, string> = {
    text: 'text', password: 'password', number: 'numeric', decimal: 'decimal',
    phone: 'tel', multiline: 'text',
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const off = registerField({
      id,
      kind,
      getValue: () => {
        const raw = value || '';
        if (!active) return raw;
        return raw.slice(0, caretRef.current) + '\u0001' + raw.slice(caretRef.current);
      },
      setValue: (next) => {
        const idx = next.indexOf('\u0001');
        if (idx >= 0) {
          setCaret(idx);
          onChange(next.replace('\u0001', ''));
        } else {
          setCaret(next.length);
          onChange(next);
        }
      },
      setCaret: (c) => setCaret(Math.max(0, Math.min(c, (value || '').length))),
      onSubmit,
      onMove,
      maxLength,
    });
    return off;
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [id, active, value, caret, maxLength]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (autoFocus) {
      focusField(id);
      setCaret((value || '').length);
    }
    return () => {
      const s = useStore.getState();
      if (s.activeFieldId === id) s.focusField(null);
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (active) setCaret((value || '').length);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [active]);

  const activate = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    focusField(id);
    if ((e.target as HTMLElement).tagName === 'INPUT') return;
  };

  if (multiline) {
    return (
      <textarea
        readOnly
        inputMode="none"
        className={`field ${active ? 'active' : ''} ${className}`}
        aria-label={ariaLabel || placeholder || 'text field'}
        value={value}
        placeholder={placeholder}
        onClick={activate}
        onFocus={(e) => { e.currentTarget.blur(); focusField(id); }}
        style={style}
        rows={3}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <div
      className={`field ${className}`}
      role="textbox"
      aria-label={ariaLabel || placeholder || 'text field'}
      aria-multiline={false}
      tabIndex={-1}
      onClick={activate}
      onFocus={() => focusField(id)}
      style={style}
      inputMode="none"
    >
      {active ? (
        <>
          <span className="fieldText">
            {value.slice(0, caret)}
            <span className="caret" />
            {value.slice(caret)}
          </span>
          {!value && <span className="placeholderText" style={{ opacity: 0.5 }}>{placeholder}</span>}
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          {kind === 'password' && value
            ? <span>{'•'.repeat(value.length)}</span>
            : <span style={{ opacity: value ? 1 : 0.55 }}>{value || placeholder}</span>}
        </>
      )}
      <input
        type="text"
        value=""
        readOnly
        inputMode="none"
        aria-hidden="true"
        tabIndex={-1}
        autoComplete="off"
        autoCorrect="off"
        autoCapitalize="off"
        spellCheck={false}
        onChange={() => {}}
        onFocus={(e) => { e.currentTarget.blur(); focusField(id); }}
        style={{ position: 'absolute', opacity: 0, width: 1, height: 1, border: 0, padding: 0, pointerEvents: 'none' }}
      />
      <span
        aria-hidden="true"
        style={{ position: 'absolute', opacity: 0, pointerEvents: 'none' }}
        data-shift={shift ? 1 : 0}
        data-caps={caps ? 1 : 0}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

/** Non-focusable read-only display that still keeps caret helpers consistent. */
export function fieldText(raw: string) {
  return fieldValue(raw);
}

export { caretOf };
export default Field;
```

```tsx
  headphones: 'M4 14v-2a8 8 0 0 1 16 0v2 M4 14h3v6H6a2 2 0 0 1-2-2z M20 14h-3v6h1a2 2 0 0 0 2-2z',
  compassRose: 'M12 21a9 9 0 1 0 0-18 9 9 0 0 0 0 18Z M12 3v3 M12 18v3 M3 12h3 M18 12h3',
};

export type IconName = keyof typeof P | string;

export function Icon({
  name,
  size = 16,
  strokeWidth = 1.7,
  className,
  style,
  fill = false,
}: {
  name: IconName;
  size?: number;
  strokeWidth?: number;
  className?: string;
  style?: React.CSSProperties;
  fill?: boolean;
}) {
  const path = P[name] || P.info;
  return (
    <svg
      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      width={size}
      height={size}
      className={className}
      style={style}
      role="img"
      aria-hidden="true"
      focusable="false"
    >
      <path
        d={path}
        fill={fill ? 'currentColor' : 'none'}
        stroke="currentColor"
        strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
        strokeLinecap="round"
        strokeLinejoin="round"
      />
    </svg>
  );
}

export default Icon;
```

### `src/components/Keyboard.tsx`

_6134 bytes · 154 lines_

```tsx
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../os/store';
import { KEY_ROWS, type KeyDef } from '../os/keys';
import { keyClick, vibrate } from '../os/audio';

interface KbProps {
  /** called for every logical key press; return true when consumed */
  onKey: (desc: { key: string; code: string; shift: boolean; caps: boolean; ctrl: boolean; alt: boolean }) => boolean;
}

/**
 * The physical keyboard of the phone. Presses come from either the real
 * keyboard (visual sync only — routing happens in the kernel) or from touch,
 * where each key is dispatched through the same handler.
 */
export function Keyboard({ onKey }: KbProps) {
  const pressed = useStore((s) => s.pressedCodes);
  const shift = useStore((s) => s.shift);
  const caps = useStore((s) => s.caps);
  const settings = useStore((s) => s.settings);
  const [bubble, setBubble] = useState<{ label: string; x: number; y: number } | null>(null);
  const touchLetter = useRef<string | null>(null);

  const activeShiftVisual = shift;

  const handlePointer = useCallback(
    (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLButtonElement>, def: KeyDef, kind: 'down' | 'up') => {
      if (kind === 'down') {
        e.preventDefault();
        (e.currentTarget as HTMLButtonElement).setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);
        if (def.kind === 'char' || def.kind === 'nav' || def.kind === 'action') {
          const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
          const label = def.kind === 'char'
            ? legendFor(def, useStore.getState().shift, useStore.getState().caps)
            : def.label || '';
          setBubble({ label, x: rect.left + rect.width / 2, y: rect.top });
        }
        if (settings.keyClick) keyClick(settings.keyClickVolume);
        if (settings.vibrateKeys) vibrate(8);
        // dispatch logical key
        const s = useStore.getState();
        let keyValue = '';
        if (def.kind === 'char') {
          keyValue = legendFor(def, s.shift, s.caps);
          if (s.shift) s.setShift(false);
        } else if (def.kind === 'nav') {
          keyValue = def.code;
        } else {
          keyValue = def.code === 'Space' ? ' ' : def.code;
        }
        onKey({
          key: keyValue,
          code: def.code,
          shift: def.kind === 'char' ? s.shift : false,
          caps: s.caps,
          ctrl: s.ctrl,
          alt: s.alt,
        });
        useStore.getState().setKeyPressed(def.code, true);
        window.setTimeout(() => useStore.getState().setKeyPressed(def.code, false), 110);
      } else {
        setBubble(null);
      }
    },
    [onKey, settings.keyClick, settings.keyClickVolume, settings.vibrateKeys]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!bubble) return;
    const t = window.setTimeout(() => setBubble(null), 900);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(t);
  }, [bubble]);

  return (
    <div className="kb" role="group" aria-label="QWERTY keyboard">
      {KEY_ROWS.map((row, ri) => (
        <div className="kbRow" key={ri}>
          {row.map((def) => {
            const isModActive =
              (def.code.startsWith('Shift') && activeShiftVisual) ||
              (def.code === 'CapsLock' && caps) ||
              (def.code === 'ControlLeft' && useStore.getState().ctrl) ||
              (def.code === 'AltLeft' && useStore.getState().alt);
            const down = pressed.includes(def.code) || (isModActive && def.kind === 'modifier');
            const tiny = def.label && def.label.length > 3;
            return (
              <button
                key={def.code}
                type="button"
                className={`key ${def.kind === 'modifier' || def.kind === 'action' ? 'mod' : ''} ${tiny ? 'small' : ''} ${def.code === 'Space' ? 'space' : ''} ${down ? 'lit down' : ''} ${isModActive ? 'on' : ''}`}
                style={{ flexGrow: def.width, flexBasis: `${def.width * 10}px` }}
                aria-label={def.aria}
                aria-pressed={down}
                tabIndex={-1}
                onPointerDown={(e) => handlePointer(e, def, 'down')}
                onPointerUp={(e) => handlePointer(e, def, 'up')}
                onPointerCancel={(e) => handlePointer(e, def, 'up')}
                onPointerLeave={(e) => { if (e.buttons === 0) handlePointer(e, def, 'up'); }}
                onContextMenu={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
              >
                <span className="legend">
                  {def.kind === 'char' && def.label ? (
                    <>
                      <b>{displayLegend(def, shift, caps)}</b>
                      {def.legend && <s>{shift ? def.label : def.legend}</s>}
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <b className="arr">{def.label}</b>
                  )}
                </span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      ))}
      <div className="leds">
        <span className={`led ${caps ? 'on' : ''}`}><i />CAPS</span>
        <span className={`led ${shift ? 'on' : ''}`}><i />SHIFT</span>
        <span className="led"><i />PWR</span>
      </div>
      {bubble && (
        <div className="keyPreview" style={{ left: bubble.x, top: bubble.y }}>
          {bubble.label}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function displayLegend(def: KeyDef, shift: boolean, caps: boolean): string {
  if (def.label && def.label.length === 1 && /[a-z]/i.test(def.label)) {
    const letter = def.label.toUpperCase();
    return shift !== caps ? letter : letter.toLowerCase();
  }
  if (shift && def.legend) {
    // show the shifted symbol in the main slot for digits/symbols
    if (def.code.startsWith('Digit')) return def.legend;
    return def.legend;
  }
  return def.label || '';
}

function legendFor(def: KeyDef, shift: boolean, caps: boolean): string {
  if (def.kind !== 'char') return def.code;
  if (def.code === 'Space') return ' ';
  if (def.label && def.label.length === 1 && /[a-z]/i.test(def.label)) {
    return shift !== caps ? def.label.toUpperCase() : def.label.toLowerCase();
  }
  if (shift && def.legend) return def.legend;
  return def.label || '';
}

export default Keyboard;
```

```tsx
import React from 'react';

/**
 * The OWL mark: a front-facing owl face drawn with layered, chrome-embossed
 * strokes (highlight + shadow + gradient) so it reads as extruded 3D — not a
 * flat glyph. Original artwork, used on the forehead, boot screen, menu key,
 * favicon and PWA icon.
 */
export function OwlLogo({
  size = 24,
  glow = false,
  mono = false,
  title = 'OWL',
}: {
  size?: number;
  glow?: boolean;
  mono?: boolean;
  title?: string;
}) {
  const id = React.useId().replace(/[:]/g, '');
  return (
    <svg
      width={size}
      height={size}
      viewBox="0 0 64 64"
      role="img"
      aria-label={title}
      style={{ display: 'block', overflow: 'visible' }}
    >
      <defs>
        <linearGradient id={`chrome${id}`} x1="0" y1="0" x2="0.3" y2="1">
          <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#ffffff" />
          <stop offset="34%" stopColor="#c8d3e2" />
          <stop offset="52%" stopColor="#6e7b8d" />
          <stop offset="70%" stopColor="#eef3fa" />
          <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#8d99ab" />
        </linearGradient>
        <linearGradient id={`amber${id}`} x1="0.2" y1="0" x2="0.8" y2="1">
          <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#ffe9b0" />
          <stop offset="45%" stopColor="#f6b93f" />
          <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#a86f10" />
        </linearGradient>
        <linearGradient id={`navy${id}`} x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
          <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#2b3e5e" />
          <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#060c18" />
        </linearGradient>
        <filter id={`glow${id}`} x="-60%" y="-60%" width="220%" height="220%">
          <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="1.6" result="b" />
          <feMerge>
            <feMergeNode in="b" />
            <feMergeNode in="SourceGraphic" />
          </feMerge>
        </filter>
      </defs>

      <g filter={glow ? `url(#glow${id})` : undefined}>
        {/* extruded drop-shadow pass */}
        <g transform="translate(0.9 1.4)" opacity="0.55" style={{ mixBlendMode: 'multiply' }}>
          <path d={EAR_PATH} fill={mono ? '#000' : '#000'} />
          <path d={HEAD_PATH} fill="none" stroke="#000" strokeWidth="5.4" strokeLinejoin="round" />
        </g>

        {/* ear tufts */}
        <path d={EAR_PATH} fill={mono ? 'none' : `url(#chrome${id})`} stroke={mono ? 'currentColor' : '#0d1525'} strokeWidth="1.2" strokeLinejoin="round" />

        {/* head outline: layered strokes = bevel */}
        <path d={HEAD_PATH} fill="none" stroke={mono ? 'currentColor' : 'rgba(8,14,26,0.9)'} strokeWidth="5.6" strokeLinejoin="round" />
        <path d={HEAD_PATH} fill={mono ? 'none' : `url(#chrome${id})`} stroke={mono ? 'currentColor' : 'none'} strokeWidth="3.4" strokeLinejoin="round" />
        <path d={HEAD_PATH} fill="none" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.85)" strokeWidth="1.1" strokeLinejoin="round" transform="translate(-0.4 -0.5)" opacity="0.7" />

        {/* eye discs */}
        <g>
          <circle cx="22" cy="29" r="11.4" fill={mono ? 'none' : `url(#navy${id})`} stroke={mono ? 'currentColor' : '#0c1322'} strokeWidth="2.6" />
          <circle cx="42" cy="29" r="11.4" fill={mono ? 'none' : `url(#navy${id})`} stroke={mono ? 'currentColor' : '#0c1322'} strokeWidth="2.6" />
          <circle cx="22" cy="29" r="8.4" fill={mono ? 'none' : `url(#amber${id})`} opacity={mono ? 1 : 0.95} />
          <circle cx="42" cy="29" r="8.4" fill={mono ? 'none' : `url(#amber${id})`} opacity={mono ? 1 : 0.95} />
          <circle cx="22" cy="29" r="4.6" fill={mono ? 'none' : '#08101e'} />
          <circle cx="42" cy="29" r="4.6" fill={mono ? 'none' : '#08101e'} />
          <circle cx="20.1" cy="27" r="1.7" fill="#fff" opacity="0.95" />
          <circle cx="40.1" cy="27" r="1.7" fill="#fff" opacity="0.95" />
        </g>

        {/* brow ridge + beak */}
        <path d="M11 18.5c6-3.4 16-4.6 21-1.2M31 17.3c5-3.4 15-2.2 21 1.2" fill="none" stroke={mono ? 'currentColor' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.65)'} strokeWidth="1.3" strokeLinecap="round" opacity="0.7" />
        <path d="M32 35.5l5.4 4.2-5.4 4.6-5.4-4.6z" fill={mono ? 'none' : `url(#amber${id})`} stroke={mono ? 'currentColor' : '#5c3a04'} strokeWidth="1.2" strokeLinejoin="round" />
        <path d="M32 36.4v7.4" stroke="rgba(90,52,4,0.55)" strokeWidth="0.9" />
        <path d="M22.5 47c3.4 3.6 15.6 3.6 19 0" fill="none" stroke={mono ? 'currentColor' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.45)'} strokeWidth="1.2" strokeLinecap="round" opacity="0.55" />
      </g>
    </svg>
  );
}

const HEAD_PATH =
  'M32 11c-9.6 0-16.4 6.1-17.8 14.6-.6 3.6-.2 7 .5 10.3 1.2 5.4 4.6 10.2 9.2 13.2 2.5 1.6 5.3 2.6 8.1 2.6s5.6-1 8.1-2.6c4.6-3 8-7.8 9.2-13.2.7-3.3 1.1-6.7.5-10.3C48.4 17.1 41.6 11 32 11Z';

const EAR_PATH =
  'M17.6 21.4c-1.4-4.4-3.6-8-6.2-10.6 2.8-1.2 6.6-.4 9.6 1.6 2 1.3 3.6 3 4.8 5 1.4-3.6 3.4-5.7 6.2-6.4 3.6-1.4 7.6-1.2 10.6.6-2.4 2.6-4.4 6.2-5.8 10.4';

/** Compact wordmark: owl mark + OWL lettering with chrome gradient */
export function OwlWordmark({ size = 18 }: { size?: number }) {
  const id = React.useId().replace(/[:]/g, '');
  return (
    <span style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: size * 0.3 }}>
      <OwlLogo size={size} />
      <svg width={size * 2.55} height={size * 0.62} viewBox="0 0 100 26" aria-hidden="true">
        <defs>
          <linearGradient id={`wm${id}`} x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
            <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#ffffff" />
            <stop offset="55%" stopColor="#dfe7f2" />
            <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#93a1b6" />
          </linearGradient>
        </defs>
        <text
          x="0"
          y="20"
          fill={`url(#wm${id})`}
          fontFamily="Rajdhani, Inter, sans-serif"
          fontWeight="700"
          fontSize="24"
          letterSpacing="4"
        >
          OWL
        </text>
      </svg>
    </span>
  );
}

export default OwlLogo;
```

```tsx
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../os/store';
import { OwlLogo } from './OwlLogo';
import { Keyboard } from './Keyboard';
import { Icon } from './Icon';
import { keyClick, navBlip, vibrate } from '../os/audio';
import type { KeyEventLike } from '../os/kernelTypes';

interface PhoneShellProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  onKey: (e: KeyEventLike) => boolean;
  screenRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;
  scale: number;
}

/**
 * The device: bezel, screen, navigation row with optical trackpad, full
 * laptop-layout QWERTY and side keys. Pure HTML/CSS/SVG.
 */
export function PhoneShell({ children, onKey, screenRef, scale }: PhoneShellProps) {
  const settings = useStore((s) => s.settings);
  const toggleCaps = useStore((s) => s.toggleCaps);
  const setShift = useStore((s) => s.setShift);
  const bumpVolume = useStore((s) => s.bumpVolume);
  const powerMenu = useStore((s) => s.setPowerMenuOpen);
  const lock = useStore((s) => s.lock);
  const screen = useStore((s) => s.screen);
  const setRecentOpen = useStore((s) => s.setRecentOpen);
  const back = useStore((s) => s.back);
  const openDrawer = useStore((s) => s.openDrawer);
  const openApp = useStore((s) => s.openApp);
  const openSearch = useStore((s) => s.openSearch);
  const appId = useStore((s) => s.appId);
  const setKeyPressed = useStore((s) => s.setKeyPressed);
  const pressed = useStore((s) => s.pressedCodes);

  const [tilt, setTilt] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const [trail, setTrail] = useState(0);
  const powerTimer = useRef<number | undefined>(undefined);
  const menuTimer = useRef<number | undefined>(undefined);
  const padRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (settings.reduceMotion) return;
    const onMove = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      const nx = (e.clientX / window.innerWidth - 0.5) * 2;
      const ny = (e.clientY / window.innerHeight - 0.5) * 2;
      setTilt({ x: nx * 2.4, y: ny * 1.6 });
    };
    window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
  }, [settings.reduceMotion]);

  useEffect(() => () => {
    window.clearTimeout(powerTimer.current);
    window.clearTimeout(menuTimer.current);
  }, []);

  const clickFeedback = () => {
    if (settings.keyClick) keyClick(settings.keyClickVolume);
    if (settings.vibrateKeys) vibrate(8);
    setTrail((t) => t + 1);
  };

  /* ------------------------------ nav keys ------------------------------ */
  const onCall = () => {
    clickFeedback();
    setKeyPressed('F2', true);
    window.setTimeout(() => setKeyPressed('F2', false), 140);
    onKey({ key: 'F2', code: 'F2', dispatch: 'physical', shift: false, caps: false, ctrl: false, alt: false });
  };

  const onMenuDown = () => {
    clickFeedback();
    window.clearTimeout(menuTimer.current);
    menuTimer.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
      setRecentOpen(true);
    }, 600);
  };
  const onMenuUp = () => {
    window.clearTimeout(menuTimer.current);
    if (useStore.getState().recentOpen) return;
    onKey({ key: 'F1', code: 'F1', dispatch: 'physical', shift: false, caps: false, ctrl: false, alt: false });
  };

  const onBackKey = () => {
    clickFeedback();
    onKey({ key: 'Escape', code: 'Escape', dispatch: 'physical', shift: false, caps: false, ctrl: false, alt: false });
  };

  const onPowerDown = () => {
    clickFeedback();
    window.clearTimeout(powerTimer.current);
    powerTimer.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
      powerMenu(true);
    }, 1000);
  };
  const onPowerUp = () => {
    const fired = powerTimer.current;
    window.clearTimeout(powerTimer.current);
    // if the long press already fired, the power menu is open — don't also lock
    if (useStore.getState().powerMenuOpen) return;
    void fired;
    onKey({ key: 'F3', code: 'F3', dispatch: 'physical', shift: false, caps: false, ctrl: false, alt: false });
  };

  /* ------------------------------ trackpad ------------------------------ */
  const dragStart = useRef<{ x: number; y: number; moved: boolean } | null>(null);
  const lastMove = useRef(0);

  const moveFocus = (dir: 'ArrowUp' | 'ArrowDown' | 'ArrowLeft' | 'ArrowRight') => {
    if (settings.keyClick) navBlip(settings.keyClickVolume);
    if (settings.vibrateKeys) vibrate(4);
    setTrail((t) => t + 1);
    onKey({ key: dir, code: dir, dispatch: 'physical', shift: false, caps: false, ctrl: false, alt: false });
  };

  const padPointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);
    dragStart.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, moved: false };
    lastMove.current = 0;
  };

  const padPointerMove = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (!dragStart.current || e.buttons === 0) return;
    const dx = e.clientX - dragStart.current.x;
    const dy = e.clientY - dragStart.current.y;
    const threshold = 16;
    if (Math.abs(dx) < threshold && Math.abs(dy) < threshold) return;
    const now = performance.now();
    if (now - lastMove.current < 95) return;
    lastMove.current = now;
    if (Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) {
      moveFocus(dx > 0 ? 'ArrowRight' : 'ArrowLeft');
    } else {
      moveFocus(dy > 0 ? 'ArrowDown' : 'ArrowUp');
    }
    dragStart.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, moved: true };
  };

  const padPointerUp = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    const start = dragStart.current;
    dragStart.current = null;
    if (!start) return;
    if (!start.moved) {
      clickFeedback();
      onKey({ key: 'Enter', code: 'Enter', dispatch: 'physical', shift: false, caps: false, ctrl: false, alt: false });
    }
  };

  const navPress = (code: string, down: boolean) => setKeyPressed(code, down);

  return (
    <div className="phoneStage">
      <div className="deskGlow" aria-hidden="true" />
      <div
        className="phoneTilt"
        style={{
          transform: settings.reduceMotion
            ? 'none'
            : `rotateY(${-4 + tilt.x}deg) rotateX(${1.2 - tilt.y}deg)`,
        }}
      >
        <div className={`phone ${screen === 'boot' ? 'booting' : ''}`} data-trail={trail} role="application" aria-label="OWL phone simulator">
          {/* side keys */}
          <button
            type="button"
            className="sideBtn left"
            style={{ top: '16%', height: '7%' }}
            aria-label="Convenience key"
            onClick={() => { clickFeedback(); useStore.getState().toast('Convenience key — assign in Settings › Home screen'); }}
          />
          <button
            type="button"
            className="sideBtn left"
            style={{ top: '27%', height: '9%' }}
            aria-label="Volume up"
            onClick={() => bumpVolume(0.05, 'media')}
          />
          <button
            type="button"
            className="sideBtn left"
            style={{ top: '38%', height: '9%' }}
            aria-label="Volume down"
            onClick={() => bumpVolume(-0.05, 'media')}
          />
          <button
            type="button"
            className="sideBtn right"
            style={{ top: '30%', height: '6%' }}
            aria-label="Power / End"
            onClick={() => onPowerDown()}
          />
          <button
            type="button"
            className="sideBtn right"
            style={{ top: '40%', height: '5%' }}
            aria-label="Mute"
            onClick={() => useStore.getState().setSetting('profile', useStore.getState().settings.profile === 'silent' ? 'normal' : 'silent')}
          />

          {/* forehead */}
          <div className="topBezel">
            <div className="earpiece" aria-label="Earpiece speaker">
              {Array.from({ length: 14 }).map((_, i) => <i key={i} />)}
            </div>
            <div className="owlPlate">
              <OwlLogo size={18} />
              <span>OWL</span>
            </div>
            <div className="sensorDot" aria-label="Ambient sensor" />
          </div>

          {/* screen */}
          <div className="screenBezel">
            <div className="screenClip">
              <div
                className="screenScale"
                style={{ transform: `scale(${scale})` }}
              >
                {children}
              </div>
              <div className="screenGlass" aria-hidden="true" />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* navigation row */}
          <div className="navRow">
            <button
              type="button"
              className={`navKey call ${pressed.includes('F2') ? 'isDown' : ''}`}
              aria-label="Call / Answer"
              onClick={onCall}
              onPointerDown={() => navPress('F2', true)}
              onPointerUp={() => navPress('F2', false)}
            >
              <Icon name="phone" size={18} strokeWidth={2} />
              <span className="navBadge">CALL</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              className="navKey menu"
              aria-label="Menu — press and hold to switch applications"
              onPointerDown={onMenuDown}
              onPointerUp={onMenuUp}
              onPointerLeave={() => window.clearTimeout(menuTimer.current)}
              onContextMenu={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
            >
              <OwlLogo size={20} />
              <span className="navBadge">MENU</span>
            </button>

            <div className="trackpadWrap">
              <div className="trackpadRing" aria-hidden="true" />
              <div
                ref={padRef}
                className={`trackpad ${trail % 2 === 0 ? 'glow' : ''} ${pressed.includes('Enter') ? 'pressed' : ''}`}
                role="button"
                tabIndex={-1}
                aria-label="Optical trackpad — drag to move, click to select"
                onPointerDown={padPointerDown}
                onPointerMove={padPointerMove}
                onPointerUp={padPointerUp}
                onContextMenu={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
              />
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              className={`navKey back ${pressed.includes('Escape') ? 'isDown' : ''}`}
              aria-label="Back"
              onClick={onBackKey}
              onPointerDown={() => navPress('Escape', true)}
              onPointerUp={() => navPress('Escape', false)}
            >
              <Icon name="enter" size={18} strokeWidth={2} style={{ transform: 'scaleX(-1)' }} />
              <span className="navBadge">BACK</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              className={`navKey end ${pressed.includes('F3') ? 'isDown' : ''}`}
              aria-label="End / Power — press and hold for power menu"
              onPointerDown={onPowerDown}
              onPointerUp={onPowerUp}
              onPointerLeave={() => window.clearTimeout(powerTimer.current)}
              onContextMenu={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
            >
              <Icon name="phoneOff" size={18} strokeWidth={2} />
              <span className="navBadge">END</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* keyboard */}
          <Keyboard onKey={onKey} />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default PhoneShell;
```

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../os/store';
import { Icon } from './Icon';
import type { SoftMenuAction as AppMenuAction } from '../os/types';

/* ------------------------------- Status bar ------------------------------- */

export function StatusBar() {
  const battery = useStore((s) => s.battery);
  const online = useStore((s) => s.online);
  const settings = useStore((s) => s.settings);
  const nowPlaying = useStore((s) => s.nowPlaying);
  const counters = useStore((s) => s.counters);
  const openApp = useStore((s) => s.openApp);
  const now = useNowLike();
  const pct = Math.round(battery.level * 100);

  return (
    <div className="statusbar" role="status" aria-label="Status bar">
      <span className="carrier">OWL</span>
      <Icon name="signal" size={11} />
      <span className="sbItem" title={online ? 'Online' : 'Offline'}>
        <Icon name={online ? 'wifi' : 'wifiOff'} size={10} />
      </span>
      <span className="sbItem" aria-label={online ? '3G' : 'No network'} style={{ fontSize: 8, opacity: 0.85 }}>
        {online ? '3G' : '—'}
      </span>
      {settings.bluetooth && (
        <span className="sbItem" title="Bluetooth on"><Icon name="bluetooth" size={10} /></span>
      )}
      <span className="sbItem" title={`${settings.profile} profile`}>
        <Icon name={settings.profile === 'silent' || settings.profile === 'vibrate' ? 'headphones' : 'volume'} size={10} />
      </span>
      {nowPlaying?.playing && (
        <button className="sbBtn" onClick={() => openApp('music')} aria-label="Now playing" title={nowPlaying.title}>
          <Icon name="music" size={10} />
          <span className="mono" style={{ maxWidth: 62, overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
            {nowPlaying.title}
          </span>
        </button>
      )}
      <span className="grow" />
      {counters.messages > 0 && (
        <button className="sbBtn" onClick={() => openApp('messages')} aria-label={`${counters.messages} unread messages`}>
          <Icon name="message" size={10} />
          {counters.messages}
        </button>
      )}
      {counters.missedCalls > 0 && (
        <button className="sbBtn" onClick={() => openApp('phone', { tab: 'log' })} aria-label={`${counters.missedCalls} missed calls`}>
          <Icon name="phone" size={10} />
          {counters.missedCalls}
        </button>
      )}
      <span className="mono" style={{ fontSize: 9 }}>
        {now.toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hour12: !settings.clock24 })}
      </span>
      <span className="batt" title={`Battery ${pct}%${battery.charging ? ' (charging)' : ''}`}>
        <i style={{ width: `${Math.max(6, pct)}%`, background: pct < 20 ? 'var(--red)' : 'var(--green)' }} />
      </span>
      <span style={{ fontSize: 8.5, opacity: 0.85, minWidth: 20 }}>{pct}%</span>
    </div>
  );
}

function useNowLike() {
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => new Date());
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = window.setInterval(() => setNow(new Date()), 15000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(t);
  }, []);
  return now;
}

/* ------------------------------- App frame ------------------------------- */

export function AppFrame({
  title,
  actions,
  children,
  noHeader,
  icon,
  menu,
}: {
  title: string;
  actions?: React.ReactNode;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  noHeader?: boolean;
  icon?: string;
  /** Rows for the hardware Menu key. Recomputed whenever the app re-renders. */
  menu?: AppMenuAction[];
}) {
  const setAppMenu = useStore((s) => s.setAppMenu);
  const menuKey = menu ? menu.map((a) => `${a.id}:${a.label}:${a.danger ? 'd' : ''}`).join('|') : '';
  // keep the latest closure without re-registering on every render
  const menuRef = useRef(menu);
  menuRef.current = menu;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!menu) return;
    setAppMenu({
      title,
      actions: (menuRef.current || []).map((a) => ({
        ...a,
        run: () => menuRef.current?.find((m) => m.id === a.id)?.run(),
      })),
    });
    return () => setAppMenu(null);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [menuKey, title, setAppMenu]);

  return (
    <div className="appFrame">
      {!noHeader && (
        <div className="appHeader">
          {icon && <Icon name={icon} size={12} />}
          <span className="title">{title}</span>
          {actions}
        </div>
      )}
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

/* --------------------------------- Dialog -------------------------------- */

export function DialogLayer() {
  const dialog = useStore((s) => s.dialog);
  const closeDialog = useStore((s) => s.closeDialog);
  const [value, setValue] = useState('');
  const activeFieldId = useStore((s) => s.activeFieldId);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (dialog) setValue(dialog.input?.value || '');
  }, [dialog?.id]);

  if (!dialog) return null;
  const isFieldActive = activeFieldId === 'dialog-input';
  return (
    <div className="dialogLayer" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label={dialog.title}>
      <div className="dialog">
        <h3>{dialog.title}</h3>
        <div className="dBody">
          {dialog.body && <div dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: dialog.body }} />}
          {dialog.input && (
            <div style={{ marginTop: 6 }}>
              <div className="fieldLabel">{dialog.input.label}</div>
              <DialogInput
                value={value}
                onChange={setValue}
                placeholder={dialog.input.placeholder}
                password={dialog.input.password}
                active={isFieldActive}
                multiline={dialog.input.multiline}
                onSubmit={() => { dialog.onConfirm(value); closeDialog(); }}
              />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="dActions">
          {dialog.showCancel && (
            <button className="btn" onClick={() => { dialog.onCancel?.(); closeDialog(); }}>
              {dialog.cancelLabel || 'Cancel'}
            </button>
          )}
          <button
            className={`btn ${dialog.danger ? 'danger' : 'primary'}`}
            autoFocus
            onClick={() => {
              dialog.onConfirm(value);
              closeDialog();
            }}
          >
            {dialog.confirmLabel || 'OK'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function DialogInput({
  value, onChange, placeholder, password, active, multiline, onSubmit,
}: {
  value: string; onChange: (v: string) => void; placeholder?: string;
  password?: boolean; active: boolean; multiline?: boolean; onSubmit?: () => void;
}) {
  const focusField = useStore((s) => s.focusField);
  const [caret, setCaret] = useState(value.length);
  const reg = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setCaret((v) => Math.min(v, value.length));
  }, [value]);

  useEffect(() => {
    // register this dialog field lazily with the focus kernel
    let off: (() => void) | undefined;
    void import('../os/focus').then(({ registerField }) => {
      off = registerField({
        id: 'dialog-input',
        kind: password ? 'password' : 'text',
        getValue: () => {
          if (!useStore.getState().activeFieldId || useStore.getState().activeFieldId !== 'dialog-input') return value;
          return value.slice(0, caret) + '\u0001' + value.slice(caret);
        },
        setValue: (raw) => {
          const i = raw.indexOf('\u0001');
          if (i >= 0) {
            setCaret(i);
            onChange(raw.replace('\u0001', ''));
          } else {
            onChange(raw);
            setCaret(raw.length);
          }
        },
        setCaret: (c) => setCaret(Math.max(0, Math.min(c, value.length))),
        onSubmit,
      });
      reg.current = true;
    });
    return () => off?.();
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [value, caret, password]);

  return (
    <div
      className="field"
      onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); focusField('dialog-input'); }}
      role="textbox"
      aria-label={placeholder || 'input'}
    >
      {active ? (
        <span>
          {password ? '•'.repeat(caret) : value.slice(0, caret)}
          <span className="caret" />
          {password ? '•'.repeat(Math.max(0, value.length - caret)) : value.slice(caret)}
        </span>
      ) : (
        <span style={{ opacity: value ? 1 : 0.55 }}>
          {value ? (password ? '•'.repeat(value.length) : value) : placeholder}
        </span>
      )}
      {multiline && <span className="tiny">↵ for new line</span>}
    </div>
  );
}

/* --------------------------------- Toasts -------------------------------- */

export function ToastStack() {
  const toasts = useStore((s) => s.toasts);
  if (!toasts.length) return null;
  return (
    <div className="toastStack" aria-live="polite">
      {toasts.map((t) => (
        <div key={t.id} className="toast">{t.text}</div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

/* -------------------------------- Soft menu ------------------------------- */

export function SoftMenuLayer() {
  const softMenu = useStore((s) => s.softMenu);
  const setSoftMenu = useStore((s) => s.setSoftMenu);
  if (!softMenu) return null;
  return (
    <div className="softMenuLayer" onClick={() => setSoftMenu(null)} role="menu" aria-label={softMenu.title}>
      <div className="softMenu" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="smTitle">{softMenu.title}</div>
        {softMenu.actions.map((a) => (
          <button
            key={a.id}
            className={a.danger ? 'danger' : ''}
            role="menuitem"
            onClick={() => {
              setSoftMenu(null);
              a.run();
            }}
          >
            {a.icon && <Icon name={a.icon} size={12} />}
            {a.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export { useNowLike };
```

```ts
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { collectionGet, collectionSet, subscribeKey, DB_KEYS } from '../os/db';

/** Reactive, persisted collection hook. Data stays in IndexedDB on this device. */
export function useCollection<T>(key: string, seed?: T[]): [T[], (next: T[] | ((prev: T[]) => T[])) => void, boolean] {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<T[]>(seed || []);
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  const keyRef = useRef(key);
  keyRef.current = key;

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    void collectionGet<T>(key).then(async (stored) => {
      if (!alive) return;
      if ((!stored || stored.length === 0) && seed && seed.length) {
        await collectionSet(key, seed);
        if (!alive) return;
        setItems(seed);
      } else {
        setItems(stored || []);
      }
      setReady(true);
    });
    const unsub = subscribeKey(key, () => {
      void collectionGet<T>(key).then((stored) => alive && setItems(stored || []));
    });
    return () => { alive = false; unsub(); };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [key]);

  const update = useCallback((next: T[] | ((prev: T[]) => T[])) => {
    setItems((prev) => {
      const value = typeof next === 'function' ? (next as (p: T[]) => T[])(prev) : next;
      void collectionSet(keyRef.current, value);
      return value;
    });
  }, []);

  return [items, update, ready];
}

/** Ticking clock for app UIs. */
export function useNow(intervalMs = 1000): Date {
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => new Date());
  useEffect(() => {
    const id = window.setInterval(() => setNow(new Date()), intervalMs);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, [intervalMs]);
  return now;
}

export function useLocalStorageState<T>(key: string, initial: T): [T, (v: T | ((prev: T) => T)) => void] {
  const [value, setValue] = useState<T>(() => {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(key);
      return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as T) : initial;
    } catch {
      return initial;
    }
  });
  const set = useCallback((v: T | ((prev: T) => T)) => {
    setValue((prev) => {
      const next = typeof v === 'function' ? (v as (p: T) => T)(prev) : v;
      try { localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(next)); } catch { /* ignore */ }
      return next;
    });
  }, [key]);
  return [value, set];
}

export function useObjectUrl(blobOrFile: Blob | null | undefined): string | null {
  const [url, setUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!blobOrFile) { setUrl(null); return; }
    const u = URL.createObjectURL(blobOrFile);
    setUrl(u);
    return () => URL.revokeObjectURL(u);
  }, [blobOrFile]);
  return url;
}

export function useLongPress(callback: () => void, ms = 600) {
  const timer = useRef<number | undefined>(undefined);
  const start = useCallback((e?: React.SyntheticEvent) => {
    if (e && 'preventDefault' in e && (e as any).type === 'contextmenu') e.preventDefault();
    window.clearTimeout(timer.current);
    timer.current = window.setTimeout(callback, ms);
  }, [callback, ms]);
  const cancel = useCallback(() => window.clearTimeout(timer.current), []);
  return { start, cancel, onPointerDown: start, onPointerUp: cancel, onPointerLeave: cancel, onKeyDown: undefined };
}

export const uid = (prefix = 'id'): string =>
  `${prefix}_${Date.now().toString(36)}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`;

export function formatBytes(n: number): string {
  if (!n) return '0 B';
  const units = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB'];
  const i = Math.min(units.length - 1, Math.floor(Math.log(n) / Math.log(1024)));
  return `${(n / 1024 ** i).toFixed(i === 0 ? 0 : 1)} ${units[i]}`;
}

export function formatDuration(sec: number): string {
  if (!Number.isFinite(sec) || sec < 0) sec = 0;
  const m = Math.floor(sec / 60);
  const s = Math.floor(sec % 60);
  return `${m}:${String(s).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

export function downloadBlob(blob: Blob, filename: string) {
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = filename;
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  a.remove();
  window.setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1500);
}

export const isCoarsePointer = () =>
  typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches;

export { DB_KEYS };
```

```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../os/store';
import { dbGet, dbSet } from '../os/db';
import { wallpaperCss } from '../assets/wallpapers';

/**
 * Resolves a wallpaper id to a CSS background value. Uploaded images are
 * stored as data URLs in IndexedDB; built-ins are generated SVG.
 */
export function useWallpaperUrl(id: string): string {
  const version = useStore((s) => s.wallpaperVersion);
  const [custom, setCustom] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    if (id.startsWith('custom:')) {
      void dbGet<string | null>(`wallpaper:${id}`, null).then((v) => alive && setCustom(v));
    } else {
      setCustom(null);
    }
    return () => { alive = false; };
  }, [id, version]);

  return wallpaperCss(id, custom);
}

export async function saveCustomWallpaper(dataUrl: string): Promise<string> {
  const id = `custom:${Date.now().toString(36)}`;
  await dbSet(`wallpaper:${id}`, dataUrl);
  return id;
}

export async function getCustomWallpaper(id: string): Promise<string | null> {
  return dbGet<string | null>(`wallpaper:${id}`, null);
}

/** Center-crop an image to a target aspect ratio and return a JPEG data URL. */
export async function cropToWallpaper(source: string | Blob, targetW = 960, targetH = 720, zoom = 1, offsetX = 0, offsetY = 0): Promise<string> {
  const url = typeof source === 'string' ? source : URL.createObjectURL(source);
  const img = await loadImage(url);
  if (typeof source !== 'string') URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = targetW;
  canvas.height = targetH;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')!;
  const scale = Math.max(targetW / img.width, targetH / img.height) * zoom;
  const w = img.width * scale;
  const h = img.height * scale;
  const dx = (targetW - w) / 2 + offsetX * (w / targetW);
  const dy = (targetH - h) / 2 + offsetY * (h / targetH);
  ctx.imageSmoothingQuality = 'high';
  ctx.drawImage(img, dx, dy, w, h);
  return canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.86);
}

function loadImage(src: string): Promise<HTMLImageElement> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const img = new Image();
    img.onload = () => resolve(img);
    img.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Could not read that image'));
    img.src = src;
  });
}
```

```ts
/**
 * Eight built-in wallpapers, generated as SVG (no bitmap assets, crisp at any
 * scale, tiny payload). Users can also upload their own image, which is then
 * cropped to the screen ratio and stored in IndexedDB.
 */

export interface WallpaperDef {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  kind: 'svg' | 'custom';
  css?: string; // for svg: a url("data:image/svg+xml,...")
  svg?: string;
}

const svg = (body: string, w = 480, h = 360) =>
  `url("data:image/svg+xml,${encodeURIComponent(
    `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${w}" height="${h}" viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}">${body}</svg>`
  )}")`;

const stars = (count: number, seed = 7, maxY = 230) => {
  let out = '';
  let s = seed;
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    s = (s * 16807) % 2147483647;
    const x = (s % 480);
    s = (s * 16807) % 2147483647;
    const y = (s % maxY);
    s = (s * 16807) % 2147483647;
    const r = 0.4 + (s % 12) / 12;
    out += `<circle cx="${x}" cy="${y}" r="${r.toFixed(2)}" fill="#fff" opacity="${(0.25 + (s % 60) / 100).toFixed(2)}"/>`;
  }
  return out;
};

const owlFace = (cx: number, cy: number, r: number, opacity = 0.55) => `

    <path d="M${-r} ${-r * 0.4}c0-${r * 0.75} ${r * 0.55}-${r * 1.1} ${r}-${r * 1.1}s${r} ${r * 0.35} ${r} ${r * 1.1}c0 ${r * 0.95}-${r * 0.5} ${r * 1.4}-${r} ${r * 1.4}s-${r}-${r * 0.45}-${r}-${r * 1.4}z" fill="none" stroke="#ffd78a" stroke-width="${Math.max(1.2, r * 0.1)}"/>
    <circle cx="${-r * 0.42}" cy="-${r * 0.05}" r="${r * 0.32}" fill="none" stroke="#ffd78a" stroke-width="${Math.max(1, r * 0.09)}"/>
    <circle cx="${r * 0.42}" cy="-${r * 0.05}" r="${r * 0.32}" fill="none" stroke="#ffd78a" stroke-width="${Math.max(1, r * 0.09)}"/>
    <path d="M${-r * 0.12} ${r * 0.3}l${r * 0.12} ${r * 0.22} ${r * 0.12}-${r * 0.22}z" fill="#ffd78a"/>

    kind: 'svg',
    css: svg(`
      <rect width="480" height="360" fill="#b9c9a4"/>
      <g stroke="#6c7d59" opacity=".5">
        ${Array.from({ length: 13 }).map((_, i) => `<path d="M${i * 40} 0v360" stroke-width="0.8"/>`).join('')}
        ${Array.from({ length: 10 }).map((_, i) => `<path d="M0 ${i * 40}h480" stroke-width="0.8"/>`).join('')}
      </g>
      <g opacity=".55" transform="translate(240 150)">
        ${owlFace(0, 0, 54, 0.6)}
      </g>
      <rect x="0" y="0" width="480" height="360" fill="none" stroke="#3c5130" stroke-width="14" opacity=".18"/>
    `),
  },
  {
    id: 'nebula',
    name: 'Nebula',
    kind: 'svg',
    css: svg(`
      <defs>
        <radialGradient id="n1" cx="0.3" cy="0.3" r="0.6">
          <stop offset="0" stop-color="#8f5cff" stop-opacity=".55"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#8f5cff" stop-opacity="0"/>
        </radialGradient>
        <radialGradient id="n2" cx="0.72" cy="0.6" r="0.55">
          <stop offset="0" stop-color="#3fa9ff" stop-opacity=".5"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#3fa9ff" stop-opacity="0"/>
        </radialGradient>
      </defs>
      <rect width="480" height="360" fill="#070a14"/>
      ${stars(70, 23, 350)}
      <rect width="480" height="360" fill="url(#n1)"/>
      <rect width="480" height="360" fill="url(#n2)"/>
      <circle cx="240" cy="190" r="66" fill="#0b1020" stroke="#ffd27a" stroke-width="1.4" opacity=".8"/>
      <ellipse cx="240" cy="190" rx="108" ry="22" fill="none" stroke="#9fe6ff" stroke-width="2" opacity=".55" transform="rotate(-14 240 190)"/>
    `),
  },
];

export const WALLPAPER_MAP: Record<string, WallpaperDef> = Object.fromEntries(WALLPAPERS.map((w) => [w.id, w]));

export function wallpaperCss(id: string, customDataUrl?: string | null): string {
  if (id && id.startsWith('custom:') && customDataUrl) return `url("${customDataUrl}")`;
  return WALLPAPER_MAP[id]?.css || WALLPAPER_MAP['owl-night-glow'].css!;
}

export function wallpaperName(id: string): string {
  if (id.startsWith('custom:')) return 'Your image';
  return WALLPAPER_MAP[id]?.name || 'Owl Night';
}
```
