_7128 bytes · 169 lines_

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { useLocalStorageState } from '../../hooks';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';
import { evaluate, formatResult } from '../../os/expr';
import { registerAppKeys } from '../../os/kernel';
import { errorBuzz } from '../../os/audio';

const BASIC = [
  ['C', '±', '%', '÷'],
  ['7', '8', '9', '×'],
  ['4', '5', '6', '−'],
  ['1', '2', '3', '+'],
  ['0', '.', '⌫', '='],
];

const SCI = [
  ['sin', 'cos', 'tan', 'π'],
  ['√', 'x²', 'xʸ', 'e'],
  ['(', ')', 'ln', 'log'],
];

export default function CalculatorApp() {
  const [expr, setExpr] = useState('');
  const [result, setResult] = useState('');
  const [memory, setMemory] = useState(0);
  const [sci, setSci] = useState(false);
  const [history, setHistory] = useLocalStorageState<{ expr: string; result: string }[]>('owl:calc-history', []);
  const [justSolved, setJustSolved] = useState(false);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);

  const normalize = (input: string) =>
    input.replace(/×/g, '*').replace(/÷/g, '/').replace(/−/g, '-').replace(/π/g, 'pi').replace(/√/g, 'sqrt').replace(/x²/g, '^2').replace(/xʸ/g, '^');

  const compute = () => {
    if (!expr.trim()) return;
    try {
      const value = evaluate(normalize(expr));
      const formatted = formatResult(value);
      setResult(formatted);
      setHistory((prev) => [{ expr, result: formatted }, ...prev].slice(0, 25));
      setJustSolved(true);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setResult('Error');
      errorBuzz();
      toast(err?.message || 'Invalid expression');
      setJustSolved(true);
    }
  };

  const insert = (token: string) => {
    if (justSolved && /[0-9.]/.test(token)) {
      setExpr(token);
      setJustSolved(false);
      return;
    }
    if (justSolved && /[+\-*/^%×÷−]/.test(token)) {
      setExpr((prev) => `${prev || result}${token}`);
      setJustSolved(false);
      return;
    }
    setJustSolved(false);
    if (token === 'sin' || token === 'cos' || token === 'tan' || token === 'ln' || token === 'log' || token === '√') {
      setExpr((prev) => `${prev}${token}(`);
      return;
    }
    if (token === 'π') { setExpr((prev) => `${prev}π`); return; }
    if (token === 'x²') { setExpr((prev) => `${prev}^2`); return; }
    if (token === 'xʸ') { setExpr((prev) => `${prev}^`); return; }
    setExpr((prev) => prev + token);
  };

  const press = (key: string) => {
    switch (key) {
      case 'C': setExpr(''); setResult(''); setJustSolved(false); break;
      case '⌫': setExpr((p) => p.slice(0, -1)); break;
      case '=': compute(); break;
      case '±': setExpr((p) => (p.startsWith('-') ? p.slice(1) : `-${p}`)); break;
      case '%': setExpr((p) => `${p}%`); break;
      default: insert(key); break;
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const off = registerAppKeys((e) => {
      if (useStore.getState().activeFieldId) return false;
      if (/^[0-9.]$/.test(e.key)) { insert(e.key); return true; }
      if (e.key === '+' || e.key === '-' || e.key === '*' || e.key === '/' || e.key === '^' || e.key === '(' || e.key === ')' || e.key === '%') {
        insert(e.key === '*' ? '×' : e.key === '/' ? '÷' : e.key === '-' ? '−' : e.key);
        return true;
      }
      if (e.key === 'Enter') { compute(); return true; }
      if (e.key === 'Backspace') { setExpr((p) => p.slice(0, -1)); return true; }
      return false;
    });
    return off;
  });

  return (
    <AppFrame
      title="Calculator"
      icon="calc"
      actions={
        <>
          <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => setSci((v) => !v)}>{sci ? 'Basic' : 'Scientific'}</button>
          <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => { setExpr(''); setResult(''); setHistory([]); }}>Clear hist.</button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <div style={{ padding: '4px 7px', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--line)', background: 'rgba(0,0,0,.22)' }}>
        <div className="tiny" style={{ minHeight: 12, textAlign: 'right', overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap', direction: 'ltr' }}>
          {memory !== 0 && <span className="pill amber" style={{ marginRight: 4 }}>M {memory}</span>}
          {expr || <span style={{ opacity: 0.4 }}>0</span>}
        </div>
        <div className="mono" style={{ fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 700, textAlign: 'right', fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)', minHeight: 26, direction: 'ltr' }}>
          {result || '\u00a0'}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="appBody" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
        {sci && (
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(4, 1fr)', gap: 2, padding: 3 }}>
            {SCI.flat().map((k) => (
              <button key={k} className="btn" style={{ fontSize: 9.5, minHeight: 20 }} onClick={() => press(k)}>{k}</button>
            ))}
            <button className="btn" style={{ fontSize: 9 }} onClick={() => setMemory(Number(result) || memory)}>MS</button>
            <button className="btn" style={{ fontSize: 9 }} onClick={() => insert(String(memory))}>MR</button>
            <button className="btn" style={{ fontSize: 9 }} onClick={() => setMemory((m) => m + (Number(result) || 0))}>M+</button>
            <button className="btn" style={{ fontSize: 9 }} onClick={() => { setMemory(0); toast('Memory cleared'); }}>MC</button>
          </div>
        )}
        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(4, 1fr)', gap: 3, padding: 4, flex: 1 }}>
          {BASIC.flat().map((key) => (
            <button
              key={key}
              className={`calcKey ${key === '=' ? 'eq' : ''} ${/[÷×−+%]/.test(key) ? 'op' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => press(key)}
              aria-label={key === '⌫' ? 'Backspace' : key}
              style={{
                borderRadius: 5, border: '1px solid var(--line)', cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: key === '⌫' ? 13 : 16,
                fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)', fontWeight: 600,
                background: key === '=' ? 'linear-gradient(180deg,var(--hl-2),var(--hl))' : /[÷×−+%C±]/.test(key) ? 'var(--panel-3)' : 'var(--panel-2)',
                color: key === '=' ? 'var(--hl-text)' : 'var(--text)',
                minHeight: 26,
              }}
            >{key}</button>
          ))}
        </div>
        {history.length > 0 && (
          <div style={{ maxHeight: 52, overflow: 'auto', borderTop: '1px solid var(--line)' }}>
            {history.slice(0, 8).map((h, i) => (
              <button
                key={i}
                className="rowBtn"
                style={{ padding: '1px 7px', minHeight: 16 }}
                onClick={() => { setExpr(h.result); setJustSolved(true); }}
                title="Use this result"
              >
                <span className="tiny" style={{ flex: 1, textAlign: 'left' }}>{h.expr}</span>
                <span className="tiny mono">= {h.result}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}
```

### `src/apps/Calendar/CalendarApp.tsx`

_18841 bytes · 372 lines_

```tsx
import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { useCollection, uid } from '../../hooks';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { Field } from '../../components/Field';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';
import { DB_KEYS } from '../../os/db';
import type { CalEvent, TaskItem } from '../../os/types';

const COLORS: Record<string, string> = {
  '#ff6b5b': 'Coral', '#4aa3ff': 'Blue', '#4cc38a': 'Green', '#f0a13c': 'Amber', '#b06cf0': 'Violet',
};

function startOfDay(d: Date) { const x = new Date(d); x.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0); return x; }
function sameDay(a: Date, b: Date) { return a.toDateString() === b.toDateString(); }
function addDays(d: Date, n: number) { const x = new Date(d); x.setDate(x.getDate() + n); return x; }

function repeatMatches(event: CalEvent, day: Date): boolean {
  const start = new Date(event.start);
  if (sameDay(start, day)) return true;
  if (day < startOfDay(start)) return false;
  switch (event.repeat) {
    case 'daily': return true;
    case 'weekly': return start.getDay() === day.getDay();
    case 'monthly': return start.getDate() === day.getDate();
    case 'yearly': return start.getDate() === day.getDate() && start.getMonth() === day.getMonth();
    default: return false;
  }
}

export default function CalendarApp() {
  const [events, setEvents] = useCollection<CalEvent>(DB_KEYS.events, [
    {
      id: 'ev-welcome', title: 'Welcome to OWL', location: '', notes: 'Press Menu for options.',
      start: startOfDay(new Date()).getTime() + 3600000 * 10, end: startOfDay(new Date()).getTime() + 3600000 * 11,
      allDay: false, reminderMinutes: 10, repeat: 'none', color: '#f0a13c',
    },
  ]);
  const [tasks] = useCollection<TaskItem>(DB_KEYS.tasks, []);
  const [view, setView] = useState<'month' | 'week' | 'day' | 'agenda'>('month');
  const [cursor, setCursor] = useState(() => new Date());
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<CalEvent | null>(null);
  const [jump, setJump] = useState('');
  const showDialog = useStore((s) => s.showDialog);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const setAppMenu = useStore((s) => s.setAppMenu);

  const monthDays = useMemo(() => {
    const first = new Date(cursor.getFullYear(), cursor.getMonth(), 1);
    const startOffset = first.getDay();
    const start = addDays(first, -startOffset);
    return Array.from({ length: 42 }, (_, i) => addDays(start, i));
  }, [cursor]);

  const eventsOn = (day: Date) => events.filter((e) => repeatMatches(e, day)).sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
  const tasksOn = (day: Date) => tasks.filter((t) => t.due && repeatMatches({ ...emptyEvent(), start: new Date(t.due).getTime(), repeat: 'none' } as CalEvent, day));

  const newEvent = (date?: Date) => {
    const base = date || cursor;
    const start = new Date(base);
    start.setHours(9, 0, 0, 0);
    if (start < new Date()) start.setHours(new Date().getHours() + 1, 0, 0, 0);
    setEditing({
      ...emptyEvent(),
      start: start.getTime(),
      end: start.getTime() + 3600000,
    });
  };

  React.useEffect(() => {
    setAppMenu({
      title: 'Calendar',
      actions: [
        { id: 'new', label: 'New event', icon: 'plus', run: () => newEvent() },
        { id: 'month', label: 'Month view', icon: 'calendar', run: () => setView('month') },
        { id: 'week', label: 'Week view', icon: 'calendar', run: () => setView('week') },
        { id: 'agenda', label: 'Agenda view', icon: 'list', run: () => setView('agenda') },
        { id: 'jump', label: 'Jump to date…', icon: 'arrowRight', run: () => setJump('') },
        { id: 'perm', label: 'Enable notifications', icon: 'bell', run: () => void requestNotifications() },
        { id: 'clear', label: 'Delete all events', icon: 'trash', danger: true, run: () => showDialog({ title: 'Delete all events?', body: 'Local calendar data only.', confirmLabel: 'Delete', danger: true, onConfirm: () => setEvents([]) }) },
      ],
    });
    return () => setAppMenu(null);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [cursor]);

  if (editing) {
    return <EventEditor event={editing} onSave={(e) => {
      const exists = events.some((x) => x.id === e.id);
      setEvents((prev) => (exists ? prev.map((x) => (x.id === e.id ? e : x)) : [...prev, e]));
      setEditing(null);
      toast(exists ? 'Event updated' : 'Event added');
    }} onCancel={() => setEditing(null)} onDelete={(id) => {
      setEvents((prev) => prev.filter((x) => x.id !== id));
      setEditing(null);
      toast('Event deleted');
    }} />;
  }

  return (
    <AppFrame
      title="Calendar"
      icon="calendar"
      actions={
        <>
          <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => newEvent(cursor)}><Icon name="plus" size={9} /> New</button>
          <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => setCursor(new Date())}>Today</button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <div className="owlTabBar">
        {(['month', 'week', 'day', 'agenda'] as const).map((v) => (
          <button key={v} className={`owlTab ${view === v ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setView(v)}>
            {v[0].toUpperCase() + v.slice(1)}
          </button>
        ))}
        <button className="owlTab" onClick={() => setView('agenda')}>Tasks</button>
      </div>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, padding: '3px 6px', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--line)' }}>
        <button className="iconBtn" onClick={() => setCursor((c) => (view === 'month' ? new Date(c.getFullYear(), c.getMonth() - 1, 1) : addDays(c, view === 'week' ? -7 : -1)))} aria-label="Previous">‹</button>
        <strong style={{ flex: 1, textAlign: 'center', fontSize: 11.5, fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)', letterSpacing: '0.04em' }}>
          {view === 'month'
            ? cursor.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' })
            : view === 'week'
              ? `Week of ${addDays(startOfDay(cursor), -cursor.getDay()).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })}`
              : cursor.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' })}
        </strong>
        {jump !== '' && (
          <input
            value={jump}
            onChange={(e) => setJump(e.target.value)}
            onKeyDown={(e) => {
              if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                const d = new Date(jump);
                if (!isNaN(d.getTime())) { setCursor(d); setJump(''); toast('Jumped to date'); }
              }
            }}
            placeholder="YYYY-MM-DD"
            style={{ width: 70, fontSize: 9, background: 'rgba(0,0,0,.3)', border: '1px solid var(--line)', color: 'var(--text)', borderRadius: 3, padding: '1px 3px' }}
            inputMode="none"
          />
        )}
        <button className="iconBtn" onClick={() => setCursor((c) => (view === 'month' ? new Date(c.getFullYear(), c.getMonth() + 1, 1) : addDays(c, view === 'week' ? 7 : 1)))} aria-label="Next">›</button>
      </div>

      <div className="appBody">
        {view === 'month' && (
          <div style={{ padding: 3 }}>
            <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(7, 1fr)', gap: 1 }}>
              {['S', 'M', 'T', 'W', 'T', 'F', 'S'].map((d, i) => (
                <div key={i} className="tiny" style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontSize: 8, paddingBottom: 2 }}>{d}</div>
              ))}
              {monthDays.map((day) => {
                const isCurrentMonth = day.getMonth() === cursor.getMonth();
                const isToday = sameDay(day, new Date());
                const dayEvents = eventsOn(day);
                return (
                  <button
                    key={day.toISOString()}
                    onClick={() => { setCursor(day); setView('day'); }}
                    onDoubleClick={() => newEvent(day)}
                    className={`dayCell ${isCurrentMonth ? '' : 'dim'}`}
                    style={{
                      minHeight: 32, border: '1px solid var(--line)', borderRadius: 3, padding: 1,
                      background: isToday ? 'rgba(246,185,63,.16)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,.02)',
                      color: isCurrentMonth ? 'var(--text)' : 'var(--muted)',
                      cursor: 'pointer', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 1, alignItems: 'stretch',
                    }}
                    aria-label={day.toDateString()}
                  >
                    <span style={{ fontSize: 8.5, fontWeight: isToday ? 700 : 500, color: isToday ? 'var(--hl)' : undefined, textAlign: 'center' }}>{day.getDate()}</span>
                    {dayEvents.slice(0, 2).map((e) => (
                      <span key={e.id} style={{ height: 3, borderRadius: 2, background: e.color, opacity: 0.9 }} />
                    ))}
                    {dayEvents.length > 2 && <span style={{ fontSize: 6, textAlign: 'center' }}>+{dayEvents.length - 2}</span>}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
            <div className="tiny" style={{ padding: 4 }}>Double-press a day to add an event.</div>
          </div>
        )}

        {view === 'week' && (
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(7, 1fr)', gap: 2, padding: 3 }}>
            {Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => addDays(startOfDay(cursor), i - cursor.getDay())).map((day) => (
              <div key={day.toISOString()} style={{ border: '1px solid var(--line)', borderRadius: 3, minHeight: 120, padding: 2 }}>
                <div className="tiny" style={{ textAlign: 'center', color: sameDay(day, new Date()) ? 'var(--hl)' : undefined }}>
                  {day.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: 'short' })}<br />
                  <b style={{ fontSize: 10 }}>{day.getDate()}</b>
                </div>
                {eventsOn(day).map((e) => (
                  <button
                    key={e.id}
                    onClick={() => setEditing(e)}
                    style={{ width: '100%', fontSize: 7, background: e.color, color: '#08101c', border: 0, borderRadius: 2, marginTop: 1, padding: '1px 2px', cursor: 'pointer', textAlign: 'left', overflow: 'hidden' }}
                  >
                    {e.title}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        {(view === 'day' || view === 'agenda') && (
          <div>
            <div className="owlSection">{view === 'day' ? cursor.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: 'long', day: 'numeric', month: 'long' }) : 'Next 30 days'}</div>
            {view === 'day' && tasksOn(cursor).length > 0 && (
              <>
                <div className="owlSection" style={{ paddingTop: 0 }}>Tasks due</div>
                {tasksOn(cursor).map((t) => (
                  <button key={t.id} className="rowBtn" onClick={() => useStore.getState().openApp('tasks')}>
                    <Icon name="task" size={11} />
                    <span style={{ textDecoration: t.status === 'completed' ? 'line-through' : undefined }}>{t.title}</span>
                    <span className="meta">{t.priority}</span>
                  </button>
                ))}
              </>
            )}
            {(view === 'day' ? eventsOn(cursor) : events.filter((e) => e.start >= Date.now() - 86400000).sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start))
              .length === 0 && (
                <div className="empty">
                  <div className="big">▦</div>
                  <div>No events.</div>
                  <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => newEvent(view === 'day' ? cursor : undefined)}>Add event</button>
                </div>
              )}
            {(view === 'day' ? eventsOn(cursor) : events.filter((e) => e.start >= Date.now() - 86400000).sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start))
              .map((e) => (
                <button key={e.id} className="rowBtn" onClick={() => setEditing(e)}>
                  <span style={{ width: 4, height: 22, background: e.color, borderRadius: 2, flex: 'none' }} />
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'flex-start' }}>
                    <span style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>{e.title}</span>
                    <span className="tiny">
                      {new Date(e.start).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })}{' '}
                      {e.allDay ? 'All day' : new Date(e.start).toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}
                      {e.location ? ` · ${e.location}` : ''}
                      {e.repeat !== 'none' ? ` · ${e.repeat}` : ''}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  {e.reminderMinutes !== null && <Icon name="bell" size={9} style={{ marginLeft: 'auto', opacity: 0.6 }} />}
                </button>
              ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}

function emptyEvent(): CalEvent {
  return {
    id: uid('ev'), title: '', location: '', notes: '', start: Date.now(), end: Date.now() + 3600000,
    allDay: false, reminderMinutes: 10, repeat: 'none', color: '#4aa3ff',
  };
}

function EventEditor({ event, onSave, onCancel, onDelete }: {
  event: CalEvent; onSave: (e: CalEvent) => void; onCancel: () => void; onDelete: (id: string) => void;
}) {
  const [e, setE] = useState(event);
  const set = <K extends keyof CalEvent>(k: K, v: CalEvent[K]) => setE((prev) => ({ ...prev, [k]: v }));
  const dateStr = (ts: number) => {
    const d = new Date(ts);
    return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  };
  const timeStr = (ts: number) => {
    const d = new Date(ts);
    return `${String(d.getHours()).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  };
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);

  const updateDate = (field: 'start' | 'end', value: string) => {
    const d = new Date(value);
    if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return;
    const old = new Date(e[field]);
    d.setHours(old.getHours(), old.getMinutes(), 0, 0);
    if (field === 'start') {
      const duration = e.end - e.start;
      setE((prev) => ({ ...prev, start: d.getTime(), end: d.getTime() + duration }));
    } else {
      setE((prev) => ({ ...prev, end: d.getTime() }));
    }
  };

  const updateTime = (field: 'start' | 'end', value: string) => {
    const [h, m] = value.split(':').map(Number);
    const d = new Date(e[field]);
    d.setHours(h || 0, m || 0, 0, 0);
    setE((prev) => ({ ...prev, [field]: d.getTime() }));
  };

  return (
    <AppFrame
      title={event.title ? 'Edit event' : 'New event'}
      icon="calendar"
      actions={<button className="hbtn" onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</button>}
    >
      <div className="appBody pad" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 2 }}>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Title</div>
        <Field value={e.title} onChange={(v) => set('title', v)} placeholder="Event title" autoFocus maxLength={80} />
        <div className="fieldLabel">Location</div>
        <Field value={e.location} onChange={(v) => set('location', v)} placeholder="Where?" maxLength={80} />
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 5 }}>
          <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            <div className="fieldLabel">Date</div>
            <input type="date" value={dateStr(e.start)} onChange={(ev) => updateDate('start', ev.target.value)} style={dateInputStyle} />
          </div>
          <div style={{ width: 78 }}>
            <div className="fieldLabel">Start</div>
            <input type="time" value={timeStr(e.start)} onChange={(ev) => updateTime('start', ev.target.value)} disabled={e.allDay} style={dateInputStyle} />
          </div>
          <div style={{ width: 78 }}>
            <div className="fieldLabel">End</div>
            <input type="time" value={timeStr(e.end)} onChange={(ev) => updateTime('end', ev.target.value)} disabled={e.allDay} style={dateInputStyle} />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4, alignItems: 'center', marginTop: 3 }}>
          <button className={`chip ${e.allDay ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => set('allDay', !e.allDay)}>All day</button>
          <button className={`chip ${e.repeat !== 'none' ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => {
            const opts: CalEvent['repeat'][] = ['none', 'daily', 'weekly', 'monthly', 'yearly'];
            set('repeat', opts[(opts.indexOf(e.repeat) + 1) % opts.length]);
          }}>Repeat: {e.repeat}</button>
          <button className={`chip ${e.reminderMinutes !== null ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => set('reminderMinutes', e.reminderMinutes === null ? 10 : null)}>
            {e.reminderMinutes === null ? 'No reminder' : `Remind ${e.reminderMinutes}m`}
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Notes</div>
        <Field value={e.notes} onChange={(v) => set('notes', v)} placeholder="Notes" multiline maxLength={600} />
        <div className="fieldLabel">Colour</div>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4 }}>
          {Object.entries(COLORS).map(([hex, label]) => (
            <button
              key={hex}
              aria-label={label}
              onClick={() => set('color', hex)}
              style={{ width: 20, height: 20, borderRadius: '50%', background: hex, border: e.color === hex ? '2px solid var(--text)' : '1px solid var(--line)', cursor: 'pointer' }}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="btnRow" style={{ marginTop: 'auto', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
          {event.title && <button className="btn danger" onClick={() => onDelete(event.id)}>Delete</button>}
          <button
            className="btn primary"
            onClick={() => {
              if (!e.title.trim()) { toast('Give the event a title'); return; }
              if (e.end <= e.start) e.end = e.start + 3600000;
              onSave({ ...e, title: e.title.trim() });
            }}
          >
            Save event
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}

const dateInputStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
  width: '100%', background: 'rgba(0,0,0,.28)', border: '1px solid var(--line)',
  color: 'var(--text)', borderRadius: 4, padding: '2px 4px', fontSize: 10, colorScheme: 'dark',
};

async function requestNotifications() {
  const toast = useStore.getState().toast;
  if (typeof Notification === 'undefined') { toast('Notifications are not supported here'); return; }
  if (Notification.permission === 'granted') { toast('Notifications already enabled'); return; }
  const result = await Notification.requestPermission();
  toast(result === 'granted' ? 'Reminders will appear as notifications' : 'Notification permission denied — reminders still show in-app');
}
```

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { useCollection, uid, downloadBlob } from '../../hooks';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';
import { DB_KEYS } from '../../os/db';
import type { PhotoItem } from '../../os/types';
import { shutterSound, tickSound } from '../../os/audio';

export default function CameraApp() {
  const [photos, setPhotos] = useCollection<PhotoItem>(DB_KEYS.photos);
  const [stream, setStream] = useState<MediaStream | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [facing, setFacing] = useState<'environment' | 'user'>('environment');
  const [timer, setTimer] = useState<0 | 3 | 10>(0);
  const [grid, setGrid] = useState(false);
  const [flash, setFlash] = useState(false);
  const [countdown, setCountdown] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'photo' | 'video'>('photo');
  const [recording, setRecording] = useState(false);
  const [recSeconds, setRecSeconds] = useState(0);
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
  const [zoomRange, setZoomRange] = useState<{ min: number; max: number }>({ min: 1, max: 1 });
  const [lastShot, setLastShot] = useState<{ blob: Blob; url: string } | null>(null);
  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null);
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  const recorder = useRef<MediaRecorder | null>(null);
  const chunks = useRef<Blob[]>([]);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);

  const start = async (f: 'environment' | 'user') => {
    setError(null);
    stopStream();
    if (!navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia) {
      setError('Camera capture needs a browser with getUserMedia (Chrome, Edge, Safari, Firefox) and a secure (https) page.');
      return;
    }
    try {
      const media = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
        video: { facingMode: f, width: { ideal: 1280 }, height: { ideal: 960 } },
        audio: mode === 'video',
      });
      setStream(media);
      if (videoRef.current) {
        videoRef.current.srcObject = media;
        await videoRef.current.play().catch(() => {});
      }
      const track = media.getVideoTracks()[0];
      const caps = (track.getCapabilities?.() || {}) as any;
      if (caps.zoom) setZoomRange({ min: caps.zoom.min || 1, max: caps.zoom.max || 1 });
    } catch (err: any) {
      const name = err?.name || '';
      if (name === 'NotAllowedError') setError('Camera permission was denied. Allow camera access in your browser settings, then reopen this app.');
      else if (name === 'NotFoundError') setError('No camera was found on this device.');
      else setError('The camera could not start. Another app may be using it.');
    }
  };

  const stopStream = () => {
    stream?.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
    setStream(null);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    void start(facing);
    return () => stopStream();
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [facing, mode]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!recording) return;
    const t = window.setInterval(() => setRecSeconds((s) => s + 1), 1000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(t);
  }, [recording]);

  const capture = async () => {
    if (!videoRef.current || !canvasRef.current) return;
    const video = videoRef.current;
    if (!video.videoWidth) { toast('Camera not ready yet'); return; }
    shutterSound();
    setFlash(true);
    window.setTimeout(() => setFlash(false), 130);
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    canvas.width = video.videoWidth;
    canvas.height = video.videoHeight;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')!;
    if (facing === 'user') {
      ctx.translate(canvas.width, 0);
      ctx.scale(-1, 1);
    }
    ctx.drawImage(video, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    const blob: Blob | null = await new Promise((resolve) => canvas.toBlob((b) => resolve(b), 'image/jpeg', 0.9));
    if (!blob) { toast('Could not capture the frame'); return; }
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    setLastShot({ blob, url });
    toast('Captured — press Save to keep it');
  };

  const shoot = () => {
    if (timer === 0) { void capture(); return; }
    let n = timer;
    setCountdown(n);
    const t = window.setInterval(() => {
      n -= 1;
      tickSound(0.5);
      if (n <= 0) {
        window.clearInterval(t);
        setCountdown(null);
        void capture();
      } else {
        setCountdown(n);
      }
    }, 1000);
  };

  const save = async () => {
    if (!lastShot) return;
    const bitmap = await createImageBitmap(lastShot.blob).catch(() => null);
    setPhotos((prev) => [{
      id: uid('photo'), name: `IMG_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 19).replace(/[:T]/g, '')}.jpg`,
      blob: lastShot.blob, at: Date.now(), width: bitmap?.width || 0, height: bitmap?.height || 0,
    }, ...prev]);
    setLastShot(null);
    toast('Saved to Pictures');
  };

  const toggleRecording = async () => {
    if (!stream) { toast('Start the camera first'); return; }
    if (recording) {
      recorder.current?.stop();
      return;
    }
    try {
      const mime = ['video/webm;codecs=vp9', 'video/webm', 'video/mp4'].find((m) => MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(m));
      const rec = new MediaRecorder(stream, mime ? { mimeType: mime } : undefined);
      chunks.current = [];
      rec.ondataavailable = (e) => { if (e.data.size) chunks.current.push(e.data); };
      rec.onstop = () => {
        const blob = new Blob(chunks.current, { type: mime || 'video/webm' });
        downloadBlob(blob, `OWL_VIDEO_${Date.now()}.webm`);
        setRecording(false);
        setRecSeconds(0);
        toast('Recording finished — saved to your downloads');
      };
      rec.start();
      recorder.current = rec;
      setRecording(true);
      setRecSeconds(0);
    } catch {
      toast('Video recording is not supported in this browser');
    }
  };

  const applyZoom = (value: number) => {
    setZoom(value);
    const track = stream?.getVideoTracks()[0];
    if (track && zoomRange.max > zoomRange.min) {
      track.applyConstraints({ advanced: [{ zoom: value } as any] }).catch(() => {});
    }
  };

  if (error) {
    return (
      <AppFrame title="Camera" icon="camera">
        <div className="empty">
          <div className="big">📷</div>
          <div><b>Camera unavailable</b></div>
          <div className="tiny" style={{ maxWidth: 310 }}>{error}</div>
          <div className="btnRow" style={{ justifyContent: 'center' }}>
            <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => void start(facing)}>Try again</button>
            <button className="btn" onClick={() => useStore.getState().openApp('pictures')}>Open Pictures</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </AppFrame>
    );
  }

  return (
    <AppFrame
      title="Camera"
      icon="camera"
      actions={
        <>
          <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => setMode(mode === 'photo' ? 'video' : 'photo')}>{mode === 'photo' ? 'Photo' : 'Video'}</button>
          <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => setFacing((f) => (f === 'environment' ? 'user' : 'environment'))} aria-label="Switch camera">
            <Icon name="cameraFlip" size={9} />
          </button>
        </>
      }
      menu={[
        { id: 'capture', label: 'Capture', icon: 'camera', run: () => { void save(); } },
        { id: 'flip', label: 'Switch camera', icon: 'cameraFlip', run: () => setFacing((f) => (f === 'environment' ? 'user' : 'environment')) },
        { id: 'mode', label: mode === 'photo' ? 'Video mode' : 'Photo mode', icon: 'video', run: () => setMode(mode === 'photo' ? 'video' : 'photo') },
        { id: 'grid', label: 'Toggle grid', icon: 'grid', run: () => setGrid((g) => !g) },
        { id: 'gallery', label: 'Open Pictures', icon: 'image', run: () => useStore.getState().openApp('pictures') },
      ]}
    >
      <div style={{ flex: 1, position: 'relative', background: '#000', overflow: 'hidden' }}>
        <video
          ref={videoRef}
          playsInline
          muted
          autoPlay
          style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'cover', transform: facing === 'user' ? 'scaleX(-1)' : undefined }}
        />
        {grid && (
          <div aria-hidden="true" style={{ position: 'absolute', inset: 0, pointerEvents: 'none' }}>
            <div style={{ position: 'absolute', left: '33.3%', top: 0, bottom: 0, width: 1, background: 'rgba(255,255,255,.28)' }} />
            <div style={{ position: 'absolute', left: '66.6%', top: 0, bottom: 0, width: 1, background: 'rgba(255,255,255,.28)' }} />
            <div style={{ position: 'absolute', top: '33.3%', left: 0, right: 0, height: 1, background: 'rgba(255,255,255,.28)' }} />
            <div style={{ position: 'absolute', top: '66.6%', left: 0, right: 0, height: 1, background: 'rgba(255,255,255,.28)' }} />
          </div>
        )}
        {flash && <div style={{ position: 'absolute', inset: 0, background: '#fff' }} aria-hidden="true" />}
        {countdown !== null && (
          <div style={{ position: 'absolute', inset: 0, display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center', fontSize: 54, color: '#fff', textShadow: '0 2px 12px #000', fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)' }}>
            {countdown}
          </div>
        )}
        {recording && (
          <div style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 6, left: 6, background: 'rgba(200,20,30,.85)', color: '#fff', fontSize: 9, padding: '1px 6px', borderRadius: 99, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4 }}>
            <span style={{ width: 5, height: 5, borderRadius: '50%', background: '#fff' }} /> REC {recSeconds}s
          </div>
        )}
        {!stream && <div className="tiny" style={{ position: 'absolute', inset: 0, display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center', color: '#8aa' }}>Starting camera…</div>}
      </div>

      <div style={{ padding: '3px 6px', borderTop: '1px solid var(--line)', display: 'grid', gap: 3 }}>
        {lastShot && (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 5 }}>
            <img src={lastShot.url} alt="Last capture" style={{ width: 34, height: 26, objectFit: 'cover', borderRadius: 3, border: '1px solid var(--hl)' }} />
            <span className="tiny" style={{ flex: 1 }}>New capture</span>
            <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => void save()}>Save to Pictures</button>
            <button className="btn" onClick={() => downloadBlob(lastShot.blob, 'owl-photo.jpg')}>Download</button>
            <button className="btn" onClick={() => setLastShot(null)}>Discard</button>
          </div>
        )}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4 }}>
          <button className={`chip ${grid ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => setGrid((g) => !g)}>Grid</button>
          <button className={`chip ${timer === 3 ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => setTimer(timer === 3 ? 0 : 3)}>3s</button>
          <button className={`chip ${timer === 10 ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => setTimer(timer === 10 ? 0 : 10)}>10s</button>
          <button className="chip" onClick={() => setFlash(true) /* simulated */} title="Simulated flash">Flash</button>
          {zoomRange.max > zoomRange.min && (
            <>
              <span className="tiny">Zoom</span>
              <input type="range" min={zoomRange.min} max={zoomRange.max} step={0.1} value={zoom} onChange={(e) => applyZoom(Number(e.target.value))} style={{ width: 70, accentColor: 'var(--hl)' }} inputMode="none" aria-label="Zoom" />
            </>
          )}
          <div style={{ flex: 1 }} />
          <button
            className={`btn ${recording ? 'danger' : ''}`}
            style={{ width: 40, height: 26, borderRadius: 99 }}
            onClick={mode === 'video' ? () => void toggleRecording() : shoot}
            aria-label={mode === 'video' ? (recording ? 'Stop recording' : 'Start recording') : 'Take photo'}
          >
            {mode === 'video' ? (recording ? '■' : '●') : <Icon name="camera" size={14} />}
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="tiny">
          {mode === 'photo' ? 'Photos are saved to Pictures (IndexedDB) or downloaded directly.' : 'Video recordings download to your device.'}
          {' '}Nothing leaves the browser.
        </div>
      </div>
      <canvas ref={canvasRef} style={{ display: 'none' }} />
    </AppFrame>
  );
}
```

### `src/apps/Clock/ClockApp.tsx`

_16914 bytes · 368 lines_

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { useCollection, uid, useNow } from '../../hooks';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { Field } from '../../components/Field';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';
import { DB_KEYS } from '../../os/db';
import type { Alarm } from '../../os/types';
import { formatDuration } from '../../hooks';
import { playRingtone, stopRingtone } from '../../os/audio';

type Tab = 'world' | 'alarm' | 'stopwatch' | 'timer' | 'bedside';

const CITY_ZONES: [string, string][] = [
  ['Cairo', 'Africa/Cairo'], ['London', 'Europe/London'], ['New York', 'America/New_York'],
  ['Tokyo', 'Asia/Tokyo'], ['Dubai', 'Asia/Dubai'], ['Sydney', 'Australia/Sydney'],
  ['Paris', 'Europe/Paris'], ['Los Angeles', 'America/Los_Angeles'], ['Riyadh', 'Asia/Riyadh'],
  ['Berlin', 'Europe/Berlin'], ['Istanbul', 'Europe/Istanbul'], ['Mumbai', 'Asia/Kolkata'],
];

export default function ClockApp() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>('world');
  const settings = useStore((s) => s.settings);
  const now = useNow(1000);

  return (
    <AppFrame
      title="Clock"
      icon="clock"
      actions={<span className="tiny mono">{now.toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour12: !settings.clock24 })}</span>}
      menu={[
        { id: 'world', label: 'World clock', icon: 'globe', run: () => setTab('world') },
        { id: 'alarms', label: 'Alarms', icon: 'bell', run: () => setTab('alarm') },
        { id: 'stopwatch', label: 'Stopwatch', icon: 'clock', run: () => setTab('stopwatch') },
        { id: 'timer', label: 'Timer', icon: 'clock', run: () => setTab('timer') },
        { id: 'bedside', label: 'Bedside mode', icon: 'sun', run: () => setTab('bedside') },
      ]}
    >
      <div className="owlTabBar">
        {(['world', 'alarm', 'stopwatch', 'timer', 'bedside'] as Tab[]).map((t) => (
          <button key={t} className={`owlTab ${tab === t ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setTab(t)}>
            {t === 'bedside' ? 'Bedside' : t[0].toUpperCase() + t.slice(1)}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="appBody">
        {tab === 'world' && <WorldClock />}
        {tab === 'alarm' && <Alarms />}
        {tab === 'stopwatch' && <Stopwatch />}
        {tab === 'timer' && <Countdown />}
        {tab === 'bedside' && <Bedside />}
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}

function WorldClock() {
  const [cities, setCities] = useState(CITY_ZONES.slice(0, 4));
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const now = useNow(1000);
  const settings = useStore((s) => s.settings);
  const allZones = useMemoZones();

  const filtered = allZones
    .filter((z) => !cities.some(([, tz]) => tz === z))
    .filter((z) => !query || z.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase()))
    .slice(0, 40);

  const timeIn = (tz: string) => {
    try {
      return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, { timeZone: tz, hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit', hour12: !settings.clock24 }).format(now);
    } catch {
      return '--:--';
    }
  };
  const dayDiff = (tz: string) => {
    try {
      const there = new Date(now.toLocaleString('en-US', { timeZone: tz }));
      const here = new Date(now.toLocaleString('en-US'));
      return Math.round((there.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0) - here.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)) / 86400000);
    } catch { return 0; }
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="owlSection">World clock</div>
      {cities.map(([name, tz], i) => {
        const diff = dayDiff(tz);
        return (
          <button key={tz} className="rowBtn" onClick={() => setCities((prev) => prev.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i))} title="Tap to remove">
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'flex-start' }}>
              <span style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>{name}</span>
              <span className="tiny">{tz.split('/')[1]?.replace(/_/g, ' ')}</span>
            </div>
            <span className="meta mono" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
              {timeIn(tz)} {diff !== 0 && <span className="tiny">({diff > 0 ? '+' : ''}{diff}d)</span>}
            </span>
          </button>
        );
      })}
      {adding ? (
        <div style={{ padding: 6 }}>
          <Field value={query} onChange={setQuery} placeholder="Filter time zones" autoFocus />
          <div style={{ maxHeight: 130, overflow: 'auto', marginTop: 4 }}>
            {filtered.map((z) => (
              <button key={z} className="rowBtn" onClick={() => { setCities((prev) => [...prev, [z.split('/').pop()!.replace(/_/g, ' '), z]]); setAdding(false); setQuery(''); }}>
                <span>{z.split('/').pop()?.replace(/_/g, ' ')}</span>
                <span className="meta">{z.split('/')[0]}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
            {filtered.length === 0 && <div className="tiny">No zones match.</div>}
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <button className="btn" style={{ margin: 6 }} onClick={() => setAdding(true)}>+ Add city</button>
      )}
      <div className="tiny" style={{ padding: '0 8px 8px' }}>Tap a city to remove it. Times use your device clock and Intl time zones.</div>
    </div>
  );
}

function useMemoZones(): string[] {
  return React.useMemo(() => {
    try {
      const anyIntl = Intl as any;
      if (typeof anyIntl.supportedValuesOf === 'function') return anyIntl.supportedValuesOf('timeZone');
    } catch { /* fall through */ }
    return [
      'Africa/Cairo', 'Africa/Lagos', 'America/Chicago', 'America/Denver', 'America/Mexico_City',
      'America/Sao_Paulo', 'Asia/Baghdad', 'Asia/Dhaka', 'Asia/Hong_Kong', 'Asia/Jakarta',
      'Asia/Karachi', 'Asia/Seoul', 'Asia/Singapore', 'Europe/Amsterdam', 'Europe/Madrid',
      'Europe/Moscow', 'Europe/Rome', 'Pacific/Auckland', 'Pacific/Honolulu', 'UTC',
    ];
  }, []);
}

function Alarms() {
  const [alarms, setAlarms] = useCollection<Alarm>(DB_KEYS.alarms);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<Alarm | null>(null);
  const settings = useStore((s) => s.settings);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const setRinging = useStore((s) => s.setAlarmRinging);
  const showDialog = useStore((s) => s.showDialog);

  const newAlarm = (): Alarm => ({ id: uid('alarm'), hour: 7, minute: 30, label: 'Alarm', days: [], enabled: true, sound: settings.alarmTone });

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="owlSection">Alarms · {alarms.filter((a) => a.enabled).length} active</div>
      {alarms.length === 0 && (
        <div className="empty"><div className="big">⏰</div><div>No alarms yet.</div></div>
      )}
      {alarms.map((a) => (
        <div key={a.id} className="rowBtn" style={{ cursor: 'default' }}>
          <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'flex-start' }}>
            <span className="mono" style={{ fontSize: 18, fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)', fontWeight: 700, opacity: a.enabled ? 1 : 0.45 }}>
              {String(a.hour).padStart(2, '0')}:{String(a.minute).padStart(2, '0')}
            </span>
            <span className="tiny">
              {a.label} · {a.days.length === 0 ? 'Once' : a.days.map((d) => ['Su', 'Mo', 'Tu', 'We', 'Th', 'Fr', 'Sa'][d]).join(' ')}
            </span>
          </div>
          <button className={`chip ${a.enabled ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => setAlarms((prev) => prev.map((x) => (x.id === a.id ? { ...x, enabled: !x.enabled } : x)))}>
            {a.enabled ? 'On' : 'Off'}
          </button>
          <button className="iconBtn" onClick={() => setEditing(a)} aria-label="Edit alarm">✎</button>
          <button className="iconBtn" onClick={() => setRinging(a.label || 'Alarm')} aria-label="Test alarm">▷</button>
          <button className="iconBtn" onClick={() => showDialog({
            title: 'Delete alarm?', body: `${String(a.hour).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(a.minute).padStart(2, '0')} ${a.label}`,
            confirmLabel: 'Delete', danger: true, onConfirm: () => setAlarms((prev) => prev.filter((x) => x.id !== a.id)),
          })} aria-label="Delete alarm">✕</button>
        </div>
      ))}
      <button className="btn primary" style={{ margin: 6 }} onClick={() => setEditing(newAlarm())}>+ New alarm</button>
      {editing && (
        <div className="dialogLayer" role="dialog" aria-label="Alarm">
          <div className="dialog">
            <h3>{alarms.some((a) => a.id === editing.id) ? 'Edit alarm' : 'New alarm'}</h3>
            <div className="dBody" style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 4 }}>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
                <input
                  type="number" min={0} max={23} value={editing.hour}
                  onChange={(e) => setEditing({ ...editing, hour: Math.max(0, Math.min(23, Number(e.target.value))) })}
                  style={numStyle} aria-label="Hour" inputMode="numeric"
                />
                <b>:</b>
                <input
                  type="number" min={0} max={59} value={editing.minute}
                  onChange={(e) => setEditing({ ...editing, minute: Math.max(0, Math.min(59, Number(e.target.value))) })}
                  style={numStyle} aria-label="Minute" inputMode="numeric"
                />
                <div style={{ flex: 1 }} />
                <button className={`chip ${editing.enabled ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => setEditing({ ...editing, enabled: !editing.enabled })}>{editing.enabled ? 'Enabled' : 'Off'}</button>
              </div>
              <div className="fieldLabel">Label</div>
              <Field value={editing.label} onChange={(v) => setEditing({ ...editing, label: v })} placeholder="Alarm" maxLength={30} />
              <div className="fieldLabel">Repeat</div>
              <div className="btnRow">
                {['Su', 'Mo', 'Tu', 'We', 'Th', 'Fr', 'Sa'].map((d, i) => (
                  <button
                    key={d}
                    className={`chip ${editing.days.includes(i) ? 'on' : ''}`}
                    onClick={() => setEditing({
                      ...editing,
                      days: editing.days.includes(i) ? editing.days.filter((x) => x !== i) : [...editing.days, i].sort(),
                    })}
                  >
                    {d}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
              <div className="tiny">No days selected = rings once tomorrow.</div>
            </div>
            <div className="dActions">
              <button className="btn" onClick={() => setEditing(null)}>Cancel</button>
              <button
                className="btn primary"
                onClick={() => {
                  const exists = alarms.some((a) => a.id === editing.id);
                  setAlarms((prev) => (exists ? prev.map((a) => (a.id === editing.id ? editing : a)) : [...prev, editing]));
                  setEditing(null);
                  toast('Alarm saved');
                }}
              >Save</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

const numStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
  width: 46, background: 'rgba(0,0,0,.3)', border: '1px solid var(--line)', color: 'var(--text)',
  borderRadius: 4, padding: '2px 4px', fontSize: 16, fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)', textAlign: 'center',
};

function Stopwatch() {
  const [running, setRunning] = useState(false);
  const [elapsed, setElapsed] = useState(0);
  const [laps, setLaps] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const startRef = useRef(0);
  const baseRef = useRef(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!running) return;
    startRef.current = performance.now();
    let raf = 0;
    const loop = () => {
      setElapsed(baseRef.current + (performance.now() - startRef.current));
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    };
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
  }, [running]);

  const fmt = (ms: number) => {
    const m = Math.floor(ms / 60000);
    const s = Math.floor((ms % 60000) / 1000);
    const cs = Math.floor((ms % 1000) / 10);
    return `${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(s).padStart(2, '0')}.${String(cs).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  };

  const reset = () => { setRunning(false); setElapsed(0); baseRef.current = 0; setLaps([]); };

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100%' }}>
      <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '14px 0 6px' }}>
        <div className="mono" style={{ fontSize: 40, fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)', fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: '0.02em' }}>{fmt(elapsed)}</div>
      </div>
      <div className="btnRow" style={{ justifyContent: 'center', padding: 4 }}>
        <button className={`btn ${running ? 'danger' : 'primary'}`} onClick={() => {
          if (running) { baseRef.current = elapsed; setRunning(false); }
          else setRunning(true);
        }}>{running ? 'Stop' : elapsed ? 'Resume' : 'Start'}</button>
        <button className="btn" onClick={() => setLaps((l) => [elapsed, ...l])} disabled={!elapsed}>Lap</button>
        <button className="btn" onClick={reset}>Reset</button>
      </div>
      <div style={{ flex: 1, overflow: 'auto' }}>
        {laps.map((l, i) => {
          const prev = laps[i + 1] ?? 0;
          return (
            <div key={i} className="kv" style={{ padding: '3px 9px' }}>
              <b>Lap {laps.length - i}</b>
              <span className="mono">{fmt(l - prev)} <span className="tiny">({fmt(l)})</span></span>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Countdown() {
  const [total, setTotal] = useState(300);
  const [remaining, setRemaining] = useState(300);
  const [running, setRunning] = useState(false);
  const [custom, setCustom] = useState('5');
  const store = useStore;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!running) return;
    const t = window.setInterval(() => {
      setRemaining((r) => {
        if (r <= 1) {
          window.clearInterval(t);
          setRunning(false);
          playRingtone(useStore.getState().settings.alarmTone, 0.9, 3);
          useStore.getState().toast("Time's up!");
          if (useStore.getState().settings.vibrate) navigator.vibrate?.([300, 200, 300]);
          return 0;
        }
        return r - 1;
      });
    }, 1000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(t);
  }, [running]);

  const start = (seconds: number) => {
    stopRingtone();
    setTotal(seconds);
    setRemaining(seconds);
    setRunning(true);
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 6, padding: 8, placeItems: 'center' }}>
      <div className="mono" style={{ fontSize: 42, fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)', fontWeight: 700 }}>
        {formatDuration(remaining).padStart(5, '0')}
      </div>
      <div className="progress" style={{ width: '80%' }}><i style={{ width: `${total ? ((total - remaining) / total) * 100 : 0}%` }} /></div>
      <div className="btnRow" style={{ justifyContent: 'center' }}>
        <button className={`btn ${running ? 'danger' : 'primary'}`} onClick={() => setRunning((r) => !r)}>{running ? 'Pause' : 'Start'}</button>
        <button className="btn" onClick={() => { setRunning(false); setRemaining(total); }}>Reset</button>
      </div>
      <div className="btnRow" style={{ justifyContent: 'center' }}>
        {[60, 300, 600, 1800, 3600].map((s) => (
          <button key={s} className="chip" onClick={() => start(s)}>{s >= 60 ? `${s / 60}m` : `${s}s`}</button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4, alignItems: 'center' }}>
        <Field value={custom} onChange={setCustom} kind="decimal" placeholder="Minutes" maxLength={4} />
        <button className="btn" onClick={() => { const m = Number(custom); if (m > 0) start(Math.round(m * 60)); }}>Set minutes</button>
      </div>
      <div className="tiny">Rings with the Alarm tone from Settings › Sound.</div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Bedside() {
  const now = useNow(1000);
  const settings = useStore((s) => s.settings);
  return (
    <div style={{ height: '100%', display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center', background: '#000', position: 'relative' }}>
      <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', color: '#ffb347', opacity: 0.9, textShadow: '0 0 24px rgba(255,150,40,.35)' }}>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)', fontSize: 58, fontWeight: 700, lineHeight: 1 }}>
          {now.toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hour12: !settings.clock24 })}
        </div>
        <div className="tiny" style={{ color: '#c98a3a', marginTop: 4, letterSpacing: '0.2em' }}>
          {now.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }).toUpperCase()}
        </div>
        <div className="tiny" style={{ color: '#6b4a1c', marginTop: 10, fontSize: 8 }}>Press Back to exit bedside mode</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';

const CARDINALS = ['N', 'NE', 'E', 'SE', 'S', 'SW', 'W', 'NW'];

/** Compass: DeviceOrientationEvent with iOS permission flow + manual demo. */
export default function CompassApp() {
  const [heading, setHeading] = useState(0);
  const [permission, setPermission] = useState<'unknown' | 'granted' | 'denied' | 'unsupported'>('unknown');
  const [demo, setDemo] = useState(false);
  const [calibrating, setCalibrating] = useState(false);
  const rafRef = useRef(0);
  const demoRef = useRef(0);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);

  useEffect(() => {
    const hasOrientation = typeof DeviceOrientationEvent !== 'undefined';
    if (!hasOrientation) { setPermission('unsupported'); setDemo(true); return; }
    const anyDOE = DeviceOrientationEvent as any;
    if (typeof anyDOE.requestPermission === 'function') {
      setPermission('unknown');
    } else {
      attach();
    }
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  function attach() {
    const handler = (e: DeviceOrientationEvent) => {
      const anyE = e as any;
      let h: number | null = null;
      if (typeof anyE.webkitCompassHeading === 'number') h = anyE.webkitCompassHeading;
      else if (e.alpha !== null && e.alpha !== undefined) {
        h = 360 - e.alpha;
        // rough tilt compensation
        if (e.beta !== null && e.gamma !== null) {
          const beta = (e.beta || 0) * Math.PI / 180;
          const gamma = (e.gamma || 0) * Math.PI / 180;
          if (Math.abs(beta) > 0.3) h = h + Math.sin(gamma) * 12;
        }
      }
      if (h === null) return;
      setHeading(((h % 360) + 360) % 360);
      setPermission('granted');
      setDemo(false);
    };
    window.addEventListener('deviceorientationabsolute' as any, handler, true);
    window.addEventListener('deviceorientation', handler, true);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('deviceorientationabsolute' as any, handler, true);
      window.removeEventListener('deviceorientation', handler, true);
    };
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!demo) return;
    let last = performance.now();
    const loop = (t: number) => {
      const dt = t - last;
      last = t;
      demoRef.current = (demoRef.current + dt * 0.02) % 360;
      setHeading(demoRef.current);
      rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    };
    rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef.current);
  }, [demo]);

  const request = async () => {
    try {
      const anyDOE = DeviceOrientationEvent as any;
      if (typeof anyDOE.requestPermission === 'function') {
        const result = await anyDOE.requestPermission();
        if (result === 'granted') {
          setPermission('granted');
          attach();
          toast('Compass enabled');
        } else {
          setPermission('denied');
          setDemo(true);
        }
      } else {
        attach();
        setPermission('granted');
      }
    } catch {
      setPermission('denied');
      setDemo(true);
    }
  };

  const cardinal = CARDINALS[Math.round(heading / 45) % 8];
  const degrees = Math.round(heading);

  return (
    <AppFrame
      title="Compass"
      icon="compass"
      actions={<button className="hbtn" onClick={() => setDemo((d) => !d)}>{demo ? 'Real' : 'Demo'}</button>}
      menu={[
        { id: 'demo', label: demo ? 'Use real sensors' : 'Use demo mode', icon: 'compass', run: () => setDemo((d) => !d) },
        { id: 'help', label: 'How to calibrate', icon: 'help', run: () => useStore.getState().toast('Wave the phone in a figure-8 away from metal') },
      ]}
    >
      <div className="appBody pad" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 7, alignItems: 'center' }}>
        <div style={{ position: 'relative', width: 200, height: 200, marginTop: 4 }}>
          <div
            style={{
              position: 'absolute', inset: 0, borderRadius: '50%',
              background: 'radial-gradient(circle at 45% 35%, #1d2b40, #0a121d 70%)',
              border: '1px solid var(--line)',
              boxShadow: '0 0 0 3px rgba(255,255,255,.05), inset 0 0 26px rgba(0,0,0,.8)',
            }}
          />
          <div style={{ position: 'absolute', inset: 0, transform: `rotate(${-heading}deg)`, transition: demo ? 'none' : 'transform 90ms linear' }}>
            <svg width="200" height="200" viewBox="0 0 200 200" aria-hidden="true">
              {Array.from({ length: 72 }).map((_, i) => {
                const major = i % 9 === 0;
                const angle = (i * 5 * Math.PI) / 180;
                const r1 = 82;
                const r2 = major ? 70 : 76;
                const x1 = 100 + Math.sin(angle) * r1;
                const y1 = 100 - Math.cos(angle) * r1;
                const x2 = 100 + Math.sin(angle) * r2;
                const y2 = 100 - Math.cos(angle) * r2;
                return <line key={i} x1={x1} y1={y1} x2={x2} y2={y2} stroke={major ? 'rgba(246,185,63,.7)' : 'rgba(180,205,235,.3)'} strokeWidth={major ? 2 : 1} />;
              })}
              {['N', 'E', 'S', 'W'].map((c, i) => {
                const angle = (i * 90 * Math.PI) / 180;
                const x = 100 + Math.sin(angle) * 60;
                const y = 100 - Math.cos(angle) * 60 + 4;
                return (
                  <text key={c} x={x} y={y} textAnchor="middle" fontSize="12" fontWeight="700" fill={c === 'N' ? '#f6b93f' : '#cfe0f7'} fontFamily="Rajdhani, Inter, sans-serif">
                    {c}
                  </text>
                );
              })}
              <polygon points="100,30 106,100 100,94 94,100" fill="#f2585b" />
              <polygon points="100,170 106,100 100,106 94,100" fill="#cfe0f7" opacity="0.75" />
              <circle cx="100" cy="100" r="4" fill="#0b1421" stroke="#8fa7c8" strokeWidth="1.5" />
            </svg>
          </div>
          <div style={{ position: 'absolute', inset: 0, display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center', pointerEvents: 'none' }}>
            <div style={{ marginTop: 66, textAlign: 'center' }}>
              <div style={{ fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)', fontSize: 26, fontWeight: 700, lineHeight: 1 }}>{degrees}°</div>
              <div className="tiny" style={{ letterSpacing: '0.2em' }}>{cardinal}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {permission === 'unsupported' && (
          <div className="card tiny" style={{ maxWidth: 320 }}>
            This device has no orientation sensor. Demo mode rotates the needle so you can see how it behaves.
          </div>
        )}
        {permission === 'unknown' && (
          <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => void request()}>
            <Icon name="compass" size={11} /> Enable compass sensor
          </button>
        )}
        {permission === 'denied' && (
          <div className="card tiny" style={{ maxWidth: 320 }}>
            Sensor permission was declined. You can still use demo mode, or re-enable motion access
            in your browser's site settings.
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="btnRow" style={{ justifyContent: 'center' }}>
          <button className={`btn ${demo ? 'primary' : ''}`} onClick={() => { demoRef.current = heading; setDemo((d) => !d); }}>
            {demo ? 'Stop demo' : 'Demo mode'}
          </button>
          <button className="btn" onClick={() => { setCalibrating(true); toast('Move the device in a figure-8 to calibrate'); window.setTimeout(() => setCalibrating(false), 2600); }}>
            {calibrating ? 'Calibrating…' : 'Calibrate'}
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="tiny" style={{ textAlign: 'center', maxWidth: 300, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
          {permission === 'granted' ? 'Live sensor data (device orientation).' : 'Sensors are optional — everything else on the phone works without them.'}
        </div>
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { useCollection, uid, downloadBlob } from '../../hooks';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { Field } from '../../components/Field';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';
import { DB_KEYS } from '../../os/db';
import type { Contact } from '../../os/types';

const SEED: Contact[] = [
  {
    id: 'c-sam', firstName: 'Sam', lastName: 'Rivera', phone: '+1 555 0142', email: 'sam@example.com',
    notes: 'Met at the retro phone meetup.', group: 'Friends', favorite: true, speedDial: 2, createdAt: Date.now() - 86400000 * 12,
  },
  {
    id: 'c-maya', firstName: 'Maya', lastName: 'Haddad', phone: '+20 100 555 8899', email: 'maya@example.com',
    notes: 'Designer — likes owls.', group: 'Work', favorite: false, speedDial: 3, createdAt: Date.now() - 86400000 * 30,
  },
  {
    id: 'c-owl', firstName: 'OWL', lastName: 'Team', phone: '+1 555 0100', email: 'hello@owl.example',
    notes: 'Your friendly phone maker.', group: 'Favorites', favorite: true, speedDial: 1, createdAt: Date.now() - 86400000 * 60,
  },
];

const GROUPS = ['All', 'Favorites', 'Friends', 'Work', 'Family', 'Other'];

export default function ContactsApp() {
  const [contacts, setContacts] = useCollection<Contact>(DB_KEYS.contacts, SEED);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [group, setGroup] = useState('All');
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<Contact | null>(null);
  const [viewing, setViewing] = useState<Contact | null>(null);
  const showDialog = useStore((s) => s.showDialog);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const openApp = useStore((s) => s.openApp);
  const setAppMenu = useStore((s) => s.setAppMenu);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    return contacts
      .filter((c) => {
        if (group === 'Favorites' && !c.favorite) return false;
        if (group !== 'All' && group !== 'Favorites' && c.group !== group) return false;
        if (!query) return true;
        const q = query.toLowerCase();
        return `${c.firstName} ${c.lastName} ${c.phone} ${c.email} ${c.notes}`.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      })
      .sort((a, b) => `${a.firstName}${a.lastName}`.localeCompare(`${b.firstName}${b.lastName}`));
  }, [contacts, query, group]);

  const save = (c: Contact) => {
    const exists = contacts.some((x) => x.id === c.id);
    setContacts((prev) => (exists ? prev.map((x) => (x.id === c.id ? c : x)) : [...prev, c]));
    setEditing(null);
    toast(exists ? 'Contact updated' : 'Contact added');
  };

  const remove = (c: Contact) => {
    showDialog({
      title: 'Delete contact?',
      body: `<strong>${c.firstName} ${c.lastName}</strong> will be removed from this device.`,
      confirmLabel: 'Delete',
      danger: true,
      onConfirm: () => { setContacts((prev) => prev.filter((x) => x.id !== c.id)); setViewing(null); toast('Contact deleted'); },
    });
  };

  const exportVcf = () => {
    const vcf = contacts.map((c) => [
      'BEGIN:VCARD',
      'VERSION:3.0',
      `N:${c.lastName};${c.firstName};;;`,
      `FN:${c.firstName} ${c.lastName}`,
      c.phone ? `TEL;TYPE=CELL:${c.phone}` : '',
      c.email ? `EMAIL:${c.email}` : '',
      c.notes ? `NOTE:${c.notes.replace(/\n/g, ' ')}` : '',
      `CATEGORIES:${c.group}`,
      'END:VCARD',
    ].filter(Boolean).join('\r\n')).join('\r\n');
    downloadBlob(new Blob([vcf], { type: 'text/vcard' }), 'owl-contacts.vcf');
    toast(`Exported ${contacts.length} contacts`);
  };

  const importVcf = () => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file';
    input.accept = '.vcf,text/vcard,text/x-vcard';
    input.onchange = async () => {
      const file = input.files?.[0];
      if (!file) return;
      const text = await file.text();
      const cards = text.split(/BEGIN:VCARD/i).slice(1);
      const parsed: Contact[] = [];
      for (const card of cards) {
        const get = (re: RegExp) => (card.match(re)?.[1] || '').trim();
        const fn = get(/FN[^:]*:(.+)/i);
        const n = get(/\nN[^:]*:(.+)/i);
        const tel = get(/TEL[^:]*:(.+)/i);
        const email = get(/EMAIL[^:]*:(.+)/i);
        const note = get(/NOTE[^:]*:(.+)/i);
        const [last = '', first = ''] = n ? n.split(';') : [];
        if (!fn && !tel) continue;
        parsed.push({
          id: uid('c'),
          firstName: first || fn.split(' ')[0] || 'Unknown',
          lastName: last || fn.split(' ').slice(1).join(' ') || '',
          phone: tel, email, notes: note, group: 'Other', favorite: false, speedDial: null,
          createdAt: Date.now(),
        });
      }
      if (!parsed.length) { toast('No contacts found in that file'); return; }
      setContacts((prev) => [...prev, ...parsed]);
      toast(`Imported ${parsed.length} contact${parsed.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}`);
    };
    input.click();
  };

  React.useEffect(() => {
    setAppMenu({
      title: 'Contacts',
      actions: [
        { id: 'new', label: 'New contact', icon: 'plus', run: () => setEditing(emptyContact()) },
        { id: 'export', label: 'Export vCard (.vcf)', icon: 'download', run: exportVcf },
        { id: 'import', label: 'Import vCard', icon: 'folder', run: importVcf },
        {
          id: 'clear', label: 'Delete all contacts', icon: 'trash', danger: true,
          run: () => showDialog({
            title: 'Delete all contacts?', body: 'This removes every contact stored on this device.',
            confirmLabel: 'Delete all', danger: true, onConfirm: () => { setContacts([]); toast('Contacts cleared'); },
          }),
        },
      ],
    });
    return () => setAppMenu(null);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [contacts.length]);

  if (editing) {
    return <ContactEditor contact={editing} onSave={save} onCancel={() => setEditing(null)} />;
  }

  if (viewing) {
    return (
      <AppFrame
        title={`${viewing.firstName} ${viewing.lastName}`}
        icon="contacts"
        actions={<button className="hbtn" onClick={() => setViewing(null)}>Back</button>}
      >
        <div className="appBody pad" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 6 }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, alignItems: 'center' }}>
            <div style={{ width: 48, height: 48, borderRadius: '50%', background: 'linear-gradient(150deg,var(--panel-3),var(--panel))', display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center', overflow: 'hidden', border: '1px solid var(--line)' }}>
              {viewing.photo ? <img src={viewing.photo} alt="" style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'cover' }} /> : <Icon name="contacts" size={22} />}
            </div>
            <div>
              <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 13 }}>{viewing.firstName} {viewing.lastName}</div>
              <div className="tiny">{viewing.group}{viewing.speedDial ? ` · speed dial ${viewing.speedDial}` : ''}</div>
              {viewing.favorite && <span className="pill amber">★ Favorite</span>}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="card" style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 4 }}>
            {viewing.phone && <div className="kv"><b>Phone</b><span>{viewing.phone}</span></div>}
            {viewing.email && <div className="kv"><b>Email</b><span>{viewing.email}</span></div>}
            {viewing.notes && <div className="kv"><b>Notes</b><span style={{ textAlign: 'right' }}>{viewing.notes}</span></div>}
          </div>
          <div className="btnRow">
            <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => openApp('phone', { number: viewing.phone })}><Icon name="phone" size={10} /> Call</button>
            <button className="btn" onClick={() => openApp('messages', { address: viewing.phone })}><Icon name="message" size={10} /> Message</button>
            <button className="btn" onClick={() => setEditing(viewing)}><Icon name="edit" size={10} /> Edit</button>
            <button className="btn danger" onClick={() => remove(viewing)}><Icon name="trash" size={10} /> Delete</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </AppFrame>
    );
  }

  return (
    <AppFrame
      title="Contacts"
      icon="contacts"
      actions={
        <>
          <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => setEditing(emptyContact())}><Icon name="plus" size={9} /> New</button>
          <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => setAppMenu({
            title: 'Contacts',
            actions: [
              { id: 'new', label: 'New contact', icon: 'plus', run: () => setEditing(emptyContact()) },
              { id: 'export', label: 'Export vCard (.vcf)', icon: 'download', run: exportVcf },
              { id: 'import', label: 'Import vCard', icon: 'folder', run: importVcf },
              {
                id: 'clear', label: 'Delete all contacts', icon: 'trash', danger: true,
                run: () => showDialog({
                  title: 'Delete all contacts?', body: 'This removes every contact stored on this device.',
                  confirmLabel: 'Delete all', danger: true, onConfirm: () => { setContacts([]); toast('Contacts cleared'); },
                }),
              },
            ],
          })}>Menu</button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <div style={{ padding: 5, display: 'flex', gap: 4 }}>
        <Field value={query} onChange={setQuery} placeholder="Search contacts" autoFocus />
      </div>
      <div className="chipRow" style={{ paddingTop: 0 }}>
        {GROUPS.map((g) => (
          <button key={g} className={`chip ${group === g ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => setGroup(g)}>{g}</button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="appBody">
        <div className="owlSection">{filtered.length} contact{filtered.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}</div>
        {filtered.length === 0 && (
          <div className="empty"><div className="big">👤</div><div>No contacts match.<br />Add one with the New button.</div></div>
        )}
        {filtered.map((c) => (
          <button key={c.id} className="rowBtn" onClick={() => setViewing(c)}>
            <span style={{ width: 22, height: 22, borderRadius: '50%', overflow: 'hidden', display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center', background: 'var(--panel-2)', flex: 'none' }}>
              {c.photo ? <img src={c.photo} alt="" style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'cover' }} /> : <Icon name="contacts" size={12} />}
            </span>
            <span>{c.firstName} {c.lastName}</span>
            {c.favorite && <span style={{ color: 'var(--hl)' }}>★</span>}
            <span className="meta">{c.speedDial ? `SD ${c.speedDial} · ` : ''}{c.phone}</span>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}

function emptyContact(): Contact {
  return {
    id: uid('c'), firstName: '', lastName: '', phone: '', email: '', notes: '',
    group: 'Other', favorite: false, speedDial: null, createdAt: Date.now(),
  };
}

function ContactEditor({ contact, onSave, onCancel }: { contact: Contact; onSave: (c: Contact) => void; onCancel: () => void }) {
  const [c, setC] = useState(contact);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const set = <K extends keyof Contact>(key: K, value: Contact[K]) => setC((prev) => ({ ...prev, [key]: value }));

  const pickPhoto = () => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file';
    input.accept = 'image/*';
    input.onchange = () => {
      const file = input.files?.[0];
      if (!file) return;
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = () => set('photo', String(reader.result));
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    };
    input.click();
  };

  return (
    <AppFrame title={contact.firstName ? 'Edit contact' : 'New contact'} icon="contacts" actions={<button className="hbtn" onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</button>}>
      <div className="appBody pad" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 3 }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <button onClick={pickPhoto} style={{ width: 44, height: 44, borderRadius: '50%', border: '1px solid var(--line)', overflow: 'hidden', background: 'var(--panel-2)', cursor: 'pointer', padding: 0 }}>
            {c.photo ? <img src={c.photo} alt="" style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'cover' }} /> : <Icon name="camera" size={18} />}
          </button>
          <span className="tiny">Tap to {c.photo ? 'change' : 'add'} photo</span>
        </div>
        <div className="fieldLabel">First name</div>
        <Field value={c.firstName} onChange={(v) => set('firstName', v)} placeholder="First" autoFocus maxLength={30} />
        <div className="fieldLabel">Last name</div>
        <Field value={c.lastName} onChange={(v) => set('lastName', v)} placeholder="Last" maxLength={30} />
        <div className="fieldLabel">Phone</div>
        <Field value={c.phone} onChange={(v) => set('phone', v)} placeholder="+1 555 0100" kind="phone" maxLength={24} />
        <div className="fieldLabel">Email</div>
        <Field value={c.email} onChange={(v) => set('email', v)} placeholder="name@example.com" maxLength={60} />
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6 }}>
          <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            <div className="fieldLabel">Group</div>
            <select
              value={c.group}
              onChange={(e) => set('group', e.target.value)}
              style={{ width: '100%', background: 'var(--panel-2)', color: 'var(--text)', border: '1px solid var(--line)', borderRadius: 4, padding: '3px 4px', fontSize: 10 }}
            >
              {['Friends', 'Work', 'Family', 'Other', 'Favorites'].map((g) => <option key={g}>{g}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div style={{ width: 80 }}>
            <div className="fieldLabel">Speed dial</div>
            <select
              value={c.speedDial ?? ''}
              onChange={(e) => set('speedDial', e.target.value ? Number(e.target.value) : null)}
              style={{ width: '100%', background: 'var(--panel-2)', color: 'var(--text)', border: '1px solid var(--line)', borderRadius: 4, padding: '3px 4px', fontSize: 10 }}
            >
              <option value="">None</option>
              {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9].map((n) => <option key={n} value={n}>{n}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Notes</div>
        <Field value={c.notes} onChange={(v) => set('notes', v)} placeholder="Notes" multiline maxLength={400} />
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 5, alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <button className={`chip ${c.favorite ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => set('favorite', !c.favorite)}>★ Favorite</button>
          <div style={{ flex: 1 }} />
          <button
            className="btn primary"
            onClick={() => {
              if (!c.firstName.trim() && !c.phone.trim()) { toast('Add a name or phone number'); return; }
              onSave({ ...c, firstName: c.firstName.trim() || 'Unknown' });
            }}
          >
            Save
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}
```
