```tsx
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { GameShell, Overlay, useScores, useGameSfx, useGameKeys } from './gameKit';
import { useLocalStorageState } from '../../hooks';

type Grid = number[]; // 81 cells, 0 = empty

const BASE = 3;
const N = 9;

function shuffled<T>(arr: T[]): T[] {
  const a = [...arr];
  for (let i = a.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    [a[i], a[j]] = [a[j], a[i]];
  }
  return a;
}

function pattern(r: number, c: number) {
  return (BASE * (r % BASE) + Math.floor(r / BASE) + c) % N;
}

function generateSolution(): Grid {
  const rows = shuffled([0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8]);
  const cols = shuffled([0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8]);
  const nums = shuffled([1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9]);
  const grid: Grid = new Array(81).fill(0);
  for (let r = 0; r < 9; r++) {
    for (let c = 0; c < 9; c++) {
      grid[r * 9 + c] = nums[pattern(rows[r], cols[c])];
    }
  }
  return grid;
}

function solve(grid: Grid): Grid | null {
  const idx = grid.indexOf(0);
  if (idx === -1) return grid;
  const row = Math.floor(idx / 9);
  const col = idx % 9;
  const used = new Set<number>();
  for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
    used.add(grid[row * 9 + i]);
    used.add(grid[i * 9 + col]);
  }
  const br = Math.floor(row / 3) * 3;
  const bc = Math.floor(col / 3) * 3;
  for (let r = 0; r < 3; r++) for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) used.add(grid[(br + r) * 9 + bc + c]);
  for (let n = 1; n <= 9; n++) {
    if (used.has(n)) continue;
    grid[idx] = n;
    const out = solve(grid);
    if (out) return out;
    grid[idx] = 0;
  }
  return null;
}

function countSolutions(grid: Grid, limit = 2): number {
  const idx = grid.indexOf(0);
  if (idx === -1) return 1;
  const row = Math.floor(idx / 9);
  const col = idx % 9;
  const used = new Set<number>();
  for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
    used.add(grid[row * 9 + i]);
    used.add(grid[i * 9 + col]);
  }
  const br = Math.floor(row / 3) * 3;
  const bc = Math.floor(col / 3) * 3;
  for (let r = 0; r < 3; r++) for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) used.add(grid[(br + r) * 9 + bc + c]);
  let total = 0;
  for (let n = 1; n <= 9; n++) {
    if (used.has(n)) continue;
    grid[idx] = n;
    total += countSolutions(grid, limit - total);
    grid[idx] = 0;
    if (total >= limit) break;
  }
  return total;
}

function makePuzzle(difficulty: 'easy' | 'medium' | 'hard'): { puzzle: Grid; solution: Grid } {
  const solution = generateSolution();
  const puzzle = [...solution];
  const holes = difficulty === 'easy' ? 38 : difficulty === 'medium' ? 48 : 54;
  const positions = shuffled(Array.from({ length: 81 }, (_, i) => i));
  let removed = 0;
  for (const pos of positions) {
    if (removed >= holes) break;
    const backup = puzzle[pos];
    puzzle[pos] = 0;
    const copy = [...puzzle];
    if (countSolutions(copy) !== 1) {
      puzzle[pos] = backup;
    } else {
      removed += 1;
    }
  }
  return { puzzle, solution };
}

export default function SudokuGame() {
  const [difficulty, setDifficulty] = useLocalStorageState<'easy' | 'medium' | 'hard'>('owl:sudoku-difficulty', 'medium');
  const [state, setState] = useLocalStorageState<{ puzzle: Grid; solution: Grid; notes: number[] } | null>('owl:sudoku-save', null);
  const [grid, setGrid] = useState<Grid>([]);
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState<number[]>(Array.from({ length: 81 }, () => 0));
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<number | null>(40);
  const [noteMode, setNoteMode] = useState(false);
  const [history, setHistory] = useState<Grid[]>([]);
  const [seconds, setSeconds] = useState(0);
  const [won, setWon] = useState(false);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');
  const sfx = useGameSfx();
  const { best, submit } = useScores('sudoku');
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const startedAt = useRef<number>(Date.now());

  const newGame = useCallback((diff = difficulty) => {
    const { puzzle, solution } = makePuzzle(diff);
    setState({ puzzle, solution, notes: Array.from({ length: 81 }, () => 0) });
    setGrid(puzzle);
    setNotes(Array.from({ length: 81 }, () => 0));
    setHistory([]);
    setSelected(puzzle.findIndex((v) => v === 0));
    setSeconds(0);
    setWon(false);
    setStatus('');
    startedAt.current = Date.now();
    sfx('click');
  }, [difficulty, setState, sfx]);

  // hydrate from saved game
  useEffect(() => {
    if (state) {
      setGrid(state.puzzle);
      setNotes(state.notes || Array.from({ length: 81 }, () => 0));
      setSelected(state.puzzle.findIndex((v) => v === 0));
    } else {
      newGame();
    }
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (won || !grid.length) return;
    const t = setInterval(() => setSeconds((s) => s + 1), 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, [won, grid.length]);

  const puzzle = state?.puzzle ?? grid;
  const solution = state?.solution ?? grid;

  const conflicts = useMemo(() => {
    const bad = new Set<number>();
    if (!grid.length) return bad;
    for (let i = 0; i < 81; i++) {
      const v = grid[i];
      if (!v) continue;
      const row = Math.floor(i / 9);
      const col = i % 9;
      for (let j = 0; j < 81; j++) {
        if (j === i || grid[j] !== v) continue;
        const r2 = Math.floor(j / 9);
        const c2 = j % 9;
        if (r2 === row || c2 === col) { bad.add(i); bad.add(j); }
        else if (Math.floor(r2 / 3) === Math.floor(row / 3) && Math.floor(c2 / 3) === Math.floor(col / 3)) { bad.add(i); bad.add(j); }
      }
    }
    return bad;
  }, [grid]);

  const remaining = useMemo(() => {
    const counts: Record<number, number> = {};
    for (let n = 1; n <= 9; n++) counts[n] = 9 - grid.filter((v) => v === n).length;
    return counts;
  }, [grid]);

  const place = useCallback((index: number, value: number) => {
    if (!grid.length || won) return;
    if (puzzle[index] !== 0) return;
    setHistory((h) => [...h.slice(-40), grid]);
    if (noteMode) {
      setNotes((prev) => {
        const next = [...prev];
        next[index] = next[index] ^ (1 << value);
        setState((s) => (s ? { ...s, notes: next } : s));
        return next;
      });
      sfx('click');
      return;
    }
    const next = [...grid];
    next[index] = next[index] === value ? 0 : value;
    setGrid(next);
    setState((s) => (s ? { ...s, puzzle: next } : s));
    sfx(next[index] ? 'drop' : 'click');
    if (next.every((v, i) => v === solution[i]) && !next.includes(0)) {
      setWon(true);
      sfx('win');
      const score = Math.max(50, 3000 - seconds * 2 - Math.floor(seconds / 1) + (difficulty === 'hard' ? 600 : difficulty === 'easy' ? 0 : 300));
      submit(score, `${difficulty} · ${Math.floor(seconds / 60)}m ${seconds % 60}s`);
      toast('Puzzle solved!');
    }
  }, [grid, puzzle, solution, noteMode, won, seconds, difficulty, sfx, setState, submit, toast]);

  const moveSelection = (dr: number, dc: number) => {
    setSelected((prev) => {
      if (prev === null) return 0;
      const r = Math.max(0, Math.min(8, Math.floor(prev / 9) + dr));
      const c = Math.max(0, Math.min(8, (prev % 9) + dc));
      return r * 9 + c;
    });
  };

  useGameKeys((key, code) => {
    if (key >= '1' && key <= '9') { if (selected !== null) place(selected, Number(key)); return true; }
    if (key === 'Backspace' || key === 'Delete' || key === '0') { if (selected !== null) place(selected, 0); return true; }
    if (key === 'ArrowUp') { moveSelection(-1, 0); return true; }
    if (key === 'ArrowDown') { moveSelection(1, 0); return true; }
    if (key === 'ArrowLeft') { moveSelection(0, -1); return true; }
    if (key === 'ArrowRight') { moveSelection(0, 1); return true; }
    if (key === 'n' || key === 'N') { setNoteMode((n) => !n); return true; }
    if (key === 'h' || key === 'H') { if (selected !== null) hint(selected); return true; }
    if (key === 'z' || key === 'Z') { undo(); return true; }
    if (key === 'Enter' && won) { newGame(); return true; }
    if (code === 'Space') { setNoteMode((n) => !n); return true; }
    return false;
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [selected, grid, noteMode, won, puzzle, noteMode]);

  const hint = (index: number) => {
    if (puzzle[index] !== 0 || !solution[index]) return;
    const value = solution[index];
    const next = [...grid];
    next[index] = value;
    setGrid(next);
    setState((s) => (s ? { ...s, puzzle: next } : s));
    sfx('bonus');
    toast('Hint revealed');
  };

  const undo = () => {
    setHistory((h) => {
      if (!h.length) return h;
      const prev = h[h.length - 1];
      setGrid(prev);
      setState((s) => (s ? { ...s, puzzle: prev } : s));
      sfx('click');
      return h.slice(0, -1);
    });
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    useStore.getState().setAppMenu({
      title: 'Sudoku',
      actions: [
        { id: 'new', label: 'New puzzle', icon: 'plus', run: () => newGame() },
        { id: 'easy', label: 'Easy', icon: 'grid', run: () => { setDifficulty('easy'); newGame('easy'); } },
        { id: 'medium', label: 'Medium', icon: 'grid', run: () => { setDifficulty('medium'); newGame('medium'); } },
        { id: 'hard', label: 'Hard', icon: 'grid', run: () => { setDifficulty('hard'); newGame('hard'); } },
        { id: 'notes', label: noteMode ? 'Typing: answers' : 'Typing: notes', icon: 'memo', run: () => setNoteMode((n) => !n) },
        { id: 'undo', label: 'Undo', icon: 'repeat', run: undo },
        { id: 'hint', label: 'Reveal cell', icon: 'help', run: () => selected !== null && hint(selected) },
        { id: 'scores', label: 'High scores', icon: 'star', run: () => useStore.getState().openApp('games', { view: 'scores', game: 'sudoku' }) },
      ],
    });
    return () => useStore.getState().setAppMenu(null);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [noteMode, selected, grid, puzzle, solution]);

  const selRow = selected !== null ? Math.floor(selected / 9) : -1;
  const selCol = selected !== null ? selected % 9 : -1;
  const selVal = selected !== null ? grid[selected] : 0;

  return (
    <GameShell
      title="SUDOKU"
      score={`${Math.floor(seconds / 60)}:${String(seconds % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`}
      best={best}
      status={status || (noteMode ? 'Notes mode' : 'Answer mode')}
      onRestart={() => newGame()}
    >
      <div className="gameCanvasWrap" style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(160deg,#101c26,#070d12)', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4 }}>
          <div
            style={{
              display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(9, 1fr)', width: 252, height: 252,
              border: '2px solid #d9c79a', background: '#0c1620', borderRadius: 3, overflow: 'hidden',
            }}
            role="grid"
            aria-label="Sudoku grid"
          >
            {grid.map((value, i) => {
              const isGiven = puzzle[i] !== 0;
              const isSel = selected === i;
              const r = Math.floor(i / 9);
              const c = i % 9;
              const sameRow = r === selRow, sameCol = c === selCol;
              const sameBox = selRow >= 0 && Math.floor(r / 3) === Math.floor(selRow / 3) && Math.floor(c / 3) === Math.floor(selCol / 3);
              const sameVal = selVal !== 0 && value === selVal;
              const conflict = conflicts.has(i);
              return (
                <button
                  key={i}
                  role="gridcell"
                  aria-label={`Row ${r + 1} column ${c + 1}: ${value || 'empty'}`}
                  onClick={() => setSelected(i)}
                  style={{
                    position: 'relative', padding: 0, border: 'none',
                    borderRight: c % 3 === 2 && c !== 8 ? '1.5px solid #d9c79a' : '1px solid rgba(217,199,154,.18)',
                    borderBottom: r % 3 === 2 && r !== 8 ? '1.5px solid #d9c79a' : '1px solid rgba(217,199,154,.18)',
                    background: isSel ? '#37536b' : sameVal ? '#25425a' : sameRow || sameCol || sameBox ? '#16283a' : 'transparent',
                    color: conflict ? '#ff7b7b' : isGiven ? '#e8eef5' : '#f6b93f',
                    fontWeight: isGiven ? 700 : 500,
                    fontSize: 13, fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)', cursor: 'pointer',
                  }}
                >
                  {value || (
                    <span style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(3,1fr)', width: '100%', height: '100%', fontSize: 5.5, color: '#7fa4c4' }}>
                      {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9].map((n) => (
                        <span key={n} style={{ display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center' }}>{notes[i] & (1 << n) ? n : ''}</span>
                      ))}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 3, flexWrap: 'wrap', justifyContent: 'center', width: 260 }}>
            {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9].map((n) => (
              <button
                key={n}
                className="btn"
                onClick={() => selected !== null && place(selected, n)}
                style={{ width: 24, height: 22, fontSize: 11, padding: 0, position: 'relative', opacity: remaining[n] === 0 ? 0.35 : 1 }}
                aria-label={`Place ${n}, ${remaining[n]} remaining`}
              >
                {n}
                <span style={{ position: 'absolute', bottom: 0, right: 1, fontSize: 5.5, opacity: 0.6 }}>{remaining[n]}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4 }}>
            <button className={`chip ${noteMode ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => setNoteMode((n) => !n)}>Notes</button>
            <button className="chip" onClick={undo} disabled={!history.length}>Undo</button>
            <button className="chip" onClick={() => selected !== null && place(selected, 0)}>Erase</button>
            <button className="chip" onClick={() => selected !== null && hint(selected)}>Hint</button>
          </div>
        </div>
        {won && (
          <Overlay>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)', fontSize: 16 }}>SOLVED</div>
            <div className="tiny">{difficulty} · {Math.floor(seconds / 60)}m {seconds % 60}s</div>
            <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => newGame()}>New puzzle</button>
          </Overlay>
        )}
      </div>
      <div style={{ padding: '2px 6px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4 }}>
        {(['easy', 'medium', 'hard'] as const).map((d) => (
          <button key={d} className={`chip ${difficulty === d ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => { setDifficulty(d); newGame(d); }}>{d}</button>
        ))}
        <span className="tiny" style={{ marginLeft: 'auto' }}>Keys 1–9 place · N notes · H hint · Z undo</span>
      </div>
    </GameShell>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { GameShell, Overlay, useScores, useGameSfx, useGameKeys } from './gameKit';

type Lang = 'en' | 'ar';

const WORDS: Record<Lang, { word: string; hint: string }[]> = {
  en: [
    { word: 'OWL', hint: 'Night hunter with silent wings' },
    { word: 'NEST', hint: 'Where the babies sleep' },
    { word: 'AMBER', hint: 'The colour of our eyes and buttons' },
    { word: 'FEATHER', hint: 'Light, keeps the cold out' },

  ],
  ar: [
    { word: 'بومة', hint: 'طائر الليل' },
    { word: 'قمر', hint: 'يضيء السماء ليلاً' },
    { word: 'ليل', hint: 'وقت الصيد' },
    { word: 'عش', hint: 'بيت الصغار' },

    { word: 'ساعة', hint: 'لقياس الوقت' },

    { word: 'زهرة', hint: 'تفتح في الربيع' },
    { word: 'سماء', hint: 'فوقنا' },
    { word: 'ريح', hint: 'هواء متحرك' },
  ],
};

const MAX_GUESSES = 6;
const ALPHABET_EN = 'QWERTYUIOPASDFGHJKLZXCVBNM';
const ALPHABET_AR = 'ابتثجحخدذرزسشصضطظعغفقكلمنهوي';

type LetterState = 'correct' | 'present' | 'absent' | 'empty';

function scoreGuess(guess: string, answer: string): LetterState[] {
  const g = [...guess];
  const a = [...answer];
  const result: LetterState[] = g.map(() => 'absent');
  const remaining: Record<string, number> = {};
  a.forEach((ch, i) => {
    if (g[i] === ch) result[i] = 'correct';
    else remaining[ch] = (remaining[ch] || 0) + 1;
  });
  g.forEach((ch, i) => {
    if (result[i] === 'correct') return;
    if (remaining[ch] > 0) {
      result[i] = 'present';
      remaining[ch] -= 1;
    }
  });
  return result;
}

export default function WordOwlGame() {
  const [lang, setLang] = useState<Lang>('en');
  const [answer, setAnswer] = useState('');
  const [hint, setHint] = useState('');
  const [guesses, setGuesses] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [current, setCurrent] = useState('');
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);
  const [won, setWon] = useState(false);
  const [showHint, setShowHint] = useState(false);
  const [streak, setStreak] = useState(0);
  const [seconds, setSeconds] = useState(0);
  const { best, submit } = useScores('wordowl');
  const sfx = useGameSfx();
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const startedAt = useRef(Date.now());

  const startNew = useCallback((nextLang = lang, keepStreak = true) => {
    const pool = WORDS[nextLang];
    const pick = pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)];
    setAnswer(pick.word);
    setHint(pick.hint);
    setGuesses([]);
    setCurrent('');
    setDone(false);
    setWon(false);
    setShowHint(false);
    setSeconds(0);
    startedAt.current = Date.now();
    if (!keepStreak) setStreak(0);
    sfx('flip');
  }, [lang, sfx]);

  useEffect(() => { startNew(lang, true); }, [lang, startNew]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (done) return;
    const t = setInterval(() => setSeconds((s) => s + 1), 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, [done]);

  const letters = useMemo(() => [...answer], [answer]);
  const width = letters.length;

  const keyStates = useMemo(() => {
    const map: Record<string, LetterState> = {};
    guesses.forEach((guess) => {
      const states = scoreGuess(guess, answer);
      [...guess].forEach((ch, i) => {
        const order: Record<LetterState, number> = { empty: 0, absent: 1, present: 2, correct: 3 };
        if (order[states[i]] > (order[map[ch]] || 0)) map[ch] = states[i];
      });
    });
    return map;
  }, [guesses, answer]);

  const submitGuess = useCallback(() => {
    if (done || !answer) return;
    if ([...current].length !== width) {
      toast(`${width} letters needed`);
      sfx('error');
      return;
    }
    const next = [...guesses, current];
    setGuesses(next);
    setCurrent('');
    if (current === answer) {
      setDone(true);
      setWon(true);
      sfx('win');
      setStreak((s) => s + 1);
      const points = Math.max(50, 600 - (next.length - 1) * 70 + (showHint ? 0 : 80));
      submit(points, `${width} letters · ${next.length}/6`);
      toast('Word owl approves!');
    } else if (next.length >= MAX_GUESSES) {
      setDone(true);
      sfx('die');
      setStreak(0);
      submit(10, 'participation');
    } else {
      sfx('drop');
    }
  }, [current, guesses, answer, width, done, showHint, sfx, submit, toast]);

  const typeLetter = (letter: string) => {
    if (done) return;
    if ([...current].length >= width) return;
    setCurrent((c) => c + letter);
    sfx('click');
  };

  useGameKeys((key, code) => {
    if (key === 'Enter') { if (done) startNew(lang, won); else submitGuess(); return true; }
    if (key === 'Backspace') { setCurrent((c) => [...c].slice(0, -1).join('')); return true; }
    if (lang === 'ar') {
      if (ALPHABET_AR.includes(key)) { typeLetter(key); return true; }
      if (key === 'Delete') { setCurrent(''); return true; }
      return false;
    }
    if (key.length === 1 && /[a-zA-Z]/.test(key)) { typeLetter(key.toUpperCase()); return true; }
    if (code === 'Delete') { setCurrent(''); return true; }
    return false;
  }, [current, done, won, width, lang, submitGuess, startNew]);

  useEffect(() => {
    useStore.getState().setAppMenu({
      title: 'Word Owl',
      actions: [
        { id: 'new', label: 'New word', icon: 'repeat', run: () => startNew(lang, true) },
        { id: 'hint', label: 'Show hint', icon: 'help', run: () => setShowHint(true) },
        { id: 'en', label: 'English words', icon: 'globe', run: () => setLang('en') },
        { id: 'ar', label: 'كلمات عربية', icon: 'globe', run: () => setLang('ar') },
        { id: 'scores', label: 'High scores', icon: 'star', run: () => useStore.getState().openApp('games', { view: 'scores', game: 'wordowl' }) },
      ],
    });
    return () => useStore.getState().setAppMenu(null);
  }, [lang, startNew]);

  const rtl = lang === 'ar';
  const alphabet = rtl ? ALPHABET_AR : ALPHABET_EN;
  const rows = rtl || MAX_GUESSES ? Array.from({ length: MAX_GUESSES }, (_, i) => guesses[i] ?? (i === guesses.length ? current : '')) : [];

  return (
    <GameShell title="WORD OWL" score={`${guesses.length}/6`} best={best} status={done ? (won ? 'Solved!' : `It was ${answer}`) : showHint ? hint : `Streak ${streak}`} onRestart={() => startNew(lang, true)}>
      <div className="gameCanvasWrap" style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(160deg,#0e1b24,#060c12)', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, padding: 4 }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 3, direction: rtl ? 'rtl' : 'ltr' }}>
          {rows.map((word, r) => {
            const lettersRow = [...(word || '')];
            const states = r < guesses.length ? scoreGuess(guesses[r], answer) : [];
            return (
              <div key={r} style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 3 }}>
                {Array.from({ length: width }).map((_, c) => {
                  const ch = lettersRow[c] || '';
                  const state = states[c] || 'empty';
                  const bg = state === 'correct' ? '#2f7a52' : state === 'present' ? '#a3801f' : state === 'absent' ? '#28323b' : 'transparent';
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={c}
                      aria-label={ch || 'empty'}
                      style={{
                        width: width > 8 ? 18 : width > 6 ? 22 : 26, height: width > 8 ? 20 : width > 6 ? 24 : 28,
                        border: `1.5px solid ${ch ? 'rgba(246,185,63,.65)' : 'rgba(160,190,210,.25)'}`,
                        background: bg, color: state === 'empty' ? '#eaf2f8' : '#fff', borderRadius: 3,
                        display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center', fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)', fontWeight: 700,
                        fontSize: width > 8 ? 10 : width > 6 ? 12 : 14, textTransform: rtl ? 'none' : 'uppercase',
                      }}
                    >
                      {ch}
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 2, justifyContent: 'center', maxWidth: 320 }}>
          {[...alphabet].map((letter) => {
            const state = keyStates[letter] || 'empty';
            return (
              <button
                key={letter}
                onClick={() => typeLetter(letter)}
                className="btn"
                style={{
                  minWidth: 17, height: 19, padding: '0 2px', fontSize: 8.5,
                  background: state === 'correct' ? '#2f7a52' : state === 'present' ? '#a3801f' : state === 'absent' ? '#28323b' : undefined,
                  color: state === 'absent' ? '#7f8f9c' : undefined,
                }}
                aria-label={`Letter ${letter}${state !== 'empty' ? ` — ${state}` : ''}`}
              >
                {letter}
              </button>
            );
          })}
          <button className="btn" onClick={submitGuess} style={{ height: 19, fontSize: 8.5, padding: '0 5px' }}>Enter</button>
          <button className="btn" onClick={() => setCurrent((c) => [...c].slice(0, -1).join(''))} style={{ height: 19, fontSize: 8.5, padding: '0 5px' }}>⌫</button>
        </div>
        {done && (
          <Overlay>
            <div style={{ fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)', fontSize: 15 }}>{won ? 'WORD OWL APPROVES' : 'OUT OF GUESSES'}</div>
            <div className="tiny">Answer: <b>{answer}</b> · {hint}</div>
            <div className="tiny">Streak {streak} · Best {best}</div>
            <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => startNew(lang, won)}>{won ? 'Next word' : 'Try another'}</button>
          </Overlay>
        )}
      </div>
      <div style={{ padding: '2px 6px', display: 'flex', gap: 3, alignItems: 'center' }}>
        <button className={`chip ${lang === 'en' ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => setLang('en')}>English</button>
        <button className={`chip ${lang === 'ar' ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => setLang('ar')}>عربي</button>
        <button className="chip" onClick={() => setShowHint(true)}>Hint</button>
        <span className="tiny" style={{ marginLeft: 'auto' }}>{Math.floor(seconds / 60)}:{String(seconds % 60).padStart(2, '0')} · {width} letters</span>
      </div>
    </GameShell>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';
import { SHORTCUT_LEGEND } from '../../os/keys';

export default function HelpApp() {
  const openApp = useStore((s) => s.openApp);
  return (
    <AppFrame title="Quick Help" icon="help" actions={<span className="tiny">tips</span>}>
      <div className="appBody pad" style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 6 }}>
        <div className="card">
          <div style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 3 }}>Driving the phone</div>
          <div className="tiny" style={{ lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
            The desk keyboard and the on-screen keyboard are the same thing. Physical presses light
            up the matching key on the device. Arrows move the highlight, Enter selects, Esc goes back.
            Tap the screen for direct touch on phones and tablets.
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="owlSection" style={{ padding: 0 }}>Keyboard map</div>
        <div className="card">
          {SHORTCUT_LEGEND.map(([k, d]) => (
            <div key={k} className="kv" style={{ borderBottom: '1px dashed var(--line)' }}>
              <b className="mono" style={{ fontSize: 9.5 }}>{k}</b>
              <span style={{ fontSize: 9.5, textAlign: 'right' }}>{d}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="owlSection" style={{ padding: 0 }}>Power user tips</div>
        <div className="card tiny" style={{ lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
          • Type any letter on the home screen to search everything.<br />
          • Hold a letter to jump to its hotkey app.<br />
          • Hold Menu to switch between recent apps.<br />
          • PageUp / PageDown adjusts the volume with a HUD.<br />
          • Hold T on the home screen for the flashlight.<br />
          • Every app is code-split, so it's fast even on old hardware.
        </div>

        <div className="btnRow">
          <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => openApp('settings')}>
            <Icon name="settings" size={10} /> Open Settings
          </button>
          <button className="btn" onClick={() => openApp('setup')}>Setup wizard</button>
        </div>
        <div className="tiny" style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
          Everything runs locally. No account, no cloud, no tracking.
        </div>
        <a
          href="https://github.com/aamrabdelhay/owl"
          target="_blank"
          rel="noreferrer noopener"
          style={{ color: 'var(--hl)', fontSize: 9, textAlign: 'center', display: 'block' }}
        >
          github.com/aamrabdelhay/owl
        </a>
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { useCollection, uid, downloadBlob } from '../../hooks';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { Field } from '../../components/Field';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';
import { DB_KEYS } from '../../os/db';
import type { Note } from '../../os/types';

export default function MemoApp() {
  const [notes, setNotes] = useCollection<Note>(DB_KEYS.notes, [
    {
      id: 'n-welcome', title: 'Welcome to Memo Pad', category: 'General',
      body: 'Type notes here. Everything is saved to this browser as you go.\n\nTry the search field, and press Menu for export options.\n\nYour notes never leave this device.',
      at: Date.now(), updatedAt: Date.now(),
    },
  ]);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [category, setCategory] = useState('All');
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<Note | null>(null);
  const showDialog = useStore((s) => s.showDialog);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const setAppMenu = useStore((s) => s.setAppMenu);

  const categories = useMemo(() => ['All', ...Array.from(new Set(notes.map((n) => n.category || 'General')))], [notes]);
  const filtered = notes
    .filter((n) => (category === 'All' || (n.category || 'General') === category))
    .filter((n) => !query || `${n.title} ${n.body}`.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase()))
    .sort((a, b) => b.updatedAt - a.updatedAt);

  const save = (note: Note) => {
    const exists = notes.some((n) => n.id === note.id);
    setNotes((prev) => (exists ? prev.map((n) => (n.id === note.id ? note : n)) : [{ ...note, at: Date.now() }, ...prev]));
    setEditing(null);
    toast('Note saved');
  };

  React.useEffect(() => {
    setAppMenu({
      title: 'Memo Pad',
      actions: [
        { id: 'new', label: 'New note', icon: 'plus', run: () => setEditing({ id: uid('note'), title: '', body: '', category: 'General', at: Date.now(), updatedAt: Date.now() }) },
        {
          id: 'export', label: 'Export all notes (.txt)', icon: 'download',
          run: () => {
            const text = notes.map((n) => `## ${n.title || 'Untitled'}\n${n.category}\n${new Date(n.updatedAt).toLocaleString()}\n\n${n.body}`).join('\n\n---\n\n');
            downloadBlob(new Blob([text], { type: 'text/plain' }), 'owl-notes.txt');
            toast('Notes exported');
          },
        },
        {
          id: 'exportJson', label: 'Export notes (.json)', icon: 'download',
          run: () => { downloadBlob(new Blob([JSON.stringify(notes, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' }), 'owl-notes.json'); },
        },
        {
          id: 'clear', label: 'Delete all notes', icon: 'trash', danger: true,
          run: () => showDialog({ title: 'Delete all notes?', body: 'Stored only on this device.', confirmLabel: 'Delete all', danger: true, onConfirm: () => setNotes([]) }),
        },
      ],
    });
    return () => setAppMenu(null);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [notes]);

  if (editing) {
    return (
      <MemoEditor
        note={editing}
        onSave={save}
        onCancel={() => setEditing(null)}
        onDelete={(id) => { setNotes((prev) => prev.filter((n) => n.id !== id)); setEditing(null); toast('Note deleted'); }}
        categories={categories.filter((c) => c !== 'All')}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <AppFrame
      title="Memo Pad"
      icon="memo"
      actions={<button className="hbtn" onClick={() => setEditing({ id: uid('note'), title: '', body: '', category: category === 'All' ? 'General' : category, at: Date.now(), updatedAt: Date.now() })}><Icon name="plus" size={9} /> New</button>}
    >
      <div style={{ padding: 5 }}>
        <Field value={query} onChange={setQuery} placeholder="Search notes" autoFocus />
      </div>
      <div className="chipRow" style={{ paddingTop: 0 }}>
        {categories.map((c) => <button key={c} className={`chip ${category === c ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => setCategory(c)}>{c}</button>)}
      </div>
      <div className="appBody">
        {filtered.length === 0 && (
          <div className="empty"><div className="big">📝</div><div>No notes{query ? ' match that search' : ' yet'}.</div></div>
        )}
        {filtered.map((n) => (
          <button key={n.id} className="rowBtn" style={{ alignItems: 'flex-start' }} onClick={() => setEditing(n)}>
            <Icon name="memo" size={12} style={{ marginTop: 2 }} />
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'flex-start', minWidth: 0, gap: 1 }}>
              <span style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>{n.title || 'Untitled note'}</span>
              <span className="tiny" style={{ maxWidth: 280, overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                {n.body.split('\n')[0].slice(0, 60) || '—'}
              </span>
            </div>
            <span className="meta">{new Date(n.updatedAt).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })}</span>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}

function MemoEditor({ note, onSave, onCancel, onDelete, categories }: {
  note: Note; onSave: (n: Note) => void; onCancel: () => void; onDelete: (id: string) => void; categories: string[];
}) {
  const [n, setN] = useState(note);
  const [newCat, setNewCat] = useState('');
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  return (
    <AppFrame
      title={note.title ? 'Edit note' : 'New note'}
      icon="memo"
      actions={
        <>
          <button className="hbtn" onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</button>
          <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => onSave({ ...n, updatedAt: Date.now() })}>Save</button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <div className="appBody pad" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 3 }}>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Title</div>
        <Field value={n.title} onChange={(v) => setN({ ...n, title: v })} placeholder="Note title" autoFocus maxLength={80} />
        <div className="fieldLabel">Category</div>
        <div className="chipRow" style={{ padding: 0 }}>
          {(categories.length ? categories : ['General']).map((c) => (
            <button key={c} className={`chip ${n.category === c ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => setN({ ...n, category: c })}>{c}</button>
          ))}
          <input
            value={newCat}
            onChange={(e) => setNewCat(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="+ new"
            inputMode="none"
            style={{ width: 54, fontSize: 9, background: 'rgba(0,0,0,.3)', border: '1px solid var(--line)', color: 'var(--text)', borderRadius: 99, padding: '1px 6px' }}
            onKeyDown={(e) => {
              if (e.key === 'Enter' && newCat.trim()) { setN({ ...n, category: newCat.trim() }); setNewCat(''); }
            }}
          />
        </div>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Note · {n.body.length} chars</div>
        <Field value={n.body} onChange={(v) => setN({ ...n, body: v })} placeholder="Write something…" multiline maxLength={20000} style={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 130 }} />
        <div className="btnRow" style={{ justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
          {note.title && <button className="btn danger" onClick={() => onDelete(note.id)}>Delete</button>}
          <button
            className="btn primary"
            onClick={() => {
              if (!n.title.trim() && !n.body.trim()) { toast('Write something first'); return; }
              onSave({ ...n, title: n.title.trim() || n.body.trim().slice(0, 24), updatedAt: Date.now() });
            }}
          >Save note</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { useCollection, uid } from '../../hooks';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { Field } from '../../components/Field';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';
import { DB_KEYS } from '../../os/db';
import type { SmsMessage, SmsThread, Contact } from '../../os/types';
import { messageTone } from '../../os/audio';

const SEED_THREADS: SmsThread[] = [
  { id: 'th-welcome', address: 'OWL', name: 'OWL Team', updatedAt: Date.now() - 60000 },
  { id: 'th-sam', address: '+1 555 0142', name: 'Sam Rivera', updatedAt: Date.now() - 3600000 },
];

const SEED_MESSAGES: SmsMessage[] = [
  {
    id: 'msg-w1', threadId: 'th-welcome', address: 'OWL', direction: 'in', read: false,
    body: 'Welcome to OWL! 🦉 Your phone is running locally in this browser — nothing you type leaves the device. Press Menu for apps, or hold any letter on the home screen to open its hotkey.',
    at: Date.now() - 60000, status: 'delivered',
  },
  {
    id: 'msg-s1', threadId: 'th-sam', address: '+1 555 0142', direction: 'in', read: true,
    body: 'Hey! Did the new Owl Snake update add the golden mouse?',
    at: Date.now() - 7200000, status: 'delivered',
  },
  {
    id: 'msg-s2', threadId: 'th-sam', address: '+1 555 0142', direction: 'out', read: true,
    body: 'Yes — golden mice give bonus points and a short speed boost. Baby owlets follow you now too.',
    at: Date.now() - 3600000, status: 'delivered',
  },
];

type Folder = 'inbox' | 'sent' | 'drafts';

export default function MessagesApp({ threadId }: { threadId?: string }) {
  const [threads, setThreads] = useCollection<SmsThread>(DB_KEYS.smsThreads, SEED_THREADS);
  const [messages, setMessages] = useCollection<SmsMessage>(DB_KEYS.smsMessages, SEED_MESSAGES);
  const [contacts] = useCollection<Contact>(DB_KEYS.contacts);
  const [openThread, setOpenThread] = useState<string | null>(threadId || null);
  const [folder, setFolder] = useState<Folder>('inbox');
  const [composeTo, setComposeTo] = useState('');
  const [composing, setComposing] = useState(false);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState('');
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [reply, setReply] = useState('');
  const settings = useStore((s) => s.settings);
  const showDialog = useStore((s) => s.showDialog);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const refreshCounters = useStore((s) => s.refreshCounters);

  useEffect(() => {
    void refreshCounters();
  }, [messages.length, refreshCounters]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (threadId) setOpenThread(threadId);
  }, [threadId]);

  const threadMessages = useMemo(
    () => messages.filter((m) => m.threadId === openThread).sort((a, b) => a.at - b.at),
    [messages, openThread]
  );

  const thread = threads.find((t) => t.id === openThread);

  const markRead = (tid: string) => {
    setMessages((prev) => prev.map((m) => (m.threadId === tid ? { ...m, read: true } : m)));
  };

  const open = (tid: string) => {
    setOpenThread(tid);
    markRead(tid);
    void refreshCounters();
  };

  const displayName = (t: SmsThread) => {
    const contact = contacts.find((c) => c.phone.replace(/\D/g, '') === t.address.replace(/\D/g, '') && t.address !== 'OWL');
    return t.name || (contact ? `${contact.firstName} ${contact.lastName}` : t.address);
  };

  const send = (body: string, address: string, tid?: string) => {
    if (!body.trim()) return;
    const id = tid || `th-${address.replace(/\W/g, '') || Date.now()}`;
    if (!threads.find((t) => t.id === id)) {
      setThreads((prev) => [{ id, address, updatedAt: Date.now() }, ...prev]);
    }
    setMessages((prev) => [
      ...prev,
      {
        id: uid('msg'), threadId: id, address, body: body.trim(), direction: 'out',
        status: 'sent', read: true, at: Date.now(),
      },
    ]);
    setThreads((prev) => prev.map((t) => (t.id === id ? { ...t, updatedAt: Date.now() } : t)));
    setReply('');
    setDraft('');
    setComposing(false);
    setOpenThread(id);
    if (settings.profile !== 'silent') messageTone(settings.messageTone, 0.4);
    // simulated delivery receipt
    window.setTimeout(() => {
      setMessages((prev) => prev.map((m) => (m.direction === 'out' && m.threadId === id && m.status === 'sent' ? { ...m, status: 'delivered' } : m)));
    }, 1400);
    if (address === 'OWL') {
      window.setTimeout(() => {
        setMessages((prev) => [
          ...prev,
          {
            id: uid('msg'), threadId: id, address, direction: 'in', read: true, at: Date.now(), status: 'delivered',
            body: '🦉 Message received. This inbox is local — replies here are simulated.',
          },
        ]);
      }, 2200);
    }
  };

  const saveDraft = () => {
    if (!draft.trim()) return;
    const id = `th-${composeTo.replace(/\W/g, '') || 'draft'}`;
    setMessages((prev) => [
      ...prev,
      { id: uid('draft'), threadId: id, address: composeTo || 'Draft', direction: 'out', body: draft, at: Date.now(), status: 'draft', read: true },
    ]);
    toast('Saved to drafts');
    setDraft('');
    setComposing(false);
  };

  /* ------------------------------- thread view ------------------------------- */
  if (openThread && thread) {
    const len = reply.length;
    const segments = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(len / 160));
    return (
      <AppFrame
        title={displayName(thread)}
        icon="message"
        actions={
          <>
            <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => showDialog({
              title: 'Delete conversation?',
              body: `All messages with ${displayName(thread)} will be removed.`,
              confirmLabel: 'Delete',
              danger: true,
              onConfirm: () => {
                setMessages((prev) => prev.filter((m) => m.threadId !== thread.id));
                setThreads((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== thread.id));
                setOpenThread(null);
              },
            })} aria-label="Delete conversation">
              <Icon name="trash" size={9} />
            </button>
            <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => { setOpenThread(null); void refreshCounters(); }}>Back</button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <div className="appBody" style={{ padding: 6, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4 }}>
          <div style={{ flex: 1, overflow: 'auto', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4 }}>
            {threadMessages.map((m) => (
              <div key={m.id} style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: m.direction === 'out' ? 'flex-end' : 'flex-start' }}>
                <div
                  style={{
                    maxWidth: '78%', padding: '4px 7px', borderRadius: 8, fontSize: 10.5, lineHeight: 1.4,
                    background: m.direction === 'out' ? 'linear-gradient(180deg,var(--hl-2),var(--hl))' : 'var(--panel-2)',
                    color: m.direction === 'out' ? 'var(--hl-text)' : 'var(--text)',
                    border: '1px solid var(--line)',
                  }}
                >
                  {m.body}
                  <div style={{ fontSize: 7.5, opacity: 0.7, marginTop: 2, textAlign: 'right' }}>
                    {new Date(m.at).toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}
                    {m.direction === 'out' && ` · ${m.status === 'delivered' ? '✓✓' : '✓'}`}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
          <Field
            value={reply}
            onChange={setReply}
            placeholder="Type a message…"
            autoFocus
            maxLength={1600}
            onSubmit={() => send(reply, thread.address, thread.id)}
          />
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 5 }}>
            <span className="tiny" style={{ flex: 1 }}>{len}/160 · {segments} segment{segments > 1 ? 's' : ''}</span>
            <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => send(reply, thread.address, thread.id)}>
              <Icon name="send" size={10} /> Send
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </AppFrame>
    );
  }

  /* ------------------------------- compose view ------------------------------- */
  if (composing) {
    const len = draft.length;
    const segments = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(len / 160));
    return (
      <AppFrame
        title="New message"
        icon="message"
        actions={<button className="hbtn" onClick={() => { setComposing(false); setDraft(''); }}>Cancel</button>}
      >
        <div className="appBody" style={{ padding: 7, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 3 }}>
          <div className="fieldLabel">To</div>
          <Field value={composeTo} onChange={setComposeTo} placeholder="Phone number" kind="phone" autoFocus maxLength={24} />
          <div className="chipRow" style={{ padding: '3px 0' }}>
            {contacts.slice(0, 5).map((c) => (
              <button key={c.id} className="chip" onClick={() => setComposeTo(c.phone)}>
                {c.firstName}
              </button>
            ))}
            <button className="chip" onClick={() => setComposeTo('OWL')}>OWL Team</button>
          </div>
          <div className="fieldLabel">Message</div>
          <Field value={draft} onChange={setDraft} placeholder="Write your message…" multiline maxLength={1600} />
          <div className="tiny">{len}/160 characters · {segments} SMS segment{segments > 1 ? 's' : ''}</div>
          <div className="btnRow" style={{ marginTop: 'auto', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
            <button className="btn" onClick={saveDraft}>Save draft</button>
            <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => send(draft, composeTo || 'Unknown')} disabled={!composeTo.trim()}>
              <Icon name="send" size={10} /> Send
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </AppFrame>
    );
  }

  /* -------------------------------- list view -------------------------------- */
  const searched = messages.filter((m) => {
    if (folder === 'drafts' && m.status !== 'draft') return false;
    if (folder === 'sent' && (m.direction !== 'out' || m.status === 'draft')) return false;
    if (search && !m.body.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) && !m.address.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())) return false;
    return true;
  });

  return (
    <AppFrame
      title="Messages"
      icon="message"
      actions={
        <>
          <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => setComposing(true)}><Icon name="plus" size={9} /> New</button>
          <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => useStore.getState().setAppMenu({
            title: 'Messages',
            actions: [
              { id: 'new', label: 'Compose message', icon: 'plus', run: () => setComposing(true) },
              { id: 'tone', label: 'Message tone setting', icon: 'bell', run: () => useStore.getState().openApp('settings', { section: 'sound' }) },
              { id: 'clear', label: 'Delete all messages', icon: 'trash', danger: true, run: () => showDialog({ title: 'Delete all messages?', body: 'This cannot be undone.', confirmLabel: 'Delete all', danger: true, onConfirm: () => { setMessages([]); setThreads([]); } }) },
            ],
          })}>Menu</button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <div className="owlTabBar">
        {(['inbox', 'sent', 'drafts'] as Folder[]).map((f) => (
          <button key={f} className={`owlTab ${folder === f ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setFolder(f)}>
            {f[0].toUpperCase() + f.slice(1)}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div style={{ padding: '4px 6px' }}>
        <Field value={search} onChange={setSearch} placeholder="Search messages" />
      </div>
      <div className="appBody">
        {folder === 'inbox' && threads
          .slice()
          .sort((a, b) => b.updatedAt - a.updatedAt)
          .filter((t) => {
            if (!search) return true;
            return displayName(t).toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase());
          })
          .map((t) => {
            const last = [...messages].filter((m) => m.threadId === t.id).sort((a, b) => b.at - a.at)[0];
            const unread = messages.filter((m) => m.threadId === t.id && m.direction === 'in' && !m.read).length;
            return (
              <button key={t.id} className="rowBtn" onClick={() => open(t.id)}>
                <Icon name="message" size={13} style={{ color: unread ? 'var(--hl)' : 'var(--muted)' }} />
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'flex-start', minWidth: 0 }}>
                  <span style={{ fontWeight: unread ? 700 : 500 }}>{displayName(t)}</span>
                  <span className="tiny" style={{ maxWidth: 250, overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                    {last ? last.body : 'No messages'}
                  </span>
                </div>
                {unread > 0 && <span className="countPill meta">{unread}</span>}
                <span className="meta">{last ? new Date(last.at).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }) : ''}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}

        {(folder === 'sent' || folder === 'drafts') && (
          <div>
            {searched.length === 0 && (
              <div className="empty">
                <div className="big">{folder === 'drafts' ? '📝' : '📤'}</div>
                <div>No {folder} messages.</div>
              </div>
            )}
            {searched.map((m) => (
              <button key={m.id} className="rowBtn" onClick={() => { setOpenThread(m.threadId); }}>
                <Icon name="message" size={12} />
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'flex-start' }}>
                  <span>{m.address}</span>
                  <span className="tiny" style={{ maxWidth: 240, overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>{m.body}</span>
                </div>
                <span className="meta">{new Date(m.at).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { useCollection, uid } from '../../hooks';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { Field } from '../../components/Field';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';
import { DB_KEYS } from '../../os/db';
import type { ChatMessage, ChatThread } from '../../os/types';
import { messageTone } from '../../os/audio';
import { evaluate } from '../../os/expr';

const BOTS: ChatThread[] = [
  {
    id: 'chat-owl', name: 'Owl Assistant', avatar: '🦉', color: '#c9922f',
    messages: [
      { id: 'm1', from: 'them', body: 'Hoot! I am the Owl Assistant. Ask me for a tip, a joke, or type "help".', at: Date.now() - 900000, read: true },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'chat-talia', name: 'Talia (bot)', avatar: '🎧', color: '#7c5cff',
    messages: [
      { id: 'm2', from: 'them', body: 'Hey! I make playlists. Ask me about music 🎵', at: Date.now() - 7200000, read: true },
      { id: 'm3', from: 'me', body: 'Favourite retro track?', at: Date.now() - 7100000, read: true },
      { id: 'm4', from: 'them', body: 'Anything synthwave. Try the Ringtones app — all the tones are synthesised live.', at: Date.now() - 7000000, read: true },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'chat-milo', name: 'Milo (bot)', avatar: '🎮', color: '#3fa36b',
    messages: [
      { id: 'm5', from: 'them', body: 'High score hunter here. Ask me about games!', at: Date.now() - 86400000 * 2, read: true },
    ],
  },
];

function botReply(name: string, text: string): string {
  const t = text.toLowerCase();
  if (/help|what can/.test(t)) {
    return 'I can share tips, tell a joke, do quick math ("12*8"), check the time, or tell you about any OWL app. Try "tip", "joke", "time", or "snake".';
  }
  if (/time|clock/.test(t)) {
    return `It's ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })} on this device. The Clock app has alarms too.`;
  }
  if (/joke|funny|laugh/.test(t)) {
    const jokes = [
      'What do you call an owl with a keyboard? A hoot-and-typer. 🦉',
      'Why did the owl use a trackpad? Its mouse was out of office.',
      'My phone has 42 keys and zero excuses for typos. Still typos.',
    ];
    return jokes[Math.floor(Math.random() * jokes.length)];
  }
  if (/tip|hint/.test(t)) {
    return 'Tip: hold a letter on the home screen to launch its hotkey. Hold Menu for recent apps. Press PageUp/PageDown to change volume.';
  }
  if (/snake|owl snake/.test(t)) {
    return 'Owl Snake: each mouse adds an owlet behind you. Golden mice score 5× and speed you up. Watch that chain!';
  }
  if (/music|song|playlist/.test(t) && name.startsWith('Talia')) {
    return 'Load your own tracks in Music → Add files. The equalizer is a live AnalyserNode, not a gif. 😎';
  }
  if (/game|score|high/.test(t)) {
    return 'Open Games and tap High scores. Nine games, all original, all saved in IndexedDB.';
  }
  const math = text.match(/^([\d\s+\-*/.()%]+)$/);
  if (math) {
    try {
      // safe evaluation via Function is avoided: use the calculator's parser
      const value = evaluate(text);
      return `${text.trim()} = ${value}`;
    } catch {
      return 'I could not parse that equation. Try "12 * 8".';
    }
  }
  if (/hi|hello|hey|hoot/.test(t)) return `Hoot hoot! ${name} here. Ask me anything.`;
  const replies = [
    'Noted! I am a rule-based bot, so my vocabulary is small but my enthusiasm is enormous.',
    'Interesting. Try asking for a "tip" or a "joke".',
    'I only run on this device — no servers, no data leaving your phone.',
    'You can also hold Menu here to open the app menu, or press Back to leave.',
  ];
  return replies[Math.floor(Math.random() * replies.length)];
}

export default function MessengerApp() {
  const [threads, setThreads] = useCollection<ChatThread>(DB_KEYS.chats, BOTS);
  const [active, setActive] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [text, setText] = useState('');
  const settings = useStore((s) => s.settings);
  const showDialog = useStore((s) => s.showDialog);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const thread = threads.find((t) => t.id === active);

  useEffect(() => {
    scrollRef.current?.scrollTo({ top: 99999 });
  }, [thread?.messages.length, thread?.typing]);

  const send = () => {
    if (!text.trim() || !thread) return;
    const body = text.trim();
    const msg: ChatMessage = { id: uid('cm'), from: 'me', body, at: Date.now(), read: false };
    setThreads((prev) => prev.map((t) => (t.id === thread.id ? { ...t, messages: [...t.messages, msg], typing: true } : t)));
    setText('');
    if (settings.profile !== 'silent') messageTone('soft-chime', 0.35);

    window.setTimeout(() => {
      setThreads((prev) => prev.map((t) => (t.id === thread.id
        ? { ...t, messages: t.messages.map((m) => (m.id === msg.id ? { ...m, read: true } : m)) }
        : t)));
    }, 900);

    window.setTimeout(() => {
      const reply = botReply(thread.name, body);
      setThreads((prev) => prev.map((t) => (t.id === thread.id
        ? {
          ...t,
          typing: false,
          messages: [...t.messages, { id: uid('cm'), from: 'them', body: reply, at: Date.now(), read: true }],
        }
        : t)));
      if (settings.profile !== 'silent') messageTone(settings.messageTone, 0.3);
    }, 1700);
  };

  if (thread) {
    return (
      <AppFrame
        title={thread.name}
        icon="chat"
        actions={
          <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => setActive(null)}>Back</button>
        }
        menu={[
          { id: 'bots', label: 'All bots', icon: 'back', run: () => setActive(null) },
          { id: 'clear', label: 'Clear this chat', icon: 'trash', danger: true, run: () => setThreads((prev) => prev.map((t) => (t.id === thread?.id ? { ...t, messages: [] } : t))) },
        ]}
      >
        <div ref={scrollRef} className="appBody" style={{ padding: 6, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4 }}>
          <div className="tiny" style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>Local demo chat · rule-based bot</div>
          {thread.messages.map((m) => (
            <div key={m.id} style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4, justifyContent: m.from === 'me' ? 'flex-end' : 'flex-start' }}>
              {m.from === 'them' && (
                <span style={{ width: 18, height: 18, borderRadius: '50%', background: thread.color, display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center', fontSize: 10, flex: 'none' }}>
                  {thread.avatar}
                </span>
              )}
              <div
                style={{
                  maxWidth: '74%', padding: '4px 7px', borderRadius: 8, fontSize: 10.5, lineHeight: 1.4,
                  background: m.from === 'me' ? 'linear-gradient(180deg,#7c5cff,#5b3fe0)' : 'var(--panel-2)',
                  color: m.from === 'me' ? '#fff' : 'var(--text)',
                  border: '1px solid var(--line)',
                }}
              >
                {m.body}
                <div style={{ fontSize: 7.5, opacity: 0.7, marginTop: 2, textAlign: 'right' }}>
                  {new Date(m.at).toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}
                  {m.from === 'me' && (m.read ? ' ✓✓' : ' ✓')}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
          {thread.typing && (
            <div className="tiny" style={{ paddingLeft: 22 }}>typing<span className="dots">…</span></div>
          )}
        </div>
        <div style={{ padding: 5, borderTop: '1px solid var(--line)', display: 'flex', gap: 4, alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <Field value={text} onChange={setText} placeholder="Message…" autoFocus maxLength={500} onSubmit={send} />
          <button className="btn primary" onClick={send} aria-label="Send"><Icon name="send" size={11} /></button>
        </div>
      </AppFrame>
    );
  }

  return (
    <AppFrame
      title="Owl Messenger"
      icon="chat"
      actions={
        <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => showDialog({
          title: 'Clear all chats?',
          body: 'Your conversation history with the bots will be reset.',
          confirmLabel: 'Reset',
          danger: true,
          onConfirm: () => { setThreads(BOTS); toast('Chats reset'); },
        })}>Menu</button>
      }
    >
      <div className="appBody">
        <div className="owlSection">Chats · fully offline</div>
        {threads.map((t) => {
          const last = t.messages[t.messages.length - 1];
          return (
            <button key={t.id} className="rowBtn" onClick={() => setActive(t.id)}>
              <span style={{ width: 24, height: 24, borderRadius: '50%', background: t.color, display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center', fontSize: 13, flex: 'none' }}>
                {t.avatar}
              </span>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'flex-start', minWidth: 0 }}>
                <span style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>{t.name}</span>
                <span className="tiny" style={{ maxWidth: 240, overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                  {t.typing ? 'typing…' : last?.body}
                </span>
              </div>
              <span className="meta">{last ? new Date(last.at).toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }) : ''}</span>
            </button>
          );
        })}
        <div className="tiny" style={{ padding: 8, textAlign: 'center' }}>
          Bots reply with simple rules — no network, no accounts.
        </div>
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { useLocalStorageState, formatDuration, uid } from '../../hooks';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';
import { pickFiles, readTags, isAudio, type TrackTags } from '../../os/media';
import { registerAppKeys } from '../../os/kernel';
import { keyClick } from '../../os/audio';

interface Track {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  url: string;
  file: File;
  duration?: number;
  tags?: TrackTags;
}

type RepeatMode = 'off' | 'one' | 'all';

/**
 * Music player. Files only ever come from the user's device via the file
 * picker and are played through object URLs — never uploaded, never stored.
 */
export default function MusicApp() {
  const [library, setLibrary] = useState<Track[]>([]);
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(-1);
  const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);
  const [position, setPosition] = useState(0);
  const [duration, setDuration] = useState(0);
  const [shuffle, setShuffle] = useState(false);
  const [repeat, setRepeat] = useState<RepeatMode>('all');
  const [loadingTags, setLoadingTags] = useState(false);
  const [view, setView] = useState<'library' | 'player'>('library');
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const audioRef = useRef<HTMLAudioElement>(null);
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  const analyserRef = useRef<AnalyserNode | null>(null);
  const sourceRef = useRef<MediaElementAudioSourceNode | null>(null);
  const ctxRef = useRef<AudioContext | null>(null);
  const [volume, setVolume] = useLocalStorageState<number>('owl:music-volume', 0.8);
  const settings = useStore((s) => s.settings);
  const setNowPlaying = useStore((s) => s.setNowPlaying);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const showDialog = useStore((s) => s.showDialog);
  const track = index >= 0 ? library[index] : null;
  // assigned below (after `play` is defined) so the Menu key can shuffle
  const shuffleAllRef = useRef<() => void>(() => {});
  const shuffleAll = () => shuffleAllRef.current();

  const addFiles = async (directory = false) => {
    const picked = await pickFiles('audio/*', true, directory);
    const audioFiles = picked.filter(isAudio);
    if (!audioFiles.length) {
      if (picked.length) toast('No audio files in that selection');
      return;
    }
    setLoadingTags(true);
    const next: Track[] = [];
    for (const f of audioFiles) {
      const tags = await readTags(f.file);
      next.push({
        id: uid('tr'),
        name: f.path || f.name,
        url: URL.createObjectURL(f.file),
        file: f.file,
        tags,
      });
    }
    setLibrary((prev) => [...prev, ...next]);
    setLoadingTags(false);
    toast(`Added ${next.length} track${next.length > 1 ? 's' : ''} — nothing was uploaded`);
    if (index < 0 && next.length) {
      setIndex(0);
      window.setTimeout(() => void play(0), 200);
    }
  };

  const setupAnalyser = () => {
    const audio = audioRef.current;
    if (!audio) return;
    try {
      if (!ctxRef.current) {
        const AC = window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext;
        ctxRef.current = new AC();
      }
      const ctx = ctxRef.current!;
      if (!sourceRef.current) {
        sourceRef.current = ctx.createMediaElementSource(audio);
        const analyser = ctx.createAnalyser();
        analyser.fftSize = 64;
        analyser.smoothingTimeConstant = 0.75;
        sourceRef.current.connect(analyser);
        analyser.connect(ctx.destination);
        analyserRef.current = analyser;
      }
      if (ctx.state === 'suspended') void ctx.resume();
    } catch {
      // Some browsers disallow MediaElementSource with blob URLs; playback still works.
    }
  };

  const draw = useCallback(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    const analyser = analyserRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')!;
    const bars = 16;
    const w = canvas.width / bars;
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    let data: Uint8Array | null = null;
    if (analyser) {
      data = new Uint8Array(analyser.frequencyBinCount);
      analyser.getByteFrequencyData(data as unknown as Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>);
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < bars; i++) {
      const value = data ? (data as any)[i] / 255 : (Math.sin(Date.now() / 260 + i * 0.5) + 1) / 2 * 0.5;
      const h = Math.max(2, value * canvas.height);
      const gradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, canvas.height, 0, canvas.height - h);
      gradient.addColorStop(0, '#e4572e');
      gradient.addColorStop(1, '#ffd27a');
      ctx.fillStyle = gradient;
      ctx.fillRect(i * w + 1, canvas.height - h, w - 2, h);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    let raf = 0;
    const loop = () => {
      if (playing) draw();
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    };
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
  }, [playing, draw]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const audio = audioRef.current;
    if (!audio) return;
    audio.volume = volume;
  }, [volume, index]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const audio = audioRef.current;
    if (!audio) return;
    const onTime = () => setPosition(audio.currentTime);
    const onMeta = () => {
      setDuration(audio.duration || 0);
      if (track) {
        setLibrary((prev) => prev.map((t) => (t.id === track.id ? { ...t, duration: audio.duration } : t)));
      }
    };
    const onEnd = () => {
      if (repeat === 'one') {
        audio.currentTime = 0;
        void audio.play();
        return;
      }
      nextTrack();
    };
    audio.addEventListener('timeupdate', onTime);
    audio.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', onMeta);
    audio.addEventListener('ended', onEnd);
    return () => {
      audio.removeEventListener('timeupdate', onTime);
      audio.removeEventListener('loadedmetadata', onMeta);
      audio.removeEventListener('ended', onEnd);
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [repeat, index, library.length, shuffle]);

  const play = async (i: number) => {
    if (i < 0 || i >= library.length) return;
    setIndex(i);
    const audio = audioRef.current;
    if (!audio) return;
    setupAnalyser();
    audio.src = library[i].url;
    try {
      await audio.play();
      setPlaying(true);
      const t = library[i];
      setNowPlaying({ title: t.tags?.title || t.name.split('/').pop() || 'Track', playing: true });
    } catch {
      setPlaying(false);
      toast('Could not play that file');
    }
  };

  shuffleAllRef.current = () => {
    if (!library.length) { toast('Add some music first'); return; }
    const start = Math.floor(Math.random() * library.length);
    setView('player');
    setShuffle(true);
    void play(start);
  };

  const toggle = () => {
    const audio = audioRef.current;
    if (!audio) return;
    if (!track) { void addFiles(false); return; }
    if (playing) {
      audio.pause();
      setPlaying(false);
      setNowPlaying(null);
    } else {
      if (!audio.src) { void play(index < 0 ? 0 : index); return; }
      setupAnalyser();
      void audio.play().then(() => {
        setPlaying(true);
        setNowPlaying({ title: track.tags?.title || track.name.split('/').pop() || 'Track', playing: true });
      }).catch(() => toast('Playback blocked — tap play again'));
    }
  };

  const nextTrack = useCallback(() => {
    if (!library.length) return;
    let next: number;
    if (shuffle) {
      next = Math.floor(Math.random() * library.length);
      if (library.length > 1 && next === index) next = (next + 1) % library.length;
    } else {
      next = index + 1;
      if (next >= library.length) {
        if (repeat === 'all') next = 0;
        else { setPlaying(false); setNowPlaying(null); return; }
      }
    }
    void play(next);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [library, index, shuffle, repeat]);

  const prevTrack = () => {
    const audio = audioRef.current;
    if (audio && audio.currentTime > 3) { audio.currentTime = 0; return; }
    if (!library.length) return;
    const prev = index - 1 < 0 ? library.length - 1 : index - 1;
    void play(prev);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const off = registerAppKeys((e) => {
      if (e.key === ' ' && !useStore.getState().activeFieldId) { toggle(); return true; }
      if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' && e.ctrl) { nextTrack(); return true; }
      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' && e.ctrl) { prevTrack(); return true; }
      return false;
    });
    return off;
  });

  useEffect(() => () => {
    const audio = audioRef.current;
    if (audio) { audio.pause(); }
    setNowPlaying(null);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  const titleOf = (t: Track) => t.tags?.title || t.name.split('/').pop()?.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '') || 'Unknown track';
  const filtered = library.filter((t) => !search || titleOf(t).toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) || (t.tags?.artist || '').toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()));

  return (
    <AppFrame
      title={view === 'player' ? 'Now playing' : 'Music'}
      icon="music"
      actions={
        <>
          <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => void addFiles(false)}><Icon name="plus" size={9} /> Files</button>
          <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => setView(view === 'player' ? 'library' : 'player')}>{view === 'player' ? 'Library' : 'Player'}</button>
        </>
      }
      menu={[
        { id: 'files', label: 'Add music files', icon: 'plus', run: () => { void addFiles(false); } },
        { id: 'folder', label: 'Add a folder', icon: 'folder', run: () => { void addFiles(true); } },
        { id: 'player', label: 'Now playing', icon: 'play', run: () => setView('player') },
        { id: 'library', label: 'Library', icon: 'list', run: () => setView('library') },
        { id: 'shuffle', label: 'Shuffle all', icon: 'shuffle', run: shuffleAll },
      ]}
    >
      <audio ref={audioRef} preload="metadata" />
      {view === 'library' && (
        <>
          <div style={{ padding: 5, display: 'flex', gap: 4 }}>
            <input
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Filter tracks"
              inputMode="none"
              style={{ flex: 1, background: 'rgba(0,0,0,.3)', border: '1px solid var(--line)', color: 'var(--text)', borderRadius: 4, padding: '2px 6px', fontSize: 10 }}
            />
            <button className="btn" onClick={() => void addFiles(true)} title="Add a whole folder">Folder</button>
          </div>
          <div className="appBody">
            {loadingTags && <div className="empty"><div className="spinner" /><div>Reading tags locally…</div></div>}
            {!loadingTags && library.length === 0 && (
              <div className="empty">
                <div className="big">🎵</div>
                <div><b>Pick music from this device</b></div>
                <div className="tiny" style={{ maxWidth: 300 }}>
                  Files are played straight from your disk with object URLs. Nothing is uploaded or
                  copied to any server.
                </div>
                <div className="btnRow" style={{ justifyContent: 'center' }}>
                  <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => void addFiles(false)}>Add audio files</button>
                  <button className="btn" onClick={() => void addFiles(true)}>Add a folder</button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
            {filtered.map((t) => {
              const realIndex = library.indexOf(t);
              return (
                <button
                  key={t.id}
                  className="rowBtn"
                  onClick={() => { void play(realIndex); setView('player'); }}
                  style={{ background: realIndex === index ? 'rgba(255,255,255,.06)' : undefined }}
                >
                  {t.tags?.picture
                    ? <img src={t.tags.picture} alt="" style={{ width: 22, height: 22, borderRadius: 4, objectFit: 'cover' }} />
                    : <Icon name="music" size={12} />}
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'flex-start', minWidth: 0 }}>
                    <span style={{ overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap', maxWidth: 200 }}>{titleOf(t)}</span>
                    <span className="tiny">{t.tags?.artist || t.name.split('/').slice(-2, -1)[0] || 'Unknown artist'}</span>
                  </div>
                  <span className="meta">{t.duration ? formatDuration(t.duration) : ''}</span>
                </button>
              );
            })}
            {library.length > 0 && (
              <div style={{ padding: 6, display: 'flex', gap: 4 }}>
                <button className="btn" onClick={() => showDialog({
                  title: 'Clear playlist?',
                  body: 'The files stay on your device — only the playlist is cleared.',
                  confirmLabel: 'Clear', danger: true,
                  onConfirm: () => {
                    library.forEach((t) => URL.revokeObjectURL(t.url));
                    setLibrary([]); setIndex(-1); setPlaying(false); setNowPlaying(null);
                  },
                })}>Clear playlist</button>
                <button className="btn" onClick={() => setShuffle((s) => !s)}>{shuffle ? 'Shuffle on' : 'Shuffle off'}</button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
          <MiniBar playing={playing} track={track ? titleOf(track) : null} onToggle={toggle} />
        </>
      )}

      {view === 'player' && (
        <div className="appBody" style={{ padding: 7, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 5, background: 'linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(228,87,46,.14),transparent 60%)' }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center', gap: 4 }}>
            <div style={{ width: 86, height: 86, borderRadius: 10, overflow: 'hidden', border: '1px solid var(--line)', background: 'linear-gradient(150deg,var(--panel-3),var(--panel))', display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center' }}>
              {track?.tags?.picture ? <img src={track.tags.picture} alt="" style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'cover' }} /> : <Icon name="music" size={36} />}
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700, textAlign: 'center', maxWidth: 320, overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
              {track ? titleOf(track) : 'No track loaded'}
            </div>
            <div className="tiny">{track?.tags?.artist || '—'}{track?.tags?.album ? ` · ${track.tags.album}` : ''}</div>
          </div>
          <canvas ref={canvasRef} width={300} height={34} style={{ width: '100%', height: 34, display: 'block' }} aria-label="Equalizer visualizer" />
          <div>
            <div className="progress" style={{ height: 4 }}><i style={{ width: `${duration ? (position / duration) * 100 : 0}%` }} /></div>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between' }} className="tiny">
              <span>{formatDuration(position)}</span><span>{formatDuration(duration)}</span>
            </div>
            <input
              type="range" min={0} max={duration || 0} value={position} step={0.5}
              onChange={(e) => { const v = Number(e.target.value); setPosition(v); if (audioRef.current) audioRef.current.currentTime = v; }}
              style={{ width: '100%', accentColor: 'var(--hl)' }}
              aria-label="Seek"
              inputMode="none"
            />
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
            <button className="iconBtn" onClick={() => setShuffle((s) => !s)} aria-label="Shuffle" style={{ opacity: shuffle ? 1 : 0.5 }}><Icon name="shuffle" size={12} /></button>
            <button className="iconBtn" onClick={prevTrack} aria-label="Previous"><Icon name="prev" size={13} /></button>
            <button
              className="btn primary"
              style={{ width: 42, height: 34, borderRadius: '50%', display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center' }}
              onClick={toggle}
              aria-label={playing ? 'Pause' : 'Play'}
            >
              <Icon name={playing ? 'pause' : 'play'} size={15} />
            </button>
            <button className="iconBtn" onClick={nextTrack} aria-label="Next"><Icon name="next" size={13} /></button>
            <button
              className="iconBtn"
              onClick={() => setRepeat(repeat === 'off' ? 'all' : repeat === 'all' ? 'one' : 'off')}
              aria-label={`Repeat ${repeat}`}
              style={{ opacity: repeat === 'off' ? 0.5 : 1 }}
            >
              <Icon name="repeat" size={12} />
              {repeat === 'one' && <span style={{ fontSize: 7 }}>1</span>}
            </button>
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 5 }}>
            <Icon name="volume" size={11} />
            <input
              type="range" min={0} max={1} step={0.02} value={volume}
              onChange={(e) => setVolume(Number(e.target.value))}
              style={{ flex: 1, accentColor: 'var(--hl)' }}
              aria-label="Volume"
              inputMode="none"
            />
            <span className="tiny mono">{Math.round(volume * 100)}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="tiny" style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
            Playback continues in the status bar when you leave this app.
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </AppFrame>
  );
}

function MiniBar({ playing, track, onToggle }: { playing: boolean; track: string | null; onToggle: () => void }) {
  if (!track) return null;
  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 5, padding: '3px 6px', borderTop: '1px solid var(--line)', alignItems: 'center', background: 'rgba(0,0,0,.25)' }}>
      <Icon name="music" size={11} />
      <span className="tiny" style={{ flex: 1, overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>{track}</span>
      <button className="iconBtn" onClick={onToggle} aria-label={playing ? 'Pause' : 'Play'} style={{ width: 18, height: 18 }}>
        <Icon name={playing ? 'pause' : 'play'} size={9} />
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}
```
