```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { uid } from '../../hooks';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { Field } from '../../components/Field';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';
import { decryptVault, encryptVault, generatePassword, passwordStrength, type EncryptedBlob } from '../../os/security';
import { dbGet, dbSet, dbDel } from '../../os/db';
import type { VaultEntry } from '../../os/types';
import { errorBuzz, successChime } from '../../os/audio';

const VAULT_KEY = 'owl:vault:blob';

/**
 * Password Keeper. The vault is encrypted with AES-GCM using a key derived
 * from the master password with PBKDF2. Only ciphertext is written to
 * IndexedDB; the master password is never stored.
 */
export default function PasswordsApp() {
  const [stage, setStage] = useState<'loading' | 'setup' | 'locked' | 'open'>('loading');
  const [master, setMaster] = useState('');
  const [master2, setMaster2] = useState('');
  const [pass, setPass] = useState('');
  const [blob, setBlob] = useState<EncryptedBlob | null>(null);
  const [entries, setEntries] = useState<VaultEntry[]>([]);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<VaultEntry | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [revealed, setRevealed] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const showDialog = useStore((s) => s.showDialog);
  const clearTimer = useRef<number | undefined>(undefined);

  useEffect(() => {
    void dbGet<EncryptedBlob | null>(VAULT_KEY, null).then((stored) => {
      setBlob(stored || null);
      setStage(stored ? 'locked' : 'setup');
    });
    return () => window.clearTimeout(clearTimer.current);
  }, []);

  const persist = async (list: VaultEntry[], password = master) => {
    const encrypted = await encryptVault(password, JSON.stringify(list));
    await dbSet(VAULT_KEY, encrypted);
    setBlob(encrypted);
    setEntries(list);
  };

  const createVault = async () => {
    if (master.length < 8) { setError('Use at least 8 characters'); return; }
    if (master !== master2) { setError('Passwords do not match'); return; }
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await persist([], master);
      setStage('open');
      setTimeout(() => { setMaster(''); setMaster2(''); }, 400);
      successChime();
      toast('Vault created — the key never leaves this device');
    } catch {
      setError('Could not create the vault');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const unlock = async () => {
    if (!blob) return;
    setBusy(true);
    const plain = await decryptVault(pass, blob);
    setBusy(false);
    if (!plain) {
      errorBuzz();
      setError('Wrong master password');
      setPass('');
      return;
    }
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(plain) as VaultEntry[];
      setEntries(parsed);
      setMaster(pass);
      setPass('');
      setError('');
      setStage('open');
      successChime(0.5);
    } catch {
      setError('Vault data is corrupted');
    }
  };

  const saveEntry = async (entry: VaultEntry) => {
    const exists = entries.some((e) => e.id === entry.id);
    const next = exists ? entries.map((e) => (e.id === entry.id ? entry : e)) : [entry, ...entries];
    await persist(next);
    setEditing(null);
    toast(exists ? 'Entry updated' : 'Entry added');
  };

  const removeEntry = async (id: string) => {
    const next = entries.filter((e) => e.id !== id);
    await persist(next);
    toast('Entry deleted');
  };

  const copy = async (text: string) => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      toast('Copied — clipboard clears in 30s');
      window.clearTimeout(clearTimer.current);
      clearTimer.current = window.setTimeout(async () => {
        try {
          const current = await navigator.clipboard.readText().catch(() => '');
          if (current === text) await navigator.clipboard.writeText('');
        } catch { /* clipboard read may be blocked */ }
      }, 30000);
    } catch {
      toast('Clipboard is blocked in this browser');
    }
  };

  if (stage === 'loading') return <AppFrame title="Password Keeper" icon="lock"><div className="empty"><div className="spinner" /></div></AppFrame>;

  if (stage === 'setup') {
    return (
      <AppFrame title="Password Keeper" icon="lock">
        <div className="appBody pad" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 5 }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, alignItems: 'center' }}>
            <Icon name="shield" size={26} />
            <div>
              <div style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700 }}>Create your vault</div>
              <div className="tiny">AES-256-GCM · key derived with PBKDF2 (120k iterations)</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="fieldLabel">Master password (8+ characters)</div>
          <Field value={master} onChange={setMaster} kind="password" placeholder="Master password" autoFocus maxLength={64} />
          <Field value={master2} onChange={setMaster2} kind="password" placeholder="Repeat" maxLength={64} />
          {master && (
            <div className="tiny">
              Strength: <b>{passwordStrength(master).label}</b>
            </div>
          )}
          {error && <div className="tiny" style={{ color: 'var(--red)' }}>{error}</div>}
          <div className="card tiny" style={{ lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
            There is no recovery: the master password is what decrypts the vault. If you forget it,
            delete the vault and start again. Nothing is sent anywhere.
          </div>
          <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => void createVault()} disabled={busy}>
            {busy ? 'Creating…' : 'Create encrypted vault'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </AppFrame>
    );
  }

  if (stage === 'locked') {
    return (
      <AppFrame title="Password Keeper" icon="lock">
        <div className="appBody pad" style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 5, placeItems: 'center', paddingTop: 18 }}>
          <Icon name="lock" size={30} />
          <div style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700 }}>Vault locked</div>
          <div className="tiny">{blob ? `Encrypted at ${new Date().toLocaleDateString()}` : ''}</div>
          <div style={{ width: 190 }}>
            <Field value={pass} onChange={setPass} kind="password" placeholder="Master password" autoFocus maxLength={64} onSubmit={() => void unlock()} />
          </div>
          {error && <div className="tiny" style={{ color: 'var(--red)' }}>{error}</div>}
          <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => void unlock()} disabled={busy}>{busy ? 'Unlocking…' : 'Unlock'}</button>
          <button
            className="btn"
            style={{ fontSize: 9 }}
            onClick={() => showDialog({
              title: 'Delete the vault?',
              body: 'All saved passwords will be permanently destroyed on this device.',
              confirmLabel: 'Delete vault', danger: true,
              onConfirm: async () => { await dbDel(VAULT_KEY); setBlob(null); setStage('setup'); setEntries([]); toast('Vault deleted'); },
            })}
          >
            I forgot the master password
          </button>
        </div>
      </AppFrame>
    );
  }

  if (editing) {
    return <EntryEditor entry={editing} onSave={(e) => void saveEntry(e)} onCancel={() => setEditing(null)} />;
  }

  const filtered = entries.filter((e) => !query || `${e.title} ${e.username} ${e.website}`.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase()));

  return (
    <AppFrame
      title="Password Keeper"
      icon="lock"
      actions={
        <>
          <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => setEditing({ id: uid('vault'), title: '', username: '', password: generatePassword(16), website: '', notes: '', updatedAt: Date.now() })}>
            <Icon name="plus" size={9} /> New
          </button>
          <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => { setStage('locked'); setEntries([]); setMaster(''); toast('Vault locked'); }}>Lock</button>
        </>
      }
      menu={[
        { id: 'new', label: 'New entry', icon: 'plus', run: () => setEditing({ id: uid('vault'), title: '', username: '', password: generatePassword(16), website: '', notes: '', updatedAt: Date.now() }) },
        { id: 'lock', label: 'Lock vault', icon: 'lock', run: () => { setStage('locked'); setEntries([]); setMaster(''); toast('Vault locked'); } },
        { id: 'reveal', label: 'Hide all passwords', icon: 'eyeOff', run: () => setRevealed(null) },
        { id: 'generator', label: 'Copy a fresh password', icon: 'key', run: () => { const pw = generatePassword(20); void copy(pw); } },
      ]}
    >
      <div style={{ padding: 5 }}>
        <Field value={query} onChange={setQuery} placeholder="Search entries" />
      </div>
      <div className="appBody">
        <div className="owlSection">{entries.length} entries · encrypted on device</div>
        {filtered.length === 0 && (
          <div className="empty"><div className="big">🔐</div><div>No entries yet.</div>
            <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => setEditing({ id: uid('vault'), title: '', username: '', password: generatePassword(16), website: '', notes: '', updatedAt: Date.now() })}>Add entry</button>
          </div>
        )}
        {filtered.map((e) => (
          <div key={e.id} className="rowBtn" style={{ cursor: 'default', alignItems: 'flex-start' }}>
            <Icon name="key" size={12} style={{ marginTop: 2 }} />
            <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
              <span style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>{e.title}</span>
              <span className="tiny">{e.username}{e.website ? ` · ${e.website}` : ''}</span>
              <span className="mono" style={{ fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: '0.06em' }}>
                {revealed === e.id ? e.password : '•'.repeat(Math.min(16, e.password.length))}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 2 }}>
              <button className="iconBtn" onClick={() => setRevealed(revealed === e.id ? null : e.id)} aria-label="Show password">
                <Icon name={revealed === e.id ? 'eyeOff' : 'eye'} size={9} />
              </button>
              <button className="iconBtn" onClick={() => void copy(e.password)} aria-label="Copy password">⧉</button>
              <button className="iconBtn" onClick={() => setEditing(e)} aria-label="Edit entry">✎</button>
              <button className="iconBtn" onClick={() => showDialog({
                title: 'Delete entry?', body: e.title, confirmLabel: 'Delete', danger: true, onConfirm: () => void removeEntry(e.id),
              })} aria-label="Delete entry">✕</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}

function EntryEditor({ entry, onSave, onCancel }: { entry: VaultEntry; onSave: (e: VaultEntry) => void; onCancel: () => void }) {
  const [e, setE] = useState(entry);
  const [show, setShow] = useState(true);
  const [len, setLen] = useState(16);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const strength = passwordStrength(e.password);
  return (
    <AppFrame title={entry.title ? 'Edit entry' : 'New entry'} icon="key" actions={<button className="hbtn" onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</button>}>
      <div className="appBody pad" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 3 }}>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Title</div>
        <Field value={e.title} onChange={(v) => setE({ ...e, title: v })} placeholder="e.g. Bank" autoFocus maxLength={60} />
        <div className="fieldLabel">Username</div>
        <Field value={e.username} onChange={(v) => setE({ ...e, username: v })} placeholder="user@example.com" maxLength={80} />
        <div className="fieldLabel">Password</div>
        <Field value={show ? e.password : '•'.repeat(e.password.length)} onChange={(v) => setE({ ...e, password: v })} kind={show ? 'text' : 'password'} placeholder="Password" maxLength={120} />
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4, alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <button className="chip" onClick={() => setShow((s) => !s)}>{show ? 'Hide' : 'Show'}</button>
          <span className="tiny">Strength:</span>
          <div className="progress" style={{ flex: 1, height: 3 }}><i style={{ width: `${(strength.score / 5) * 100}%`, background: strength.score >= 4 ? 'var(--green)' : strength.score >= 2 ? 'var(--hl)' : 'var(--red)' }} /></div>
          <span className="tiny">{strength.label}</span>
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4, alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <input type="range" min={8} max={32} value={len} onChange={(ev) => setLen(Number(ev.target.value))} style={{ flex: 1, accentColor: 'var(--hl)' }} inputMode="none" aria-label="Generator length" />
          <button className="btn" onClick={() => { const p = generatePassword(len); setE({ ...e, password: p }); toast('Password generated'); }}>Generate {len}</button>
        </div>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Website</div>
        <Field value={e.website} onChange={(v) => setE({ ...e, website: v })} placeholder="example.com" maxLength={100} />
        <div className="fieldLabel">Notes</div>
        <Field value={e.notes} onChange={(v) => setE({ ...e, notes: v })} placeholder="Notes" multiline maxLength={600} />
        <div className="btnRow" style={{ justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
          <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => {
            if (!e.title.trim()) { toast('Add a title'); return; }
            onSave({ ...e, title: e.title.trim(), updatedAt: Date.now() });
          }}>Save to vault</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}
```

_12785 bytes · 281 lines_

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { useCollection, uid } from '../../hooks';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';
import { DB_KEYS } from '../../os/db';
import type { CallLogEntry, Contact } from '../../os/types';
import { formatDuration } from '../../hooks';
import { Icon as I } from '../../components/Icon';
import { errorBuzz, playRingtone, stopRingtone, vibrate } from '../../os/audio';
import { registerAppKeys } from '../../os/kernel';

type View = 'dial' | 'log' | 'contacts';

export default function PhoneApp({ tab, number: initialNumber, activeCall }: { tab?: View; number?: string; activeCall?: boolean }) {
  const [view, setView] = useState<View>(tab || 'dial');
  const [number, setNumber] = useState(initialNumber || '');
  const [callState, setCallState] = useState<'idle' | 'dialing' | 'active'>(activeCall ? 'active' : 'idle');
  const [callSeconds, setCallSeconds] = useState(0);
  const [muted, setMuted] = useState(false);
  const [speaker, setSpeaker] = useState(false);
  const [log, setLog] = useCollection<CallLogEntry>(DB_KEYS.callLog);
  const [contacts] = useCollection<Contact>(DB_KEYS.contacts);
  const settings = useStore((s) => s.settings);
  const showDialog = useStore((s) => s.showDialog);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const refreshCounters = useStore((s) => s.refreshCounters);
  const timer = useRef<number | undefined>(undefined);

  const known = useMemo(
    () => contacts.find((c) => c.phone.replace(/\s/g, '') === number.replace(/\s/g, '')),
    [contacts, number]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (callState !== 'active') return;
    timer.current = window.setInterval(() => setCallSeconds((s) => s + 1), 1000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer.current);
  }, [callState]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (callState === 'dialing') {
      playRingtone(settings.ringtone, 0.25);
      if (settings.vibrate) vibrate([300, 400]);
      const t = window.setTimeout(() => {
        stopRingtone();
        setCallState('active');
        setCallSeconds(0);
      }, 2600);
      return () => { window.clearTimeout(t); stopRingtone(); };
    }
    if (callState === 'idle') stopRingtone();
  }, [callState, settings.ringtone, settings.vibrate]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const off = registerAppKeys((e) => {
      if (e.key.length === 1 && /[0-9*+#]/.test(e.key) && callState === 'idle') {
        setView('dial');
        setNumber((n) => (n.length < 18 ? n + e.key : n));
        return true;
      }
      if (e.key === 'Backspace' && callState === 'idle' && !useStore.getState().activeFieldId) {
        setNumber((n) => n.slice(0, -1));
        return true;
      }
      return false;
    });
    return off;
  }, [callState]);

  const placeCall = () => {
    if (!number) { errorBuzz(); toast('Enter a number first'); return; }
    setView('dial');
    setCallState('dialing');
    setLog([
      { id: uid('call'), number, name: known ? `${known.firstName} ${known.lastName}` : undefined, type: 'out', at: Date.now(), duration: 0 },
      ...log,
    ]);
  };

  const endCall = () => {
    const duration = callSeconds;
    setCallState('idle');
    setCallSeconds(0);
    stopRingtone();
    if (duration > 0) {
      setLog((prev) => {
        const copy = [...prev];
        if (copy[0]) copy[0] = { ...copy[0], duration };
        return copy;
      });
      toast(`Call ended · ${formatDuration(duration)}`);
    }
    setMuted(false);
    setSpeaker(false);
    void refreshCounters();
  };

  const telLink = () => {
    if (!number) return;
    const isMobile = window.matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches;
    if (!isMobile) { toast('Real calls need a phone — this is a simulator'); return; }
    showDialog({
      title: 'Open the dialer?',
      body: `This will leave the simulator and hand <strong>${number}</strong> to your device's dialer.`,
      confirmLabel: 'Open dialer',
      onConfirm: () => { window.location.href = `tel:${number.replace(/[^0-9+*#]/g, '')}`; },
    });
  };

  const press = (d: string) => {
    setNumber((n) => (n.length < 18 ? n + d : n));
  };

  const speedDial = (digit: string) => {
    const contact = contacts.find((c) => c.speedDial === Number(digit));
    if (contact) {
      setNumber(contact.phone);
      setTimeout(() => setCallState('dialing'), 120);
    } else {
      toast(`Speed dial ${digit} is empty — assign it in Contacts`);
    }
  };

  /* -------------------------------- call UI -------------------------------- */
  if (callState !== 'idle') {
    return (
      <div className="appFrame" style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(180deg,#0b1a2c,#050a12)' }}>
        <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 5, color: '#fff' }}>
          <div style={{ width: 52, height: 52, borderRadius: '50%', background: 'linear-gradient(160deg,#2b4460,#101a28)', display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center', border: '1px solid #2f4a6b' }}>
            <Icon name="contacts" size={24} />
          </div>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700 }}>{known ? `${known.firstName} ${known.lastName}` : number || 'Unknown'}</div>
          <div className="tiny" style={{ color: '#9fb4d0' }}>
            {callState === 'dialing' ? 'Calling…' : formatDuration(callSeconds)}
          </div>
          <div className="tiny" style={{ color: '#6f86a3', fontSize: 8 }}>Simulated call — no network is used</div>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, marginTop: 6 }}>
            <button className={`btn ${muted ? 'primary' : ''}`} onClick={() => setMuted((m) => !m)} style={{ fontSize: 9 }}>
              {muted ? 'Unmute' : 'Mute'}
            </button>
            <button className={`btn ${speaker ? 'primary' : ''}`} onClick={() => setSpeaker((s) => !s)} style={{ fontSize: 9 }}>
              Speaker
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style={{ padding: 8, display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
          <button
            className="btn danger"
            style={{ padding: '6px 22px', borderRadius: 99, fontSize: 11 }}
            onClick={callState === 'dialing' ? () => { setCallState('idle'); stopRingtone(); toast('Call cancelled'); } : endCall}
          >
            <Icon name="phoneOff" size={12} /> End call
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <AppFrame
      title="Phone"
      icon="phone"
      actions={
        <>
          <button className="hbtn" onClick={telLink} title="Use this device's dialer"><Icon name="phone" size={9} /> tel:</button>
          <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => useStore.getState().setAppMenu({
            title: 'Phone',
            actions: [
              { id: 'dial', label: 'Dial pad', icon: 'phone', run: () => setView('dial') },
              { id: 'log', label: 'Call log', icon: 'list', run: () => setView('log') },
              { id: 'contacts', label: 'Contacts', icon: 'contacts', run: () => setView('contacts') },
              { id: 'clear', label: 'Clear call log', icon: 'trash', danger: true, run: () => showDialog({ title: 'Clear call log?', body: 'All call history will be removed from this device.', confirmLabel: 'Clear', danger: true, onConfirm: () => setLog([]) }) },
            ],
          })}>Menu</button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <div className="owlTabBar">
        <button className={`owlTab ${view === 'dial' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setView('dial')}>Dial pad</button>
        <button className={`owlTab ${view === 'log' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setView('log')}>Call log</button>
        <button className={`owlTab ${view === 'contacts' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setView('contacts')}>Contacts</button>
      </div>
      <div className="appBody">
        {view === 'dial' && (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', height: '100%' }}>
            <div style={{ flex: 1, padding: 6, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4 }}>
              <div className="field" style={{ fontSize: 15, letterSpacing: '0.06em', justifyContent: 'center', minHeight: 28 }} aria-label="Number">
                {number || <span style={{ opacity: 0.4, fontSize: 11 }}>Type a number<br />numbers row or keypad</span>}
                <span className="caret" />
              </div>
              {known && <div className="tiny" style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>{known.firstName} {known.lastName}</div>}
              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(3, 1fr)', gap: 3, marginTop: 2 }}>
                {['1', '2', '3', '4', '5', '6', '7', '8', '9', '*', '0', '#'].map((d) => (
                  <button
                    key={d}
                    className="btn"
                    style={{ height: 25, fontSize: 14, fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)' }}
                    onClick={() => press(d)}
                    onContextMenu={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); speedDial(d); }}
                    onDoubleClick={() => speedDial(d)}
                    title={`Press for ${d} · double-press speed dial${d !== '*' && d !== '#' && d !== '0' && d !== '1' ? ' (hold 2–9)' : ''}`}
                    aria-label={`Digit ${d}`}
                  >
                    {d}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 3 }}>
                <button className="btn" style={{ flex: 1 }} onClick={() => setNumber('')}>Clear</button>
                <button
                  className="btn primary"
                  style={{ flex: 2, background: 'linear-gradient(180deg,#6ff0b0,#2fbf71)', color: '#03200f' }}
                  onClick={placeCall}
                  aria-label="Call"
                >
                  <I name="phone" size={11} /> Call
                </button>
                <button className="btn" style={{ width: 32 }} onClick={() => setNumber((n) => n.slice(0, -1))} aria-label="Backspace">⌫</button>
              </div>
              <div className="tiny" style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontSize: 8 }}>
                Calls are simulated. Double-press 2–9 for speed dial.
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {view === 'log' && (
          <div>
            <div className="owlSection">Recent calls · {log.length}</div>
            {log.length === 0 && (
              <div className="empty"><div className="big">☎</div><div>No calls yet.<br />Dial someone from the dial pad.</div></div>
            )}
            {log.map((entry) => (
              <button
                key={entry.id}
                className="rowBtn"
                onClick={() => { setNumber(entry.number); setView('dial'); }}
              >
                <Icon
                  name={entry.type === 'missed' ? 'phoneOff' : 'phone'}
                  size={12}
                  style={{ color: entry.type === 'missed' ? 'var(--red)' : entry.type === 'in' ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--muted)' }}
                />
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'flex-start' }}>
                  <span style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>{entry.name || entry.number}</span>
                  <span className="tiny">
                    {entry.type === 'missed' ? 'Missed' : entry.type === 'in' ? 'Received' : 'Dialed'} ·{' '}
                    {new Date(entry.at).toLocaleString(undefined, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <span className="meta">{entry.duration ? formatDuration(entry.duration) : ''}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
            {log.length > 0 && (
              <div style={{ padding: 6 }}>
                <button className="btn" onClick={() => showDialog({ title: 'Clear call log?', body: 'Removes all call history.', confirmLabel: 'Clear', danger: true, onConfirm: () => setLog([]) })}>
                  Clear log
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {view === 'contacts' && (
          <div>
            {contacts.length === 0 && <div className="empty"><div className="big">👤</div><div>No contacts yet.</div></div>}
            {contacts.map((c) => (
              <button key={c.id} className="rowBtn" onClick={() => { setNumber(c.phone); setView('dial'); }}>
                <Icon name="contacts" size={12} />
                <span>{c.firstName} {c.lastName}</span>
                <span className="meta">{c.phone}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}
```

### `src/apps/Pictures/PicturesApp.tsx`

_13827 bytes · 298 lines_

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { useCollection, uid, downloadBlob } from '../../hooks';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';
import { DB_KEYS } from '../../os/db';
import type { PhotoItem } from '../../os/types';
import { pickFiles, isImage } from '../../os/media';
import { cropToWallpaper, saveCustomWallpaper } from '../../hooks/useWallpaper';
import { registerAppKeys } from '../../os/kernel';

/**
 * Gallery: user images from the device plus photos captured in the Camera app
 * (which are stored in IndexedDB on this device only).
 */
export default function PicturesApp() {
  const [photos, setPhotos] = useCollection<PhotoItem>(DB_KEYS.photos);
  const [session, setSession] = useState<{ id: string; name: string; url: string; blob: Blob }[]>([]);
  const [viewing, setViewing] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [rotation, setRotation] = useState(0);
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
  const [slideshow, setSlideshow] = useState(false);
  const [cropMode, setCropMode] = useState<'home' | 'lock' | null>(null);
  const [cropZoom, setCropZoom] = useState(1);
  const [cropOffset, setCropOffset] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const cropSrc = useRef<string>('');
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const updateSettings = useStore((s) => s.updateSettings);
  const bumpWallpaper = useStore((s) => s.bumpWallpaper);
  const showDialog = useStore((s) => s.showDialog);

  const all = [
    ...photos.map((p) => ({ id: p.id, name: p.name, url: '', blob: p.blob, at: p.at })),
    ...session.map((s) => ({ ...s, at: Date.now() })),
  ];

  const urls = useUrls(all.map((p) => p.blob));

  const add = async () => {
    const picked = await pickFiles('image/*', true, false);
    const images = picked.filter(isImage);
    if (!images.length) { if (picked.length) toast('No images selected'); return; }
    setSession((prev) => [
      ...prev,
      ...images.map((p) => ({ id: uid('pic'), name: p.name, url: URL.createObjectURL(p.file), blob: p.file })),
    ]);
    toast(`Loaded ${images.length} image${images.length > 1 ? 's' : ''} from this device`);
  };

  const saveToGallery = async (blob: Blob, name: string) => {
    const bitmap = await createImageBitmap(blob).catch(() => null);
    setPhotos((prev) => [...prev, {
      id: uid('photo'), name, blob, at: Date.now(),
      width: bitmap?.width || 0, height: bitmap?.height || 0,
    }]);
    return bitmap;
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!slideshow || viewing === null) return;
    const t = window.setInterval(() => setViewing((v) => (v === null ? null : (v + 1) % all.length)), 2800);
    return () => window.clearInterval(t);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [slideshow, viewing, all.length]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const off = registerAppKeys((e) => {
      if (viewing === null) return false;
      if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') { setViewing((v) => (v === null ? null : (v + 1) % all.length)); return true; }
      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') { setViewing((v) => (v === null ? null : (v - 1 + all.length) % all.length)); return true; }
      if (e.key === 'Escape') { setViewing(null); return true; }
      return false;
    });
    return off;
  });

  const applyCrop = async () => {
    if (!cropMode || !cropSrc.current) return;
    const data = await cropToWallpaper(cropSrc.current, 960, 720, cropZoom, cropOffset.x, cropOffset.y);
    const id = await saveCustomWallpaper(data);
    if (cropMode === 'lock') updateSettings({ lockWallpaperId: id, useSeparateLockWallpaper: true });
    else updateSettings({ wallpaperId: id });
    bumpWallpaper();
    setCropMode(null);
    toast('Wallpaper saved to this device');
  };

  const startCrop = (blobUrl: string, mode: 'home' | 'lock') => {
    cropSrc.current = blobUrl;
    setCropZoom(1);
    setCropOffset({ x: 0, y: 0 });
    setCropMode(mode);
  };

  if (cropMode) {
    return (
      <AppFrame
        title={`Set ${cropMode} wallpaper`}
        icon="image"
        actions={<button className="hbtn" onClick={() => setCropMode(null)}>Cancel</button>}
        menu={[
          { id: 'cancel', label: 'Cancel crop', icon: 'close', run: () => setCropMode(null) },
          { id: 'apply', label: 'Apply wallpaper', icon: 'check', run: () => { void applyCrop(); } },
          { id: 'reset', label: 'Reset crop', icon: 'repeat', run: () => { setCropZoom(1); setCropOffset({ x: 0, y: 0 }); } },
        ]}
      >
        <div className="appBody pad" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 5 }}>
          <div
            style={{
              width: '100%', aspectRatio: '4 / 3', border: '1px solid var(--hl)', borderRadius: 6,
              overflow: 'hidden', position: 'relative', background: '#000',
            }}
          >
            <img
              src={cropSrc.current}
              alt="Crop preview"
              style={{
                width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'cover',
                transform: `scale(${cropZoom}) translate(${cropOffset.x}%, ${cropOffset.y}%)`,
                transition: 'transform 90ms linear',
              }}
            />
            <div
              aria-hidden="true"
              style={{ position: 'absolute', inset: 0, boxShadow: 'inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(255,255,255,.14)', pointerEvents: 'none' }}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="tiny">Drag-free crop: adjust zoom and position until it fits the 4:3 screen.</div>
          <div className="fieldLabel">Zoom</div>
          <input type="range" min={1} max={3} step={0.02} value={cropZoom} onChange={(e) => setCropZoom(Number(e.target.value))} style={{ accentColor: 'var(--hl)' }} inputMode="none" />
          <div className="fieldLabel">Horizontal position</div>
          <input type="range" min={-50} max={50} value={cropOffset.x} onChange={(e) => setCropOffset((o) => ({ ...o, x: Number(e.target.value) }))} style={{ accentColor: 'var(--hl)' }} inputMode="none" />
          <div className="fieldLabel">Vertical position</div>
          <input type="range" min={-50} max={50} value={cropOffset.y} onChange={(e) => setCropOffset((o) => ({ ...o, y: Number(e.target.value) }))} style={{ accentColor: 'var(--hl)' }} inputMode="none" />
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 5, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
            <button className="btn" onClick={() => { setCropZoom(1); setCropOffset({ x: 0, y: 0 }); }}>Reset</button>
            <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => void applyCrop()}>
              Set wallpaper
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </AppFrame>
    );
  }

  if (viewing !== null && all[viewing]) {
    const photo = all[viewing];
    const url = urls[viewing];
    return (
      <AppFrame
        title={photo.name}
        icon="image"
        actions={
          <>
            <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => setSlideshow((s) => !s)}>{slideshow ? 'Stop' : 'Slideshow'}</button>
            <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => { setViewing(null); setZoom(1); setRotation(0); }}>Grid</button>
          </>
        }
        menu={[
          { id: 'slideshow', label: slideshow ? 'Stop slideshow' : 'Slideshow', icon: 'play', run: () => setSlideshow((v) => !v) },
          { id: 'wallpaper', label: 'Set as wallpaper', icon: 'image', run: () => { if (viewing !== null && urls[viewing]) startCrop(urls[viewing], 'home'); } },
          { id: 'grid', label: 'Back to grid', icon: 'grid', run: () => { setViewing(null); setZoom(1); setRotation(0); } },
        ]}
      >
        <div style={{ flex: 1, background: '#04070c', display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center', overflow: 'hidden', position: 'relative' }}>
          <img
            src={url || undefined}
            alt={photo.name}
            onClick={() => setZoom((z) => (z >= 2 ? 1 : z + 0.5))}
            style={{ maxWidth: '100%', maxHeight: '100%', transform: `scale(${zoom}) rotate(${rotation}deg)`, transition: 'transform 140ms ease', cursor: 'zoom-in' }}
          />
          <span style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 4, right: 6, fontSize: 8, background: 'rgba(0,0,0,.55)', padding: '1px 5px', borderRadius: 99 }}>
            {viewing + 1}/{all.length} · {zoom}×
          </span>
        </div>
        <div style={{ padding: 4, borderTop: '1px solid var(--line)' }}>
          <div className="btnRow">
            <button className="btn" onClick={() => { setRotation((r) => r + 90); }}>Rotate</button>
            <button className="btn" onClick={() => setZoom((z) => Math.max(1, z - 0.5))}>Zoom −</button>
            <button className="btn" onClick={() => setZoom((z) => Math.min(4, z + 0.5))}>Zoom +</button>
            <button className="btn" onClick={() => startCrop(url, 'home')}>Wallpaper</button>
            <button className="btn" onClick={() => startCrop(url, 'lock')}>Lock wp</button>
            <button
              className="btn"
              onClick={async () => {
                const rotated = await rotateBlob(photo.blob, rotation);
                if (photos.some((p) => p.id === photo.id)) {
                  setPhotos((prev) => prev.map((p) => (p.id === photo.id ? { ...p, blob: rotated } : p)));
                  toast('Photo rotated and saved');
                } else {
                  await saveToGallery(rotated, photo.name);
                  toast('Added to gallery');
                }
              }}
            >Save</button>
            <button className="btn" onClick={() => downloadBlob(photo.blob, photo.name || 'owl-photo.jpg')}>Download</button>
            <button
              className="btn danger"
              onClick={() => showDialog({
                title: 'Delete photo?',
                body: 'This removes the photo from this device.',
                confirmLabel: 'Delete', danger: true,
                onConfirm: () => {
                  setPhotos((prev) => prev.filter((p) => p.id !== photo.id));
                  setSession((prev) => prev.filter((p) => p.id !== photo.id));
                  setViewing(null);
                },
              })}
            >Delete</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </AppFrame>
    );
  }

  return (
    <AppFrame
      title="Pictures"
      icon="image"
      actions={<button className="hbtn" onClick={() => void add()}><Icon name="plus" size={9} /> Add</button>}
    >
      <div className="appBody">
        {all.length === 0 && (
          <div className="empty">
            <div className="big">🖼</div>
            <div><b>Your gallery is empty</b></div>
            <div className="tiny" style={{ maxWidth: 300 }}>
              Add images from this device, or capture photos in the Camera app. Everything stays
              in your browser's storage — nothing is uploaded.
            </div>
            <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => void add()}>Pick images</button>
          </div>
        )}
        {all.length > 0 && (
          <>
            <div className="owlSection">{photos.length ? `${photos.length} saved` : ''} {session.length ? `· ${session.length} loaded` : ''}</div>
            <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(4, 1fr)', gap: 3, padding: 4 }}>
              {all.map((p, i) => (
                <button
                  key={p.id}
                  onClick={() => { setViewing(i); setZoom(1); setRotation(0); }}
                  aria-label={p.name}
                  style={{ padding: 0, border: '1px solid var(--line)', borderRadius: 4, overflow: 'hidden', aspectRatio: '1', background: 'var(--panel-2)', cursor: 'pointer' }}
                >
                  {urls[i] ? <img src={urls[i]} alt={p.name} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'cover' }} loading="lazy" /> : null}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
            <div style={{ padding: 5, display: 'flex', gap: 5 }}>
              <button className="btn" onClick={() => setSlideshow(true)}>Slideshow</button>
              <button className="btn" onClick={() => void add()}>Add more</button>
              {session.length > 0 && (
                <button
                  className="btn"
                  onClick={async () => {
                    for (const s of session) await saveToGallery(s.blob, s.name);
                    setSession([]);
                    toast('Saved loaded images to the gallery');
                  }}
                >
                  Save {session.length} to gallery
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}

function useUrls(blobs: Blob[]): string[] {
  const [urls, setUrls] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const key = blobs.map((b) => (b as any).size + ':' + (b as any).type + ':' + (b as any).name).join('|');
  useEffect(() => {
    const created = blobs.map((b) => URL.createObjectURL(b));
    setUrls(created);
    return () => created.forEach((u) => URL.revokeObjectURL(u));
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [key]);
  return urls;
}

async function rotateBlob(blob: Blob, degrees: number): Promise<Blob> {
  if (!degrees) return blob;
  const bitmap = await createImageBitmap(blob);
  const radians = (degrees * Math.PI) / 180;
  const swap = degrees % 180 !== 0;
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = swap ? bitmap.height : bitmap.width;
  canvas.height = swap ? bitmap.width : bitmap.height;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')!;
  ctx.translate(canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2);
  ctx.rotate(radians);
  ctx.drawImage(bitmap, -bitmap.width / 2, -bitmap.height / 2);
  return new Promise((resolve) => canvas.toBlob((b) => resolve(b || blob), blob.type || 'image/jpeg', 0.9));
}
```

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import { useStore, profileLabel } from '../../os/store';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';
import type { ProfileId } from '../../os/types';

const PROFILES: { id: ProfileId; desc: string; icon: string; ring: number; keyClick: boolean; vibrate: boolean }[] = [
  { id: 'normal', desc: 'Balanced ring and key clicks', icon: 'volume', ring: 0.75, keyClick: true, vibrate: true },
  { id: 'loud', desc: 'Maximum ring, alarms and key clicks', icon: 'bell', ring: 1, keyClick: true, vibrate: true },
  { id: 'vibrate', desc: 'No sound, vibration only', icon: 'headphones', ring: 0, keyClick: false, vibrate: true },
  { id: 'silent', desc: 'No sound and no vibration', icon: 'headphones', ring: 0, keyClick: false, vibrate: false },
  { id: 'meeting', desc: 'Low ring, no key clicks', icon: 'volume', ring: 0.25, keyClick: false, vibrate: true },
  { id: 'custom', desc: 'Your own mix of the sliders', icon: 'settings', ring: 0.6, keyClick: true, vibrate: true },
];

export default function ProfilesApp() {
  const settings = useStore((s) => s.settings);
  const updateSettings = useStore((s) => s.updateSettings);
  const setSetting = useStore((s) => s.setSetting);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);

  const apply = (id: ProfileId) => {
    const p = PROFILES.find((x) => x.id === id)!;
    updateSettings({
      profile: id,
      ringVolume: id === 'custom' ? settings.ringVolume : p.ring,
      keyClick: id === 'custom' ? settings.keyClick : p.keyClick,
      vibrate: id === 'custom' ? settings.vibrate : p.vibrate,
      volume: id === 'silent' || id === 'vibrate' ? 0 : id === 'loud' ? 1 : settings.volume,
    });
    toast(`${profileLabel(id, settings.language)} profile on`);
  };

  return (
    <AppFrame title="Sound profiles" icon="volume">
      <div className="appBody">
        <div className="owlSection">Choose a profile</div>
        {PROFILES.map((p) => (
          <button
            key={p.id}
            className="rowBtn"
            onClick={() => apply(p.id)}
            style={{ background: settings.profile === p.id ? 'rgba(246,185,63,.12)' : undefined }}
          >
            <Icon name={p.icon} size={13} />
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'flex-start' }}>
              <span style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>{profileLabel(p.id, settings.language)}</span>
              <span className="tiny">{p.desc}</span>
            </div>
            {settings.profile === p.id && <span className="pill amber meta">active</span>}
          </button>
        ))}

        <div className="owlSection">Fine tune</div>
        <div style={{ padding: '0 8px 6px', display: 'grid', gap: 3 }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 5, alignItems: 'center' }}>
            <span className="tiny" style={{ width: 56 }}>Ring</span>
            <input type="range" min={0} max={1} step={0.05} value={settings.ringVolume} onChange={(e) => { setSetting('ringVolume', Number(e.target.value)); setSetting('profile', 'custom'); }} style={{ flex: 1, accentColor: 'var(--hl)' }} inputMode="none" aria-label="Ring volume" />
            <span className="tiny mono" style={{ width: 24 }}>{Math.round(settings.ringVolume * 100)}</span>
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 5, alignItems: 'center' }}>
            <span className="tiny" style={{ width: 56 }}>Media</span>
            <input type="range" min={0} max={1} step={0.05} value={settings.volume} onChange={(e) => { setSetting('volume', Number(e.target.value)); setSetting('profile', 'custom'); }} style={{ flex: 1, accentColor: 'var(--hl)' }} inputMode="none" aria-label="Media volume" />
            <span className="tiny mono" style={{ width: 24 }}>{Math.round(settings.volume * 100)}</span>
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 5, alignItems: 'center' }}>
            <span className="tiny" style={{ width: 56 }}>Alarm</span>
            <input type="range" min={0} max={1} step={0.05} value={settings.alarmVolume} onChange={(e) => setSetting('alarmVolume', Number(e.target.value))} style={{ flex: 1, accentColor: 'var(--hl)' }} inputMode="none" aria-label="Alarm volume" />
            <span className="tiny mono" style={{ width: 24 }}>{Math.round(settings.alarmVolume * 100)}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="btnRow" style={{ marginTop: 2 }}>
            <button className={`chip ${settings.keyClick ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => { setSetting('keyClick', !settings.keyClick); setSetting('profile', 'custom'); }}>Key clicks</button>
            <button className={`chip ${settings.vibrate ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => { setSetting('vibrate', !settings.vibrate); setSetting('profile', 'custom'); }}>Vibration</button>
            <button className={`chip ${settings.vibrateKeys ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => setSetting('vibrateKeys', !settings.vibrateKeys)}>Vibrate on key</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useLocalStorageState } from '../../hooks';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { Field } from '../../components/Field';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';

/** Owl Radio: plays any CORS-friendly stream URL the user provides. */
export default function RadioApp() {
  const [url, setUrl] = useState('');
  const [stations, setStations] = useLocalStorageState<{ name: string; url: string }[]>('owl:radio-stations', []);
  const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(false);
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  const audioRef = useRef<HTMLAudioElement>(null);
  const [volume, setVolume] = useLocalStorageState<number>('owl:radio-volume', 0.8);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const setNowPlaying = useStore((s) => s.setNowPlaying);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (audioRef.current) audioRef.current.volume = volume;
  }, [volume]);

  const play = async (streamUrl: string, label?: string) => {
    const audio = audioRef.current;
    if (!audio) return;
    setFailed(false);
    audio.src = streamUrl;
    try {
      await audio.play();
      setPlaying(true);
      setName(label || labelFromUrl(streamUrl));
      setNowPlaying({ title: label || 'Owl Radio', playing: true });
    } catch {
      setPlaying(false);
      setFailed(true);
      toast('Stream could not be played — check the URL');
    }
  };

  const stop = () => {
    audioRef.current?.pause();
    setPlaying(false);
    setNowPlaying(null);
  };

  const addStation = () => {
    if (!url.trim()) return;
    const label = url.split('/')[2] || 'Custom stream';
    setStations((prev) => [...prev.filter((s) => s.url !== url), { name: label, url: url.trim() }]);
    setUrl('');
    setExpanded(false);
    toast('Station saved');
  };

  return (
    <AppFrame
      title="Owl Radio"
      icon="radio"
      actions={<button className="hbtn" onClick={() => setExpanded((v) => !v)}>{expanded ? 'Hide' : 'Add stream'}</button>}
      menu={[
        { id: 'add', label: 'Add a stream', icon: 'plus', run: () => setExpanded(true) },
        { id: 'stop', label: 'Stop playback', icon: 'pause', run: stop },
        { id: 'music', label: 'Open local music', icon: 'music', run: () => useStore.getState().openApp('music') },
      ]}
    >
      <audio ref={audioRef} preload="none" />
      <div className="appBody pad" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 7 }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center', gap: 5, padding: 6 }}>
          <div
            style={{
              width: 92, height: 92, borderRadius: '50%',
              background: 'conic-gradient(from 0deg, #38b6a4, #0b2a2f, #38b6a4)',
              display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center',
              animation: playing ? 'spin 6s linear infinite' : undefined,
              border: '1px solid var(--line)',
            }}
            aria-hidden="true"
          >
            <span style={{ width: 62, height: 62, borderRadius: '50%', background: 'var(--panel)', display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center' }}>
              <Icon name="radio" size={28} />
            </span>
          </div>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700 }}>{name || 'No station tuned'}</div>
          <div className="tiny">{playing ? 'Live stream' : 'Stopped'}</div>
          <div className="btnRow" style={{ justifyContent: 'center' }}>
            <button className="btn primary" onClick={playing ? stop : () => play(stations[0]?.url || url || '', stations[0]?.name)}>
              <Icon name={playing ? 'pause' : 'play'} size={10} /> {playing ? 'Stop' : 'Play'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {failed && (
          <div className="card tiny">
            That stream would not play. Many stations block direct browser playback (CORS or codec
            issues). Try an <b>.mp3</b> or <b>.m3u8</b> HLS-free stream.
          </div>
        )}

        {expanded && (
          <div className="card" style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 4 }}>
            <div className="fieldLabel">Stream URL</div>
            <Field value={url} onChange={setUrl} placeholder="https://example.com/stream.mp3" autoFocus maxLength={300} onSubmit={addStation} />
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4 }}>
              <button className="btn primary" onClick={addStation}>Save station</button>
              <button className="btn" onClick={() => void play(url)}>Play now</button>
            </div>
            <div className="tiny">Nothing is proxied: the browser fetches the stream directly from its host.</div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="owlSection">Saved stations</div>
        {stations.length === 0 && <div className="tiny">Add any MP3/OGG stream URL you have the right to listen to.</div>}
        {stations.map((s) => (
          <button key={s.url} className="rowBtn" onClick={() => void play(s.url, s.name)}>
            <Icon name="radio" size={12} />
            <span>{s.name}</span>
            <span className="meta">Play</span>
          </button>
        ))}

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 5 }}>
          <Icon name="volume" size={11} />
          <input type="range" min={0} max={1} step={0.02} value={volume} onChange={(e) => setVolume(Number(e.target.value))} style={{ flex: 1, accentColor: 'var(--hl)' }} inputMode="none" aria-label="Volume" />
        </div>
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}

function labelFromUrl(url: string): string {
  try {
    return new URL(url).hostname.replace(/^www\./, '');
  } catch {
    return 'Custom stream';
  }
}
```

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';
import { RINGTONES, playRingtone, stopRingtone, messageTone } from '../../os/audio';

/** All tones are synthesised with oscillators — no copyrighted audio. */
export default function RingtonesApp() {
  const settings = useStore((s) => s.settings);
  const setSetting = useStore((s) => s.setSetting);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const [previewing, setPreviewing] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const preview = (id: string, kind: 'ring' | 'message' | 'alarm') => {
    if (previewing === id) { stopRingtone(); setPreviewing(null); return; }
    stopRingtone();
    setPreviewing(id);
    if (kind === 'message') {
      messageTone(id, 0.9);
      window.setTimeout(() => setPreviewing(null), 2200);
    } else {
      playRingtone(id, 0.8, kind === 'alarm' ? 3 : 2);
      window.setTimeout(() => { setPreviewing(null); }, 7000);
    }
  };

  return (
    <AppFrame
      title="Ringtones"
      icon="bell"
      actions={<span className="tiny">synthesised</span>}
      menu={[
        { id: 'stop', label: 'Stop preview', icon: 'pause', run: () => { stopRingtone(); setPreviewing(null); } },
        { id: 'random', label: 'Preview a random tone', icon: 'shuffle', run: () => preview(RINGTONES[Math.floor(Math.random() * RINGTONES.length)].id, 'ring') },
        { id: 'profiles', label: 'Sound profiles', icon: 'volume', run: () => useStore.getState().openApp('profiles') },
      ]}
    >
      <div className="appBody">
        <div className="owlSection">12 original tones · generated live</div>
        {RINGTONES.map((rt) => (
          <div key={rt.id} style={{ borderBottom: '1px solid var(--line)', padding: '3px 5px' }}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 5 }}>
              <button className="iconBtn" onClick={() => preview(rt.id, 'ring')} aria-label={`Preview ${rt.name}`}>
                <Icon name={previewing === rt.id ? 'pause' : 'play'} size={9} />
              </button>
              <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                <div style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600 }}>{rt.name}</div>
                <div className="tiny">
                  {rt.notes.length} notes · {rt.tempo} BPM
                  {settings.ringtone === rt.id && ' · ringtone'}
                  {settings.messageTone === rt.id && ' · message'}
                  {settings.alarmTone === rt.id && ' · alarm'}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 2 }}>
                <button className={`chip ${settings.ringtone === rt.id ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => { setSetting('ringtone', rt.id); toast(`Ringtone: ${rt.name}`); }}>Ring</button>
                <button className={`chip ${settings.messageTone === rt.id ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => { setSetting('messageTone', rt.id); messageTone(rt.id, 0.8); toast(`Message tone: ${rt.name}`); }}>Msg</button>
                <button className={`chip ${settings.alarmTone === rt.id ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => { setSetting('alarmTone', rt.id); toast(`Alarm tone: ${rt.name}`); }}>Alarm</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
        <div className="tiny" style={{ padding: 8 }}>
          Every tone is built from oscillators at runtime. No samples, no licences, no downloads.
        </div>
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}
```
