```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore, defaultSettings, profileLabel } from '../../os/store';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { Field } from '../../components/Field';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';
import { dbEstimate, dbClear } from '../../os/db';
import { hashPassword, randomSalt, verifyPassword } from '../../os/security';
import { WALLPAPERS } from '../../assets/wallpapers';
import { useWallpaperUrl, saveCustomWallpaper, cropToWallpaper } from '../../hooks/useWallpaper';
import { RINGTONES, playRingtone, successChime } from '../../os/audio';
import { formatBytes } from '../../hooks';
import { APPS, appName } from '../../os/appRegistry';
import type { Lang, ThemeId } from '../../os/types';
import { SHORTCUT_LEGEND } from '../../os/keys';

type Section =
  | 'root' | 'display' | 'sound' | 'wallpaper' | 'themes' | 'home' | 'security' | 'datetime'
  | 'language' | 'battery' | 'storage' | 'accessibility' | 'reset' | 'about' | 'help';

export default function SettingsApp({ section }: { section?: string }) {
  const [stack, setStack] = useState<Section[]>([(section as Section) || 'root']);
  const current = stack[stack.length - 1];
  const push = (s: Section) => setStack((prev) => [...prev, s]);
  const back = () => setStack((prev) => (prev.length > 1 ? prev.slice(0, -1) : prev));
  const settings = useStore((s) => s.settings);

  const title = current === 'root' ? 'Settings' : LABELS[current as Section] || 'Settings';

  return (
    <AppFrame
      title={title}
      icon="settings"
      actions={stack.length > 1 ? <button className="hbtn" onClick={back}>Back</button> : undefined}
    >
      <div className="appBody">
        {current === 'root' && <Root onOpen={push} />}
        {current === 'display' && <Display />}
        {current === 'sound' && <Sound />}
        {current === 'wallpaper' && <Wallpaper />}
        {current === 'themes' && <ThemeSection />}
        {current === 'home' && <HomeSection />}
        {current === 'security' && <Security />}
        {current === 'datetime' && <DateTime />}
        {current === 'language' && <Language />}
        {current === 'battery' && <Battery />}
        {current === 'storage' && <Storage />}
        {current === 'accessibility' && <Accessibility />}
        {current === 'reset' && <Reset onDone={() => setStack(['root'])} />}
        {current === 'about' && <About />}
        {current === 'help' && <Help />}
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}

const LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  display: 'Display', sound: 'Sound', wallpaper: 'Wallpaper', themes: 'Themes', home: 'Home screen',
  security: 'Security', datetime: 'Date & Time', language: 'Language', battery: 'Battery',
  storage: 'Storage', accessibility: 'Accessibility', reset: 'Reset OS', about: 'About', help: 'Quick help',
};

function Root({ onOpen }: { onOpen: (s: Section) => void }) {
  const s = useStore((state) => state.settings);
  const rows: { id: Section; label: string; meta: string; icon: string }[] = [
    { id: 'display', label: 'Display', meta: `Brightness ${Math.round(s.brightness * 100)}%`, icon: 'sun' },
    { id: 'sound', label: 'Sound', meta: `${profileLabel(s.profile, s.language)}`, icon: 'volume' },
    { id: 'wallpaper', label: 'Wallpaper', meta: s.wallpaperId.startsWith('custom:') ? 'Custom image' : 'Built-in', icon: 'image' },
    { id: 'themes', label: 'Themes', meta: s.theme, icon: 'palette' },
    { id: 'home', label: 'Home screen', meta: `${s.dockRows} row${s.dockRows === 'one' ? '' : 's'}`, icon: 'grid' },
    { id: 'security', label: 'Security', meta: s.passwordHash ? 'Password set' : 'No password', icon: 'lock' },
    { id: 'datetime', label: 'Date & Time', meta: s.clock24 ? '24-hour' : '12-hour', icon: 'clock' },
    { id: 'language', label: 'Language', meta: s.language === 'ar' ? 'العربية' : 'English', icon: 'globe' },
    { id: 'battery', label: 'Battery', meta: s.powerSaver ? 'Power saver on' : 'Normal', icon: 'battery' },
    { id: 'storage', label: 'Storage', meta: 'IndexedDB usage', icon: 'folder' },
    { id: 'accessibility', label: 'Accessibility', meta: s.highContrast ? 'High contrast' : 'Standard', icon: 'eye' },
    { id: 'help', label: 'Quick help', meta: 'Keyboard map', icon: 'help' },
    { id: 'reset', label: 'Reset OS', meta: 'Wipes local data', icon: 'trash' },
    { id: 'about', label: 'About', meta: 'OWL OS 1.0', icon: 'info' },
  ];
  return (
    <div>
      <div style={{ padding: 8, display: 'flex', gap: 7, alignItems: 'center', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--line)' }}>
        <div style={{ width: 34, height: 34, borderRadius: '50%', background: 'linear-gradient(150deg,var(--panel-3),var(--panel))', display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center', border: '1px solid var(--line)' }}>
          <Icon name="contacts" size={17} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700 }}>{s.userName || 'OWL owner'}</div>
          <div className="tiny">OS 1.0 · all data stays on this device</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      {rows.map((r) => (
        <button key={r.id} className="rowBtn" onClick={() => onOpen(r.id)}>
          <Icon name={r.icon} size={12} />
          <span>{r.label}</span>
          <span className="meta">{r.meta}</span>
          <Icon name="arrowRight" size={8} style={{ opacity: 0.5 }} />
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

/* -------------------------------- Display -------------------------------- */

function Display() {
  const s = useStore((st) => st.settings);
  const set = useStore((st) => st.setSetting);
  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 8, display: 'grid', gap: 7 }}>
      <Slider label="Brightness" value={s.brightness} min={0.3} max={1} onChange={(v) => set('brightness', v)} format={(v) => `${Math.round(v * 100)}%`} />
      <div>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Backlight timeout</div>
        <div className="btnRow">
          {[15, 30, 60, 120, 0].map((v) => (
            <button key={v} className={`chip ${s.backlight === v ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => set('backlight', v)}>{v === 0 ? 'Never' : `${v}s`}</button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      <Slider label="Font size" value={s.fontScale} min={0.85} max={1.35} onChange={(v) => set('fontScale', v)} format={(v) => `${Math.round(v * 100)}%`} />
      <Slider label="UI scale" value={s.uiScale} min={0.85} max={1.15} onChange={(v) => set('uiScale', v)} format={(v) => `${Math.round(v * 100)}%`} />
      <div className="btnRow">
        <button className={`chip ${s.largeText ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => set('largeText', !s.largeText)}>Large text</button>
        <button className={`chip ${s.highContrast ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => set('highContrast', !s.highContrast)}>High contrast</button>
        <button className={`chip ${s.powerSaver ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => set('powerSaver', !s.powerSaver)}>Power saver</button>
      </div>
      <div className="tiny">Screen: 480 × 360 logical pixels (4:3), scaled to fit the device.</div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Slider({ label, value, min, max, onChange, format }: {
  label: string; value: number; min: number; max: number; onChange: (v: number) => void; format?: (v: number) => string;
}) {
  return (
    <div>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
        <span className="fieldLabel">{label}</span>
        <span className="tiny">{format ? format(value) : value}</span>
      </div>
      <input
        type="range" min={min} max={max} step={0.01} value={value}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(Number(e.target.value))}
        style={{ width: '100%', accentColor: 'var(--hl)' }}
        inputMode="none"
        aria-label={label}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

/* --------------------------------- Sound --------------------------------- */

function Sound() {
  const s = useStore((st) => st.settings);
  const set = useStore((st) => st.setSetting);
  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 8, display: 'grid', gap: 7 }}>
      <div>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Profile</div>
        <div className="btnRow">
          {(['normal', 'loud', 'vibrate', 'silent', 'meeting'] as const).map((p) => (
            <button key={p} className={`chip ${s.profile === p ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => set('profile', p)}>{profileLabel(p, s.language)}</button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      <Slider label="Volume" value={s.volume} min={0} max={1} onChange={(v) => set('volume', v)} format={(v) => `${Math.round(v * 100)}%`} />
      <Slider label="Ring volume" value={s.ringVolume} min={0} max={1} onChange={(v) => set('ringVolume', v)} format={(v) => `${Math.round(v * 100)}%`} />
      <Slider label="Alarm volume" value={s.alarmVolume} min={0} max={1} onChange={(v) => set('alarmVolume', v)} format={(v) => `${Math.round(v * 100)}%`} />
      <Slider label="Key click volume" value={s.keyClickVolume} min={0} max={1} onChange={(v) => set('keyClickVolume', v)} format={(v) => `${Math.round(v * 100)}%`} />
      <div>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Ringtone</div>
        <select
          value={s.ringtone}
          onChange={(e) => set('ringtone', e.target.value)}
          style={selectStyle}
        >
          {RINGTONES.map((r) => <option key={r.id} value={r.id}>{r.name}</option>)}
        </select>
        <button className="btn" style={{ marginTop: 3, fontSize: 9 }} onClick={() => playRingtone(s.ringtone, s.ringVolume, 2)}>Preview</button>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Message tone</div>
        <select value={s.messageTone} onChange={(e) => set('messageTone', e.target.value)} style={selectStyle}>
          {RINGTONES.map((r) => <option key={r.id} value={r.id}>{r.name}</option>)}
        </select>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Alarm tone</div>
        <select value={s.alarmTone} onChange={(e) => set('alarmTone', e.target.value)} style={selectStyle}>
          {RINGTONES.map((r) => <option key={r.id} value={r.id}>{r.name}</option>)}
        </select>
      </div>
      <div className="btnRow">
        <button className={`chip ${s.keyClick ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => set('keyClick', !s.keyClick)}>Key clicks</button>
        <button className={`chip ${s.vibrate ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => set('vibrate', !s.vibrate)}>Vibration</button>
        <button className={`chip ${s.vibrateKeys ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => set('vibrateKeys', !s.vibrateKeys)}>Vibrate on key</button>
      </div>
      <div className="tiny">All tones are synthesised live with the Web Audio API. No samples or copyrighted audio.</div>
    </div>
  );
}

const selectStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
  width: '100%', background: 'var(--panel-2)', color: 'var(--text)', border: '1px solid var(--line)',
  borderRadius: 4, padding: '3px 5px', fontSize: 10,
};

/* -------------------------------- Wallpaper ------------------------------- */

function Wallpaper() {
  const s = useStore((st) => st.settings);
  const update = useStore((st) => st.updateSettings);
  const bump = useStore((st) => st.bumpWallpaper);
  const toast = useStore((st) => st.toast);
  const [target, setTarget] = useState<'home' | 'lock'>('home');
  const [crop, setCrop] = useState<{ src: string; zoom: number; x: number; y: number } | null>(null);
  const toastRef = toast;
  const activeId = target === 'home' ? s.wallpaperId : s.lockWallpaperId;

  const upload = () => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file';
    input.accept = 'image/*';
    input.onchange = () => {
      const file = input.files?.[0];
      if (!file) return;
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = () => setCrop({ src: String(reader.result), zoom: 1, x: 0, y: 0 });
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    };
    input.click();
  };

  if (crop) {
    return (
      <div style={{ padding: 8, display: 'grid', gap: 6 }}>
        <div style={{ width: '100%', aspectRatio: '4 / 3', borderRadius: 6, overflow: 'hidden', border: '1px solid var(--hl)', background: '#000' }}>
          <img src={crop.src} alt="crop" style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'cover', transform: `scale(${crop.zoom}) translate(${crop.x}%, ${crop.y}%)` }} />
        </div>
        <Slider label="Zoom" value={crop.zoom} min={1} max={3} onChange={(v) => setCrop({ ...crop, zoom: v })} format={(v) => `${v.toFixed(2)}×`} />
        <Slider label="Horizontal" value={crop.x} min={-50} max={50} onChange={(v) => setCrop({ ...crop, x: v })} />
        <Slider label="Vertical" value={crop.y} min={-50} max={50} onChange={(v) => setCrop({ ...crop, y: v })} />
        <div className="btnRow" style={{ justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
          <button className="btn" onClick={() => setCrop(null)}>Cancel</button>
          <button
            className="btn primary"
            onClick={async () => {
              const data = await cropToWallpaper(crop.src, 960, 720, crop.zoom, crop.x, crop.y);
              const id = await saveCustomWallpaper(data);
              if (target === 'lock') update({ lockWallpaperId: id, useSeparateLockWallpaper: true });
              else update({ wallpaperId: id });
              bump();
              setCrop(null);
              toastRef('Wallpaper saved');
            }}
          >Crop & set</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 8, display: 'grid', gap: 6 }}>
      <div className="btnRow">
        <button className={`chip ${!s.useSeparateLockWallpaper ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => { update({ useSeparateLockWallpaper: false }); setTarget('home'); }}>Same for both</button>
        <button className={`chip ${s.useSeparateLockWallpaper ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => update({ useSeparateLockWallpaper: true })}>Separate lock wallpaper</button>
      </div>
      {s.useSeparateLockWallpaper && (
        <div className="btnRow">
          <button className={`chip ${target === 'home' ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => setTarget('home')}>Home</button>
          <button className={`chip ${target === 'lock' ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => setTarget('lock')}>Lock screen</button>
        </div>
      )}
      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(4, 1fr)', gap: 4 }}>
        {WALLPAPERS.map((w) => (
          <button
            key={w.id}
            onClick={() => {
              if (target === 'lock') update({ lockWallpaperId: w.id, useSeparateLockWallpaper: true });
              else update({ wallpaperId: w.id });
              bump();
              toastRef(`${w.name} set`);
            }}
            aria-label={w.name}
            style={{
              padding: 0, height: 40, borderRadius: 5, cursor: 'pointer',
              border: activeId === w.id ? '2px solid var(--hl)' : '1px solid var(--line)',
              backgroundImage: w.css, backgroundSize: 'cover',
            }}
          />
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="btnRow">
        <button className="btn primary" onClick={upload}>Upload & crop image</button>
      </div>
      <WallpaperPreview id={activeId} />
      <div className="tiny">Wallpapers are generated SVG or your own images, stored in IndexedDB. Nothing is uploaded.</div>
    </div>
  );
}

function WallpaperPreview({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const url = useWallpaperUrl(id);
  return (
    <div style={{ aspectRatio: '4 / 3', borderRadius: 6, border: '1px solid var(--line)', backgroundImage: url, backgroundSize: 'cover', backgroundPosition: 'center', display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center' }}>
      <span style={{ fontSize: 9, color: '#fff', textShadow: '0 1px 4px #000', background: 'rgba(0,0,0,.35)', padding: '1px 6px', borderRadius: 99 }}>
        Preview · 4:3
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

/* --------------------------------- Themes -------------------------------- */

function ThemeSection() {
  const current = useStore((s) => s.settings.theme);
  const set = useStore((s) => s.setSetting);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const themes: ThemeId[] = ['owl-night', 'classic-silver', 'forest', 'ruby', 'ocean', 'lcd'];
  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 8, display: 'grid', gap: 4 }}>
      {themes.map((t) => (
        <button key={t} className="rowBtn" onClick={() => { set('theme', t); toast(`${t} applied`); }}>
          <span style={{ width: 14, height: 14, borderRadius: 3, background: `linear-gradient(140deg,var(--panel-2),var(--hl))`, border: '1px solid var(--line)' }} />
          <span style={{ textTransform: 'capitalize' }}>{t.replace('-', ' ')}</span>
          {current === t && <span className="pill amber meta">active</span>}
        </button>
      ))}
      <button className="btn" onClick={() => useStore.getState().openApp('themes')}>Open full theme gallery</button>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------ Home screen ------------------------------ */

function HomeSection() {
  const s = useStore((st) => st.settings);
  const update = useStore((st) => st.updateSettings);
  const toast = useStore((st) => st.toast);
  const [editingHotkeys, setEditingHotkeys] = useState(false);
  const [hotkeyDraft, setHotkeyDraft] = useState(s.hotkeys);

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 8, display: 'grid', gap: 7 }}>
      <div>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Shortcut rows</div>
        <div className="btnRow">
          {(['one', 'two', 'hidden'] as const).map((r) => (
            <button key={r} className={`chip ${s.dockRows === r ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => update({ dockRows: r })}>
              {r === 'one' ? '1 row' : r === 'two' ? '2 rows' : 'Hidden'}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Favorites ({s.favorites.length})</div>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 3 }}>
          {APPS.slice(0, 24).map((a) => (
            <button
              key={a.id}
              className={`chip ${s.favorites.includes(a.id) ? 'on' : ''}`}
              style={{ fontSize: 8 }}
              onClick={() => update({ favorites: s.favorites.includes(a.id) ? s.favorites.filter((f) => f !== a.id) : [...s.favorites, a.id] })}
            >
              {a.name[s.language] || a.name.en}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Hotkeys</div>
        <button className="btn" onClick={() => setEditingHotkeys((v) => !v)}>{editingHotkeys ? 'Done' : 'Edit hotkeys'}</button>
        {(editingHotkeys ? Object.entries(hotkeyDraft) : Object.entries(s.hotkeys).slice(0, 6)).map(([key, appId]) => (
          <div key={key} style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4, alignItems: 'center', padding: '1px 0' }}>
            <span className="mono" style={{ width: 16, textAlign: 'center', background: 'var(--panel-2)', borderRadius: 3, border: '1px solid var(--line)', fontSize: 9 }}>
              {key.toUpperCase()}
            </span>
            <select
              value={appId}
              onChange={(e) => {
                if (editingHotkeys) setHotkeyDraft((prev) => ({ ...prev, [key]: e.target.value }));
                else update({ hotkeys: { ...s.hotkeys, [key]: e.target.value } });
              }}
              style={{ ...selectStyle, flex: 1, fontSize: 9 }}
              disabled={!editingHotkeys}
            >
              {APPS.map((a) => <option key={a.id} value={a.id}>{a.name[s.language] || a.name.en}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>
        ))}
        {editingHotkeys && (
          <button className="btn primary" style={{ marginTop: 3 }} onClick={() => { update({ hotkeys: hotkeyDraft }); setEditingHotkeys(false); toast('Hotkeys saved'); }}>
            Save hotkeys
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
      <div>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Hidden apps ({s.hiddenApps.length})</div>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 3 }}>
          {s.hiddenApps.length === 0 && <span className="tiny">None hidden. Use Menu on an app icon to hide it.</span>}
          {s.hiddenApps.map((id) => (
            <button key={id} className="chip" onClick={() => update({ hiddenApps: s.hiddenApps.filter((x) => x !== id) })}>
              {appName(id, s.language)} ✕
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Folders ({s.folders.length})</div>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 3 }}>
          {s.folders.map((f) => (
            <span key={f.id} className="chip">{f.name} · {f.apps.length}</span>
          ))}
          {s.folders.length === 0 && <span className="tiny">Create folders from Menu in the app drawer.</span>}
        </div>
      </div>
      <button className="btn" onClick={() => update({ appOrder: [] })}>Reset icon order</button>
    </div>
  );
}

/* -------------------------------- Security ------------------------------- */

function Security() {
  const s = useStore((st) => st.settings);
  const update = useStore((st) => st.updateSettings);
  const toast = useStore((st) => st.toast);
  const showDialog = useStore((st) => st.showDialog);
  const lock = useStore((st) => st.lock);
  const [oldPass, setOldPass] = useState('');
  const [pass, setPass] = useState('');
  const [pass2, setPass2] = useState('');
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'idle' | 'set' | 'change'>('idle');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const savePassword = async () => {
    if (mode === 'change') {
      const ok = await verifyPassword(oldPass, s.passwordSalt, s.passwordHash);
      if (!ok) { setError('Current password is wrong'); return; }
    }
    if (pass.length < 4) { setError('Use 4–12 characters'); return; }
    if (pass.length > 12) { setError('Maximum 12 characters'); return; }
    if (pass !== pass2) { setError('Passwords do not match'); return; }
    const salt = randomSalt();
    const hash = await hashPassword(pass, salt);
    update({ passwordHash: hash, passwordSalt: salt, passwordIsPin: /^\d+$/.test(pass) });
    setMode('idle');
    setPass(''); setPass2(''); setOldPass(''); setError('');
    successChime();
    toast('Password saved (PBKDF2 hash)');
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 8, display: 'grid', gap: 7 }}>
      <div className="card" style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, alignItems: 'center' }}>
        <Icon name="shield" size={18} />
        <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700 }}>{s.passwordHash ? 'Keyboard is locked on boot' : 'No password set'}</div>
          <div className="tiny">{s.passwordHash ? `Stored as a salted PBKDF2 hash${s.passwordIsPin ? ' (PIN)' : ''}` : 'Press End to lock at any time'}</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {(mode === 'set' || mode === 'change') ? (
        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 3 }}>
          {mode === 'change' && (
            <>
              <div className="fieldLabel">Current password</div>
              <Field value={oldPass} onChange={setOldPass} kind="password" placeholder="Current password" autoFocus maxLength={12} />
            </>
          )}
          <div className="fieldLabel">New password or PIN (4–12)</div>
          <Field value={pass} onChange={setPass} kind="password" placeholder="New password" autoFocus={mode === 'set'} maxLength={12} />
          <Field value={pass2} onChange={setPass2} kind="password" placeholder="Repeat new password" maxLength={12} onSubmit={() => void savePassword()} />
          {error && <div className="tiny" style={{ color: 'var(--red)' }}>{error}</div>}
          <div className="btnRow">
            <button className="btn" onClick={() => { setMode('idle'); setError(''); }}>Cancel</button>
            <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => void savePassword()}>Save password</button>
          </div>
          <div className="tiny">5 wrong attempts trigger a 10-second cool-down. Forgot it? Reset OS (wipes data).</div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="btnRow">
          <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => setMode(s.passwordHash ? 'change' : 'set')}>
            {s.passwordHash ? 'Change password' : 'Set password'}
          </button>
          {s.passwordHash && (
            <button
              className="btn danger"
              onClick={() => showDialog({
                title: 'Remove password?',
                body: 'The lock screen will stop asking for a password.',
                confirmLabel: 'Remove', danger: true,
                onConfirm: () => { update({ passwordHash: '', passwordSalt: '', passwordIsPin: false }); toast('Password removed'); },
              })}
            >Remove password</button>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      <div>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Auto-lock</div>
        <div className="btnRow">
          {[30, 60, 120, 300, 0].map((v) => (
            <button key={v} className={`chip ${s.autoLock === v ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => update({ autoLock: v })}>
              {v === 0 ? 'Never' : v < 60 ? `${v}s` : `${v / 60}m`}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      <button className="btn" onClick={lock}>Lock keyboard now</button>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------- Date & Time ----------------------------- */

function DateTime() {
  const s = useStore((st) => st.settings);
  const set = useStore((st) => st.setSetting);
  const [zones, setZones] = useState<string[]>(['local']);
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const anyIntl = Intl as any;
      if (typeof anyIntl.supportedValuesOf === 'function') setZones(['local', ...anyIntl.supportedValuesOf('timeZone')]);
      else setZones(['local', 'UTC', 'Africa/Cairo', 'Europe/London', 'America/New_York', 'Asia/Tokyo']);
    } catch { /* ignore */ }
  }, []);
  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 8, display: 'grid', gap: 7 }}>
      <div>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Clock format</div>
        <div className="btnRow">
          <button className={`chip ${!s.clock24 ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => set('clock24', false)}>12-hour</button>
          <button className={`chip ${s.clock24 ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => set('clock24', true)}>24-hour</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Time zone</div>
        <select value={s.timezone} onChange={(e) => set('timezone', e.target.value)} style={selectStyle}>
          {zones.map((z) => <option key={z} value={z}>{z === 'local' ? 'Device time zone' : z}</option>)}
        </select>
      </div>
      <div className="tiny">
        The clock follows your device's time; the time-zone list is used by the world clock and time formatting.
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* -------------------------------- Language ------------------------------- */

function Language() {
  const s = useStore((st) => st.settings);
  const set = useStore((st) => st.setSetting);
  const toast = useStore((st) => st.toast);
  const choose = (lang: Lang) => {
    set('language', lang);
    toast(lang === 'ar' ? 'تم التبديل إلى العربية' : 'Switched to English');
  };
  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 8, display: 'grid', gap: 5 }}>
      <button className="rowBtn" onClick={() => choose('en')} style={{ border: s.language === 'en' ? '1px solid var(--hl)' : undefined, borderRadius: 5 }}>
        <span>English</span><span className="meta">Left to right</span>
      </button>
      <button className="rowBtn" onClick={() => choose('ar')} style={{ border: s.language === 'ar' ? '1px solid var(--hl)' : undefined, borderRadius: 5, direction: 'rtl' }}>
        <span>العربية</span><span className="meta">من اليمين إلى اليسار</span>
      </button>
      <div className="tiny">
        App names, labels and numbers switch locale. Dates and times use Intl for the active language.
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* --------------------------------- Battery -------------------------------- */

function Battery() {
  const s = useStore((st) => st.settings);
  const battery = useStore((st) => st.battery);
  const set = useStore((st) => st.setSetting);
  const pct = Math.round(battery.level * 100);
  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 8, display: 'grid', gap: 7 }}>
      <div className="card" style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 4 }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 7 }}>
          <div style={{ width: 42, height: 20, border: '2px solid var(--text)', borderRadius: 4, padding: 2, position: 'relative' }}>
            <div style={{ width: `${pct}%`, height: '100%', background: pct < 20 ? 'var(--red)' : 'var(--green)', borderRadius: 1 }} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700 }}>{pct}%</div>
            <div className="tiny">{battery.charging ? 'Charging' : 'On battery'} · simulated drain</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="tiny">Uses the Battery Status API when available, otherwise drains ~1% per minute of use.</div>
      </div>
      <div className="btnRow">
        <button className={`chip ${s.powerSaver ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => set('powerSaver', !s.powerSaver)}>Power saver</button>
        <button className="btn" onClick={() => set('brightness', 0.5)}>Dim to 50%</button>
      </div>
      <div className="tiny">Power saver dims the screen and lowers saturation. Music and alarms keep running.</div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* --------------------------------- Storage ------------------------------- */

function Storage() {
  const [estimate, setEstimate] = useState({ usage: 0, quota: 0 });
  const [keys, setKeys] = useState<number>(0);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const showDialog = useStore((s) => s.showDialog);
  useEffect(() => {
    void dbEstimate().then(setEstimate);
    void import('../../os/db').then(({ dbKeys }) => dbKeys().then((k) => setKeys(k.length)));
  }, []);
  const pct = estimate.quota ? Math.min(100, (estimate.usage / estimate.quota) * 100) : 0;
  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 8, display: 'grid', gap: 7 }}>
      <div>
        <div className="fieldLabel">IndexedDB usage</div>
        <div className="progress"><i style={{ width: `${Math.max(2, pct)}%` }} /></div>
        <div className="tiny" style={{ marginTop: 2 }}>{formatBytes(estimate.usage)} used of {formatBytes(estimate.quota)} · {keys} keys</div>
      </div>
      <div className="card tiny" style={{ lineHeight: 1.55 }}>
        Photos, wallpapers, voice notes, notes, contacts, scores and settings live here. Clearing
        local data removes them permanently — nothing is stored on a server.
      </div>
      <button
        className="btn danger"
        onClick={() => showDialog({
          title: 'Clear local data?',
          body: 'All apps, wallpapers, notes, photos and passwords stored in this browser will be deleted.',
          confirmLabel: 'Clear everything', danger: true,
          onConfirm: async () => {
            await dbClear();
            localStorage.clear();
            toast('Local data cleared — reloading');
            window.setTimeout(() => window.location.reload(), 800);
          },
        })}
      >Clear local data</button>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------ Accessibility ---------------------------- */

function Accessibility() {
  const s = useStore((st) => st.settings);
  const update = useStore((st) => st.updateSettings);
  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 8, display: 'grid', gap: 6 }}>
      <button className={`rowBtn ${s.highContrast ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => update({ highContrast: !s.highContrast })}>
        <Icon name="eye" size={12} />
        <span>High contrast</span>
        <span className="meta">{s.highContrast ? 'On' : 'Off'}</span>
      </button>
      <button className="rowBtn" onClick={() => update({ largeText: !s.largeText })}>
        <Icon name="memo" size={12} />
        <span>Large text</span>
        <span className="meta">{s.largeText ? 'On' : 'Off'}</span>
      </button>
      <button className="rowBtn" onClick={() => update({ reduceMotion: !s.reduceMotion })}>
        <Icon name="sun" size={12} />
        <span>Reduce motion</span>
        <span className="meta">{s.reduceMotion ? 'On' : 'Off'}</span>
      </button>
      <div className="tiny">
        The OS respects <b>prefers-reduced-motion</b> from your system too. Every control is reachable
        with the arrow keys, Enter and Back, and screens carry ARIA labels for screen readers.
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------------------------------- Reset -------------------------------- */

function Reset({ onDone }: { onDone: () => void }) {
  const showDialog = useStore((s) => s.showDialog);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const confirmReset = () => {
    showDialog({
      title: 'Reset OWL OS?',
      body: 'This wipes every setting and all local data on this device. Are you sure?',
      confirmLabel: 'Continue', danger: true,
      onConfirm: () => {
        showDialog({
          title: 'Last confirmation',
          body: 'There is no undo. Export anything important first.',
          confirmLabel: 'Erase everything', danger: true,
          onConfirm: async () => {
            await dbClear();
            try { localStorage.clear(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
            toast('OS reset — restarting');
            onDone();
            window.setTimeout(() => { window.location.hash = ''; window.location.reload(); }, 700);
          },
        });
      },
    });
  };
  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 8, display: 'grid', gap: 7 }}>
      <div className="card tiny" style={{ lineHeight: 1.55 }}>
        Reset returns the phone to factory state: the setup wizard runs again and all notes,
        contacts, photos, passwords and settings are removed from this browser.
      </div>
      <button className="btn danger" onClick={confirmReset}>Reset OS…</button>
      <button
        className="btn"
        onClick={() => {
          useStore.getState().updateSettings(defaultSettings());
          useStore.getState().toast('Settings restored to defaults');
        }}
      >
        Restore default settings only
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------------------------------- About -------------------------------- */

function About() {
  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 8, display: 'grid', gap: 5 }}>
      <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '4px 0' }}>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 34 }}>🦉</div>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)', fontSize: 20, letterSpacing: '0.3em' }}>OWL</div>
        <div className="tiny">OS 1.0 “Retro Edition”</div>
      </div>
      <div className="card tiny" style={{ lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
        <b>Privacy:</b> Your files, photos, and passwords never leave your device. There is no
        tracking, no analytics and no cookies. The only network call this app makes on its own is
        the reader-mode proxy for pages you explicitly open.
        <hr className="sep" />
        <b>Credits:</b> React, Vite, Zustand, idb-keyval, jsmediatags. Icons and device artwork are
        original. All sounds are synthesised with the Web Audio API.
        <hr className="sep" />
        Device design inspired by classic QWERTY phones of 2010 — no third-party trademarks used.
      </div>
      <div className="kv"><b>Screen</b><span>480 × 360</span></div>
      <div className="kv"><b>Version</b><span>1.0.0</span></div>
      <div className="kv"><b>Storage</b><span>IndexedDB + localStorage</span></div>
      <div className="kv"><b>Source</b><span>github.com/aamrabdelhay/owl</span></div>
      <div className="tiny" style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 4 }}>
        Made for keyboard people. Hold a letter on the home screen to launch its hotkey.
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------------------------------- Help --------------------------------- */

function Help() {
  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 8, display: 'grid', gap: 5 }}>
      <div className="owlSection" style={{ padding: 0 }}>Keyboard map</div>
      {SHORTCUT_LEGEND.map(([k, d]) => (
        <div key={k} className="kv"><b className="mono">{k}</b><span style={{ textAlign: 'right' }}>{d}</span></div>
      ))}
      <div className="owlSection" style={{ padding: '6px 0 0' }}>Trackpad</div>
      <div className="tiny">Drag in any direction to move the highlight, click to select. Arrow keys do the same.</div>
      <div className="owlSection" style={{ padding: '6px 0 0' }}>Menus</div>
      <div className="tiny">Menu opens the app drawer on the home screen and the context menu inside apps. Hold Menu for recent apps. Hold Enter (or click and hold the trackpad) for long-press actions.</div>
      <div className="owlSection" style={{ padding: '6px 0 0' }}>Power</div>
      <div className="tiny">Short press End locks the phone. Hold End for one second for Lock / Restart / Shutdown.</div>
      <button className="btn" onClick={() => useStore.getState().openApp('setup')} style={{ marginTop: 4 }}>Re-run setup wizard</button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { OwlLogo } from '../../components/OwlLogo';
import { Field } from '../../components/Field';
import { WALLPAPERS } from '../../assets/wallpapers';
import { hashPassword, randomSalt } from '../../os/security';
import { successChime } from '../../os/audio';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';

const STEPS = ['Welcome', 'Language', 'Your name', 'Wallpaper', 'Password', 'Done'];

export default function SetupApp() {
  const settings = useStore((s) => s.settings);
  const updateSettings = useStore((s) => s.updateSettings);
  const closeApp = useStore((s) => s.closeApp);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const [step, setStep] = useState(0);
  const [name, setName] = useState(settings.userName);
  const [pass, setPass] = useState('');
  const [pass2, setPass2] = useState('');
  const [wp, setWp] = useState(settings.wallpaperId);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const next = () => setStep((s) => Math.min(STEPS.length - 1, s + 1));
  const back = () => setStep((s) => Math.max(0, s - 1));

  const finishPassword = async () => {
    if (!pass) { next(); return; }
    if (pass.length < 4) { setError('Use at least 4 characters'); return; }
    if (pass !== pass2) { setError('Passwords do not match'); return; }
    const salt = randomSalt();
    const hash = await hashPassword(pass, salt);
    updateSettings({ passwordHash: hash, passwordSalt: salt, passwordIsPin: /^\d+$/.test(pass) });
    setError('');
    next();
  };

  return (
    <AppFrame
      title="Setup Wizard"
      icon="wand"
      actions={<span className="tiny">{step + 1}/{STEPS.length}</span>}
      menu={[
        { id: 'next', label: 'Next step', icon: 'arrowRight', run: next },
        { id: 'back', label: 'Previous step', icon: 'arrowLeft', run: back },
        { id: 'skip', label: 'Skip to the end', icon: 'check', run: () => setStep(STEPS.length - 1) },
      ]}
    >
      <div className="appBody pad" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 8 }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 3 }}>
          {STEPS.map((s, i) => (
            <div key={s} style={{ flex: 1, height: 3, borderRadius: 2, background: i <= step ? 'var(--hl)' : 'var(--line)' }} />
          ))}
        </div>

        {step === 0 && (
          <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '6px 4px', display: 'grid', gap: 6, placeItems: 'center' }}>
            <OwlLogo size={54} glow />
            <div style={{ fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)', fontSize: 22, letterSpacing: '0.28em' }}>OWL</div>
            <div className="tiny" style={{ maxWidth: 300, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
              Welcome to your new phone. This wizard sets up language, wallpaper and an optional
              lock password. Everything is stored on this device only.
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 1 && (
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 6 }}>
            <div className="fieldLabel">Language / اللغة</div>
            {([['en', 'English', 'Left to right'], ['ar', 'العربية', 'من اليمين إلى اليسار (RTL)']] as const).map(([code, label, hint]) => (
              <button
                key={code}
                className="rowBtn"
                onClick={() => updateSettings({ language: code })}
                style={{ border: settings.language === code ? '1px solid var(--hl)' : undefined, borderRadius: 5 }}
              >
                <span>{label}</span>
                <span className="meta">{hint}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 2 && (
          <div>
            <div className="fieldLabel">What should the phone call you?</div>
            <Field value={name} onChange={setName} placeholder="Your name" autoFocus maxLength={24} onSubmit={next} />
            <div className="tiny" style={{ marginTop: 4 }}>Shown on the home screen. Optional.</div>
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 3 && (
          <div>
            <div className="fieldLabel">Pick a wallpaper</div>
            <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(4, 1fr)', gap: 4 }}>
              {WALLPAPERS.map((w) => (
                <button
                  key={w.id}
                  onClick={() => setWp(w.id)}
                  aria-label={w.name}
                  style={{
                    height: 42, borderRadius: 5, border: wp === w.id ? '2px solid var(--hl)' : '1px solid var(--line)',
                    backgroundImage: w.css, backgroundSize: 'cover', cursor: 'pointer', padding: 0,
                  }}
                />
              ))}
            </div>
            <div className="tiny" style={{ marginTop: 4 }}>You can add your own photo later in Settings › Wallpaper.</div>
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 4 && (
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 4 }}>
            <div className="fieldLabel">Lock password (optional)</div>
            <Field value={pass} onChange={setPass} kind="password" placeholder="Password or PIN (4–12)" maxLength={12} autoFocus />
            <Field value={pass2} onChange={setPass2} kind="password" placeholder="Repeat password" maxLength={12} onSubmit={() => void finishPassword()} />
            {error && <div className="tiny" style={{ color: 'var(--red)' }}>{error}</div>}
            <div className="tiny">Stored as a salted PBKDF2 hash — never as plain text. You can remove it any time.</div>
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 5 && (
          <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', display: 'grid', gap: 5, placeItems: 'center' }}>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 26 }}>✓</div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700 }}>You're all set{name ? `, ${name}` : ''}.</div>
            <div className="tiny" style={{ maxWidth: 300 }}>
              Hold <b>Menu</b> for recent apps. Type any letter on the home screen to search.
              Press <b>Esc</b> to go back, <b>F1</b> for the menu.
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div style={{ marginTop: 'auto', display: 'flex', gap: 5 }}>
          {step > 0 && step < 5 && <button className="btn" onClick={back}>Back</button>}
          <div style={{ flex: 1 }} />
          {step < 4 && <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => { updateSettings({ wallpaperId: wp }); next(); }}>Next</button>}
          {step === 4 && (
            <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => void finishPassword()}>
              {pass ? 'Set password' : 'Skip'}
            </button>
          )}
          {step === 5 && (
            <button
              className="btn primary"
              onClick={() => {
                updateSettings({ onboarded: true, userName: name, wallpaperId: wp });
                successChime();
                toast('Welcome to OWL');
                closeApp();
              }}
            >
              Start using OWL
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
        {step === 5 && (
          <button className="btn" onClick={() => closeApp()} style={{ fontSize: 9 }}>
            <Icon name="back" size={9} /> Go to home screen
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}
```

### `src/apps/Tasks/TasksApp.tsx`

_10634 bytes · 204 lines_

```tsx
import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { useCollection, uid } from '../../hooks';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { Field } from '../../components/Field';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';
import { DB_KEYS } from '../../os/db';
import type { TaskItem, CalEvent } from '../../os/types';

const STATUSES: TaskItem['status'][] = ['not-started', 'in-progress', 'completed'];
const STATUS_LABEL: Record<TaskItem['status'], string> = {
  'not-started': 'Not started', 'in-progress': 'In progress', completed: 'Completed',
};

export default function TasksApp() {
  const [tasks, setTasks] = useCollection<TaskItem>(DB_KEYS.tasks, [
    { id: 't-welcome', title: 'Try Owl Snake', notes: 'Games › Owl Snake', priority: 'normal', status: 'not-started', category: 'Play', createdAt: Date.now() },
    { id: 't-setup', title: 'Set a lock password', notes: 'Settings › Security', priority: 'high', status: 'not-started', category: 'Setup', createdAt: Date.now() - 1000 },
  ]);
  const [events] = useCollection<CalEvent>(DB_KEYS.events, []);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<TaskItem | null>(null);
  const [hideCompleted, setHideCompleted] = useState(false);
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<'all' | 'today' | 'overdue' | 'open'>('all');
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const showDialog = useStore((s) => s.showDialog);

  const todayStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const visible = useMemo(() => {
    return tasks
      .filter((t) => (hideCompleted ? t.status !== 'completed' : true))
      .filter((t) => {
        if (filter === 'today') return t.due === todayStr;
        if (filter === 'overdue') return !!t.due && t.due < todayStr && t.status !== 'completed';
        if (filter === 'open') return t.status !== 'completed';
        return true;
      })
      .sort((a, b) => {
        const order = { high: 0, normal: 1, low: 2 } as const;
        if (a.status === 'completed' && b.status !== 'completed') return 1;
        if (b.status === 'completed' && a.status !== 'completed') return -1;
        if (a.due && b.due) return a.due.localeCompare(b.due);
        if (a.due) return -1;
        if (b.due) return 1;
        return order[a.priority] - order[b.priority];
      });
  }, [tasks, hideCompleted, filter, todayStr]);

  const save = (task: TaskItem) => {
    const exists = tasks.some((t) => t.id === task.id);
    setTasks((prev) => (exists ? prev.map((t) => (t.id === task.id ? task : t)) : [task, ...prev]));
    setEditing(null);
    toast(exists ? 'Task updated' : 'Task added');
  };

  const cycle = (task: TaskItem) => {
    const idx = STATUSES.indexOf(task.status);
    const next = STATUSES[(idx + 1) % STATUSES.length];
    setTasks((prev) => prev.map((t) => (t.id === task.id ? { ...t, status: next } : t)));
  };

  const addToCalendar = (task: TaskItem) => {
    if (!task.due) { toast('Give the task a due date first'); return; }
    const start = new Date(`${task.due}T09:00:00`).getTime();
    const exists = events.some((e) => e.taskId === task.id);
    if (exists) { toast('Already in the calendar'); return; }
    useStore.getState().openApp('calendar');
    // Persist via the collection store used by Calendar
    void import('../../os/db').then(async ({ collectionGet, collectionSet }) => {
      const list = await collectionGet<CalEvent>('events');
      await collectionSet('events', [...(list || []), {
        id: uid('ev'), title: task.title, location: '', notes: task.notes, start, end: start + 1800000,
        allDay: false, reminderMinutes: 10, repeat: 'none', color: '#4cc38a', taskId: task.id,
      }]);
      toast('Added to calendar');
    });
  };

  if (editing) {
    return <TaskEditor task={editing} onSave={save} onCancel={() => setEditing(null)} onDelete={(id) => { setTasks((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== id)); setEditing(null); }} />;
  }

  return (
    <AppFrame
      title="Tasks"
      icon="task"
      menu={[
        { id: 'new', label: 'New task', icon: 'plus', run: () => setEditing(emptyTask()) },
        { id: 'hide', label: hideCompleted ? 'Show completed' : 'Hide completed', icon: 'check', run: () => setHideCompleted((h) => !h) },
        { id: 'calendar', label: 'Jump to calendar', icon: 'calendar', run: () => useStore.getState().openApp('calendar') },
      ]}
      actions={
        <>
          <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => setEditing(emptyTask())}><Icon name="plus" size={9} /> New</button>
          <button className="hbtn" onClick={() => setHideCompleted((h) => !h)}>{hideCompleted ? 'Show done' : 'Hide done'}</button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <div className="chipRow" style={{ padding: 4 }}>
        {(['all', 'today', 'overdue', 'open'] as const).map((f) => (
          <button key={f} className={`chip ${filter === f ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => setFilter(f)}>
            {f === 'all' ? 'All' : f === 'today' ? 'Due today' : f === 'overdue' ? 'Overdue' : 'Open'}
          </button>
        ))}
        <span className="tiny" style={{ marginLeft: 'auto' }}>
          {tasks.filter((t) => t.status !== 'completed').length} open
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="appBody">
        {visible.length === 0 && (
          <div className="empty"><div className="big">✓</div><div>Nothing to do here.</div></div>
        )}
        {visible.map((t) => (
          <div key={t.id} className="rowBtn" style={{ cursor: 'default', opacity: t.status === 'completed' ? 0.6 : 1 }}>
            <button
              className="iconBtn"
              onClick={() => cycle(t)}
              aria-label={`Status: ${STATUS_LABEL[t.status]}. Press to change.`}
              style={{
                borderColor: t.status === 'completed' ? 'var(--green)' : t.status === 'in-progress' ? 'var(--hl)' : 'var(--line)',
                color: t.status === 'completed' ? 'var(--green)' : 'inherit',
              }}
            >
              {t.status === 'completed' ? '✓' : t.status === 'in-progress' ? '◐' : '○'}
            </button>
            <button className="rowBtn" style={{ padding: 0, border: 0, flex: 1 }} onClick={() => setEditing(t)}>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'flex-start' }}>
                <span style={{ textDecoration: t.status === 'completed' ? 'line-through' : undefined, fontWeight: 600 }}>{t.title}</span>
                <span className="tiny">
                  {t.category}
                  {t.due ? ` · due ${t.due}` : ''}
                  {t.due && t.due < todayStr && t.status !== 'completed' ? ' · overdue' : ''}
                </span>
              </div>
            </button>
            <span className={`pill ${t.priority === 'high' ? 'red' : t.priority === 'low' ? '' : 'amber'}`}>{t.priority}</span>
            <button className="iconBtn" onClick={() => addToCalendar(t)} aria-label="Add to calendar" title="Add to calendar">▦</button>
            <button className="iconBtn" onClick={() => showDialog({
              title: 'Delete task?', body: t.title, confirmLabel: 'Delete', danger: true,
              onConfirm: () => setTasks((prev) => prev.filter((x) => x.id !== t.id)),
            })} aria-label="Delete task">✕</button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}

function emptyTask(): TaskItem {
  return { id: uid('task'), title: '', notes: '', priority: 'normal', status: 'not-started', category: 'General', createdAt: Date.now() };
}

function TaskEditor({ task, onSave, onCancel, onDelete }: {
  task: TaskItem; onSave: (t: TaskItem) => void; onCancel: () => void; onDelete: (id: string) => void;
}) {
  const [t, setT] = useState(task);
  const [newCat, setNewCat] = useState('');
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  return (
    <AppFrame title={task.title ? 'Edit task' : 'New task'} icon="task" actions={<button className="hbtn" onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</button>}>
      <div className="appBody pad" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 3 }}>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Task</div>
        <Field value={t.title} onChange={(v) => setT({ ...t, title: v })} placeholder="What needs doing?" autoFocus maxLength={100} />
        <div className="fieldLabel">Due date</div>
        <input
          type="date" value={t.due || ''} onChange={(e) => setT({ ...t, due: e.target.value })}
          style={{ background: 'rgba(0,0,0,.28)', border: '1px solid var(--line)', color: 'var(--text)', borderRadius: 4, padding: '2px 5px', fontSize: 10, colorScheme: 'dark' }}
        />
        <div className="fieldLabel">Priority</div>
        <div className="btnRow">
          {(['low', 'normal', 'high'] as const).map((p) => (
            <button key={p} className={`chip ${t.priority === p ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => setT({ ...t, priority: p })}>{p}</button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Status</div>
        <div className="btnRow">
          {STATUSES.map((s) => (
            <button key={s} className={`chip ${t.status === s ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => setT({ ...t, status: s })}>{STATUS_LABEL[s]}</button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Category</div>
        <div className="chipRow" style={{ padding: 0 }}>
          {['General', 'Work', 'Home', 'Play'].map((c) => (
            <button key={c} className={`chip ${t.category === c ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => setT({ ...t, category: c })}>{c}</button>
          ))}
          <input
            value={newCat} onChange={(e) => setNewCat(e.target.value)} placeholder="+ new" inputMode="none"
            style={{ width: 54, fontSize: 9, background: 'rgba(0,0,0,.3)', border: '1px solid var(--line)', color: 'var(--text)', borderRadius: 99, padding: '1px 6px' }}
            onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter' && newCat.trim()) { setT({ ...t, category: newCat.trim() }); setNewCat(''); } }}
          />
        </div>
        <div className="fieldLabel">Notes</div>
        <Field value={t.notes} onChange={(v) => setT({ ...t, notes: v })} placeholder="Optional notes" multiline maxLength={800} />
        <div className="btnRow" style={{ marginTop: 'auto', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
          {task.title && <button className="btn danger" onClick={() => onDelete(task.id)}>Delete</button>}
          <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => {
            if (!t.title.trim()) { toast('Add a task title'); return; }
            onSave({ ...t, title: t.title.trim() });
          }}>Save task</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';
import { OwlLogo } from '../../components/OwlLogo';
import type { ThemeId } from '../../os/types';

const THEMES: { id: ThemeId; name: string; blurb: string; swatch: string[]; font: string }[] = [
  { id: 'owl-night', name: 'Owl Night', blurb: 'Default dark navy with amber eyes', swatch: ['#0b1729', '#f6b93f', '#17304f'], font: 'Inter' },
  { id: 'classic-silver', name: 'Classic Silver', blurb: 'Bright chrome, blue highlights', swatch: ['#eef1f6', '#2a6fdb', '#b6c2d3'], font: 'Inter' },
  { id: 'forest', name: 'Forest Green', blurb: 'Deep evergreen, mint accents', swatch: ['#0b2a1d', '#63d29b', '#14543a'], font: 'Inter' },
  { id: 'ruby', name: 'Ruby', blurb: 'Crimson body, rose highlights', swatch: ['#2a0b17', '#ff5e7d', '#571630'], font: 'Rajdhani' },
  { id: 'ocean', name: 'Ocean Blue', blurb: 'Midnight sea, cyan glow', swatch: ['#072033', '#45c8ff', '#0f456b'], font: 'Rajdhani' },
  { id: 'lcd', name: 'Retro LCD', blurb: 'Monochrome green pixel display', swatch: ['#b9c9a4', '#33471f', '#93a17f'], font: 'Press Start 2P' },
];

export default function ThemesApp() {
  const current = useStore((s) => s.settings.theme);
  const setSetting = useStore((s) => s.setSetting);
  const togglePreview = useStore((s) => s.toggleThemePreview);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const preview = useStore((s) => s.themePreview);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<ThemeId | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => () => togglePreview(null), [togglePreview]);

  return (
    <AppFrame
      menu={[
        { id: 'reset', label: 'Cancel preview', icon: 'repeat', run: () => { togglePreview(null); setSelected(null); } },
        { id: 'apply', label: 'Apply selected theme', icon: 'check', run: () => { if (selected) { setSetting('theme', selected); togglePreview(null); toast('Theme applied'); } } },
        { id: 'exit', label: 'Exit', icon: 'close', run: () => useStore.getState().closeApp() },
      ]}
      title="Themes"
      icon="palette"
      actions={<span className="tiny">preview → apply</span>}
    >
      <div className="appBody pad" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 5 }}>
        <div className="card" style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 7, alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <OwlLogo size={28} />
          <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: 700 }}>Make it yours</div>
            <div className="tiny">A theme recolours the OS, icons, menus and fonts. The phone body stays chrome and graphite.</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 5 }}>
          {THEMES.map((t) => (
            <div
              key={t.id}
              className="card"
              style={{
                padding: 5, display: 'grid', gap: 3,
                borderColor: current === t.id ? 'var(--hl)' : undefined,
                outline: preview === t.id ? '1px dashed var(--hl)' : undefined,
              }}
            >
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', height: 26, borderRadius: 4, overflow: 'hidden', border: '1px solid var(--line)' }}>
                {t.swatch.map((c) => <span key={c} style={{ flex: 1, background: c }} />)}
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 3 }}>
                <strong style={{ fontSize: 10.5, fontFamily: t.font === 'Press Start 2P' ? 'Press Start 2P' : undefined }}>{t.name}</strong>
                {current === t.id && <span className="pill amber">active</span>}
              </div>
              <div className="tiny" style={{ lineHeight: 1.35 }}>{t.blurb}</div>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 3, marginTop: 1 }}>
                <button
                  className="btn"
                  style={{ fontSize: 8.5, padding: '1px 5px' }}
                  onClick={() => { togglePreview(preview === t.id ? null : t.id); setSelected(t.id); }}
                >
                  {preview === t.id ? 'Stop' : 'Preview'}
                </button>
                <button
                  className="btn primary"
                  style={{ fontSize: 8.5, padding: '1px 5px' }}
                  onClick={() => { setSetting('theme', t.id); togglePreview(null); toast(`${t.name} applied`); }}
                >
                  Apply
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="tiny">
          Themes persist with your settings. High contrast is available in Settings › Accessibility.
        </div>
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { useLocalStorageState, formatDuration, uid } from '../../hooks';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';
import { pickFiles, isVideo } from '../../os/media';
import { registerAppKeys } from '../../os/kernel';

interface VideoItem {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  url: string;
  file: File;
  size: number;
}

/** Video player: files stream from the user's device only. */
export default function VideosApp() {
  const [videos, setVideos] = useState<VideoItem[]>([]);
  const [active, setActive] = useState<VideoItem | null>(null);
  const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);
  const [duration, setDuration] = useState(0);
  const [position, setPosition] = useState(0);
  const [speed, setSpeed] = useState(1);
  const [brightness, setBrightness] = useState(1);
  const [subtitleUrl, setSubtitleUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [subName, setSubName] = useState('');
  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null);
  const [positions, setPositions] = useLocalStorageState<Record<string, number>>('owl:video-positions', {});
  const [volume, setVolume] = useLocalStorageState<number>('owl:video-volume', 0.9);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);

  const add = async () => {
    const picked = await pickFiles('video/*', true, false);
    const items = picked.filter(isVideo).map((p) => ({
      id: uid('vid'), name: p.name, url: URL.createObjectURL(p.file), file: p.file, size: p.size,
    }));
    if (!items.length) { if (picked.length) toast('No video files selected'); return; }
    setVideos((prev) => [...prev, ...items]);
    toast(`Added ${items.length} video${items.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}`);
  };

  const openVideo = (v: VideoItem) => {
    setActive(v);
    setPlaying(false);
    window.setTimeout(() => {
      const el = videoRef.current;
      if (!el) return;
      el.currentTime = positions[v.id] || 0;
      el.playbackRate = speed;
      void el.play().then(() => setPlaying(true)).catch(() => {});
    }, 120);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = videoRef.current;
    if (!el) return;
    el.volume = volume;
    el.playbackRate = speed;
  }, [volume, speed, active]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = videoRef.current;
    if (!el || !active) return;
    const save = () => setPositions((prev) => ({ ...prev, [active.id]: el.currentTime }));
    el.addEventListener('timeupdate', () => setPosition(el.currentTime));
    el.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', () => setDuration(el.duration));
    el.addEventListener('pause', save);
    const int = window.setInterval(save, 5000);
    return () => { save(); window.clearInterval(int); };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [active]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const off = registerAppKeys((e) => {
      if (!active) return false;
      const el = videoRef.current;
      if (!el) return false;
      if (e.key === ' ') { if (el.paused) { void el.play(); setPlaying(true); } else { el.pause(); setPlaying(false); } return true; }
      if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') { el.currentTime = Math.min(el.duration || 0, el.currentTime + 5); return true; }
      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') { el.currentTime = Math.max(0, el.currentTime - 5); return true; }
      return false;
    });
    return off;
  }, [active]);

  const pickSubtitle = () => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file';
    input.accept = '.vtt,.srt,text/vtt';
    input.onchange = async () => {
      const file = input.files?.[0];
      if (!file) return;
      let text = await file.text();
      if (/\.srt$/i.test(file.name)) text = srtToVtt(text);
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([text], { type: 'text/vtt' }));
      setSubtitleUrl(url);
      setSubName(file.name);
      toast(`Subtitles loaded: ${file.name}`);
    };
    input.click();
  };

  if (active) {
    return (
      <AppFrame
        title={active.name}
        icon="video"
        actions={<button className="hbtn" onClick={() => { setActive(null); setPlaying(false); setSubtitleUrl(null); }}>Library</button>}
        menu={[
          { id: 'library', label: 'Back to library', icon: 'list', run: () => { setActive(null); setPlaying(false); setSubtitleUrl(null); } },
          { id: 'subs', label: 'Load subtitles', icon: 'memo', run: () => pickSubtitle() },
          { id: 'speed', label: 'Next speed', icon: 'play', run: () => setSpeed((s) => (s >= 2 ? 0.5 : +(s + 0.25).toFixed(2))) },
          { id: 'brightness', label: 'Reset brightness', icon: 'sun', run: () => setBrightness(1) },
        ]}
      >
        <div style={{ flex: 1, background: '#000', position: 'relative', display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center', overflow: 'hidden' }}>
          <video
            ref={videoRef}
            src={active.url}
            controls={false}
            playsInline
            crossOrigin="anonymous"
            onClick={() => {
              const el = videoRef.current;
              if (!el) return;
              if (el.paused) { void el.play(); setPlaying(true); } else { el.pause(); setPlaying(false); }
            }}
            style={{ maxWidth: '100%', maxHeight: '100%', filter: `brightness(${brightness})`, background: '#000' }}
          >
            {subtitleUrl && <track kind="subtitles" src={subtitleUrl} default label="Subtitles" />}
          </video>
          {!playing && (
            <button
              onClick={() => { const el = videoRef.current; if (el) { void el.play(); setPlaying(true); } }}
              aria-label="Play video"
              style={{ position: 'absolute', width: 42, height: 42, borderRadius: '50%', border: '1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.5)', background: 'rgba(0,0,0,.55)', color: '#fff', display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center', cursor: 'pointer' }}
            >
              <Icon name="play" size={18} />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
        <div style={{ padding: '3px 6px', borderTop: '1px solid var(--line)' }}>
          <div className="progress" style={{ height: 3 }}><i style={{ width: `${duration ? (position / duration) * 100 : 0}%` }} /></div>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between' }} className="tiny">
            <span>{formatDuration(position)}</span><span>{formatDuration(duration)}</span>
          </div>
          <input
            type="range" min={0} max={duration || 0} value={position} step={0.5}
            onChange={(e) => { const v = Number(e.target.value); setPosition(v); if (videoRef.current) videoRef.current.currentTime = v; }}
            style={{ width: '100%', accentColor: 'var(--hl)' }} aria-label="Seek" inputMode="none"
          />
          <div className="btnRow" style={{ alignItems: 'center' }}>
            <button className="btn" onClick={() => {
              const el = videoRef.current;
              if (!el) return;
              if (el.paused) { void el.play(); setPlaying(true); } else { el.pause(); setPlaying(false); }
            }}>{playing ? 'Pause' : 'Play'}</button>
            <button className="btn" onClick={() => { setSpeed(speed === 2 ? 0.5 : speed === 0.5 ? 1 : speed === 1 ? 1.5 : speed === 1.5 ? 2 : 1); }}>{speed}×</button>
            <button className="btn" onClick={pickSubtitle}>{subName ? 'Subs ✓' : 'Subtitles'}</button>
            <button className="btn" onClick={() => setBrightness((b) => (b >= 1.4 ? 0.7 : +(b + 0.15).toFixed(2)))}>☀ {Math.round(brightness * 100)}%</button>
            <button
              className="btn"
              onClick={() => {
                const el = videoRef.current as any;
                if (!el) return;
                if (el.requestFullscreen) void el.requestFullscreen();
                else if (el.webkitEnterFullscreen) el.webkitEnterFullscreen();
                else toast('Fullscreen is not supported here');
              }}
            >Full</button>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 3, flex: 1 }}>
              <Icon name="volume" size={10} />
              <input type="range" min={0} max={1} step={0.02} value={volume} onChange={(e) => setVolume(Number(e.target.value))} style={{ flex: 1, accentColor: 'var(--hl)' }} aria-label="Volume" inputMode="none" />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </AppFrame>
    );
  }

  return (
    <AppFrame
      title="Videos"
      icon="video"
      actions={<button className="hbtn" onClick={() => void add()}><Icon name="plus" size={9} /> Add</button>}
      menu={[
        { id: 'add', label: 'Add videos', icon: 'plus', run: () => { void add(); } },
        { id: 'pictures', label: 'Open Pictures', icon: 'image', run: () => useStore.getState().openApp('pictures') },
        { id: 'flip', label: 'Open Owl Web', icon: 'globe', run: () => useStore.getState().openApp('web') },
      ]}
    >
      <div className="appBody">
        {videos.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="empty">
            <div className="big">🎬</div>
            <div><b>Play videos from this device</b></div>
            <div className="tiny" style={{ maxWidth: 300 }}>
              Everything plays locally through object URLs. Nothing is uploaded, and playback
              positions are remembered per file.
            </div>
            <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => void add()}>Pick video files</button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="owlSection">{videos.length} video{videos.length > 1 ? 's' : ''} · local only</div>
            {videos.map((v) => (
              <button key={v.id} className="rowBtn" onClick={() => openVideo(v)}>
                <Icon name="video" size={13} />
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'flex-start', minWidth: 0 }}>
                  <span style={{ overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap', maxWidth: 230 }}>{v.name}</span>
                  <span className="tiny">{positions[v.id] ? `Resume at ${formatDuration(positions[v.id])}` : 'Not started'}</span>
                </div>
                <span className="meta">{(v.size / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB</span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}

function srtToVtt(srt: string): string {
  const body = srt
    .replace(/\r+/g, '')
    .replace(/^\d+\n/gm, '')
    .replace(/(\d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2}),(\d{3})/g, '$1.$2');
  return `WEBVTT\n\n${body}`;
}
```

### `src/apps/VoiceNotes/VoiceNotesApp.tsx`

_8621 bytes · 184 lines_

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../os/store';
import { useCollection, uid, formatDuration, downloadBlob } from '../../hooks';
import { AppFrame } from '../../components/Shell';
import { Field } from '../../components/Field';
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon';
import { DB_KEYS } from '../../os/db';
import type { VoiceNote } from '../../os/types';
import { tickSound } from '../../os/audio';

/** Voice recorder: MediaRecorder → IndexedDB on this device. Never uploaded. */
export default function VoiceNotesApp() {
  const [notes, setNotes] = useCollection<VoiceNote>(DB_KEYS.voiceNotes);
  const [recording, setRecording] = useState(false);
  const [elapsed, setElapsed] = useState(0);
  const [playingId, setPlayingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [renaming, setRenaming] = useState<{ id: string; value: string } | null>(null);
  const [level, setLevel] = useState(0);
  const recorder = useRef<MediaRecorder | null>(null);
  const chunks = useRef<Blob[]>([]);
  const streamRef = useRef<MediaStream | null>(null);
  const analyser = useRef<AnalyserNode | null>(null);
  const audioCtx = useRef<AudioContext | null>(null);
  const rafRef = useRef(0);
  const toast = useStore((s) => s.toast);
  const showDialog = useStore((s) => s.showDialog);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!recording) return;
    const t = window.setInterval(() => setElapsed((e) => e + 1), 1000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(t);
  }, [recording]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!recording) return;
    const loop = () => {
      if (analyser.current) {
        const data = new Uint8Array(analyser.current.frequencyBinCount);
        analyser.current.getByteFrequencyData(data);
        setLevel(data.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / data.length / 255);
      }
      rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    };
    rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef.current);
  }, [recording]);

  const start = async () => {
    if (!navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia || typeof MediaRecorder === 'undefined') {
      toast('Recording is not supported in this browser');
      return;
    }
    try {
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
      streamRef.current = stream;
      const AC = window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext;
      audioCtx.current = new AC();
      const src = audioCtx.current.createMediaStreamSource(stream);
      const a = audioCtx.current.createAnalyser();
      a.fftSize = 64;
      src.connect(a);
      analyser.current = a;
      const mimeCandidates = ['audio/webm;codecs=opus', 'audio/webm', 'audio/mp4', 'audio/ogg'];
      const mimeType = mimeCandidates.find((m) => MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(m));
      const rec = new MediaRecorder(stream, mimeType ? { mimeType } : undefined);
      chunks.current = [];
      rec.ondataavailable = (e) => { if (e.data.size) chunks.current.push(e.data); };
      rec.onstop = async () => {
        const blob = new Blob(chunks.current, { type: mimeType || 'audio/webm' });
        const duration = elapsed;
        if (blob.size > 0) {
          setNotes((prev) => [
            { id: uid('vn'), name: `Voice ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}`, blob, at: Date.now(), duration },
            ...prev,
          ]);
          toast('Voice note saved on this device');
        }
      };
      rec.start();
      recorder.current = rec;
      setRecording(true);
      setElapsed(0);
      tickSound(0.4);
    } catch {
      toast('Microphone permission denied or unavailable');
    }
  };

  const stop = () => {
    recorder.current?.stop();
    streamRef.current?.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
    audioCtx.current?.close().catch(() => {});
    audioCtx.current = null;
    analyser.current = null;
    setRecording(false);
    tickSound(0.3);
  };

  const play = (note: VoiceNote) => {
    if (playingId === note.id) {
      setPlayingId(null);
      return;
    }
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(note.blob);
    const audio = new Audio(url);
    audio.onended = () => { setPlayingId(null); URL.revokeObjectURL(url); };
    audio.onerror = () => { setPlayingId(null); toast('Could not play that note'); };
    void audio.play().then(() => setPlayingId(note.id)).catch(() => toast('Playback blocked'));
  };

  return (
    <AppFrame
      title="Voice Notes"
      icon="mic"
      actions={<span className="tiny">{notes.length} saved</span>}
      menu={[
        { id: recording ? 'stop' : 'record', label: recording ? 'Stop recording' : 'Start recording', icon: recording ? 'pause' : 'mic', run: () => { if (recording) stop(); else void start(); } },
        { id: 'rename-prompt', label: 'Rename newest note', icon: 'memo', run: () => { const first = notes[notes.length - 1]; if (first) setRenaming({ id: first.id, value: first.name }); } },
        { id: 'storage', label: 'App storage', icon: 'database', run: () => useStore.getState().openApp('files') },
      ]}
    >
      <div className="appBody pad" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 6 }}>
        <div className="card" style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 5, placeItems: 'center', padding: 8 }}>
          <div style={{ width: 58, height: 58, borderRadius: '50%', border: '1px solid var(--line)', display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center', background: 'radial-gradient(circle at 40% 35%, var(--panel-3), var(--panel))', position: 'relative' }}>
            <Icon name="mic" size={24} style={{ opacity: recording ? 1 : 0.75 }} />
            {recording && (
              <span
                style={{
                  position: 'absolute', inset: -4, borderRadius: '50%',
                  border: `2px solid rgba(242,88,91,${0.2 + level * 0.8})`,
                  transform: `scale(${1 + level * 0.25})`, transition: 'transform 70ms linear',
                }}
              />
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="mono" style={{ fontSize: 17, fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)' }}>{formatDuration(elapsed)}</div>
          <button className={`btn ${recording ? 'danger' : 'primary'}`} onClick={recording ? stop : start}>
            {recording ? '■ Stop recording' : '● Start recording'}
          </button>
          <div className="tiny" style={{ textAlign: 'center', maxWidth: 280 }}>
            Recordings are saved to this browser's IndexedDB. They never leave your device.
          </div>
        </div>

        {notes.length === 0 && <div className="empty"><div className="tiny">No voice notes yet.</div></div>}
        {notes.map((n) => (
          <div key={n.id} style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4, alignItems: 'center' }}>
            {renaming?.id === n.id ? (
              <>
                <Field value={renaming.value} onChange={(v) => setRenaming({ id: n.id, value: v })} autoFocus maxLength={40} onSubmit={() => {
                  setNotes((prev) => prev.map((x) => (x.id === n.id ? { ...x, name: renaming.value || x.name } : x)));
                  setRenaming(null);
                }} />
                <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => {
                  setNotes((prev) => prev.map((x) => (x.id === n.id ? { ...x, name: renaming.value || x.name } : x)));
                  setRenaming(null);
                }}>OK</button>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <button className="rowBtn" style={{ flex: 1 }} onClick={() => play(n)}>
                  <Icon name={playingId === n.id ? 'pause' : 'play'} size={11} />
                  <span style={{ overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap', maxWidth: 180 }}>{n.name}</span>
                  <span className="meta">{formatDuration(n.duration || 0)}</span>
                </button>
                <button className="iconBtn" onClick={() => setRenaming({ id: n.id, value: n.name })} aria-label="Rename">✎</button>
                <button className="iconBtn" onClick={() => downloadBlob(n.blob, `${n.name}.webm`)} aria-label="Export">⤓</button>
                <button
                  className="iconBtn"
                  onClick={() => showDialog({
                    title: 'Delete voice note?', body: 'This cannot be undone.', confirmLabel: 'Delete', danger: true,
                    onConfirm: () => setNotes((prev) => prev.filter((x) => x.id !== n.id)),
                  })}
                  aria-label="Delete"
                >✕</button>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </AppFrame>
  );
}
```
